# Qinao Core AI Agent Controlled-Document Convergence Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Convert the approved Qinao Core AI/Agent/context/memory/RSI addendum into one non-vacuous W0 controlled-document and gate closure, so the existing W1–W6 domain plans can be executed without contradictory authority, wave, or release semantics.

**Architecture:** This is a W0 plan-of-plans, not a seventh domain implementation plan. It first pins the approved spec-only commit/blob/digest as an immutable decision source, imports one exact fail-closed W0 safety patch that removes unreceipted-effect claims and repairs already-approved platform-floor/schema-test invariants, then atomically reconciles the existing seven controlled documents, maps every adopted decision onto an existing Owner Ledger owner, and adds one thin wave-gate orchestrator that composes the repository's existing owner, review, non-empty Swift-filter, platform-proof, and static checkers. The safety patch creates no production authority, persistence schema, model default, or deployment route; the six existing implementation-plan documents—one convergence master plus five domain plans—remain the only W1–W6 execution units.

**Tech Stack:** Markdown, canonical JSON, Python 3 standard library and `unittest`, Git candidate-index/tree plumbing, Swift Package Manager test discovery, Xcode 27/iOS 27 platform evidence.

**Execution status:** **BLOCKED before Task 0.** This plan may be reviewed and published, but no execution branch may be created until an independent reviewer supplies the signed `qinao-source-baseline-selection-v1` record, its public-only key/principal/role trust-root bundle, the complete tested import/source-state writer-verifier named by Task 0, and an unambiguous decision for every index/worktree byte mismatch. Task 2 is likewise not authorized from the illustrative RED slices alone: before its implementation starts, its worker must first turn the closed call graph, schemas, semantic predicates, and mutation inventory below into a complete executable subplan or commit-ready TDD sequence and obtain review of that sequence. Task 4 is independently blocked until its implementer supplies a valid signed `qinao-w0-execution-intent-v1` bound to the exact Task-3 base and twelve-path write set, a reviewer distinct by both key and principal supplies the exact logical Oracle-cell schema registry and row/relation-law golden tables, their canonical bytes/root, all 165 exact case bindings and closed `OracleOutcomeV1` values, the complete canonical `qinao-k3-case-oracle-catalog-v1` bytes/root, one signed selection record nesting that exact execution intent and covering every root, and the frozen standalone Task-2 verifier publishes a passing mode-`0600` admission receipt over all four external inputs and its own complete immutable source/test identity. Task 4 must byte-copy those admitted bytes into the K3 addendum, bind their exact roots and admission receipt digest in the ledger, and never generate a schema, key, fact, law, or expectation from observed implementation output. The W1 ablation contract and W6 canary expansion are also blocked at their respective entry waves until the two separately reviewed preregistration-policy records named below fix their exact universes, metrics, numeric thresholds, sample/confidence rules, guardrails, cohorts, and freshness windows. None of these blockers may be converted into an implementation guess or coder-selected “reasonable default.”

## Global Constraints

- The minimum iOS deployment target is exactly `27.0` for every workspace-owned iOS package/app/extension slice; vendored package manifests remain upstream-owned.
- Architecture cardinality remains exactly `14 Semantic LayerCores / 4 Physical Kernels / 4 bounded ControlRings / 7 orthogonal planes`.
- The approved decision source is exactly commit `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b`, tree `e544727c57ffd340fde274b8caeded6bd39bde09`, spec blob `15e82ec63b9e9bf1ebf17575799b9474061a292e`, and spec SHA-256 `a5599c402b110951aee108d18625153d3d90ddef9c93933c580970c0be0b71dd` for `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-qinao-coreai-agent-context-memory-rsi-design.md`. Candidate HEAD must descend from that commit, the path must retain that exact blob, and no commit in `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b..HEAD` may touch the path, including a later revert to identical bytes.
- The exact seven controlled documents are the architecture design, the convergence master, and five domain plans listed below. The 2026-07-19 addendum, the K3 addendum, the recovery companion, Xcode release-build policy, this plan, evidence, and gate manifests are inputs or proof surfaces—not additional architecture authorities.
- Qinao SDK remains model-neutral. Concrete Qwen, MiniCPM, Granite, Core AI, MLX, and FoundationModels packages stay outside the SDK and depend inward through value-only Provider/Proposal contracts.
- The Agent-runtime research inputs are pinned only as design evidence: OpenAI Codex `0fb559f0f6e231a88ac02ea002d3ecd248e2b515`, Pi `8dc78834cde4e329284cf505f9e3f99763df5529`, Hermes Agent `36f2a966c7f9f69987494b867c3dcf96b69a5766`, and Claude Agent SDK Python `2d4ef9466427970a6db5e567253bae2b2825010a`. No Node, Python, CLI harness, transcript/session store, or external framework authority enters the iPhone runtime.
- Core AI-only applies to the Qwen/MiniCPM/Granite portfolio after sealed W6 cutover; it does not force an evidence-losing migration of the allowlisted Core ML context-classifier microhead.
- The reviewed source candidates are full commits `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B@851bf6e806efd8d0a36b00ddf55e13ccb7b8cd0a`, `openbmb/MiniCPM5-1B@4e9de7a0778dc1c362e983e6858f0e77542cbdca`, `openbmb/MiniCPM-V-4.6@8169864629825dc1d755a5aa1cd8b5935dcbc83f`, and `ibm-granite/granite-embedding-97m-multilingual-r2@835ad14087e140460703cf0fae09f97d469d65c2`; they are source candidates, not certification. W1 remains blocked until the exact selected files/LFS-or-Xet objects and digests are sealed. The initial Qwen variant is independently named `qwen3.5-4b-text-only/no-mtp-certified`: `vision=false` and `mtpEnabled=false` are certification flags, not claims that the source lacks vision/MTP material. Enabling either requires a distinct signed material/profile/StateABI/plan and independent certification.
- No task may introduce `ModelPromotionStore`, a second model registry, an asset/specialization/cache authority, a shared recovery authority/store, a second deployment state machine, or an independent `BASStateCommitStore`.
- `ModelMaterialManifestArtifactID` is the sole Core AI evolution of canonical `bundleDigest`; pointer paths, bookmarks, and caches never select model identity.
- W1 declares immutable model-neutral values only, including finite typed delegation slots, ContextContinuity, Provider-profile variants, progressive-disclosure metadata, source-scoped delivery values, ΩE read-set contracts, and the pre-registered source-mechanism/finite-interaction ablation vocabulary. W1 contains no ablation verdict or adoption. W2 owns K3 durability, idempotent Input Event disposition, strict rebase, continuity, and recovery. W3 remains pre-physical and owns historical episode retrieval plus the one exact context compiler. W4 exposes only single-Provider-step pure prepare/currentness hooks while the incumbent high-level Agent remains the sole production semantic/adoption authority and Core AI/AFM remain shadow/device-validation. W5 keeps production publication disabled. W6 alone integrates the mechanical all-member executor, complete replay/delivery manifest, incumbent retirement, ablation evidence/adoption, and both deployment joins.
- E4 is immutable candidate evidence, not deployment authority. Join 1 is `E4 + separately sealed canary tree/cohort -> canary deployment -> immutable E5`; Join 2 is `E5 + separately sealed full Release tree -> full production cutover`.
- Runtime revocation order is `L14 decision -> K3 RevocationFenceReceipt -> K4 revoke`; ingress, queue, chunk, claim, and seal revalidate the same revocation generation.
- A Provider call, publication, or effect that may have crossed its boundary is query/reconcile/seal-only. It is never reinvoked, republished, or redispatched.
- The current `coreai-torch 0.4.0` `.aimodel` outputs are denied historical evidence on iOS 27 beta 2 or later; exact compatible reconversion and full recertification are mandatory.
- Cold 40 token/s and sustained 30 token/s remain workload-qualified optional claims. They become a release gate only for a product/marketing surface that explicitly selects or claims the named `cold40`/`sustained30` tier for one exact profile; ordinary production-profile selection, W6 architecture completion, and a base release do not activate that tier.
- Every negative source scan first proves that every input exists and that its scan set is non-empty, then uses its manifest-declared scanner over the admitted candidate bytes. `utf8-regex-v1` scans exact decoded text; `swift-active-source-v1` first reuses the Owner Ledger checker's checked, offset-preserving Swift lexical seam so comments, string bytes, and unambiguous extended-regex bytes cannot satisfy a source invariant while executable string interpolation remains visible. Because bare-slash regex and prefix/infix slash syntax are lexically ambiguous without the Swift parser, scanner v1 rejects an expression-prefix bare slash instead of masking possibly executable code. Scanner selection is explicit, never inferred from a glob. A decode/read/lex/regex error is a gate error, never “no match.” Legacy shell gates that still use `rg` must separately distinguish status `0` (match), `1` (no match), and `2+` (gate error).
- Every cumulative gate independently validates the current wave's `m_creates`, `ea_extensions`, and `schema_fixtures` manifests. A zero count is legal only through that category's typed, reviewed `notApplicable` object bound to the approved spec pin and current wave; historical Artifact Mesh candidates/receipts never satisfy a current-wave category. Because this W0 changes controlled documents and gates but no production owner/schema/wire, W0 is exact `0/0/0`, with three separately typed `notApplicable` records. The 36 adopted E/A decisions below become non-empty ExtensionGate candidates only in their named W1–W6 implementation waves.
- Every Swift test filter is executed through `scripts/run_nonempty_swift_filter.py` with exactly one `--require-suite`; raw `swift test --filter` is not an acceptance gate. Local validation is one Task-0-pinned `compile -> nonexecuting discovery -> deterministic schedule -> nonempty sharded execution -> common result -> successful Completed` protocol. A distinct native, non-App-Sandbox, dedicated-unprivileged **compile broker** may run only the registry-fixed SwiftPM build-tests operation against an immutable gateway materialization and unique external scratch, with SwiftPM's own plugin/build sandbox enabled; it never runs tests or candidate shell/Python. It freezes `Package.swift`, `Package.resolved`, every dependency/plugin/macro/resource input, Xcode/Swift/CoreML tool identities, effective argv, environment, process tree, product/source/runtime-load closure, and auxiliary artifacts. Signed App-Sandbox discovery/execution profiles and the disjoint toolchain-validation broker then consume only adopted installations from that seal. XCTest execution always uses explicit `-XCTest` selectors and the sealed bundle directory; Swift Testing uses the pinned helper, sealed bundle executable, absent event leaf, and repeated exact filters. Neither framework ever runs its all-tests form, and there is no fictitious `xctest -l`: typed IDs come from separately authenticated nonexecuting discovery and independently decoded native/helper evidence. The runner never invokes SwiftPM, `coremlc`, a compiler, plugin, or macro. The literal SwiftPM flag `--disable-sandbox` is forbidden. A clean ephemeral CI boundary may retain its separately pinned ordinary SwiftPM path. The Xcode-27 golden must prove both packages, both frameworks, every profile/lane, exact discovery/event/path/argv mapping, nonempty shards, RED and GREEN outcomes, relocatable source/product/load closure, CoreML/resource/tool confinement, and sandbox inheritance; until it does, Task 0 remains BLOCKED.
- Python gate tests use standard-library `unittest` through the runner-owned `PYTHON_UNIT_TEST_CONTRACTS_V1`; Qinao gates do not depend on a locally installed `pytest` and write no `__pycache__` into a candidate. The only direct workflow Python entry is the deterministic, unprivileged CI runner itself under the clean ephemeral-runner exception below; local implementation/review/certification invokes Python only through the gateway.
- Every normative shell block is fail-fast: execute it under `set -euo pipefail` even where repeated boilerplate is omitted from a displayed snippet. A RED is accepted only when `candidate-review --expected-outcome exact-red` returns an authenticated `redConfirmed` receipt for the registry-frozen expected validator/test ID, exact error code/stage, non-zero discovered/executed test surface, passing positive control, and zero unauthorized effect. Generic “any non-zero”, `if command; then exit 1; fi`, import/syntax/argument failure, zero discovery, or a later successful command can never establish RED.
- Every Python invocation in baseline/certification shells and CI uses `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1` (or `python3 -B`), every Swift gate uses its external private scratch path, and the candidate gate rejects even ignored untracked inputs. No checker may create an input under the candidate root while certifying it.
- Task 0D is the sole production-code exception in W0: its exact 47-path fail-closed safety unit modifies five existing production Swift files and three existing iOS-floor scripts, adds three test-support/freeze files, migrates the remaining test callers needed for full-package health, removes one duplicate async-test helper, and freezes the approved-proof Git-attribute policy. The Step-0B seed separately imports the final reviewed W0 evidence checker/tests already converted to authenticated observation/execution transports. These changes remove synthetic execution/direct-effect claims and impossible backward-fixture assertions; they create no production owner, persistence schema, model asset/default, network path, or deployment route. Tasks 1–5 change only controlled documents and gate/tooling/fixture files.
- The current shared worktree contains pre-existing staged, unstaged, and untracked W0 work. Execution must use an isolated clean worktree made from a reviewed committed descendant of `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b`; never stage or commit unrelated work from the current dirty worktree.

- Create by audited migration: the exact 21-path `scripts/fixtures/qinao-k4-platform-probe/` tuple in the File Responsibility Map.
- Create after bootstrap through one gateway-produced CandidateUnit: the exact 13-path sequence-1 silicon/runtime successor tuple in Step 3; sequence-0 evidence remains immutable historical input.
- Sanitize/seal: `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/README.md`
- Sanitize/seal: `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/status.json`
- Keep absent from every committed candidate: every normalized path with prefix `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/spike/logs/`, plus exact source-only paths `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/spike/pre-w5-extension-signed-entitlements.plist` and `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/spike/pre-w5-host-signed-entitlements.plist`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: a reviewed committed descendant of approved spec commit `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b` that contains exactly one later single-path publication commit for this revised plan, preserves the approved spec blob without any intervening path touch, and keeps every unrelated dirty source byte outside that commit.
- Produces: one clean execution branch/worktree plus one first ordinary evidence transaction. All unrelated current dirty state remains byte-for-byte untouched; the only selected source-worktree exceptions are the exact 32-path seed, three Artifact Mesh receipts, 47-path safety unit, and 25-path K4 sanitation unit declared below. Each target path appears in exactly one bootstrap unit and consumes exactly one selected final postimage; no directory, index-wide snapshot, intermediate version, or newest-looking worktree version is implicitly accepted.

- [ ] **Step 0A: Snapshot the dirty source and create a clean sanitation worktree**

The currently staged subtree at normalized prefix `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/` is not yet immutable evidence: it is absent from HEAD, files beneath its exact `spike/logs/` prefix contain an absolute `/Users/...` workspace/account path, and a later log claims that such a path is absent. Do **not** commit or push that staged shape. Before creating the execution worktree, make one separately reviewed baseline commit that (a) migrates only the exact 21 probe paths in the File Responsibility Map into `scripts/fixtures/qinao-k4-platform-probe/` with `source-manifest-v1.json`, and (b) retains only `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/README.md` and `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/status.json` as a redacted structured preliminary summary plus non-secret digests. Exclude raw build/archive/install logs, provisioning/account diagnostics, embedded profiles, raw UUID/UDID/certificate data, and every self-contradictory redaction claim. The tooling fixture remains explicitly mutable through Task 3 hardening; the sealed preliminary observation summary is immutable and is never copied, renamed, or promoted as approved proof.

Create `scripts/check_qinao_preliminary_k4_source.py` and its standard-library mutation suite in the same baseline commit. It owns the exact two summary paths plus the exact 21 migrated fixture paths and reads only gateway-supplied stage-0 mode/blob/content records through the fixed read-only blob-provider FD; it never shells out to `git show` or opens the caller's index. The native bootstrap engine independently reconstructs those records through `git-read`. The checker rejects missing/extra/mode-other-than-`100644`/symlink/conflict/worktree drift, rejects every tracked path under the old `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-spike/spike/` prefix, and scans the actual admitted bytes for `/Users/`, account-home paths, provisioning portal text, raw profile UUID/UDID/certificate bodies, and contradictions between summary claims and retained bytes. The tuple is globally sorted and byte-pinned by its test; no directory/glob is accepted as an inventory substitute. The review records only non-secret digests/counts and commits the checker, test, migrated fixture, and sanitized preliminary summary as one exact baseline unit. If safe source inputs cannot be separated from private observations, leave W0 BLOCKED and do not create the execution branch.

The writing session publishes this plan first as one separately reviewed single-path commit whose **only parent is exactly** `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b`; that one-time publication is outside execution and is the only admitted path-only commit. Immediately after publication, an external mode-`0600` canonical receipt records exactly `schema_version`, `parent_commit`, `publication_commit`, `publication_tree`, `plan_path`, `plan_blob`, `plan_sha256`, and `commit_changes`. The values are reconstructed from Git; `commit_changes` is exactly `[["M","docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-19-qinao-coreai-agent-controlled-document-convergence.md"]]`, and the receipt is never committed because embedding its own publication identity would be self-referential.

Current bootstrap is an explicit human-review stop. Before selection, reviewer authorizes one read-only `snapshot-source-state` with `QinaoSourceStateSnapshotAuthorizationV1 = {schema_version:1,repository_path_commitment,output_directory_path_commitment,expected_source_head,expected_source_head_tree,expected_source_index_tree,expected_object_format,record_kinds:["cached_index","tracked_worktree","untracked_ignored"],reviewer_trust_root,reviewer_trust_root_sha256,issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,reviewer_key_id,reviewer_principal_id,reviewer_role:"source-baseline-reviewer",reviewer_epoch,signature_algorithm,signature}` under its declared signature domain. `GatewayPreselectionRepositoryIdentityV1 = {schema_version:1,object_format,repository_root_device_inode_digest,repository_root_path_commitment,common_git_directory_device_inode_digest,common_git_directory_path_commitment,expected_source_head,expected_source_head_tree,expected_source_index_tree,snapshot_authorization_sha256,identity_root}`; root uses `QINAO-PRESELECTION-REPOSITORY-IDENTITY-V1\0` over object without itself and contains no future source-selection field. The snapshot invocation's repository identity digest, body before/after, and authorization all use this type; postselection Candidate identity is derived only later.

After a stable double observation but before any output effect, daemon journals `GatewaySourceStateObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,authorization_sha256,preselection_repository_identity:GatewayPreselectionRepositoryIdentityV1,cached_index_record,tracked_worktree_record,untracked_ignored_record,record_sha256s,record_stream_roots,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,observed_at_utc,disposition:"immutableRecordsObserved"}`. The three fields embed the **complete canonical metadata records**, including entry arrays, not private raw postimage contents; every root/SHA rederives from them. Source drift after this child does not authorize resnapshotting. Atomic-directory publication Prepared names that observation and exact absent output. `SourceSnapshotLeafWriteObservationRowV1 = {leaf_ordinal,kind,relative_leaf,filesystem_mode:"0600",record_length,record_sha256,leaf_identity,parent_directory_identity_after_fsync}` occurs in exact `cached_index,tracked_worktree,untracked_ignored` order; `leaf_write_observation_root = SHA256("QINAO-SOURCE-SNAPSHOT-LEAF-WRITES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`. `SourceSnapshotStagingAttemptRowV1 = {attempt_ordinal,staging_directory_identity,leaf_write_observation_rows,leaf_write_observation_root,outcome}` uses contiguous ordinals and outcome `completeSealed({tree_seal_receipt_sha256}) | partialQuarantined({quarantine_receipt_sha256})`; one or two rows exist and only final is complete. `staging_attempt_root = SHA256("QINAO-SOURCE-SNAPSHOT-STAGING-ATTEMPTS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalAttemptRows)) || canonicalAttemptRows)`. `SourceSnapshotTreeSealBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,observation_receipt_sha256,attempt_ordinal,staging_directory_identity,leaf_write_observation_rows,leaf_write_observation_root,staging_tree_root,sealed_at_utc}` reopens all leaves. Its Adoption result is acyclic `GatewaySourceStateSnapshotPublicationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,observation_receipt_sha256,authorization_sha256,output_parent_identity_before,output_parent_identity_after,staging_attempt_rows,staging_attempt_root,final_tree_seal_receipt_sha256,staging_directory_identity,final_directory_identity,record_leaf_identities,record_output_root,rename_no_replace_observation_sha256,parent_fsync_observation_sha256,published_at_utc}`. `GatewaySourceStateSnapshotBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,authorization_sha256,reviewer_trust_root_sha256,preselection_repository_identity:GatewayPreselectionRepositoryIdentityV1,source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,source_state_records,record_output_rows,record_output_root,output_directory_identity,observation_receipt_sha256,publication_prepared_receipt_sha256,publication_adoption_receipt_sha256,staging_attempt_rows,staging_attempt_root,final_tree_seal_receipt_sha256,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,observed_at_utc,disposition:"readOnlySnapshot"}` cross-equals the seal/publication leaf and attempt rows/roots and uses separate output root `QINAO-SOURCE-STATE-SNAPSHOT-OUTPUTS-V1\0`. A partial staging directory is quarantined whole and permits at most one retry while source/target/parent remain exact. Root/domain/order mutations fail; matching Completed is mandatory.

The canonical external mode-`0600` `qinao-source-baseline-selection-v1` top level is exactly: `schema_version = 1`; five-field `approved_spec`; `source_state_snapshot:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1`; `source_head`; `source_head_tree`; `source_index_tree`; `source_state_records`; `selected_inputs`; `ordered_commit_units`; `trusted_execution_gateway`; `source_import_tool`; `admission_python`; `signature_verifier`; `validation_contract_registry`; `git_service_identity`; canonical public trust root/SHA; reviewer key/principal/role/epoch; issued/expiry/nonce/algorithm/signature. Records are exact `cached_index`, `tracked_worktree`, `untracked_ignored`, each `{record_sha256,stream_sha256,entry_count}` equal snapshot. Tracked entry is `{path,status,old,new}` where sides are `absent({reason}) | present({git_mode,git_blob,content_length,raw_sha256})`; `git_mode` is `100644|100755`. Snapshot uses no-renames, rejects conflict/typechange, stable-opens bytes, and computes Git blob itself rather than trusting zero OID. Cached entries use analogous stage-0 Git mode. Untracked/ignored rows are `{path,classification,filesystem_mode,derived_target_git_mode,content_length,content_sha256}`; only owner/group/other executable-bit presence maps deterministically to `100755`, otherwise regular file maps `100644`. Symlink/special file, setuid/setgid/sticky bits, ACL/xattr, hardlink, or other ambiguous metadata is unselectable. Importer independently derives blob and mode. Mutations cover M/D/typechange/rename-looking pairs, zero OID, permission/metadata drift, and post-snapshot rewrite.

`selected_inputs[]` is sorted/unique by target and exactly `{source_kind,source_record_sha256,source_entry_key,source_path,target_path,target_git_mode,git_blob,raw_sha256}`. `source_kind = cachedIndex([]) | trackedWorktree([]) | untracked([])`; its keyed record resolves one present new postimage whose Git mode (or untracked derived target Git mode), blob, and bytes match; ignored/deleted/ambiguous rows are unselectable. Entry keys hash complete tagged records under kind-specific domains. `ordered_commit_units[]` is contiguous and each member exactly `{ordinal,parent_binding,result_tree,message,changes:GatewayBootstrapChangeRowV1[],validation_suite_ids,swift_validation_inventories}`. The signed unit directly embeds complete bootstrap change rows with closed old/new/source-locator tags, sorted by path, so runtime does not reinterpret older `(status,path,blob)` tuples. Inventories are complete acyclic objects sorted by package (or `[]`), signed with unit, and rederived from result tree. `parent_binding = sourceHead({commit}) | priorUnit({immediately_preceding_ordinal})`; suites sorted/unique. Every selected input is consumed once, trees reconstructed, future commit IDs/times never predicted.

`trusted_execution_gateway` is exactly `{gateway_id,path_commitment,real_path_commitment,raw_sha256,cdhash,designated_requirement,version,trust_anchor_manifest_sha256,test_vector_set_sha256}`. `source_import_tool` is exactly `{tool_id,path_commitment,real_path_commitment,raw_sha256,version,test_vector_set_sha256}` and selects one complete reviewed standard-library-only source-state writer/verifier/importer outside the dirty repository. `admission_python` is exactly `{python_id,path_commitment,real_path_commitment,raw_sha256,version,implementation,stdlib_closure,stdlib_closure_root}`. `signature_verifier` is exactly `{verifier_id,path_commitment,real_path_commitment,raw_sha256,version,test_vector_set_sha256}` for the differential OpenSSL verifier. `validation_contract_registry` is exactly `{registry_id:"qinao-validation-contract-registry-v1",raw_sha256,registry_root,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`. `git_service_identity` is exactly `{author_name_utf8:"Qinao Execution Gate",author_email_utf8:"execution-gate@qinao.invalid",committer_name_utf8:"Qinao Execution Gate",committer_email_utf8:"execution-gate@qinao.invalid",timezone:"+0000",identity_commitment_domain:"QINAO-GIT-SERVICE-IDENTITY-V1",identity_commitment}` where the commitment hashes the complete canonical object without itself. Every selection field is signed. Before use, the gateway compares only shape-compatible projections: `trusted_execution_gateway` without its manifest-self-hash matches the anchor's CLI identity plus gateway test-vector digest; `source_import_tool`, `admission_python`, `signature_verifier`, `validation_contract_registry`, and `git_service_identity` byte-match their explicitly named anchor records/projections below. No unlike whole objects are compared. A local path is supplied only through the mode-`0600` execution environment; it is normalized to absolute UTF-8 bytes and committed as `SHA256("QINAO-LOCAL-ABSOLUTE-PATH-V1\0" || uint64be(len(pathBytes)) || pathBytes)`, while its separately resolved path uses the distinct `QINAO-LOCAL-REAL-PATH-V1\0` domain. The signed record contains only those commitments and stable non-path IDs—never `/Users/...`, an account name, or another raw machine path. Each supplied executable/source leaf must commitment-match, be byte-identical after resolution to the committed real-path identity, and be a regular, link-count-one, owner-held, non-writable reviewed file. `stdlib_closure[]` is sorted/unique by `(module,path_commitment)` and each member is exactly `{module,path_commitment,real_path_commitment,mode,raw_sha256}` for every non-built-in module transitively imported by the importer or standalone admission verifier; its root is `SHA256("QINAO-ADMISSION-PYTHON-STDLIB-CLOSURE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalClosureBytes)) || canonicalClosureBytes)`. The raw path-to-commitment observation exists only in the external private source-state evidence. Mutation tests move equal bytes to another path, alter either commitment, inject a user-home path into any commit-safe object, and require failure.

The validation registry is a separately reviewed Task-0 input at exact root-owned path `/Library/Application Support/Qinao/ExecutionGate/qinao-validation-contract-registry-v1.json`, never generated by candidate bytes. `QinaoValidationContractRegistryV1 = {schema_version:1,registry_id:"qinao-validation-contract-registry-v1",profile_rows,invocation_contract_rows,validation_primitive_contract_rows,swift_gate_contract_rows,ordinary_validation_contract_rows,derivation_contract_rows,failure_contract_rows,registry_root,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`. Profile rows sort/unique by profile ID and are `{profile_id,source_view_kind,ordered_invocation_contract_ids,swift_inventory_derivation_ids,ordinary_validation_ids,expected_dispositions,forbidden_error_classes,time_limit_ms,memory_limit_bytes}`; invocation arrays are declaration-order unique/nonempty and other ID arrays sorted/unique.

`ValidationCapabilityProjectionRowV1` is the closed union `backend({backend_id,backend_identity_sha256,event_transport_contract_id}) | child({child_kind,payload_schema_digest,parent_binding_rule_id}) | process({process_id,executable_identity_sha256,argv_contract_id,environment_contract_id,load_profile_id}) | effect({effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest,dispatcher_contract_id,recovery_query_contract_id})`. Backend rows sort by `(backend_id,event_transport_contract_id)`, child by `(child_kind,payload_schema_digest,parent_binding_rule_id)`, process by `(process_id,argv_contract_id,environment_contract_id)`, and effect by `(effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest)`; each key is unique. Their respective root formulas are `SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-BACKEND-CAPABILITIES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`, `SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-CHILD-CAPABILITIES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`, `SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-PROCESS-CAPABILITIES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`, and `SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-EFFECT-CAPABILITIES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`; a variant/domain mismatch fails. `ValidationPrimitiveContractRowV1 = {contract_id,contract_kind,implementation_identity_sha256,input_schema_digest,output_schema_digest,capability_projection_rows,capability_projection_root,test_vector_set_sha256}` sorts/uniques by `(contract_kind,contract_id)` and permits only rows of matching kind. `ValidationInvocationContractRowV1 = {invocation_contract_id,source_view_kind,capability_profile,backend_contract_id,ordered_child_contract_ids,ordered_process_contract_ids,ordered_effect_contract_ids,input_schema_digest,output_schema_digest,time_limit_ms,memory_limit_bytes}` sorts/uniques by invocation ID; all three contract arrays are declaration-order unique. Every referenced ID resolves exactly one primitive of the required kind, and the primitive row set equals the transitive union of all invocation references—no orphan primitive may silently acquire later authority. The invocation row set likewise equals the union of every profile's ordered IDs in both directions; each order resolves exactly, and source-view/budget bounds refine rather than exceed the profile. Unknown capability/tag, orphan/reordered contract, row/root/domain, schema/vector, or implementation substitution fails.

`SwiftGateContractRowV1 = {validation_contract_id,validation_profile_id,package_path,package_id,filter_projection,required_suite_ids,required_suite_id_set_root,expected_disposition}`; rows sort/unique by contract ID, suite IDs are declaration-order unique, their independent set root hashes the UTF-8-sorted copy, profile IDs resolve exactly one profile row, and `(profile,package_path)` maps to at most one active contract. `filter_projection` is exactly the declaration-order `"|".join(required_suite_ids)` and is diagnostic only; execution uses typed membership plus the literal-ID encoder. The wave manifest repeats contract/profile/path/filter/suites and must byte-equal those projections; it cannot nominate argv, backend, package ID, or expectation.

`OrdinaryAllowedImportRowV1 = {module_id,source_selector,allowed_symbols,allowed_symbol_root}` sorts/uniques by module ID; selector is `trustedTree({path,git_blob}) | candidateUnitPostimage({path,role})` and cannot contain its enclosing import root, a wildcard, or latest path. Symbols sort/unique by unsigned UTF-8 and `allowed_symbol_root` uses `QINAO-ORDINARY-ALLOWED-SYMBOLS-V1\0` over that complete array. `OrdinaryValidationContractRowV1 = {validation_profile_id,validation_id,script_selector,invocation_contract_id,test_surface_derivation_id,failure_contract_ids,expected_disposition,allowed_import_rows,allowed_import_root,harness_derivation_id}` sorts/uniques by `(validation_profile_id,validation_id)`; failure IDs sort/unique and the complete import rows root under `QINAO-ORDINARY-ALLOWED-IMPORTS-V1\0`. Only the enclosing contract/script selector references that root, so no fixed point exists. Every profile's `ordinary_validation_ids` equals its row set bidirectionally, every checker/manifest slot resolves once, and invocation/derivation/failure/harness IDs resolve their owning tables. Unresolved/orphan selector, import, symbol, harness, root substitution, or row fails.

Derivation rows sort by ID and bind `{derivation_id,schema_digest,implementation_identity_sha256,test_vector_set_sha256}`. `FailureContractRowV1 = {failure_contract_id,expected_outcome:"failure"|"error"|"unexpectedSuccess",failure_stage,reason_code,exact_test_ids,positive_control_test_ids,forbidden_error_classes,decoder_mapping_contract_id}`; all three arrays sort/unique, exact/control sets are disjoint, decoder ID resolves one primitive, and rows sort/unique by failure-contract ID under `QINAO-VALIDATION-FAILURE-CONTRACTS-V1\0`. The row set equals the union referenced by Swift/ordinary contracts in both directions. `registry_root = SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObjectWithoutRegistryRootBytes)) || canonicalObjectWithoutRegistryRootBytes)`. Before source selection is signed, an out-of-band reviewer stable-opens this mode-`0644`, root-owned, nonwritable, regular link-count-one artifact and pins raw SHA/root/schema/vectors into both selection and anchor; clean CI receives the same immutable artifact by pinned raw SHA. Missing bytes, unknown contract/profile, tuple/filter/set-root mismatch, duplicate/orphan row, self-selected implementation, or vector mismatch leaves Task 0 BLOCKED.

The gateway is the pre-execution trust boundary. `/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate` is a signed unprivileged CLI. The separately administrator-installed reviewed bundle contains that CLI; daemon; logged-in-session worker; signed App-Sandbox runner; compile-only broker; and distinct toolchain-validation broker. Each broker has its own no-login UID, scratch, channel, and root-owned no-network/process policy. The runner installs **five** signed profiles: `discoveryOnly`, `pureProduct`, `sourceRead`, `fixtureProcess`, and `processControl`. All system services require enabled approval; only worker is in the intended user session. CLI/worker/brokers establish mutually authenticated XPC channels and daemon validates live audit token/designated requirement. Compile and toolchain-test capabilities are disjoint. Missing approval/UID/policy/profile, shared/login broker, cross-channel, or partial install leaves Task 0 BLOCKED under the Service Management/XPC split.

The root-owned trust anchor at `/Library/Application Support/Qinao/ExecutionGate/qinao-trust-anchor-v1.json` is exactly `{schema_version:1,cli_identity,daemon_identity,worker_identity,swift_compile_broker_identity,swift_toolchain_validation_broker_identity,runner_identity,gateway_test_vector_set_sha256,source_import_tool_identity,admission_python_identity,cms_decoder_identity,native_signature_verifier_identity,differential_signature_verifier_identity,git_service_identity,service_specs,service_rebind_reason_rule_rows,service_rebind_reason_rule_root,service_rebind_reason_rule_schema_digest,service_rebind_reason_rule_test_vector_set_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry_root,effect_support_contract_registry_schema_digest,effect_support_contract_registry_test_vector_set_sha256,process_termination_observation_schema_digest,process_termination_observation_test_vector_set_sha256,k4_trusted_clock_policy,k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256,k4_verify_inclusion_seed_rows,k4_verify_inclusion_seed_root,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_sha256,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_root,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_schema_digest,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_test_vector_set_sha256,k4_crypto_erasure_operator_policy,swift_test_backend_identities,swift_static_auxiliary_tool_identities,swift_tool_dispatch_contract_identity,swift_runtime_static_profile_root,runner_sandbox_profile_set,execution_observation_policy,receipt_authentication_key,reviewer_trust_root_sha256,reviewer_key_set_sha256,validation_contract_registry_sha256,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_contract_registry_schema_digest,validation_contract_registry_test_vector_set_sha256}`. Rebind-rule rows sort/unique by `(reason,service_role,reason_rule_id)`, cover every permitted `reason × service_role` exactly once, root under `QINAO-SERVICE-REBIND-REASON-RULES-V1\0`, and are rejected on unknown/extra/missing/overlapping field path or schema/vector/root drift. Effect-support rows uniquely resolve `(subcommand,effect_class,result_kind)` to dispatcher/query/process-termination contracts; inclusion seeds sort by field path and root under `QINAO-K4-VERIFY-INCLUSION-SEEDS-V1\0`; the dependency registry is separately raw-SHA/root/schema/vector pinned. Erasure-operator policy fixes role `k4-crypto-erasure-operator`, active key/principal/epoch/interval/revocation allowlist. `ProcessIdentityV1 = {path,raw_sha256,cdhash,designated_requirement,version}`. `SwiftBackendIdentityV1 = {xcode_developer_dir_commitment,developer_relative_path,resolved_developer_relative_path,path_kind,resolved_raw_sha256,resolved_cdhash,resolved_designated_requirement,version}` where `path_kind = regularFile([]) | pinnedSymlink({link_raw_sha256,link_target_bytes_sha256,resolved_developer_relative_path})`; symlink resolution stays beneath committed DeveloperDir and both link/target identities are verified. XCTest fixes launcher/link/resolved Agent; helper fixes its complete developer-relative path. `SwiftEventTransportIdentityV1 = {contract_id,implementation_sha256,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`; backend object is exactly `{xctest:SwiftBackendIdentityV1,swift_testing_helper:SwiftBackendIdentityV1,xctest_event_transport:SwiftEventTransportIdentityV1,swift_testing_event_transport:SwiftEventTransportIdentityV1}`.

The effect-support registry is a separately reviewed Task-0 input at exact root-owned path `/Library/Application Support/Qinao/ExecutionGate/qinao-effect-support-contract-registry-v1.json`, never generated by candidate or invocation bytes. `GatewayEffectSupportExecutableContractV1 = {contract_id,service_role,implementation_identity_sha256,input_schema_digest,output_schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`. `GatewayDispatcherLockContractV1 = {contract_id,service_role,implementation_identity_sha256,namespace_id,storage_identity_sha256,lock_state_schema_digest,lock_key_derivation_schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}` and `GatewayDispatcherLockIdentityV1 = {contract_id,namespace_id,storage_identity_sha256,scope_id,effect_id,lock_key_digest}`. `lock_key_digest = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-DISPATCHER-LOCK-KEY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(namespaceIDUTF8)) || namespaceIDUTF8 || storageIdentityRaw32 || scopeIDRaw32 || effectIDRaw32)`. The namespace/storage pair identifies one persistent daemon-owned lock table, not a process-local mutex or caller path; all generations derive the same logical slot for the same scope/effect and may acquire it only after verifying the preceding holder's terminal observation or authenticated handoff.

`GatewayEffectSupportContractRowV1 = {support_contract_id,subcommand,effect_family:"filesystem"|"git"|"platform"|"credential"|"lease"|"publication",effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest,operation_plan_contract_id,operation_plan_schema_digest,operation_plan_row_schema_digest,plan_projection_contract_id,plan_projection_implementation_sha256,plan_projection_test_vector_set_sha256,dispatcher_contract:GatewayEffectSupportExecutableContractV1,dispatcher_lock_contract:GatewayDispatcherLockContractV1,recovery_query_contract:GatewayEffectSupportExecutableContractV1,process_termination_observation_contract:GatewayEffectSupportExecutableContractV1}`. Rows sort by unsigned UTF-8 `(subcommand,effect_family,effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest,support_contract_id)`, are unique by both `support_contract_id` and `(subcommand,effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest)`, and every executable or lock implementation identity resolves exactly one pinned trust-anchor service identity. The row set and every effect-capable operation in the subcommand/operation registries cover each other bidirectionally; one effect class maps to exactly one family and one lock-derivation contract, and no orphan row can acquire authority.

`GatewayEffectSupportContractRegistryV1 = {schema_version:1,registry_id:"qinao-effect-support-contract-registry-v1",rows,registry_root,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}` and `registry_root = SHA256("QINAO-EFFECT-SUPPORT-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObjectWithoutRegistryRootBytes)) || canonicalObjectWithoutRegistryRootBytes)`. `effect_support_contract_registry_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete duplicate-key-rejected compact sorted-key canonical object with no trailing LF. Before bootstrap and every service registration, the daemon stable-opens this mode-`0644`, root-owned, nonwritable, regular link-count-one artifact and requires its raw SHA, root, schema digest, and vector digest to byte-equal all four trust-anchor pins. Missing/extra/duplicate rows, an orphan operation or row, unresolved service identity, family/schema/projection/implementation drift, wrong sort/root/domain, or changed artifact blocks before Accepted.

`CMSDecoderIdentityV1 = {process_identity:ProcessIdentityV1,decoder_contract_id:"qinao-k4-cms-signeddata-metadata-v1",schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256,allowed_content_type_oids:["1.2.840.113549.1.7.2"],allowed_digest_algorithm_oids:["2.16.840.1.101.3.4.2.1"],allowed_signature_algorithm_oids:["1.2.840.10045.4.3.2"],required_signer_count:1,required_detached_content:false}`; arrays are exact sorted/unique dotted strings and match K4 policy. Unknown/BER/alternate OID blocks. Static auxiliary tools are exact identities for `xcrun`, `swiftc`, `llvm-objdump`, `git`, `sqlite3`, `perl`, `sleep`, and `/usr/bin/env`; product CLIs are runtime bindings. Dispatch contract is `{contract_id:"qinao-swift-sealed-tool-dispatch-v1",implementation_sha256,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`. Runner profiles are exactly `{discoveryOnly,pureProduct,sourceRead,fixtureProcess,processControl}`. No ambient PATH/Xcode discovery is accepted. Receipt key remains daemon-only P-256 with closed public/key/time/revocation fields.

`service_specs[]` is the exact role-tagged union `daemon({label:"com.qinao.execution-gate.daemon",executable_path:"/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate.daemon",launchd_plist_sha256,process_identity_sha256}) | worker({label:"com.qinao.execution-gate.worker",executable_path:"/Library/Application Support/Qinao/ExecutionGate/QinaoExecutionWorker.app/Contents/MacOS/QinaoExecutionWorker",launchd_plist_sha256,process_identity_sha256,required_login_session:true}) | compileBroker({label:"com.qinao.execution-gate.swift-compile-broker",executable_path:"/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate.swift-compile-broker",launchd_plist_sha256,policy_sha256,process_identity_sha256,dedicated_no_login_uid:true}) | toolchainValidationBroker({label:"com.qinao.execution-gate.swift-toolchain-validation-broker",executable_path:"/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate.swift-toolchain-validation-broker",launchd_plist_sha256,policy_sha256,process_identity_sha256,dedicated_no_login_uid:true})`; CLI/runner paths are fixed and incompatible fields absent. `GatewayDaemonBootstrapIdentityV1 = {schema_version:1,daemon_identity_sha256,trust_anchor_manifest_sha256,daemon_launchd_plist_sha256,boot_session_commitment,service_epoch}`. `GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1 = {pid:uint32,process_start_monotonic_ns:uint64,audit_token_digest,boot_session_commitment,code_identity_sha256,channel_endpoint_commitment}` is sampled from the authenticated live process/channel. `GatewayRegisteredServiceV1` adds `{status:"enabled",sm_app_service_status:"enabled"|notApplicable({reason:"rootLaunchdService"}),process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,channel_generation,observed_at_utc}` with role-specific session/UID/policy facts. `GatewayRunnerRegistrationV1 = {runner_identity_sha256,worker_channel_generation}` only. The live observation semantically carries daemon identity, service rows, runner registration, all backend/aux/profile/policy roots, generation, and time; the **sole exact registration wire** is declared below after those row types. Before every Accepted/capability, daemon stable-FD-verifies the complete live projection. Missing/drifted state blocks.

Those registration roots are closed, not prose digests. `RunnerSandboxProfileRowV1 = {profile_id,raw_sha256}` sorts/uniques by profile ID and roots under `QINAO-RUNNER-SANDBOX-PROFILES-V1\0`. `SwiftBackendCatalogRowV1 = {backend_id,backend_kind,process_identity:SwiftBackendIdentityV1,event_transport_identity:SwiftEventTransportIdentityV1}` sorts/uniques by backend ID and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-BACKEND-CATALOG-V1\0`. `SwiftAuxiliaryToolIdentityRowV1 = {tool_id,process_identity:ProcessIdentityV1}` sorts/uniques by tool ID and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-AUXILIARY-TOOL-CATALOG-V1\0`. `SwiftRuntimeStaticProfileRowV1 = {profile_id,executor_class,backend_id_or_NA,operation_kind,capability_lane_or_NA,allowed_static_image_ids,mandatory_static_image_ids,static_load_edge_root}` sorts/uniques by profile ID with sorted/unique ID arrays and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-RUNTIME-STATIC-PROFILES-V1\0`. `ExecutionObservationPolicyV1 = {policy_id,mode:"enforcementAndCustody",implementation_sha256,test_vector_set_sha256}` is stored in full and its raw canonical SHA occupies `execution_observation_policy_sha256`. Registration embeds/reopens the complete four row arrays; every computed root and policy SHA byte-equals the corresponding trust-anchor projection, including exact equality of `runtime_static_profile_root` and anchor `swift_runtime_static_profile_root`. Reorder, omission, domain swap, alias, or row/root drift blocks.

For wire exactness the compact registration declaration above is superseded by `GatewayServiceRegistrationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,daemon_bootstrap_identity:GatewayDaemonBootstrapIdentityV1,service_rows,runner_registration:GatewayRunnerRegistrationV1,runner_profile_rows,profile_set_root,backend_catalog_rows,backend_identity_root,auxiliary_tool_identity_rows,auxiliary_tool_identity_root,runtime_static_profile_rows,runtime_static_profile_root,service_rebind_reason_rule_rows,service_rebind_reason_rule_root,service_rebind_reason_rule_schema_digest,service_rebind_reason_rule_test_vector_set_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry:GatewayEffectSupportContractRegistryV1,effect_support_contract_registry_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry_root,effect_support_contract_registry_schema_digest,effect_support_contract_registry_test_vector_set_sha256,process_termination_observation_schema_digest,k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256,k4_verify_inclusion_seed_root,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_root,execution_observation_policy:ExecutionObservationPolicyV1,execution_observation_policy_sha256,observation_generation,registered_at_utc}`. Every row array/root pair is present and byte-equals the anchor. The embedded support registry is the complete stable-opened object; its raw SHA and three internal pins byte-equal the trust anchor, and every later plan/dispatch resolves only through the object embedded in the current live registration. No digest-only reconstruction, caller projection, or second registry authority exists.

Service observation has a dedicated non-invocation scope. `service_observation_scope_id = SHA256("QINAO-SERVICE-OBSERVATION-SCOPE-V1\0" || trustAnchorManifestRaw32 || uint64be(service_epoch) || uint64be(observation_generation))`; a `GatewayServiceRegistrationBodyV1` envelope is its sole `scope_sequence=0` record with zero previous scope root, participates normally in the global journal, and has no Accepted/Completed. A second record in that scope, a registration under an invocation scope, or reuse of `(service_epoch,observation_generation)` fails.

An exact invocation may survive one or several daemon/host restarts without weakening unique authority only through a no-effect `GatewayServiceGenerationRebindBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,rebind_ordinal,from_registration_envelope,transition_rows,transition_root,to_live_registration_envelope,unchanged_service_projection,unchanged_service_projection_root,rebound_at_utc}`. `GatewayServiceRebindTransitionRowV1 = {edge_ordinal,reason,reason_rule_ids,from_registration_receipt_sha256,to_registration_receipt_sha256,from_observation_generation,to_observation_generation,service_observation_rows,service_observation_root}` has contiguous ordinals/generations and roots under `QINAO-SERVICE-REBIND-TRANSITIONS-V1\0`. `reason_rule_ids` sort/unique and contain exactly one rule for every service role present in both complete registration envelopes, including roles whose observed values remain unchanged. `service_observation_rows` likewise contain exactly one row for every such role; each ID/row pair has the same edge, role, and transition reason, and the two sets cover each other bidirectionally. A missing unchanged role, an extra changed role, unequal before/after role sets, or more than one rule/row for an `(edge_ordinal,service_role)` fails. The first edge starts at the historical tip, each edge's `to` byte-equals the next edge's `from`, and the last registration is freshly verified live. Complete registration envelopes must be valid in their dedicated scopes. The unchanged projection contains trust anchor/service epoch, code/plist/policy/profile/backend/tool/runtime-static bytes and roots and byte-equals across **every** edge. `GatewayServiceRebindReasonRuleV1 = {reason_rule_id,reason:"daemonRestart"|"hostRestart",service_role,must_equal_fields,may_change_fields,must_change_fields}` is Task-0-pinned, arrays sorted/unique/disjoint, and collectively covers every field of `GatewayRegisteredServiceV1`; observation rows are the exact total two-registration role projection governed by their resolved rules. Upgrade, identity/policy/profile/tool drift, disabled service, a missing intermediate generation, rule mismatch, or an unproved restart remains BLOCKED.

The rebind child's wrapper uses `service_generation_binding = transitionChain({from_historical_registration_receipt_sha256,to_live_registration_receipt_sha256,from_generation,to_generation,transition_root})`: append authority comes from the freshly verified final live registration, while the complete continuous historical chain proves lineage. The no-effect child atomically advances the invocation tip to `rebound(to)`; no intermediate non-live generation acquires append authority and no ordinary child may use `transitionChain`. `GatewayServiceGenerationChainRowV1 = initial({ordinal:0,registration_receipt_sha256,observation_generation}) | rebind({ordinal,rebind_receipt_sha256,from_registration_receipt_sha256,to_registration_receipt_sha256,from_generation,to_generation,transition_root})`; ordinals are contiguous, each rebind starts at the preceding tip, and Completed's final registration/generation equals the last tip. The array roots under `QINAO-SERVICE-GENERATION-CHAIN-V1\0`. Crash tests cut before/after each dedicated registration, each rebind append, Prepared, dispatch marker, native effect, Adoption, and Completed; they include two restarts before the first rebind and reject a skipped generation, fabricated old-live state, new signer, cross-journal edge, or direct generation substitution.

`GatewayServiceUnchangedProjectionV1 = {trust_anchor_manifest_sha256,service_epoch,service_spec_rows,runner_profile_rows,profile_set_root,backend_catalog_rows,backend_identity_root,auxiliary_tool_identity_rows,auxiliary_tool_identity_root,runtime_static_profile_rows,runtime_static_profile_root,service_rebind_reason_rule_rows,service_rebind_reason_rule_root,service_rebind_reason_rule_schema_digest,service_rebind_reason_rule_test_vector_set_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry:GatewayEffectSupportContractRegistryV1,effect_support_contract_registry_sha256,effect_support_contract_registry_root,effect_support_contract_registry_schema_digest,effect_support_contract_registry_test_vector_set_sha256,process_termination_observation_schema_digest,k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256,k4_verify_inclusion_seed_root,gateway_receipt_dependency_edge_registry_root,execution_observation_policy_sha256}` has the same canonical arrays and complete support-registry object as registration and roots under `QINAO-SERVICE-UNCHANGED-PROJECTION-V1\0`. `GatewayServiceObservationRowV1 = {edge_ordinal,service_role,reason_rule_id,from_process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,to_process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,from_channel_generation,to_channel_generation,from_login_session_commitment_or_NA,to_login_session_commitment_or_NA,from_enabled_state,to_enabled_state}` is a total per-edge role projection: it sorts/uniques by `(edge_ordinal,service_role)`, and for every transition its role set byte-equals the complete `service_rows` role set in both registration envelopes. Changed and unchanged roles each occur exactly once. `service_observation_root = SHA256("QINAO-SERVICE-REBIND-SERVICE-OBSERVATIONS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalServiceObservationRows)) || canonicalServiceObservationRows)`. Every row is mechanically projected from its two complete registration envelopes; its uniquely resolved pinned rule classifies every leaf field into must-equal/may-change/must-change and the gateway evaluates all three sets exhaustively. No open-ended “reason-specific” field or caller-chosen rule exists.

The daemon opens itself, CLI, worker, compile broker, runner, manifest, selection, interpreter, importer/verifier, repository root, and every candidate script through no-follow stable FDs; verifies ownership/mode/link count, code signature, raw/path/real-path commitments, the independently pinned trust-root/receipt-key digests, selection signature/time/role, interpreter/stdlib/tool bytes, candidate Git blob, and all XPC audit tokens before the first candidate-controlled instruction. Candidate Python is never root and never runs inside the privileged daemon, capable worker, or compile broker: the per-user worker spawns only the fixed sandbox runner as its own UID, clears supplementary groups/privileges, and binds UID/GID/login-session/audit-token commitments into the invocation receipt. The runner has App Sandbox enabled with no network, keychain access group, device/USB, user-selected file, or repository-write entitlement. It receives only admitted read-only FDs, custody-separated private output dirfds, one broker socket, and for Swift execution a daemon-installed verified test-product closure inside its own container; descendants inherit the same sandbox. A direct `subprocess`/`posix_spawn` may execute only inside that powerless sandbox and is never an authority source.

The compile broker is a separate launchd service under one dedicated no-login UID with no supplementary groups, GUI/login session, home secrets, Keychain identity, device access, repository-write permission, or signing/journal key. A root-owned per-UID network policy denies every outbound socket, and filesystem ACLs expose only one immutable gateway review-tree snapshot plus one fresh broker-owned scratch allocation. It accepts only fixed `swift.build-tests.v1` messages from the daemon, never caller argv/path, and invokes the trust-anchor-pinned Xcode-27 SwiftPM toolchain with automatic dependency resolution/update disabled and the package/dependency/plugin/macro/resource closure already hash-frozen. SwiftPM's normal manifest/plugin/build sandbox remains enabled; the broker never adds `--disable-sandbox`, never runs a test bundle/helper/product, and writes only beneath its unique scratch dirfd. A distinct no-login/no-network **toolchain-validation broker** accepts only registry-derived `swift.toolchain-test-shard.v1` capabilities for test IDs whose reviewed behavior requires `swiftc`, `llvm-objdump`, or a generated executable; it receives an immutable source mirror plus isolated scratch, may execute only the pinned shard/backend/subprocess capability graph, and has no compile-broker scratch, repository, device, credential, authority, or publication access. The daemon rejects any cross-service capability, unregistered process leaf, source identity drift, write outside the one lane scratch, network attempt, unresolved dependency, changed Package/Resolved/plugin/macro/resource/tool input, or product with an **unclosed** external load/tool reference. Relocatability is proved by the explicit source/product/runtime-load projections below rather than by a blanket pathname assertion.

The compile lane is usable from bootstrap, ordinary candidate review, and detached certification without inventing N/A authority. `SwiftValidationSourceViewV1` is the closed union `bootstrapImport({source_selection_sha256,run_state_receipt_sha256,unit_ordinal,result_tree}) | candidateReview({candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,result_tree}) | detachedCertification({worktree_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,final_tree})`; the global terminal resolver closes the candidate materialization locator before use. The selected variant and tree byte-equal the enclosing bootstrap unit, CandidateUnit/materialization, or certification spec/provision respectively, and the other variants' fields are absent.

Expected tests are **test-surface specific**, not predicted forever by Task 0 and not allowed to hash a future envelope or changing implementation tree. `SwiftTestBundleContractV1 = {bundle_id,target_name,expected_bundle_directory_relative_path,expected_bundle_executable_relative_path,framework_applicability}`; framework applicability is the positive/negative evidence-bearing closed union defined below. The acyclic signed `SwiftValidationTestInventoryV1 = {schema_version:1,test_surface_root,package_id,bundle_contracts,selector_rows,expected_test_rows,subprocess_capabilities,allowed_skip_ids,inventory_root}` contains no full source tree or future identity. Its `test_surface_root` is `SHA256("QINAO-VALIDATION-TEST-SURFACE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRowsBytes)) || canonicalRowsBytes)` over exact path-sorted/unique `{path,git_mode,git_blob,raw_sha256,test_ids,fixture_contract_ids}` rows whose two ID arrays sort/unique; manifest/test source/resource/derivation inputs appear, ordinary implementation blobs do not. `bundle_contracts` sort/unique by `bundle_id`; inside each row the framework map is encoded as an array sorted/unique by framework ID, and target name plus both relative paths are globally one-to-one. They equal the complete Swift package manifest-evaluation/product-build projection of all test targets; an omitted target or self-declared framework N/A fails. `selector_rows[]` sort/unique by `(bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,selector_id)` and are `{bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,selector_id,selector_kind,effective_selector,expected_test_ids,test_id_set_root}` with sorted/unique expected IDs and kind `exactTest | fullyQualifiedXCTestClass | swiftTestingFilter`; class compaction requires exact complete expansion/no collision. Expected rows sort/unique by `(bundle_id,framework,canonical_test_id)` and are `{canonical_test_id,bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,selector_id,suite_id,case_semantics:singleton([]),observed_event_mapping:SwiftObservedEventMappingV1,subprocess_capability_ids}` with sorted/unique capability IDs. V1 rejects parameterized/dynamic Swift Testing: source/manifest derivation rejects `@Test(arguments:)` and registered dynamic case generators, helper discovery yields one unique function/event identity per expected row, and a case/argument suffix or duplicate function identity blocks rather than collapsing multiple cases into `executed_count:1`. `subprocess_capabilities` sort/unique by capability ID and their test-ID sets are sorted/unique; `allowed_skip_ids` sort/unique by typed ID and are a subset of expected rows. Inventory root hashes the complete object without itself. Runtime `SwiftValidationInventoryBindingV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view_digest,current_source_tree,test_surface_root,inventory_sha256,inventory_root,package_id}` independently rederives the surface. Bootstrap/CandidateUnit sign inventory; RED and GREEN require identical inventory/surface semantics while runtime bindings name their trees. Certification reopens the inventory terminally referenced by the final transaction chain—never a latest-file lookup—and rederives it from final tree. Any reorder, duplicate, target/framework/test shrink, map ambiguity, parameterized-case introduction, or drift fails and is covered by mutation vectors.

`source_view_digest = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-VALIDATION-SOURCE-VIEW-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSourceViewBytes)) || canonicalSourceViewBytes)`. `SwiftSourceMirrorProjectionV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view_digest,package_root_relative_path,directories,entries,source_mirror_root}` is the complete authenticated package/dependency/resource source view copied no-replace read-only into a stable runner/broker-local root. Its directory rows are `{relative_path,mode,ordered_child_names_root}` and file rows are `{relative_path,mode,content_length,raw_sha256,git_blob,role}`; normalized paths are parent-complete, globally sorted/unique, and forbid symlink, hardlink, special file, xattr, sparse, collision, or unlisted object. `source_mirror_root` hashes both arrays under `QINAO-SWIFT-SOURCE-MIRROR-V1\0`. `SwiftDebugPathRemapV1 = {compile_materialization_root_commitment,installed_source_mirror_root_commitment,file_prefix_map_argv_sha256,debug_prefix_map_argv_sha256}`; the compile command uses the Xcode-27-golden exact supported prefix-map flags so every `#filePath`/debug path resolves beneath the installed mirror, and each execution lane has fixed cwd equal to its mirror package root. `SwiftAuxiliaryBuildProjectionV1 = {schema_version:1,entries,auxiliary_root}` contains only registry-required object/module/tool outputs copied into the matching read-only `.build` relative layout; every row names its compile-scratch source row/hash and role, and unselected intermediates never leave broker custody. `SwiftTestBundleContractV1.framework_applicability` is now the closed union `applicable({derivation_reason_code,evidence_row_sha256}) | notApplicable({reviewed_reason_code,evidence_row_sha256})`; both variants therefore join one-to-one to the product inventory evidence row rather than letting a positive decision float without a preimage.

The exact non-self-referential `SwiftTestProductProjectionV1` is `{schema_version:1,source_view_digest,package_id,directories,entries,bundle_bindings,helper_executable_paths,export_tree_root}`. The same complete-tree rules apply; `entries[]` is `{relative_path,mode,content_length,raw_sha256,code_identity,role}` and `export_tree_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-TEST-EXPORT-TREE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalDirectoriesAndEntriesBytes)) || canonicalDirectoriesAndEntriesBytes)`. `bundle_bindings[]` is exact `bundle_id` order and each row is `{bundle_id,bundle_directory_path_index,bundle_executable_path_index,bundle_tree_root}`; it joins one-to-one to the signed logical bundle contract's expected relative paths, every bundle has exactly one test executable, and no executable belongs to two bundles. `helper_executable_paths[]` is sorted/unique and every helper lies outside every bundle. `code_identity` is `executable({mach_o_sha256,load_command_root,architecture_set,code_signature_state}) | notApplicable({reason:"nonExecutableRegularFile"})`; Mach-O test/dynamic/helper entries require executable identity, while plist, `.mlmodelc`, resource, source, object metadata, and other non-executable roles require N/A.

Compile scratch has exact top-level `build/`, `export/`, and `auxiliary/`. `SwiftCompileScratchProjectionV1 = {schema_version:1,entries,scratch_layout_root}` covers every post-build object; each sorted/unique row is `{relative_path,mode,classification,object}` where `classification = buildIntermediate | exportProduct | exportAuxiliary` and `object = directory({ordered_child_names_root}) | regular({content_length,raw_sha256,code_identity_or_NA}) | symlink({target_bytes_sha256})`. Symlinks exist only under `build/`, resolve beneath it, and are forbidden in both export roots; directories never carry fictitious content length and regular files never carry target hashes. Every exported row maps one-to-one to the product or auxiliary projection; no unselected intermediate is installed or loaded. `scratch_layout_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-COMPILE-SCRATCH-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalScratchEntriesBytes)) || canonicalScratchEntriesBytes)` and an extra/missing/unclassified object blocks.

Product closure is distinct from the platform runtime-loader catalog and from the discovery-dependent shard binding. `SwiftRuntimeLoadClosureV1 = {schema_version:1,xcode_build,os_build,static_platform_profile_root,test_product_root,loader_environment,allowed_static_images,allowed_static_image_root,static_load_edges,static_load_edge_root,base_load_profiles,base_load_profile_root,generated_image_templates,generated_image_template_root,closure_root}`. `loader_environment` is daemon-supplied and exactly `{DYLD_FRAMEWORK_PATH:[{developer_relative_path:"Platforms/MacOSX.platform/Developer/Library/Frameworks",path_commitment,tree_root},{developer_relative_path:"Platforms/MacOSX.platform/Developer/Library/PrivateFrameworks",path_commitment,tree_root}],DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH:[{developer_relative_path:"Platforms/MacOSX.platform/Developer/usr/lib",path_commitment,tree_root}]}` in that order. `allowed_static_images[]` sorts/uniques by `image_id` and is `product({image_id,product_path_index,raw_sha256,code_identity}) | xcode({image_id,developer_relative_path,path_commitment,raw_sha256,code_identity}) | libraryDeveloper({image_id,normalized_absolute_path_commitment,raw_sha256,code_identity}) | systemSharedCache({image_id,cache_uuid,image_uuid,cache_mapping_digest,code_identity})`; its root uses `QINAO-SWIFT-ALLOWED-STATIC-IMAGES-V1\0`. `static_load_edges[]` sort by `(source_image_id,load_ordinal)` and are `{source_image_id,load_ordinal,raw_install_name,rpath_stack,resolution_kind,resolution_locator_commitment,resolved_image_id}`; every compile-known Mach-O command occurs once and the root uses `QINAO-SWIFT-STATIC-LOAD-EDGES-V1\0`. `base_load_profiles[]` cover compile and each concrete discovery backend plus the static base for each executor/lane; rows are `{base_profile_id,executor_class,backend_id_or_NA,operation_kind,capability_lane_or_NA,allowed_static_image_ids,mandatory_static_image_ids,static_load_edge_root}`. Allowed is a closed superset and mandatory a subset; a base profile never claims a future shard's exact set. `generated_image_templates[]` sort by ID and are `{template_id,producer_capability_id,output_slot_id,expected_artifact_kind,toolchain_commitment_root,logical_load_edge_templates}`; they predict no future hash/code identity. Roots use distinct length-framed `QINAO-SWIFT-BASE-LOAD-PROFILES-V1\0` and `QINAO-SWIFT-GENERATED-IMAGE-TEMPLATES-V1\0` domains. `closure_root` hashes the complete object without itself. The anchor pins the static platform profile root; compile time instantiates only existing bytes. Stale absolute `.build` rpaths are legal only when they resolve to the same sealed dependency. Duplicate/unresolved/ambiguous/outside static edges or post-hoc catalog invention blocks.

After discovery and deterministic packing, each schedule row carries exactly one `SwiftShardLoadPlanV1 = {schema_version:1,schedule_binding_root,package_id,bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,shard_ordinal,test_set_root,executor_class,backend_id,base_profile_id,required_capability_ids,required_static_image_ids,required_generated_template_ids,generated_load_edge_template_rows,generated_load_edge_template_root,logical_plan_root}` derived only from signed inventory/capabilities and selected IDs. `SwiftGeneratedLoadEdgeTemplateRowV1 = {template_id,load_ordinal,source_slot_id,raw_install_name,rpath_stack,resolution_kind,resolution_locator_commitment,expected_target_class}`; rows sort/unique by `(template_id,load_ordinal)`, exactly project the required templates, and root under `QINAO-SWIFT-GENERATED-LOAD-EDGE-TEMPLATES-V1\0`. All ID arrays sort/unique, the plan `test_set_root` byte-equals its containing schedule row, and `logical_plan_root` hashes the complete object without itself under `QINAO-SWIFT-SHARD-LOAD-PLAN-V1\0`; no later paragraph adds an implicit field or aliases it as `selected_test_id_set_root`. At execution, `SwiftGeneratedImageBindingV1 = {template_id,producer_execution_receipt_sha256,artifact_adoption_receipt_sha256,resolved_image_id,raw_sha256,code_identity,resolved_load_edges}` instantiates a generated template only after its toolchain-probe producer and artifact Adoption. `SwiftResolvedLoadEdgeV1 = {template_id,load_ordinal,source_image_id,raw_install_name,rpath_stack,resolution_kind,resolution_locator_commitment,resolved_image_id}` sorts/uniques by `(template_id,load_ordinal,source_image_id)` and must be a total one-to-one instantiation of the logical template rows. `SwiftShardLoadProfileBindingV1 = {shard_load_plan:SwiftShardLoadPlanV1,static_image_ids,generated_image_bindings,resolved_load_edges,expected_concrete_image_ids,concrete_load_edge_root,binding_root}`; the embedded plan reopens byte-identically from the signed schedule, `concrete_load_edge_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-CONCRETE-LOAD-EDGES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalResolvedRowsBytes)) || canonicalResolvedRowsBytes)`, and binding root hashes the complete object without itself under `QINAO-SWIFT-SHARD-LOAD-PROFILE-BINDING-V1\0`. Static IDs equal the plan's derived set; generated bindings join one-to-one to required templates and producer capabilities; final expected set is their exact union. The shard attestation names `binding_root` and its observed image-ID set equals `expected_concrete_image_ids`. This permits `swiftc -> generated executable` without pretending that executable existed at compile time, while fixture-specific git/sqlite/perl/sleep/product-CLI images cannot leak into another shard.

Canonical binding rule: `static_image_ids` and `expected_concrete_image_ids` sort/unique by image ID; `generated_image_bindings` sort/unique by template ID; every binding's internal `resolved_load_edges` sorts by the shared row key; and the binding's top-level `resolved_load_edges` is byte-for-byte the sorted union of those same-template internal arrays, with no duplicate, omitted, or independently authored edge. Reorder or internal/top-level split-brain mutations fail.

`SwiftTestSubprocessCapabilityV1 = {capability_id,capability_lane,test_id_set_root,tool_kind,logical_executable_binding,argv_grammar,environment_allowlist,read_set_root,write_region,descendant_policy}`. Signed logical binding is `anchorTool({tool_identity_id}) | productTool({logical_relative_path,product_role}) | generatedTool({producer_capability_id,output_slot_id,input_commitment_root,toolchain_commitment_root,expected_artifact_kind})`; runtime `SwiftSubprocessExecutionBindingV1 = {capability_id,logical_binding_sha256,resolved_executable_identity,resolution_evidence}` resolves through anchor/product/generated evidence without future hashes in inventory. `SwiftToolDispatchViewV1 = {schema_version:1,dispatch_directory_identity,path_environment_value_commitment,entries,dispatch_root}` has sorted exact `{command_name,execution_binding,installed_leaf_identity}` rows and `dispatch_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-SEALED-TOOL-DISPATCH-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObjectWithoutRootBytes)) || canonicalObjectWithoutRootBytes)`. Installation uses one universal Prepared→Adoption pair; no body can call an unjournaled directory “adopted.” A capability that launches pinned `/usr/bin/env` receives exactly this one PATH value, and the attestation joins env→declared command→resolved child; no other directory/name is searchable. Pure/source/fixture/toolchain/process-control lanes retain their closed child sets; product/generated tools resolve dynamically. SwiftPM and `coremlc` remain forbidden in test-execution lanes, and QinaoProviderBoundary's W0 postimage below removes its runtime `swift package dump-package` call rather than widening this rule.

`read_set_root` in every `SwiftTestSubprocessCapabilityV1` has a required canonical preimage `read_set_rows:SwiftCapabilityReadRowV1[]`; the array is part of that capability wire immediately before the root even where the compact field list elides it. `SwiftCapabilityReadRowV1 = {read_id,source_class,logical_locator,expected_content_or_tree_root,access:"readOnly"}` sorts/uniques by read ID; source class is `sourceMirror | productProjection | auxiliaryProjection | anchorTool | generatedArtifact`, locator is normalized relative or an anchor ID, and root uses `QINAO-SWIFT-CAPABILITY-READ-SET-V1\0`. Rows derive from signed inventory/registry and join one-to-one to installed mirror/product/aux/tool/generated identities; sandbox grants and attestation prove exactly that set. Missing/extra/reordered read, locator substitution, or a read outside the set fails.

`GatewayProcessExecutionAttestationV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,executor,policy_sha256,observation_policy_sha256,load_profile_binding,environment_digest,process_rows,process_tree_root,network_observation,write_rows,write_set_root,loaded_image_rows,loaded_image_root}`. `load_profile_binding = base({base_profile_id}) | shard({binding_root})`; compile/discovery use only `base`, while every shard uses only its schedule-derived concrete binding. `executor` is exactly `compileBroker({service_registration_receipt_sha256,broker_identity_sha256}) | appSandboxRunner({profile_id,runner_identity_sha256}) | toolchainValidationBroker({service_registration_receipt_sha256,broker_identity_sha256})`; compile, ordinary/discovery runner, and toolchainProbe require their disjoint variants. `process_rows[]` is contiguous spawn order `{ordinal,parent_ordinal,role,test_id_or_NA,capability_id_or_NA,executable_identity,argv_digest,environment_digest,started_at_utc,exited_at_utc,exit_status}` and hashes under `QINAO-PROCESS-TREE-V1\0`. `network_observation = {enforcement_policy_sha256,enforcement_active_observation_sha256,permitted_flow_rows,permitted_flow_root,denied_attempt_telemetry}`; permitted rows hash under `QINAO-EXECUTION-PERMITTED-FLOWS-V1\0` and PASS requires `[]`, while telemetry is `observed({attempt_rows,attempt_root,loss_count:0}) | notCollected({reason:"enforcementPolicyIsAuthority"})` and never fabricates zero denied attempts. `write_rows[]` is sorted `{path_commitment,object_kind,operation,before_digest,after_digest,allowed_region}` under `QINAO-EXECUTION-WRITE-SET-V1\0`; custody walks plus immutable ACL/profile prove only the invocation scratch is writable. `loaded_image_rows[]` is sorted `{process_ordinal,image_id,locator_class,locator_commitment,raw_or_cache_identity,code_identity}` under `QINAO-EXECUTION-LOADED-IMAGES-V1\0`. Base attestations contain every mandatory image and no image outside their allowed set; shard attestations equal the concrete binding's exact expected set and its generated bindings. The pinned observation policy states which projections are enforcement/custody versus audited telemetry; unsupported completeness is BLOCKED or explicitly N/A, never asserted from stdout.

`SwiftCompileScratchIdentityV1 = {allocation_id,parent_directory_device_inode_digest,directory_device_inode_digest,path_commitment,mode:"0700",owner_uid_commitment}`.
