One compiled `#filePath` requires one physical source location. `SwiftSharedSourceMirrorIdentityV1 = {path_commitment,parent_directory_device_inode_digest,directory_device_inode_digest,source_mirror_root,mode:"readOnly",grant_policy_template_root}` names the daemon-owned mirror and contains no future grant digest. `SwiftInstallationDestinationIdentityV1 = {executor_instance_id,executor_class,container_root_device_inode_digest,relative_install_path,path_commitment,pre_state:"absent",mode:"readOnly"}`. `SwiftSourceAccessGrantV1 = {grant_kind,grantee_identity_sha256,shared_source_mirror_identity_sha256,rights:["read"],issued_generation,grant_digest}` where kind is `sandboxExtension | filesystemACL`, the mirror SHA is the already adopted acyclic base identity, and digest hashes without itself. `SwiftSourceAccessGrantSetV1 = {shared_source_mirror_identity_sha256,grant_policy_template_root,grant_rows,access_grants_root}` sorts/uniques complete grants by `(grantee_identity_sha256,grant_kind)` and roots them under `QINAO-SWIFT-SOURCE-ACCESS-GRANTS-V1\0`. `product_copy = present({copy_root,installed_tree_identity})`; `auxiliary_copy = present({copy_root,installed_tree_identity,injection_root}) | notApplicable({reason:"laneNeedsNoAuxiliaryArtifacts"})`. `tool_dispatch = present({dispatch_view:SwiftToolDispatchViewV1,dispatch_prepared_receipt_sha256,dispatch_adoption_receipt_sha256}) | notApplicable({reason:"laneHasNoSubprocessCapability"})`.

`SwiftSourceAccessGrantPolicyTemplateRowV1 = {grant_kind,grantee_class,rights:["read"],generation_rule,allowed_mirror_role:"validationSourceMirror"}` sorts/uniques by `(grantee_class,grant_kind)` and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-SOURCE-ACCESS-GRANT-POLICY-TEMPLATES-V1\0` before mirror Adoption. `generation_rule = currentRunnerGeneration | currentBrokerGeneration`. The grant set is the complete one-to-one instantiation of every applicable template row for the scheduled executor set; an extra/missing template or grant, class/kind substitution, or stale generation fails.

`GatewaySwiftInstallationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,installation_kind,destination_identity:SwiftInstallationDestinationIdentityV1,product_copy,auxiliary_copy,shared_source_mirror_identity_sha256,source_access_grant:SwiftSourceAccessGrantV1,runtime_load_closure_root,tool_dispatch}` contains no wrapper digest. `SwiftLaneInstallationRowV1 = {installation_kind,executor_class,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,installation_adoption_receipt_sha256,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,installation_body:GatewaySwiftInstallationBodyV1,dispatch_root_or_NA,installed_at_utc}` where kind is `discovery({bundle_id,framework}) | executionLane({capability_lane})`. Discovery uses runner `discoveryOnly` and exact auxiliary/dispatch N/A; each other lane selects present/N/A strictly from inventory capabilities. Runner uses sandbox-extension grant, broker uses ACL, and all grant/mirror/generation identities cross-equal. A subprocess lane requires present dispatch and `dispatch_root_or_NA=present(root)`; other lanes require exact N/A. Shared mirror has its own atomic-directory Prepared/Adoption. `SwiftCompileSealHandoffV1 = {schema_version:1,broker_process_tree_root,broker_exit_status,broker_channel_closed_at_utc,broker_write_capability_revoked_at_utc,scratch_directory_identity:SwiftCompileScratchIdentityV1,preseal_scratch_layout_root,sealed_scratch_layout_root,stable_read_dirfd_identity,product_tree_root,auxiliary_root,shared_source_mirror_identity:SwiftSharedSourceMirrorIdentityV1,shared_source_mirror_prepared_receipt_sha256,shared_source_mirror_adoption_receipt_sha256,source_access_grant_set:SwiftSourceAccessGrantSetV1,runtime_load_closure_root,installation_rows,sealed_at_utc}`. Order is mirror Adoption, every lane grant/install Adoption, complete grant-set construction, then handoff. The grant set is the sorted complete projection of installation-row grants and every row binds the base mirror SHA; no mirror↔grant fixed point exists. Every copy/grant/dispatch is complete only after Adoption; rows are exact discovery-then-lane order, roots equal compile projections, and recovery accepts only unique exact poststates. Capabilities, `/usr/bin/env` PATH, injection rows, dispatch, attestation, grant set, and handoff cross-equal. Fixed-point, mirror substitution, missing/extra/reordered grant, and post-handoff grant mutations fail. Golden opens compiled `#filePath` from both executor classes.

The compile child payload is exactly `GatewaySwiftTestProductBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view:SwiftValidationSourceViewV1,source_view_digest,inventory_binding:SwiftValidationInventoryBindingV1,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,test_inventory:SwiftValidationTestInventoryV1,package_id,source_tree,source_mirror:SwiftSourceMirrorProjectionV1,debug_path_remap:SwiftDebugPathRemapV1,package_manifest_blob,package_product_inventory:SwiftPackageProductInventoryV1,package_resolved_state,dependency_closure_root,plugin_macro_closure_root,resource_closure_root,toolchain_identity_root,logical_build_argv,effective_build_argv,scratch_identity:SwiftCompileScratchIdentityV1,scratch_projection:SwiftCompileScratchProjectionV1,product_projection:SwiftTestProductProjectionV1,auxiliary_projection:SwiftAuxiliaryBuildProjectionV1,runtime_load_closure:SwiftRuntimeLoadClosureV1,test_product_root,execution_attestation:GatewayProcessExecutionAttestationV1,seal_handoff:SwiftCompileSealHandoffV1,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,built_at_utc,disposition:"compiledTestsOnly"}`. `package_resolved_state` is `absent` or `present({git_blob,raw_sha256})`. `source_tree` equals the selected source-view tree and `inventory_binding.current_source_tree`; source-view digest equals every runtime projection. Inventory raw SHA/root/test-surface/package equal the binding, and the complete bundle-contract set equals the independently derived product inventory's test-target set. `scratch_identity` byte-equals `seal_handoff.scratch_directory_identity`. `test_product_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-TEST-PRODUCT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalProductProjectionBytes)) || canonicalProductProjectionBytes)`. The daemon reconstructs every root and physical/logical bundle join and installs only sealed product/source/auxiliary bytes. Compile attestation requires `compileBroker`; shard/discovery bodies require only authorized executor variants.

The four compile-closure roots have fixed replayable preimages. `SwiftDependencyClosureRowV1 = {package_identity,source_kind,source_locator,revision_or_blob,manifest_raw_sha256,resolved_state_sha256}` sorts/uniques by package identity and derives bidirectionally from manifest, `Package.resolved`, and source-mirror dependency rows; root domain is `QINAO-SWIFT-DEPENDENCY-CLOSURE-V1\0`. `SwiftPluginMacroClosureRowV1 = {target_id,kind,source_root,toolchain_identity_id,output_contract_root}` sorts by `(kind,target_id)` and derives from manifest/build graph plus sealed auxiliary rows; domain is `QINAO-SWIFT-PLUGIN-MACRO-CLOSURE-V1\0`. `SwiftResourceClosureRowV1 = {target_id,resource_relative_path,rule,source_row_sha256,output_row_sha256}` sorts by `(target_id,resource_relative_path)` and is a complete source-mirror→product projection under `QINAO-SWIFT-RESOURCE-CLOSURE-V1\0`. `SwiftToolchainIdentityRowV1 = {tool_id,process_identity_sha256,role}` sorts/uniques by tool ID, exactly projects the anchor identities actually used, and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-TOOLCHAIN-IDENTITIES-V1\0`. Every root length-frames its complete canonical array, deterministically reconstructed from the product body's embedded source/product/auxiliary/manifest/resolved/attestation fields plus anchor. Omitted/extra/reordered rows, domain swap, or source/output mismatch fails.

`SwiftTestFrameworkV1 = xctest([]) | swiftTesting([])`. It fixes the complete join `xctest ↔ nativeXCTestTransport ↔ exactTest/fullyQualifiedXCTestClass ↔ xctestObjectiveCSelector` and `swiftTesting ↔ swiftTestingHelper ↔ swiftTestingFilter ↔ swiftTestingSourceSuffix`; a cross-join is unrepresentable. `SwiftTypedTestIDV1 = {bundle_id,framework:SwiftTestFrameworkV1,canonical_test_id}`. `SwiftObservedEventMappingV1` is the closed union `xctestObjectiveCSelector({module_name,class_name,method_name,raw_native_form}) | swiftTestingSourceSuffix({function_id,event_file_token,event_payload_file_id,source_mirror_relative_path,line,column})`; the XCTest raw native form must be exactly `-[Module.Class method]` and canonicalizes only to `Module.Class/method`. For Swift Testing, the independently decoded helper function ID remains distinct from renderer token, payload `fileID`, full mirror path, line, and column; the inventory/source mirror proves the unique join. `SwiftObservedTestEventV1 = {event_ordinal,typed_test_id,event_kind,raw_event_length,raw_event_sha256,observed_event_mapping}`. The decoder additionally emits contiguous `SwiftDecodedEventRowV1 = {event_ordinal,byte_offset,raw_event_length,raw_event_sha256,event_class,payload}` where class is `test({observed_test_event_sha256}) | suite({suite_id,event_kind}) | diagnostic({severity,message_digest,source_location_or_NA}) | infrastructure({event_kind,payload_digest})`. Rows cover the event leaf exactly from byte offset zero to EOF with no overlap/gap/trailing bytes, root under `QINAO-SWIFT-DECODED-EVENT-LEDGER-V1\0`, and each raw record appears once. Test rows project one-to-one to `observed_test_events`; suite/run/plan/infrastructure rows remain authenticated evidence, and an unknown tag, malformed payload, error/fatal diagnostic, or infrastructure failure blocks even if all test terminals look green. No generic suffix stripping, stdout/stderr regex, ignored-record path, framework inference, or cross-form fallback is allowed.

Before scheduling, each applicable `(bundle_id,framework)` emits `GatewaySwiftTestDiscoveryBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view_digest,inventory_binding,compile_receipt_sha256,test_product_root,bundle_binding,resolved_product_paths,framework,discovery_contract_id,discovery_backend,lane_installation_row,executor,effective_argv,effective_environment_digest,discovery_evidence,observed_event_mapping_rows,canonical_discovered_test_ids,canonical_discovered_test_set_root,source_mirror_root,runtime_load_closure_root,base_load_profile_id,execution_attestation,execution_destination_identity_before,execution_destination_identity_after,exit_status:SwiftProcessTerminationV1,observed_at_utc,status:"discoveryCompleteNoTestMethodsExecuted"}`. Resolved paths are `{bundle_directory_path_index,bundle_executable_path_index,bundle_directory_identity,bundle_executable_identity}` from sealed path table. Backend is `nativeXCTestTransport({transport_identity}) | swiftTestingHelper({backend_identity,transport_identity})`. `discovery_evidence = xctest({selector_rows,selector_root,native_discovery_observation_rows,native_discovery_observation_root,event_capture:nativeTransportOnly({transport_observation_rows,transport_observation_root})}) | swiftTesting({raw_stdout_length,raw_stdout_sha256,function_rows,function_root,suite_metadata_rows,suite_metadata_root,decoded_event_rows,decoded_event_root,event_capture:helperPresent({capture:SwiftEventStreamCaptureV1})})`; incompatible fields are absent. XCTest selector row is `{selector_ordinal,raw_native_selector,typed_test_id,selector_mapping_sha256}` sorted after ordinal proof and rooted under `QINAO-SWIFT-XCTEST-DISCOVERY-SELECTORS-V1\0`. `SwiftXCTestNativeDiscoveryObservationRowV1 = {ordinal,raw_native_selector,raw_length,raw_sha256,selector_mapping_sha256}` uses contiguous raw order and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-XCTEST-NATIVE-DISCOVERY-OBSERVATIONS-V1\0`; `nativeTransportOnly.transport_observation_rows/root` byte-equal this array/root, and selector rows are its exact typed projection. Swift function/suite/decoded rows use the types below and distinct roots. XCTest exact argv is `[transport,"--list-test-methods","--bundle-directory",<bundle-directory>]`; Swift helper exact argv is `[helper,"--test-bundle-path",<bundle-executable>,"--testing-library","swift-testing","--enable-swift-testing","--event-stream-version","0","--event-stream-output-path",<fresh-absent-leaf>,"--list-tests"]` with no filter suffix. Stdout function set, helper event function set, inventory, and canonical discovery are equal; suite metadata validates ownership but adds no IDs. Both use discoveryOnly installation/base profile, destination before=after, and `execution_attestation.load_profile_binding=base({base_profile_id})`; root process termination equals body and must be exited(0) for any accepted discovery. Sentinel proves zero method execution; SwiftPM remains forbidden. Discovered-ID root uses `QINAO-SWIFT-DISCOVERED-TEST-SET-V1\0`. No body-only child is terminal.

Discovery row wires are closed. `SwiftDiscoveryFunctionRowV1 = {function_ordinal,function_id,typed_test_id,raw_stdout_row_sha256,event_row_sha256}` sorts by `(function_id,typed_test_id)` after ordinals prove the raw stream; `SwiftDiscoverySuiteMetadataRowV1 = {suite_id,parent_suite_id_or_NA,source_owner_root,member_typed_test_ids,member_set_root}` sorts by suite ID with sorted/unique members; `SwiftDiscoveryMappingRowV1 = {typed_test_id,observed_event_mapping:SwiftObservedEventMappingV1}` sorts/uniques by the typed key. Mapping rows join one-to-one to each applicable inventory partition. Function rows and suite-member partition requirements apply **only** to Swift Testing; XCTest instead uses the complete native observation array above and has no fabricated function/suite rows. Discovery `event_capture` is exactly `helperPresent({capture:SwiftEventStreamCaptureV1}) | nativeTransportOnly({transport_observation_rows:SwiftXCTestNativeDiscoveryObservationRowV1[],transport_observation_root})`; no stdout/stderr regex path exists.

`SwiftShardScheduleRowV1 = {bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,shard_ordinal,shard_count,selector_ids,selected_test_ids,selector_set_root,test_set_root,kern_argmax,max_attempt_path_bytes,argv_environment_worst_case_bytes,safety_margin_bytes,logical_argv_template_digest,logical_environment_template_digest,shard_load_plan:SwiftShardLoadPlanV1}`. `SwiftExecutionScheduleV1 = {schema_version:1,package_id,inventory_root,test_product_root,canonical_discovered_test_set_root,schedule_binding_root,rows,schedule_root}` sorts by `(bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,shard_ordinal)`. Binding root hashes inventory/product/discovery plus rows without load plans; each plan contains the binding root and exact package/bundle/framework/lane/shard/test-set/executor/backend identity. Schedule root hashes the complete object without itself. Packing begins only after discovered equals inventory and uses deterministic maximal prefixes. `safety_margin_bytes = max(65536,ceil(kern_argmax/8))`; worst-case estimator substitutes a registry-fixed maximum fresh attempt-container/absent-leaf path of `max_attempt_path_bytes` and counts strings/pointers/terminators/environment below the limit. Schedule freezes logical templates, not an attempt-specific pathname. XCTest argv is `[resolved-xctest-target,"-XCTest",<comma-selectors>,<bundle-directory>]`; Swift Testing logical template is `[helper,"--test-bundle-path",<bundle-executable>,"--testing-library","swift-testing","--enable-swift-testing","--event-stream-version","0","--event-stream-output-path",<ATTEMPT_ABSENT_LEAF>]` plus declaration-order exact `--filter <effective_selector>` pairs. Each actual attempt substitutes exactly its fresh committed leaf. All-tests, helper comma/filter regex invention, alternate packing, zero shard, mixed framework, discovery drift, or `E2BIG` fails.

`GatewaySwiftEventLeafAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,capture_scope,attempt_ordinal,absent_leaf_path_commitment,post_leaf_identity,event_stream_length,event_stream_sha256,writer_backend_identity,writer_exit_status,frozen_at_utc}` contains no wrapper digest. `GatewayQuarantinedSwiftAttemptBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,capture_scope,attempt_ordinal,reason,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,process_tree_terminated_observation_sha256,container_identity,container_tree_root,decoded_prefix_rows,decoded_prefix_root,test_process_row_count,subprocess_row_count,effect_projection_root,quarantined_at_utc}` permits only `daemonInterrupted | transportLost | hostRestartedBeforeResult`. `SwiftEventAttemptRowV1 = {attempt_ordinal,attempt_container_identity,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,actual_argv_digest,actual_environment_digest,process_attestation_projection_sha256,outcome}` where outcome is `completeAdopted({event_leaf_adoption_receipt_sha256}) | partialQuarantined({quarantine_receipt_sha256,reason})`. Actual argv must instantiate the signed template once and remain within worst-case size. `SwiftEventStreamCaptureV1 = {parent_directory_identity,capture_scope,attempt_count,attempt_rows,attempt_history_root,final_event_leaf_adoption_receipt_sha256,event_stream_length,event_stream_sha256,decoded_event_rows,decoded_event_root}` has count 1 or 2, exact `0..<count`, final complete row, and at most one prior quarantined row under `QINAO-SWIFT-EVENT-ATTEMPT-HISTORY-V1\0`. Retry is legal only when the authenticated prefix contains zero test-class rows at or after `testStarted`, process attestation proves `test_process_row_count=0` and `subprocess_row_count=0`, and effect projection is the canonical empty root; otherwise execution is indeterminate/BLOCKED because at-most-once cannot be proved. Its final decoded rows/root byte-equal the enclosing discovery/shard fields and cover the adopted leaf exactly. Shard body `effective_argv/effective_environment_digest` are exactly those of the final complete attempt; attempt audit retains a different partial-attempt digest. Assertion failure, any started test followed by transport loss, complete nonzero, signal/crash, timeout, or decoder failure is evidence, not retry eligibility; second infrastructure partial blocks. Mutation tests inject `testStarted` immediately before every retry reason and require no retry/acceptance.

The registry's partial-state exceptions are the closed set `ephemeralSwiftEventAttempt`, `reconstructibleScratch({effect_class:"worktreePostimage"|"privateBundleLeaf"|"sourceSnapshotDirectory",max_attempts:2})`, and no others. Swift partial containers use the quarantine body above. Reconstructible scratch uses `GatewayReconstructibleScratchQuarantineBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,effect_id,effect_class,attempt_ordinal,source_receipt_sha256,partial_identity,process_terminated_observation_sha256,quarantine_identity,rename_no_replace_observation_sha256,quarantined_at_utc}` and is retryable only while target/prestate/source remain exact; the final attempt partial blocks. For `sourceSnapshotDirectory`, the observation child is the sole source receipt, any incomplete three-leaf staging directory is quarantined whole, and retry restarts at leaf zero; no leaf is adopted independently. No exception grants authority to partial bytes.

`SwiftTestTerminalOutcomeV1` is the closed union `passed([]) | skipped({reason_code}) | failure({failure_contract_id,stage,reason_code}) | error({stage,reason_code}) | unexpectedSuccess({expected_failure_contract_id})`. `SwiftRawEventReferenceV1 = {event_ordinal,event_kind,raw_event_length,raw_event_sha256,mapping_sha256}` sorts by ordinal and joins one-to-one to the shard's observed-event rows. `SwiftPerTestTerminalV1 = {typed_test_id,executed_count:1,outcome,raw_event_refs,terminal_event_root}` has a nonempty exact `SwiftRawEventReferenceV1` list and `terminal_event_root = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-TEST-TERMINAL-EVENTS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRefsBytes)) || canonicalRefsBytes)`. Every selected typed ID has one terminal row; no unselected ID has one. `SwiftOutputStreamEvidenceV1 = {stream_kind,content_length,raw_sha256,leaf_identity,adoption_receipt_sha256}` uses kind `stdout | stderr`; the exact two-row array hashes under `QINAO-SWIFT-OUTPUT-STREAMS-V1\0`. Each scheduled shard emits `GatewaySwiftTestShardExecutionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view:SwiftValidationSourceViewV1,source_view_digest,inventory_binding:SwiftValidationInventoryBindingV1,validation_expectation,compile_receipt_sha256,test_product_root,package_id,bundle_binding,resolved_product_paths,framework,capability_lane,shard_schedule_row,lane_installation_row,dispatch_root_or_NA,executor,backend_identity:SwiftBackendIdentityV1,event_transport_identity,event_capture,decoded_event_rows,decoded_event_root,logical_test_argv,effective_argv,effective_environment_digest,source_mirror_root,auxiliary_root,runtime_load_closure_root,shard_load_profile_binding:SwiftShardLoadProfileBindingV1,discovery_receipt_sha256,selected_test_ids,observed_test_events,per_test_terminals,observed_skip_ids,observed_failure_ids,observed_error_ids,observed_unexpected_success_ids,used_subprocess_capability_ids,subprocess_execution_bindings,allowed_skip_ids,output_stream_rows,output_stream_root,execution_attestation:GatewayProcessExecutionAttestationV1,execution_destination_identity_before,execution_destination_identity_after,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,executed_at_utc,exit_status:SwiftProcessTerminationV1,disposition:"terminalEvidenceComplete"}`. `event_capture = helperExecution({capture:SwiftEventStreamCaptureV1}) | nativeExecution({event_observation_rows,event_observation_root})`; native rows are contiguous `SwiftNativeExecutionEventObservationRowV1 = {ordinal,event_kind,raw_event_length,raw_event_sha256,typed_test_id,mapping_sha256}` and byte-equal the corresponding test-class projection of the complete decoded ledger, rooted under `QINAO-SWIFT-NATIVE-EXECUTION-EVENTS-V1\0`. Helper and native paths both require the complete byte-covering `decoded_event_rows/root`; discovery-only `helperPresent/nativeTransportOnly` variants are illegal here. The schedule row's complete `shard_load_plan` byte-equals `shard_load_profile_binding.shard_load_plan`, and `execution_attestation.load_profile_binding = shard({binding_root})`. A generated binding's producer child/artifact Adoption precedes every consumer process and this shard child in Accepted child/effect order; cross-shard or future-producer binding fails. Observed sets derive only from sorted/disjoint terminal rows; skips are a subset of inventory allowlist. `toolchainProbe` uses only its broker; every other lane its exact runner profile. Capability bindings, dispatch, PATH, source/inventory/product/bundle/install/discovery/schedule/load bytes cross-equal. A nonzero exit is evidence and cannot alone label RED/GREEN. Golden proves every lane/framework/bundle, outcome/event mapping, selector packing, generated producer ordering, source relocation, actual `#filePath`, auxiliary/CoreML/helper closure, fixture tools, process control, and sandbox inheritance.

`SwiftTestTerminalReducerV1` is the only constructor of `SwiftPerTestTerminalV1`. Each framework-specific decoder normalizes to the same state machine: exactly one `testStarted`, zero or more nonterminal diagnostics, exactly one terminal class (`testPassed`; `testSkipped`; one-or-more same-contract `testFailure`; one-or-more same-reason `testError`; or `testUnexpectedSuccess`), then exactly one `testFinished`. Missing/duplicate start or finish, mixed terminal classes, conflicting failure/error identities, terminal-after-finish, error/fatal diagnostic, or an event for an unselected ID blocks. Terminals form the complete typed-ID partition of test-class decoded rows; the reducer deterministically constructs both outcome and raw refs. A mutation that changes only `outcome` while preserving events/refs therefore fails even after all enclosing roots are recomputed.

`SwiftProcessTerminationV1 = exited({code:int32}) | signaled({signal:int32}) | timedOut({deadline_ms:uint64}) | spawnFailed({reason_code})`; every compile/discovery/shard `exit_status` and root process-row status uses this type and must be byte-equal. `SwiftValidationExpectationV1 = green([]) | exactRed({expected_failure_contract_id,failure_stage,reason_code,exact_test_ids,positive_control_test_ids,forbidden_error_classes})` is registry-derived; the registry never predicts an ARG_MAX-dependent shard ordinal. Common child is `GatewaySwiftValidationResultBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_view:SwiftValidationSourceViewV1,source_view_digest,inventory_binding:SwiftValidationInventoryBindingV1,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,validation_expectation,package_id,compile_receipt_sha256,compile_receipt,discovery_rows,canonical_discovered_test_set_root,discovery_receipt_row_root,execution_schedule,derived_allowed_nonzero_shard_keys,derived_allowed_nonzero_shard_key_root,execution_rows,execution_row_root,attempt_audit_rows,attempt_audit_root,typed_expected_test_ids,typed_observed_test_ids,per_test_terminals,per_test_terminal_root,observed_skip_ids,observed_failure_ids,observed_error_ids,observed_unexpected_success_ids,ordered_output_rows,ordered_output_root,validated_at_utc,status:"acceptedValidationEvidence",disposition}`. `SwiftShardKeyV1 = {bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,shard_ordinal}`; for exact RED the derived sorted/unique key set is exactly the schedule rows whose `selected_test_ids` intersect `exact_test_ids`, while GREEN derives `[]`, and its root uses `QINAO-SWIFT-DERIVED-ALLOWED-NONZERO-SHARDS-V1\0`. Discovery row is `{bundle_id,framework,discovery_receipt_sha256,discovery_receipt:GatewayReceiptV1}` and its body carries discovery payload; rows sort `(bundle_id,framework)` and root under `QINAO-SWIFT-DISCOVERY-RECEIPT-ROWS-V1\0`, distinct from discovered-ID root. Execution row is `{bundle_id,framework,capability_lane,shard_ordinal,execution_receipt_sha256,execution_receipt:GatewayReceiptV1}` in exact schedule order and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-EXECUTION-RECEIPT-ROWS-V1\0`. Attempt row is `{capture_scope,attempt_ordinal,attempt_outcome_sha256,actual_argv_digest,actual_environment_digest,process_attestation_projection_sha256}` sorted by scope then ordinal and roots under `QINAO-SWIFT-ATTEMPT-AUDIT-V1\0`.

Typed ID/set arrays sort/unique by `(bundle_id,framework,canonical_test_id)`. Common `per_test_terminals` is the sorted/disjoint union of shard terminal rows under `QINAO-SWIFT-PER-TEST-TERMINALS-V1\0`; every observed set derives from it. Event kind/root and ordered outputs are closed as above. Expected=discovered=selected=observed=terminal sets; shards disjoint; bindings cross-equal. GREEN requires compile/discovery/all shards exited zero and pass/allowed-skip only. Exact RED requires compile/discovery/controls and every key outside the **derived allowed-nonzero shard-key set** exited zero; only derived keys intersecting registered exact test failures may exit nonzero, and precise stage/reason occurs. Signal, timeout, spawn failure, transport/backend/post-terminal error, other nonzero, or attestation mismatch is never expected RED. Candidate review consumes only these dispositions; one invocation contains compile/discovery/schedule/shards/common then successful Completed.

Root-owned policy/journal/fence/signing live in the daemon. CoreDevice/Xcode/login-Keychain operations live only in the separately signed native per-user worker so they run in the exact authorized GUI login session; candidate Python cannot enter that code path. The daemon sends one-shot operation capabilities over the worker-initiated mutually authenticated XPC channel, validates worker UID/session/audit token immediately before effect, and signs the returned raw-byte commitments/time/pre/post identities into its journal. Raw device/profile/key selectors travel only in daemon↔worker XPC/FD messages and are never placed in Python environment/argv. Escape tests attempt direct `security`/`xcrun`, mutating or caller-configured Git, network, Keychain, repository/private-dir writes, FD inheritance, privilege regain, worker/runner/session substitution, confused-deputy XPC, and fast-user switching and require kernel/daemon denial plus zero authoritative effect. The separate clean ephemeral-CI boundary may execute fixed-config read-only `/usr/bin/git`; local `candidate-review`/`read-only-checker` code instead consumes the gateway-authenticated repository/index/object observation protocol below. Test-only Git inside a newly created isolated temporary repository is no-effect fixture setup and can never become authority for the admitted repository.

Its exact subcommands are `snapshot-source-state`, `bootstrap-import`, `candidate-materialize`, `candidate-review`, `python-script`, `python-module`, `git-read`, `git-transaction`, `worktree-provision`, `tool-broker`, `publish-directory`, `crypto-erasure-register`, and `certify`. Every postselection call uses literal CLI/subcommand plus source-selection/repository/receipt-root grammar. The sole preselection grammar is exact `snapshot-source-state --snapshot-authorization <external-mode-0600-record> --repository-root <absolute-root> --source-state-output-directory <authorized-absent-or-recoverable-mode-0700-directory> --invocation-receipt-directory <stable-external-mode-0700-directory>`—no source selection. Snapshot keys bind authorization/repository/output/service; schedule is Accepted→observation→atomic-directory effect→result→Completed. Clean CI creates no authority.

`candidate-materialize` takes `--candidate-unit <external-mode-0600-canonical-record> --bundle-directory <stable-external-mode-0700-directory> --bundle-manifest <stable-external-mode-0600-canonical-manifest> --review-directory <stable-gateway-owned-external-directory> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>`. For `ordinary`, the manifest is the exact `candidate-bundle-manifest-v1.json` leaf beside `blobs/`; for `gatewayPublished`, it is the exact external `K4PublicationManifestV1` reopened with the publication receipt. The gateway opens the explicit manifest no-follow and requires its raw digest/root to equal the unit provenance; directory enumeration or “first JSON file” discovery is forbidden. `candidate-review` takes `--candidate-unit <record> --materialization-receipt <receipt> --validation-contract-registry <Task-0-pinned-record> --expected-outcome exact-red|green --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>`.

`python-script` first takes the common options, then exactly `--capability-profile <profile> --script <normalized-repository-relative-path> --script-blob <repository-object-format-derived-lowercase-Git-OID|trusted-tree|candidate-unit>`, followed by the **profile-closed native option tuple**, then `--` and the profile-closed FD-only child argv. A concrete OID is exactly 40 hex for admitted `sha1` and 64 hex for admitted `sha256`; any other length/algorithm pair fails. `candidate-review` permits exactly `--candidate-unit <record> --materialization-receipt <receipt>`. `read-only-checker` permits exactly `--execution-intent <record> --oracle-selection <record> --oracle-catalog <record> --result-receipt <cache>`; source selection comes from the common option. `k4-producer` and `k4-attester` both require exactly `--private-run-directory-env QINAO_K4_CAPTURE_DIR --producer-authorization <record> --native-credential-set <public-set-id>`; `k4-attester` `attest-or-verify` additionally requires `--result-receipt <attested-envelope-cache>`, and only `k4-producer` `seal-finalized-or-verify` additionally requires `--proof-git-transaction-receipt <receipt> --result-receipt <finalized-envelope-cache>`. No other pre-`--` option is legal. Every record/path option is opened by the native gateway and replaced with its fixed FD number before sandbox launch; no raw private path or selector is forwarded. Literal `trusted-tree` resolves once against the gateway's authenticated trusted-tree head and stores the exact object-format-correct blob OID plus trusted-tree-advance receipt in the invocation receipt; restart reuses that blob and never re-resolves against a later HEAD. Literal `candidate-unit` is accepted only for a path/postimage in the signed unit and authenticated materialization receipt, runs powerless against the gateway-owned review tree/index, and cannot request effects or create authority. Mutation tests cover wrong OID length, uppercase, mixed object formats, and a SHA-1/SHA-256 substitution with equal-looking prefixes.

`git-read` takes `--operation <closed-read-ID> --` and its closed argv. `git-transaction` takes `--candidate-unit <record> --materialization-receipt <receipt> --candidate-review-receipt <receipt> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>`. `worktree-provision` takes `--provision-spec <external-mode-0600-canonical-record> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>`. `tool-broker` has one closed contract-indexed argv union. Its base tuple is exactly `--native-contract <registry-closed-native-contract-id> --native-credential-set <public-nonsecret-set-id> --authorization <external-mode-0600-signed-record> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>` with no child argv. Only `w0.capture-open-set-successors.v1` appends exact `--private-output-directory-env QINAO_W0_SUCCESSOR_BUNDLE`; the daemon reads that fixed environment variable privately, stable-opens its approved encrypted parent no-follow, commitment-matches the **final** destination to the authorization, and permits either an initially absent leaf or the one exact journal-proved incumbent created by recovery of the same Accepted invocation. It writes only a private sibling staging directory until the complete tree is sealed and then publishes by atomic no-replace rename; no raw private path reaches the sandbox. The logical bundle contains exactly the generated manifest plus 13 destination rows, while its physical `blobs/` leaves are the sorted unique digest set and may be fewer than 13 only for byte-identical shared payloads. The pre-policy CLI allowlist is exactly the disjoint pair `w0.capture-open-set-successors.v1 | k4.read-only.container-semantics-probe.v1`: the former is the Task-0 no-repository-write capture above and alone accepts the private output option; the latter remains no-effect and rejects any output-directory option. Post-admission broker operations arrive only on an already accepted sandbox broker FD. Missing/duplicate/wrong env name, absent/mismatched authorization field, an unjournaled or mismatched incumbent, symlink/hardlink/alias, another volume/parent, directory substitution, cross-contract option, or private locator leakage fails before generation. `publish-directory` takes `--destination-class approved-candidate-bundle|private-projection --source-directory <absolute-private-sibling> --destination-directory <absolute-external-path> --manifest <canonical-mode-0600-manifest> --expected-root <64-lowercase-hex> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-external-mode-0600-path>`. Both classes require authorization-bound commitments and the same approved encrypted volume; `approved-candidate-bundle` is external, read-only input to a later signed proof CandidateUnit and can never write the repository. A sandboxed producer requests publication over its existing broker FD; it never recursively invokes the CLI. Option reorder, abbreviation, omission, duplicate, relative private path, repository destination, symlink component, wrong destination class, or a non-byte-identical incumbent fails.

`git-read` is read-only and forces `core.hooksPath=/dev/null`, no filters/attributes from outside the admitted tree, no signing, no replace/alternate objects, no external diff/textconv/fsmonitor, and fixed config/home. There is no candidate-owned transaction planner. Before review/adoption, a distinct implementer signs `QinaoCandidateUnitV1 = {schema_version:1,unit_kind,transaction_id,repository_identity,worktree_identity,head_binding,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,changes,bundle_provenance,result_tree,message,prerequisites,author_identity_commitment,committer_identity_commitment,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,swift_validation_inventories,expected_red_receipt_sha256s,issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,implementer_key_id,implementer_principal_id,implementer_role:"w0-implementer",implementer_epoch,signature_algorithm,signature}`. `swift_validation_inventories` is the exact sorted complete acyclic inventory array (or exact empty when profile has no Swift); its test surfaces are independently derived from `result_tree`, and a GREEN unit's applicable inventory/test-surface semantics byte-equal its required RED proposal. `GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1 = {result_receipt_sha256,invocation_completed_receipt_sha256}` is valid only when both complete envelopes reopen from one scope, the result is the registered child, Completed succeeds, ordered children place it in the fixed terminal position, and effect projection matches. Result-before-Completed is recoverable prefix, never cross-scope authority.

Some already-frozen exact payloads and CLI option names below retain a single `*_receipt_sha256` or a result-cache pathname on their wire. That representation is only a locator, never a weaker cross-scope authority. Before any such value is consumed by a different invocation, the daemon reopens the complete child envelope, obtains its `(scope_id, invocation_nonce, parent Accepted)` identity, and uses the root journal plus the scope's unique terminal index to resolve **exactly one** matching `GatewayInvocationCompleted` envelope. It then performs every `GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1` check above and internally promotes the locator to that two-envelope pair; zero, multiple, failed, wrong-order, wrong-effect, wrong-parent, or not-yet-completed matches block. This resolver applies uniformly to materialization, RED, GREEN, Git-transaction, publication, K4, observation, and certification inputs—including `expected_red_receipt_sha256s`, `required_red_receipt_sha256s`, `materialization_receipt_sha256`, `candidate_review_receipt_sha256`, and shell arguments that name their cache leaves. A result may be consumed without a terminal pair only by later children inside its **same fixed invocation schedule** before that invocation's Completed; it cannot be exported, signed into another scope, or used by a new top-level call. Golden tests publish each named child before Completed, substitute another Completed, create a failed/ambiguous terminal, and mutate child order/effect projection; all cross-scope consumers must reject them while the one valid pair passes.

`prerequisites[]` is sorted and unique by `kind`, and every member is exactly `{kind,reference}` where `kind` belongs to the registry-closed set `executionProvision | priorGitTransaction | executionIntent | oracleAdmission | w0OpenSetCapture | k4Predecessor | platformPolicyProbe | platformPolicyGit | k4CandidateBundlePublication | k4Attested | k4Finalized`; `reference` is `signedRecord({record_sha256})` only for `executionIntent` and is `terminalResult(GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1)` for every other kind. `executionProvision` names only the symbolic execution-worktree result and is the genesis prerequisite of the first normal Git transaction; bootstrap/sanitation provision is an internal nonterminal child of bootstrap-import and is never a CandidateUnit prerequisite. The gateway reopens every signed record, result, matching successful Completed, and root-journal edge rather than trusting a digest. `bundle_provenance` is exactly `ordinary({bundle_manifest_sha256,bundle_root}) | gatewayGenerated({bundle_manifest_sha256,bundle_root,generator:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1}) | gatewayPublished({bundle_manifest_sha256,expected_root,candidate_bundle_publication:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,k4_attested_seal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1})`. `gatewayGenerated` is admitted only for the Task-0 open-set successor bundle and byte-equals its `w0OpenSetCapture` prerequisite; `gatewayPublished` is mandatory only for the K4 proof unit. That K4 unit's prerequisite kinds are exactly one each of `priorGitTransaction`, `platformPolicyProbe`, `platformPolicyGit`, `k4CandidateBundlePublication`, and `k4Attested`, with no other kind. Its two provenance terminal references byte-equal the corresponding prerequisite values; `k4Attested` accepts only the authenticated `K4AttestedSealBodyV1` result plus its matching successful Completed. The gateway requires publication, seal, platform-policy Git result, manifest, expected root, run-state SHA, producer authorization, and platform-policy SHA to describe the same run and completed schedules. `unit_kind = reviewOnlyProposal | adoptableCandidate`; a review-only unit can produce only an exact-RED receipt and cannot enter `git-transaction`, while every adoptable unit references the complete sorted/unique required RED-receipt tuple (empty only for an explicitly registry-marked no-RED unit) and must obtain GREEN. `repository_identity` is exactly `{object_format,common_git_directory_device_inode_digest,source_selection_sha256}`; `worktree_identity` is `{registered_slot_digest,per_worktree_git_directory_device_inode_digest,worktree_real_path_commitment,provision_receipt_sha256}`. `head_binding` is the tagged value `symbolic({ref_name,ref_old_oid,head_raw_sha256,unique_checkout_registration_digest})`; detached is admitted only by the separate certification provision/certify schemas. `ref_name` is one fully qualified `refs/heads/...` literal and must byte-equal HEAD; aliases such as `checked-out`, shorthand names, or post-write inference are invalid.

Both unit identity commitments must equal the Task-0-pinned `git_service_identity.identity_commitment`; callers cannot choose Git names, emails, timezone, signing, or date. The daemon supplies the raw pinned identity and one Accepted-time UTC second with literal `+0000`, ensuring the reviewed parent/tree/message deterministically yield one commit object across resume.

`changes[]` is unsigned-UTF-8 sorted/unique and every member is exactly `{status,path,old,new}`. `old` and `new` are the closed tagged union `notApplicable(reason:add|delete) | present({git_mode,git_blob,content_length,raw_sha256,materialization_source})`; `git_mode` is only `100644 | 100755`. `A` is old-N/A/new-present, `M` both present, and `D` old-present/new-N/A. `materialization_source` is `sourceTree([]) | ordinaryBundle({bundle_leaf}) | gatewayGeneratedBundle({bundle_leaf}) | gatewayPublishedBundle({published_path})`. An unchanged old image uses `sourceTree`; reviewer bytes use `ordinaryBundle`; Task 0 successor uses `gatewayGeneratedBundle`; those bundle variants are `{bundle_leaf:"blobs/<raw_sha256>"}` and require matching provenance. K4 uses exact `published_path`. Source joins are one-to-one by target path, content length, raw digest, and Git blob; target `git_mode` is separately reconstructed from the signed change and never compared to custody filesystem mode. A deletion has no zero sentinel. Ordinary/generated bundles contain a link-count-one regular **unique physical blob set** with `bundle_filesystem_mode:"0400"` under a mode-`0700` directory. Multiple logical changes may share a blob only when bytes/length are identical; each logical change still has one manifest join and its own target Git mode. `QinaoValidationContractRegistryV1` maps immutable profiles to exact invocations/selectors/argv, expectations, error classes, limits, views, and source-specific inventory derivations; it predicts no future blob/test ID. Candidate signature uses `QINAO-CANDIDATE-UNIT-V1\0` over canonical bytes without signature under fixed key/role/time rules. `result_tree` is independently reconstructed with an isolated index.

`QinaoCandidateBundleManifestV1` is the independent non-self-referential object `{schema_version:1,entries,bundle_root}`. `entries[]` is the exact sorted unique `{bundle_leaf,bundle_filesystem_mode:"0400",content_length,raw_sha256}` tuple for non-delete postimages; `bundle_root = SHA256("QINAO-CANDIDATE-BUNDLE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEntries)) || canonicalEntries)`, and manifest SHA is raw SHA-256 of its complete canonical bytes. It is stored as sibling `candidate-bundle-manifest-v1.json`, never inside `blobs/`; CandidateUnit binds SHA/root. `ordinary` uses reviewer-supplied leaves; `gatewayGenerated` uses the same manifest grammar plus native generator terminal pair. K4 instead binds its separately defined publication manifest/root. No manifest contains or hashes its CandidateUnit, and no `bundle_filesystem_mode` is encoded as a Git tree mode.

The validation registry never predicts a future Git blob. Its script selector is exactly `trustedTree(path,git_blob) | candidateUnitPostimage(path,role,allowed_import_root,harness_derivation_id)`. The latter ID byte-equals the enclosing ordinary contract's `harness_derivation_id` and resolves the derivation table exactly once. The former pins an existing blob; the latter resolves only to the unique signed-unit postimage, records that exact blob in the invocation receipt, and is constrained by the registry's role/import/harness derivation. RED `test_surface_root` then freezes the concrete future-test blob through GREEN. A wildcard path, “latest” blob, arbitrary candidate import, or selector not resolved by one signed unit is invalid.

`candidate-materialize` verifies the signed unit, trusted-tree/head/source cleanliness, bundle provenance/bytes, and path rules. Generated/published provenance reopens every complete terminal/publication/support chain and stable-FD manifest/blob tree; bare phase/manifest/body digest is powerless. It never writes canonical worktree/index/ref, instead materializing one isolated review tree/index with pinned dirfds/no-follow/fsync/atomic rename. Its semantic terminal projection contains candidate/unit/head/source/result/review identities plus unchanged canonical identities; the **sole exact terminal wire** appears below after the plan/outcome/seal schemas. Legal resume is absent or exact frozen-plan prefix/result; foreign state blocks. Candidate scripts/shell/editors/Git porcelain have no write authority.

Before its first isolated write, materialization journals `GatewayCandidateMaterializationPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,candidate_unit_sha256,transaction_id,source_tree,result_tree,review_slot_rows,review_slot_root,rows,plan_root}`. `CandidateReviewSlotRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,slot_kind:"directory"|"leaf"|"index",depth,relative_path,final_mode,source_image_or_NA,expected_content_or_tree_identity}` is complete, sorted by `(slot_kind,depth,relative_path)`, unique by slot/path, and roots under `QINAO-CANDIDATE-MATERIALIZATION-REVIEW-SLOTS-V1\0`; no opaque slot digest remains. `CandidateMaterializationEffectPlanRowV1 = {ordinal,effect_kind,target_slot_ordinal,source_image,pre_effect_projection,expected_post_projection}` uses contiguous ordinals and kind `reviewDirectoryCreate | reviewLeafAdoption | isolatedIndexInstall | reviewDirectorySeal`; source image is `parentTree({path,git_mode,git_blob}) | candidatePostimage({path,git_mode,git_blob,bundle_leaf,raw_sha256}) | derivedIndex({result_tree}) | directorySeal({final_mode:"0500"})`. Directory creation rows run parent-before-child at private mode `0700`; leaves are installed mode `0400`; index is installed; then every directory seal is its own interruptible child-before-parent `0700→0500` row. No recursive chmod exists. Pre/post projections contain only logical slot/path/mode/content/tree/index facts—never future inode, timestamp, or receipt digest. Rows root under `QINAO-CANDIDATE-MATERIALIZATION-EFFECT-PLAN-V1\0`; the plan root hashes the complete plan without itself.

Each Prepared binds the plan receipt and row digest. `CandidateMaterializationEffectOutcomeRowV1 = {ordinal,plan_row_digest,actual_post_identity,outcome}` uses the closed union `existingVerified({stable_observation_receipt_sha256}) | writtenAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256})`; actual identity must project exactly to planned poststate, and existing verification never fabricates an effect. Outcomes are contiguous and root under `QINAO-CANDIDATE-MATERIALIZATION-EFFECT-OUTCOMES-V1\0`. After `P(n)`, two independent stable-FD walks publish no-effect `GatewayReadOnlyTreeSealObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,plan_receipt_sha256,walk_one_rows,walk_one_root,walk_two_rows,walk_two_root,result_tree,observed_at_utc}`; roots use distinct observation domains but both row projections byte-equal the frozen slots and each other. The earlier compact terminal is superseded by `GatewayCandidateMaterializationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,candidate_unit_sha256,transaction_id,unit_kind,bundle_provenance,head_binding,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,exact_change_tuple_root,result_tree,materialization_plan_receipt_sha256,materialization_effect_outcome_rows,materialization_effect_outcome_root,review_directory_identity,review_index_tree,read_only_tree_seal_observation_receipt_sha256,canonical_worktree_identity_before,canonical_worktree_identity_after,canonical_index_tree_after,materialized_at_utc,disposition:"materializedIsolatedCandidate"}`. Legal recovery state is exactly prefix `P(k)` of the frozen plan: every prior row has one matching outcome, current/remaining slots have their planned prestates, and no unjournaled mode change, leaf, index, or directory exists. Exact poststate may be adopted; partial/foreign/out-of-order state blocks. No validation begins before result plus successful Completed reopens the full plan/outcome/seal chain.

The gateway runs frozen no-effect validations against that review root/index in **independent validation scopes first**. `CandidatePackageValidationRowV1 = {package_id,inventory_sha256,inventory_root,test_surface_root,validation_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,expected_expectation:SwiftValidationExpectationV1,observed_expectation:SwiftValidationExpectationV1,validation_disposition}` sorts/uniques by package; disposition is `greenPassed([]) | exactRedConfirmed([])` and both expectation objects byte-equal the common terminal expectation, including sorted/unique `forbidden_error_classes`. Rows are bidirectionally equal to CandidateUnit Swift inventories—no package may be omitted or invented—and each terminal reopens complete common result plus successful Completed. `OrdinaryValidationExpectationV1 = green([]) | exactRed({expected_failure_contract_id,failure_stage,reason_code,exact_test_ids,positive_control_ids,forbidden_error_classes})`, with every ID/class array sorted/unique. `OrdinaryValidationRowV1 = {validation_id,test_surface_root,validation_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,expected_expectation:OrdinaryValidationExpectationV1,observed_expectation:OrdinaryValidationExpectationV1,validation_disposition}` sorts/uniques by validation ID and uses the analogous `greenPassed([]) | exactRedConfirmed([])` disposition; expected and observed expectation byte-equal. Its surface is the current result-tree projection of the registry-fixed Python/module/fixture/source rows under `QINAO-ORDINARY-VALIDATION-TEST-SURFACE-V1\0`, never a cached RED surface. The ordinary row-ID set equals the registry profile's complete `ordinary_validation_ids` in both directions, and each row's current projection is then compared byte-for-byte with the corresponding RED row where continuity is required. Package and ordinary roots use distinct `QINAO-CANDIDATE-PACKAGE-VALIDATIONS-V1\0` and `QINAO-CANDIDATE-ORDINARY-VALIDATIONS-V1\0` domains. Only after every referenced validation scope has successful Completed does a distinct candidate-review Accepted begin; it cannot reference its own future completion.

`GatewayCandidateReviewBodyV1` is `redConfirmed({schema_version:1,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,transaction_id,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,package_validation_rows,package_validation_root,ordinary_validation_rows,ordinary_validation_root,red_validation_ids,review_invocation_receipt_sha256s,review_output_root,reviewed_at_utc,disposition:"proposalRedConfirmedNoAdoption"}) | greenApproved({schema_version:1,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,transaction_id,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,package_validation_rows,package_validation_root,ordinary_validation_rows,ordinary_validation_root,required_red_receipt_sha256s,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,result_tree,exact_change_tuple_root,review_invocation_receipt_sha256s,review_output_root,reviewed_at_utc,disposition:"candidateApprovedForGitTransaction"})`. `red_validation_ids` is the sorted nonempty tagged set `swiftPackage({package_id}) | ordinary({validation_id})` and equals exactly the registry expected-RED set; thus ordinary-only RED is legal with `package_validation_rows=[]`. Every red row precisely matches expectation/controls and every other row is GREEN, with no extra import/syntax/argument/parser/fixture failure. GREEN requires every row GREEN, all prior RED receipts named, and package/ordinary test surfaces byte-equal their corresponding RED rows. BAS RED plus Qinao GREEN and ordinary RED with no Swift are representable; scalar-surface or exit-status inference is not.

Each package row's `test_surface_root` uses the single shared `QINAO-VALIDATION-TEST-SURFACE-V1\0` row type `{path,git_mode,git_blob,raw_sha256,test_ids,fixture_contract_ids}` declared above. `redConfirmed` freezes each package surface/inventory; `greenApproved` recomputes the same package-keyed rows and requires byte equality with every corresponding required RED receipt, while `required_red_receipt_sha256s` byte-equals the unit's expectation. Package add/remove/reorder, test/fixture/catalog/expected-failure drift, or aggregate-root substitution requires a new RED proposal; only implementation or explicitly non-test documentation postimages may differ.

The candidate/Git identity vocabulary is closed rather than name-only. `exact_change_tuple_root = SHA256("QINAO-CANDIDATE-CHANGE-TUPLE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalChangesBytes)) || canonicalChangesBytes)` over the complete signed `changes[]`. `GatewayWorktreeIdentityV1` is exactly `{registered_slot_digest,worktree_real_path_commitment,per_worktree_git_directory_device_inode_digest,head_binding,index_tree,worktree_tree,clean_status_digest}`; `clean_status_digest` hashes the exact empty-or-nonempty NUL-framed porcelain-v2 byte stream with `QINAO-GIT-CLEAN-STATUS-V1\0`. `GatewayReviewDirectoryIdentityV1` is exactly `{allocation_id,parent_directory_device_inode_digest,directory_device_inode_digest,path_commitment,mode:"0700",materialized_tree,read_only_tree_projection_digest}`; the final digest uses `QINAO-READ-ONLY-TREE-PROJECTION-V1\0` over the sorted complete `{path,mode,content_length,raw_sha256}` array. The fields `canonical_worktree_identity_before`, `canonical_worktree_identity_after`, and `canonical_worktree_result_identity` contain the complete first object, while `review_directory_identity` contains the complete second object. `review_output_root = SHA256("QINAO-CANDIDATE-REVIEW-OUTPUTS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalOrderedOutputArray)) || canonicalOrderedOutputArray)` over the non-empty registry-ordered `{invocation_receipt_sha256,structured_result_digest,stdout_length,stdout_sha256,stderr_length,stderr_sha256,exit_status}` array. Device/inode digests use the fixed `QINAO-FILESYSTEM-OBJECT-IDENTITY-V1\0` domain over canonical `{volume_identity_sha256,st_dev,st_ino,object_kind}` and are privacy-clean commitments, never raw paths. Golden and field-by-field mutation vectors make each value independently recomputable.

Only `greenApproved` may enter Git. The native gateway constructs closed internal `GitTransactionIntentV1 = {schema_version:1,transaction_id,head_binding,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,exact_path_mode_blob_tuple,result_tree,message,author_identity_commitment,committer_identity_commitment,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,candidate_review_receipt_sha256}` directly from the signed unit and two authenticated receipts. Thus Task 1, creation of `run_qinao_wave_gate.py`, and any later modification of that same file do not self-host their authority. An advisory candidate runner may compute comparisons, but neither its worktree bytes nor `--script-blob trusted-tree` can originate or alter the transaction intent.

Ref, checked-out worktree, and real-index adoption are one journaled state machine, not `update-ref` alone. At `GatewayInvocationAccepted`, the daemon fixes one canonical UTC Git time plus numeric timezone and resolves the Task-0-fixed service author/committer identities. `GatewayGitCommitObjectV1` is exact `{schema_version:1,parent_commit,result_tree,author_name_utf8,author_email_utf8,author_time_seconds,author_timezone,committer_name_utf8,committer_email_utf8,committer_time_seconds,committer_timezone,message_utf8,raw_commit_sha256,git_object_id}`. Message has the exact unit bytes and one terminal LF; raw commit bytes use the fixed Git object-format header/field order. Resume reuses the accepted time/identities and must regenerate byte-identical raw commit bytes/OID before any adoption.

All mutation operations reuse one acyclic vocabulary: `GatewayMutationContextV1 = gitTransaction({transaction_id}) | bootstrapProvision({run_state_receipt_sha256}) | bootstrapUnit({run_state_receipt_sha256,ordinal}) | worktreeProvision({provision_spec_sha256})`. `GatewayMutationTargetSlotV1 = gitObject({object_type,git_object_id}) | objectFanoutDirectory({fanout_component}) | worktreeDirectory({depth,path}) | worktreePath({path}) | index({index_slot}) | ref({fully_qualified_ref}) | trustedTree({chain_slot}) | registration({registered_slot_digest}) | head({head_slot}) | checkout({worktree_slot}) | reviewSlot({slot_ordinal})`; `GatewayMutationSourceImageV1 = journalBytes({receipt_sha256,content_length,raw_sha256}) | treeEntry({tree,path,git_mode,git_blob}) | derivedObject({object_type,git_object_id,raw_sha256}) | derivedIndex({tree}) | derivedRef({oid}) | derivedTrustedTree({commit,tree}) | notApplicable({reason})`; `GatewayMutationStateProjectionV1 = absent({slot_digest}) | filesystem({kind,mode,content_length,content_sha256,path_commitment}) | gitObject({object_type,git_object_id,raw_sha256}) | index({tree,raw_sha256}) | ref({fully_qualified_ref,oid,storage_identity}) | registration({registered_slot_digest,worktree_path_commitment}) | head({head_binding,raw_sha256}) | trustedTree({commit,tree,worktree_identity})`. `GatewayMutationPlanRowV1 = {ordinal,context,effect_kind,target_slot:GatewayMutationTargetSlotV1,source_image:GatewayMutationSourceImageV1,pre_effect_projection:GatewayMutationStateProjectionV1,expected_post_projection:GatewayMutationStateProjectionV1}` uses fixed legal variant combinations from the effect registry and never future inode/time/receipt. `GatewayMutationOutcomeRowV1 = {ordinal,plan_row_digest,actual_post_identity:GatewayMutationStateProjectionV1,outcome}` where outcome is `existingVerified({stable_observation_receipt_sha256}) | writtenAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256}) | removedAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256}) | sealedAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256})`. Actual projection equals frozen poststate. Outcome arrays use `QINAO-GIT-MUTATION-OUTCOMES-V1\0`, `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-PROVISION-MUTATION-OUTCOMES-V1\0`, `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-UNIT-MUTATION-OUTCOMES-V1\0`, or `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-MUTATION-OUTCOMES-V1\0` according to context, each length-framing its complete canonical rows. Prepared binds authenticated plan receipt, root, ordinal, and row digest.

Before the first Git object/directory effect, gateway journals `GatewayGitTransactionPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context:GatewayMutationContextV1,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,candidate_review_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,result_commit_object,result_tree,required_object_rows,required_object_root,object_fanout_directory_rows,object_fanout_directory_root,worktree_directory_rows,worktree_directory_root,worktree_path_rows,result_index_row,ref_cas_row,trusted_tree_advance_row,mutation_rows,transaction_plan_root}`. `GitRequiredObjectPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,object_type,git_object_id,raw_object_length,raw_object_sha256,source_image}` covers **all** required blobs, child-before-parent trees, and commit in stable order independent of current ODB presence. `GitObjectFanoutDirectoryPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,fanout_component,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}` and `GitWorktreeDirectoryPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,depth,path,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}` are complete sorted arrays. Roots use distinct `QINAO-GIT-REQUIRED-OBJECT-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-GIT-OBJECT-FANOUT-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-GIT-WORKTREE-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, and `QINAO-GIT-TRANSACTION-PLAN-V1\0` domains. An object becoming present after planning changes only its outcome to `existingVerified`; no ordinal shifts.

Closed adoption payloads are `GatewayGitObjectFanoutDirectoryAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context,slot_ordinal,fanout_component,parent_identity_before,directory_identity_after,parent_identity_after,fsynced_at_utc}`, `GatewayGitObjectAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context,slot_ordinal,object_type,git_object_id,raw_object_length,raw_object_sha256,object_identity_after,fanout_directory_identity,parent_fsync_observation_sha256,adopted_at_utc}`, and `GatewayWorktreeDirectoryAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context,slot_ordinal,depth,path,parent_identity_before,directory_identity_after,parent_identity_after,fsynced_at_utc}`. `GatewayReachableObjectClosureBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context,result_commit,required_object_rows,required_object_root,observed_object_rows,observed_object_root,verified_at_utc,disposition:"completeReachableClosure"}` requires observed rows to byte-equal the planned ID/type/length/SHA set. Each written object uses immutable source bytes plus Prepared→no-replace install→Adoption; closure succeeds before any index/ref effect. Deep `A` paths never rely on implicit mkdir.

`GitObjectAdoptionRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,plan_row_digest,object_type,git_object_id,raw_object_sha256,outcome}` where outcome is `existingVerified({stable_object_observation_receipt_sha256}) | writtenAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,object_adoption_receipt_sha256})`; it roots under `QINAO-GIT-OBJECT-ADOPTION-ROWS-V1\0`. `GatewayGitObjectFanoutDirectoryAdoptionRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,plan_row_digest,fanout_component,expected_post_projection,outcome}` and `GatewayWorktreeDirectoryAdoptionRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,plan_row_digest,depth,path,expected_post_projection,outcome}` use `existingVerified({stable_observation_receipt_sha256}) | writtenAdopted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256})`, sort by slot ordinal, and root respectively as `SHA256("QINAO-GIT-OBJECT-FANOUT-DIRECTORY-ADOPTIONS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)` and `SHA256("QINAO-GIT-WORKTREE-DIRECTORY-ADOPTIONS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`. Directory creation order is `(depth ascending,unsigned-path)` parent-before-child. Each adoption array is the exact indexed projection of its frozen plan slots and joins mutation outcomes one-to-one; existing rows have no fabricated effect. Row/domain/plan-digest swaps fail.

`GitWorktreePathPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,path,operation:"addNoReplace"|"modifyAtomicReplace"|"deleteUnlink",source_image,pre_effect_projection,expected_post_projection}`, while `GitIndexPlanRowV1`, `GitRefCASPlanRowV1`, and `GitTrustedTreeAdvancePlanRowV1` are exact singletons `{slot_ordinal,target_slot,source_image,pre_effect_projection,expected_post_projection}` with effect-kind constrained by the registry. `worktree_path_rows`, `result_index_row`, `ref_cas_row`, and `trusted_tree_advance_row` are disjoint indexed projections of `mutation_rows`; together with object/fanout/directory rows they cover it bidirectionally. `transaction_plan_root = SHA256("QINAO-GIT-TRANSACTION-PLAN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPlanWithoutRoot)) || canonicalPlanWithoutRoot)`. Bootstrap unit path/index/ref/trusted fields use the same named row types under bootstrap context and the same partition invariant.

The gateway then prepares every worktree leaf/deletion and one lock-held result index. `GatewayFilesystemObjectStateV1` is the closed union `absent({parent_directory_device_inode_digest,path_component}) | present({device_inode_digest,object_kind:"regularFile",mode,content_length,content_sha256})`; `GatewayParentDirectoryIdentityV1` is exactly `{device_inode_digest,mode,path_commitment,entry_name_set_digest}`. The `new_identity` of a deletion is `absent`, never null or zero. `worktree_path_adoption_order` is the exact sorted change tuple. Each result is `GatewayWorktreePathAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,transaction_id,ordinal,path,old_identity:GatewayFilesystemObjectStateV1,new_identity:GatewayFilesystemObjectStateV1,parent_directory_identity_before:GatewayParentDirectoryIdentityV1,parent_directory_identity_after:GatewayParentDirectoryIdentityV1,operation,prepared_sibling_identity_or_NA,replacement_observation_or_NA,fsynced_at_utc}` where operation is `addNoReplace | modifyAtomicReplace | deleteUnlink`. `A` writes/fsyncs an immutable-source-backed sibling created `O_EXCL`, rechecks absent target, then no-replace renames it. `M` holds the path lock/ACL, rechecks expected old inode/mode/length/digest immediately before an atomic replacing rename, and records both identities. `D` rechecks the same old identity immediately before unlink. A mid-write sibling is registered `reconstructibleScratch` and may be quarantined/recreated only under the same Prepared while target and old identity remain exact and complete source bytes are already journaled; otherwise it blocks. No operation writes a final leaf in place.

Legal worktree recovery is only prefix `P(k)` for `0 <= k <= changes.count`, where the first `k` paths exactly equal postimages/deletions, the suffix equals source, and every crossed edge has its Adoption or the unique Prepared/exact poststate. Reordered/extra/missing/foreign bytes block. After `P(n)`, the gateway installs result index, performs ref CAS as the final Git-authority change, then advances trusted tree. The sole legal monotonic states are `objects/directories/P(k)/source-index/source-ref/source-trusted -> objects/directories/result-worktree/source-index/source-ref/source-trusted -> objects/directories/result-worktree/result-index/source-ref/source-trusted -> objects/directories/result-worktree/result-index/result-ref/source-trusted -> objects/directories/result-worktree/result-index/result-ref/result-trusted -> transaction-receipt-published`; until ref CAS, admission locks prevent any consumer from treating the temporary result worktree/index as HEAD. Every crossed-but-unadopted dimension requires its exact Prepared and unique poststate. Every other tuple blocks without rollback/reset.

The three non-path adoption schemas are closed. Ref CAS uses exclusive no-follow lock, exact old identity/OID recheck, `newOID + LF`, atomic replace, and every changed-directory fsync; `GatewayRefCASBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,transaction_id,fully_qualified_ref,expected_old_oid,new_oid,loose_ref_identity_before,loose_ref_identity_after,packed_ref_observation,lock_created_exclusive_identity,lock_content_sha256,replacement_observation_sha256,reflog_disposition:"notWritten",cas_result:"targetReplacedAtomically",fsynced_directory_identities,fsynced_at_utc}`. Index similarly uses only `index.lock`; `GatewayIndexInstallBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,transaction_id,index_identity_before,index_identity_after,index_raw_sha256_before,index_raw_sha256_after,index_tree_before,index_tree_after,lock_created_exclusive_identity,lock_raw_sha256,target_installation,parent_directory_identity_before,parent_directory_identity_after,fsynced_at_utc}` with exact create-no-replace/replace variant. The trusted-tree semantic projection carries chain heads, previous/result commit/tree, and reserved Adoption scope sequence without future digest; the **sole exact trusted-advance wire** is declared immediately below with its tagged predecessor/worktree/context. Wrapper identities byte-equal payload projections.

Trusted predecessor is not a scalar pretending genesis is an advance. `GatewayTrustedTreePredecessorV1 = genesis({execution_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,commit,tree,worktree_identity:GatewayWorktreeIdentityV1}) | priorAdvance({advance_adoption_receipt_sha256,commit,tree,worktree_identity:GatewayWorktreeIdentityV1})`; it byte-equals `chain_head_before`. The exact trusted-advance body is `GatewayTrustedTreeAdvanceBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context:GatewayMutationContextV1,chain_head_before:GatewayTrustedTreePredecessorV1,chain_head_after,previous_commit,previous_tree,result_commit,result_tree,worktree_identity,advanced_at_utc}`. `chain_head_after = {commit,tree,worktree_identity,adoption_scope_sequence}` uses the journal-reserved Adoption sequence and predicts no future digest. Execution provision genesis embeds the complete worktree identity and its own terminal result; later predecessors reopen the prior Adoption. Scalar `previous_trusted_tree_advance_receipt_sha256` is prohibited.

The successful receipt contains `GatewayGitTransactionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,transaction_id,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,candidate_review_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,head_binding,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,result_commit_object,result_tree,result_index_tree,transaction_plan_receipt_sha256,mutation_outcome_rows,mutation_outcome_root,git_object_adoption_rows,git_object_adoption_root,git_object_fanout_directory_adoption_rows,git_object_fanout_directory_adoption_root,worktree_directory_adoption_rows,worktree_directory_adoption_root,worktree_path_adoption_rows,worktree_path_adoption_root,index_install_adoption_receipt_sha256,ref_cas_adoption_receipt_sha256,trusted_tree_advance_adoption_receipt_sha256,reachable_object_closure_receipt_sha256,canonical_worktree_result_identity,committed_at_utc,trusted_tree_predecessor:GatewayTrustedTreePredecessorV1,new_trusted_tree_advance}`. Path rows are `{ordinal,path,plan_row_digest,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256,actual_post_identity}` and root under `QINAO-WORKTREE-PATH-ADOPTION-ROWS-V1\0`; directory/fanout/outcome roots use their distinct declared domains. Every plan row joins one outcome and every written/removed/sealed outcome joins one Prepared/Adoption. `new_trusted_tree_advance` is the complete result payload and predecessor byte-equals its `chain_head_before`. `GatewayGitTransactionChainV1 = {schema_version:1,execution_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,ordered_transactions:[{git_transaction_receipt_sha256,invocation_completed_receipt_sha256}],final_commit,final_tree}` is nonempty; every row is a valid terminal reference and follows the unique tagged predecessor. `transaction_chain_root = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-GIT-TRANSACTION-CHAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalChainBytes)) || canonicalChainBytes)` only after all successful Completed envelopes exist. A transaction never hashes its future envelope/chain. It runs no hooks, filters, signing, checkout, reset, or generic porcelain. Recovery accepts only frozen-plan prefix `P(k)`; an ambient object/directory appearing mid-run can be verified into its fixed slot but cannot reorder effects. Tests interrupt before/after plan, each object/fanout/directory/leaf/index/ref/trusted-tree Prepared/native-effect/fsync/Adoption, closure, result, and Completed; explicitly cover A/M/D, absent/present index, loose/packed ref, partial reconstructible sibling, and complete reachable closure. A result without successful Completed is never a predecessor. `publish-directory` retains its closed same-parent `RENAME_EXCL` protocol. Every normative **post-bootstrap** W0 mutation is `reviewOnlyProposal` exact-RED when required, followed by `adoptableCandidate -> candidate-materialize -> candidate-review green -> git-transaction`; historical `git add`/`git commit` spellings are migration targets only.

`QinaoWorktreeProvisionSpecV1` is exact `{schema_version:1,provision_id,mode_payload,repository_identity,repository_worktree_root_identity,relative_worktree_slot,worktree_path_commitment,target_index_tree,target_worktree_tree,issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,signer_key_id,signer_principal_id,signer_role:"w0-implementer",signer_epoch,signature_algorithm,signature}`. `mode_payload` is the disjoint union `executionSymbolic({source_selection_sha256,bootstrap_import_final:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,base_ref,base_commit,base_tree,target_ref,ref_transitions,target_head_binding,expected_initial_state}) | certificationDetached({final_candidate_observation:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,source_commit,source_tree,final_commit,final_tree,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,trusted_transaction_chain_root,target_head_binding,expected_initial_state})`. Execution requires a fully qualified target ref, exact absent-or-byte-identical base/target transitions, symbolic HEAD, a successful bootstrap-import terminal pair, and clean index/worktree at the base commit/tree. Certification has no ref field or transition, creates no ref, and requires detached HEAD at the chain-verified final commit/tree. Before any certification worktree effect, the gateway reopens the complete authenticated final-candidate observation result plus its matching successful Completed, reruns its static-scope/history/K4-finalized checks, and requires its commit/tree/final-transaction pair/transaction chain to byte-equal every repeated field in the signed provision spec. `repository_worktree_root_identity` is the no-follow device/inode/path-commitment identity of the admitted non-bare repository worktree root—the parent of the common Git directory in this repository layout—not the common Git directory itself. Slot is one normalized relative path beneath that root; per-worktree administrative data remains beneath the common Git directory. Callers never supply an arbitrary absolute path. The signature is `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-SPEC-V1\0 || uint64be(len(canonicalWithoutSignature)) || canonicalWithoutSignature` under the Task-0 low-S/key/role/time rules.

Before its first effect, `worktree-provision` journals `GatewayWorktreeProvisionPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context:GatewayMutationContextV1,provision_spec_sha256,mode,repository_identity,repository_worktree_root_identity,common_git_directory_identity,mode_specific_rows,mode_specific_root,directory_rows,directory_root,path_rows,path_root,mutation_rows,mutation_plan_root}`. `mode` is the closed union `executionSymbolic({source_selection_sha256,bootstrap_import_final:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,base_ref,base_commit,base_tree,target_ref,target_head_binding}) | certificationDetached({final_candidate_observation:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,final_commit,final_tree,target_head_binding})`. `WorktreeProvisionModePlanRowV1 = refCAS(...) | registration(...) | headInstall(...) | indexInstall(...) | checkoutSeal(...) | trustedTreeGenesis(...)`; incompatible variants are absent, rows keep fixed slot ordinals, execution has ref/genesis and certification does not. `WorktreeProvisionDirectoryPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,depth,relative_slot,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}` and `WorktreeProvisionPathPlanRowV1 = {slot_ordinal,relative_path,source_image,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}` include every parent/admin/content path; creation sorts parent-before-child. Roots length-frame complete rows under `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-MODE-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, and `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-PATH-PLAN-V1\0`; mutation root uses `QINAO-WORKTREE-PROVISION-MUTATION-PLAN-V1\0` over the whole plan without itself. The three projections are disjoint and cover mutation rows bidirectionally. Each Prepared binds plan receipt/root/row digest/ordinal.

The terminal wire is `GatewayWorktreeProvisionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,provision_spec_sha256,provision_plan_receipt_sha256,mutation_outcome_rows,mutation_outcome_root,repository_identity,repository_worktree_root_identity,common_git_directory_identity,per_worktree_git_directory_identity,registered_slot_digest,worktree_real_path_commitment,index_raw_sha256,index_tree,worktree_tree,clean_status_digest,provision_result,provisioned_at_utc}`. `provision_result` is the disjoint union `executionSymbolic({ref_state_before,ref_state_after,head_raw_sha256,head_binding,trusted_tree_genesis}) | certificationDetached({final_candidate_observation:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,head_raw_sha256,head_binding,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,trusted_transaction_chain_root,detached_commit,detached_tree})`; neither mode serializes the other mode's fields or an N/A sentinel. Execution's `trusted_tree_genesis` is exact `GatewayTrustedTreeGenesisV1 = {source_selection_sha256,bootstrap_import_final:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,provision_receipt_scope_sequence,target_ref,commit,tree,index_tree,worktree_identity}`. Certification independently recomputes the complete transaction-chain root and authenticated observation terminal pair and has no genesis. Every frozen plan row joins exactly one ordered outcome; an existing verified slot has no fabricated effect receipt, while a changed slot has one Prepared/dispatch/Adoption chain. Legal recovery is exactly `P(k)` of the plan: completed prefix outcomes match poststate, suffix slots match planned prestate, and at most the current slot is reconciled from its unique Prepared/dispatch state. Ref-only, admin-only, checkout-only, reordered, extra, foreign, moved, advanced, dirty, or duplicate-registered states outside that prefix block without delete/reset. The first Git transaction references the complete successful execution-provision terminal pair as its predecessor, and every later session reopens both envelopes before any task. Tests interrupt before/after plan and every ref, registry, administrative/content directory, HEAD, index, leaf, checkout, genesis, dispatch, Adoption, result, Completed, and fsync edge in both modes.

`QinaoCertificationSpecV1` is exact `{schema_version:1,certification_id,source_selection_sha256,repository_identity,detached_worktree_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,source_commit,source_tree,final_commit,final_tree,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,manifest_blob,manifest_sha256,validation_contract_registry_root,validation_profile_id,k4_platform_policy_sha256,k4_approved_evidence_root,k4_private_artifact_path_commitment,k4_approved_encrypted_volume_identity_sha256,k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256,k4_erasure_registry_contract_id,k4_policy_acceptance_registry_id,k4_key_revocation_registry_id,k4_custody_policy_id,k4_final_publication:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,k4_finalized:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,stable_state_root_commitment,k4_verification_receipt_name,final_all_receipt_name,handoff_receipt_name,issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,signer_key_id,signer_principal_id,signer_role:"w0-implementer",signer_epoch,signature_algorithm,signature}`. Its signature preimage is `QINAO-CERTIFICATION-SPEC-V1\0 || uint64be(len(canonicalWithoutSignature)) || canonicalWithoutSignature`. It pins policy/volume/clock/erasure/activity/custody expectations only—not future observation/lease receipts. Every terminal reference is reopened and byte-matched before start. Artifact commitment and volume equal producer/ciphertext/retention/verify projections.
