Stable state is the dedicated encrypted `certify-state` tree with exact no-replace cache layout; runner `/tmp` is never authority. Archival remains optional outside v1.

`certify` takes `--certification-spec <external-mode-0600-record> --worktree-provision-receipt <receipt> --stable-state-root-env QINAO_W0_CERTIFY_STATE_DIR --native-credential-set <public-nonsecret-set-id> --result-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-mode-0600-path> --handoff-receipt <absent-or-byte-identical-mode-0600-path>`. The environment path is consumed only by the native daemon, opened no-follow, and commitment-matched to the spec; it is never forwarded to sandbox argv/environment. It is one resumable gateway state machine: revalidate the detached-provision/final-observation/transaction-chain terminal pairs; run K4 `verify-existing`; publish/reopen its authenticated result; acquire the custody lease and fresh retention observation defined in Task 5; then launch the runner in certification-only local-broker mode with the verify receipt on preopened FD 8 **before any gate executes**. The runner writes one non-authoritative canonical inner receipt to a gateway-owned ephemeral `/tmp` leaf. The daemon independently validates all inner bytes and derives the structural array.

The exact first terminal payload is `GatewayFinalAllBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,certification_spec_sha256,k4_verification_receipt_sha256,k4_custody_lease_receipt_sha256,k4_retention_observation_receipt_sha256,final_all_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,inner_final_all_receipt_sha256,inner_final_all_receipt,repository_observation_session_completed_receipt_sha256,structural_verification_results,structural_verification_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,validated_at_utc,status:"pass"}`. Next is `GatewayCertificationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,certification_spec_sha256,detached_worktree_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,source_commit,source_tree,final_commit,final_tree,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,manifest_blob,manifest_sha256,validation_contract_registry_root,k4_verification_receipt_sha256,k4_custody_lease_receipt_sha256,k4_retention_observation_receipt_sha256,certification_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,k4_final_publication:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,k4_finalized:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,final_all_receipt_sha256,structural_verification_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,certified_at_utc,status:"pass"}`. Final child is `W1HandoffBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,certification_receipt_sha256,source_commit,source_tree,final_commit,final_tree,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,manifest_sha256,k4_verification_receipt_sha256,k4_custody_lease_receipt_sha256,k4_retention_observation_receipt_sha256,handoff_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,k4_final_publication:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,k4_finalized:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,final_all_receipt_sha256,gateway_key_id,gateway_journal_epoch,issued_at_utc,status:"handoffBoundPendingScopeCompletion"}`. Each clock child is chained/fresh immediately before its payload. Certify Completed uses `terminal_policy_observation = k4Retention({custody_lease_receipt_sha256,retention_observation_receipt_sha256,completed_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,erasure_registry_snapshot,policy_acceptance_snapshot,key_revocation_snapshot})`; every other invocation uses `notApplicable({reason_code})`. Completed's final observation is appended immediately before the atomic Completed+lease-release transition.

`QinaoStructuralVerificationResultV1` is exactly `{ordinal,gate_id,validation_contract_registry_root,execution_receipt_sha256,execution_receipt,structured_result_digest,exit_status,disposition:"pass"}`; the complete duplicate-key-rejected sanitized gate receipt is embedded and its raw SHA equals the locator. The non-empty array follows fixed registry order and includes every cumulative, owner-ledger, review, K4, manifest, history, and cleanliness gate once. `structural_verification_root = SHA256("QINAO-STRUCTURAL-VERIFICATION-RESULTS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalResultArrayBytes)) || canonicalResultArrayBytes)`. Final-all is copied as a complete authenticated envelope to stable `final-all.json`; certification recomputes the root from it. W1 eligibility remains `W1EligibilityProofV1 = {schema_version:1,handoff_receipt_sha256,certify_completed_receipt_sha256}`: the consumer reopens the same scope and requires successful Completed whose final children are the exact final-all, certification, and handoff envelopes and whose effect/support roots verify. Handoff-before-Completed is only cache. Interruption resumes the exact suffix; missing initial K4 input, lease/retention drift, structural failure, mismatched incumbent, or handoff without successful Completed blocks without overwrite.

`python-script --capability-profile k4-producer|k4-attester|read-only-checker|candidate-review` creates one authenticated, close-on-exec Unix `SOCK_SEQPACKET` broker FD and passes only that FD to the sandboxed child. Candidate code sends declarative closed requests; the native gateway—not Python—executes tools, parses raw result bytes with frozen native schemas, evaluates the policy/lease/effect fence, and constructs authoritative evidence/result records from its own journal. K4 producer may request the frozen xcrun/devicectl, xcodebuild, codesign-read, plutil-read, CMS-encryption, CSPRNG, and external-no-replace-write contracts, but receives **no raw device/profile/key selector, decryption or signing operation/keychain/identity**. K4 attester may request read-only codesign/plutil/CMS-decrypt and its separately authorized CMS-sign contract, but no device mutation/build/install operation. Checker receives read-only Git/CMS/certificate verification only. Candidate review can inspect only the signed unit's materialized postimages and fixed source/trusted-tree objects and can emit non-authoritative structured test results; the daemon independently binds those results to the exact unit and never lets candidate code plan a Git mutation.

All checker/runner Git observations use one acyclic pair. `GatewayRepositorySnapshotBodyV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,repository_identity,view,object_format,commit,tree,index_tree,worktree_tree,clean_status_digest,path_table_root,object_table_root,untracked_ordinary_root,untracked_ignored_root,observed_at_utc}`; `view` is `sourceIndex({source_selection_sha256}) | trustedExecution({trusted_tree_advance_receipt_sha256}) | isolatedReview({candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,review_directory_identity,review_index_tree}) | detachedCertification({worktree_provision_receipt_sha256,transaction_chain_root})`. `canonical_snapshot_body_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of those complete canonical body bytes. `GatewayRepositoryObservationSessionV1 = {schema_version:1,backend,snapshot,session_digest}` embeds that exact body as `snapshot`; `backend` is the disjoint union `ephemeralCIReadOnlyGit({git_identity,fixed_environment_digest,canonical_snapshot_body_sha256}) | localGatewayBroker({broker_session_id,repository_snapshot_receipt_sha256})`. In CI the backend digest equals the independently recomputed body hash. Locally `repository_snapshot_receipt_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete authenticated child envelope whose payload byte-equals `snapshot`; neither body contains the envelope digest. `session_digest = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-REPOSITORY-OBSERVATION-SESSION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSessionWithoutDigestBytes)) || canonicalSessionWithoutDigestBytes)`. The runner's single `run_git_bytes(session, semantic_argv)` seam dispatches to fixed read-only Git only for the first variant and to sequence-numbered messages on the existing broker FD for the second. `GitObservationRequestV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,sequence,operation_id,semantic_argv,request_digest}` and `GitObservationResponseV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,sequence,request_digest,status,payload_length,payload_sha256,payload,response_digest}`. Their digest fields respectively use `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-REQUEST-V1\0` and `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-RESPONSE-V1\0` over length-framed canonical objects without the digest field. `ordered_request_response_root = SHA256("QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-REQUEST-RESPONSE-ROOT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRowsBytes)) || canonicalRowsBytes)` over declaration-ordered exact `{sequence,request_digest,response_digest}` rows. Path, object, ordinary-untracked, and ignored-untracked tables use the four distinct domains `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-PATH-TABLE-V1\0`, `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-OBJECT-TABLE-V1\0`, `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-ORDINARY-UNTRACKED-TABLE-V1\0`, and `QINAO-GIT-OBSERVATION-IGNORED-UNTRACKED-TABLE-V1\0`, each length-framing canonical sorted/unique closed rows: path `{path,mode,stage,object_id,content_length,content_sha256}`, object `{object_id,object_type,content_length,content_sha256}`, and each untracked class `{path,mode,content_length,content_sha256}`. The registry closes operations to object type/read, blob read, stage-0 entry listing, untracked/ignored listing, parent walk, tree diff, status, and authenticated index-tree observation. Local sandbox code never receives `.git`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE`, an object-directory path, or a Git executable. In both backends `candidate_index_snapshot` consumes `session.snapshot.index_tree` and the authenticated tables; it never invokes Git tree creation, and the daemon recomputes the same tree in its isolated native object store before review/adoption. Request order, view identity, payload length/hash, and pre/post snapshot identity are closed by terminal no-effect `GatewayRepositoryObservationSessionCompletedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,session_digest,snapshot_body_sha256,ordered_request_response_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,completed_at_utc,disposition:"readOnlyComplete"}`; locally its receipt digest names the complete authenticated envelope, not bare payload bytes. A source-index/review-index mix, missing response, second backend, write-capable request, direct local subprocess, or body/envelope substitution fails. The preliminary K4 checker consumes the same source-view blob projection. Unit tests exercise both backends against identical golden byte streams and prove equal results, while mutation tests swap canonical/review indexes, request a tree-creation operation, omit ignored paths, replay responses, or change a payload after its authenticated hash.

Every gateway call begins with `GatewayInvocationV1 = {schema_version:1,authority_key,operation_key,cache_projection,invocation_nonce,subcommand,execution_source,repository_context,capability_profile,selection_binding,credential_commitments,ordered_argv_preimage,argv_digest,repository_identity_sha256,scope_id,requested_at_utc,invocation_digest}`. `selection_binding = preselectionSnapshot({snapshot_authorization_sha256}) | sourceSelection({source_baseline_selection_sha256})`; only `snapshot-source-state` may use the former. Cache projection is `{receipt_directory_path_commitment,result_cache_path_commitments}` and carries no authority. `execution_source = native({native_contract_id}) | trustedTree({path,git_blob,trusted_tree_advance_receipt_sha256}) | candidateUnit({path,git_blob,candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256})`. `repository_context = preselectionSource({source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,snapshot_authorization_sha256}) | bootstrapSource({source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,source_selection_sha256}) | trustedExecution({commit,tree,trusted_tree_advance_receipt_sha256}) | detachedCertification({final_commit,final_tree,transaction_chain_root})`; the registry permits only the corresponding source/context pairs. `capability_profile = nativeProfile(contract_id) | sandboxProfile(k4-producer|k4-attester|read-only-checker|candidate-review)`. Credential commitments are sorted name/digest pairs. Arg atoms remain `literal | repositoryRelative | pathCommitment | secretCommitment | derivedDigest`; raw private paths/selectors/secrets are unrepresentable. Arg digest uses `QINAO-GATEWAY-ARGV-V1\0`. The registry derives the cache-free semantic request projection, retaining every behavior/authority/output-destination atom; unknown classification blocks.

`semantic_authority` is the closed union `sourceSnapshot({snapshot_authorization_sha256}) | bootstrap({source_selection_sha256}) | candidate({candidate_unit_sha256,expected_outcome}) | provision({provision_spec_sha256}) | gitTransaction({candidate_unit_sha256,materialization_receipt_sha256,candidate_review_receipt_sha256}) | k4({producer_authorization_sha256,verb,run_state:notCreatedYet|present(sha256)}) | cryptoErasure({erasure_key_digest,operator_receipt_sha256}) | nativeTool({native_contract_id,authorization_sha256,native_credential_set_commitment}) | certification({certification_spec_sha256,worktree_provision_receipt_sha256}) | gitRead({operation_id,input_commitments}) | python({script_blob,profile,input_record_sha256s}) | publication({authorization_sha256,manifest_sha256,expected_root})`. Authority/operation preimages retain their declared domains; snapshot uses preselection and all other variants source selection. Operation key excludes cache/nonce/time. Semantic drift blocks under incumbent authority; alternate cache only copies identical envelopes.

To avoid a child/completion cycle, the daemon first constructs `GatewayInvocationAcceptedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,invocation,service_registration_receipt_sha256,service_observation_generation,accepted_at_utc,disposition:"accepted"}` from one freshly reverified live registration; capability issue rechecks it again. Every begin-invocation transaction atomically commits/fsyncs its unique-authority reservation/index row and Accepted envelope; the first invocation in a scope additionally commits genesis in that same indivisible batch. This applies equally to later K4 invocations in an existing scope. Recovery therefore observes reservation+Accepted (and, when first, genesis) all-or-none. Terminal `GatewayInvocationCompletedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,invocation_accepted_receipt_sha256,service_generation_chain_rows,initial_service_registration_receipt_sha256,service_generation_rebind_receipt_sha256s,service_generation_chain_root,final_service_registration_receipt_sha256,final_service_observation_generation,ordered_child_receipt_sha256s,effect_projection_rows,effect_projection_root,terminal_policy_observation,exit_status,completed_at_utc,disposition:"completed"|"failed"|"blocked"}` follows every child. `terminal_policy_observation = notApplicable({reason_code}) | k4Retention({custody_lease_receipt_sha256,retention_observation_receipt_sha256,completed_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,erasure_registry_snapshot,policy_acceptance_snapshot,key_revocation_snapshot})`; only verify-existing/certify policies may select the latter and exact registry rows define which. Chain rows use the exact tagged schema and final pair equals the last tip. `GatewayEffectProjectionRowV1 = {effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,effect_class,pre_effect_identity,service_generation_binding,terminal_outcome}` where outcome is `adopted({adoption_child_receipt_sha256,post_effect_identity,disposition}) | verifiedExisting({stable_observation_receipt_sha256,post_effect_identity,reason_code:"preexistingExactPoststate"}) | notStarted({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256_or_NA,reason_code}) | failed({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256_or_NA,failure_observation_receipt_sha256,reason_code}) | ambiguousBlocked({prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256,recovery_observation_receipt_sha256,reason_code})`. Rows sort by globally frozen effect ordinal and cover the ordered union of every authenticated universal plan child one-to-one on success; success uses exactly `adopted` or `verifiedExisting`, and every operation-specific `existingVerified` outcome maps to the latter without fabricating Prepared/dispatch/Adoption. Failed/blocked covers the maximal attempted prefix plus typed not-started suffix. Root uses `QINAO-GATEWAY-EFFECT-PROJECTION-V1\0`; an empty projection is legal only when every authenticated plan is a registry-declared explicit empty plan. Crash goldens cover all begin transactions and marker/native ambiguity. Retry never duplicates or drifts chain/plan/outcome/cache/service.

The sole executable effect-schedule wire is a `GatewayChildBodyV1` with `child_kind:"effectPlan"` and payload `GatewayEffectPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,operation_key,subcommand,effect_support_contract_registry_root,operation_plan_contract_id,operation_plan_schema_digest,operation_plan,operation_plan_sha256,effect_plan_rows,effect_plan_root,planned_at_utc}`. `GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1 = {effect_ordinal,support_contract_id,effect_id,effect_class,result_kind,result_payload_schema_digest,operation_plan_row_schema_digest,operation_plan_row,operation_plan_row_digest,exact_intent_digest,pre_effect_identity,expected_post_effect_identity,fence:notApplicable(noDynamicFence)|present(fence_digest)}` and the closed tagged row is `GatewayEffectPlanRowV1 = filesystem(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1) | git(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1) | platform(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1) | credential(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1) | lease(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1) | publication(GatewayPlannedEffectProjectionV1)`. Its tag byte-equals the resolved support row's `effect_family`; unknown or cross-family tags are unrepresentable.

`operation_plan` and each `operation_plan_row` are complete duplicate-key-rejected canonical objects. Their contract/schema digests and the pinned projection implementation come from the one resolved `GatewayEffectSupportContractRowV1`. `operation_plan_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical operation-plan bytes. `operation_plan_row_digest = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-OPERATION-PLAN-ROW-V1\0" || operationPlanRowSchemaDigestRaw32 || uint64be(len(canonicalOperationPlanRowBytes)) || canonicalOperationPlanRowBytes)` and `effect_plan_row_digest = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-EFFECT-PLAN-ROW-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalTaggedGatewayEffectPlanRowBytes)) || canonicalTaggedGatewayEffectPlanRowBytes)`. `effect_id` uses the formula below and is recomputed before root calculation. Rows are sorted by, unique on, and globally contiguous in `effect_ordinal` within the invocation. `effect_plan_root = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-EFFECT-PLAN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalGatewayEffectPlanBodyWithoutEffectPlanRootBytes)) || canonicalGatewayEffectPlanBodyWithoutEffectPlanRootBytes)`. The body cannot cite its own envelope SHA, and retry reopens its one byte-identical child rather than resampling `planned_at_utc`.

The registry-pinned projection derives every universal row deterministically from the embedded operation-specific plan and verification requires a bidirectional exact join: every effect-bearing operation-plan row appears once in `effect_plan_rows`; every universal row projects from exactly one embedded row; and family, schema, support, intent, prestate, expected poststate, fence, result, ordinal, ID, and both row digests byte-equal. Existing `GatewayCandidateMaterializationPlanBodyV1`, `GatewayGitTransactionPlanBodyV1`, `GatewayWorktreeProvisionPlanBodyV1`, `GatewayBootstrapProvisionPlanBodyV1`, `GatewayBootstrapIsolatedMaterializationPlanBodyV1`, and `GatewayBootstrapUnitPlanBodyV1` values are exact `operation_plan` payloads inside this wire, never sibling schedule receipts or a second authority. Their internal roots validate domain structure; the enclosing `effect_plan_root` and universal row digest alone authorize execution. Every other effect-capable subcommand—including source snapshot, directory publication, K4 device operations, crypto-erasure registry CAS, certification custody acquire/rebind/release, and credential/key effects—uses its one registry-pinned operation-plan and row schema through the same wrapper. Multiple phase plans have disjoint increasing ordinal ranges. `effect_plan_rows:[]` is legal only for a registry-declared explicit no-effect plan.

Accordingly, prose saying that an operation-specific plan is “journaled” means that the complete object is embedded in this authenticated `effectPlan` child. Later fields named `*_plan_receipt_sha256` or `plan_receipt_sha256` identify that complete outer child envelope; `operation_plan_sha256` identifies only the embedded domain object. Every otherwise unqualified `plan_row_digest` in an operation-specific outcome/adoption row byte-equals the enclosing universal `effect_plan_row_digest`; the distinct embedded source-row commitment is always named `operation_plan_row_digest`. This naming rule prevents a body/envelope or domain/universal digest substitution.

Every receipt between Accepted and Completed uses one universal `GatewayChildBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,parent_invocation_receipt_sha256,service_generation_binding,child_kind,payload}` as the actual `GatewayReceiptV1.body`; Accepted, Completed, scope genesis, and service registration are the only exceptions. The exact closed union is `service_generation_binding = initial({registration_receipt_sha256,observation_generation}) | rebound({rebind_receipt_sha256,registration_receipt_sha256,observation_generation}) | transitionChain({from_historical_registration_receipt_sha256,to_live_registration_receipt_sha256,from_generation,to_generation,transition_root})`. `initial/rebound` must equal the unique current tip; transitionChain is rebind-only and installs its final live tip atomically. Every external or OS effect first publishes/fsyncs `GatewayEffectPreparedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,operation_key,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,effect_class,exact_intent_digest,pre_effect_identity,authorized_at_utc,fence:notApplicable(noDynamicFence)|present(fence_digest)}`. The referenced `child_kind:"effectPlan"` child has the same parent/scope, precedes every Prepared it governs, and its recomputed universal root/ordinal/row digest resolves one unique frozen tagged row; dynamic service children never renumber it. `effect_id = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-EFFECT-ID-V1\0" || operationKeyRaw32 || uint64be(effect_ordinal) || uint64be(len(effectClassUTF8)) || effectClassUTF8)`. Every later support/Adoption/projection value repeats and byte-equals the five plan/effect identifiers. Prepared plus exact prestate permits dispatch only through the membrane below; an exact poststate publishes Adoption, while ambiguity produces a typed blocked projection. The plan row's `support_contract_id` resolves exactly one row in the complete registry embedded in the current live registration; subcommand, family, effect class, result kind, result-payload schema, operation-plan contracts/schemas, and projection contract all byte-equal before Prepared. This covers every filesystem/ref/index/trusted-tree/registration/publication/crypto/lease mutation. Copying an already journaled envelope to absent cache is non-authoritative. Prepared/dispatch/recovery/reauthorization are no-effect support and are included by Completed/exported closure. A repeated/missing/wrong parent/generation, unregistered kind/schema, plan substitution, native call without marker, or duplicate Adoption fails.

Cross-generation effect recovery refines the preceding shorthand with a dispatch membrane. After Prepared and before **any** native visibility, the daemon atomically appends/fsyncs `GatewayEffectDispatchEnteredBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,service_generation_binding,fence,dispatcher_contract_id,dispatcher_lock_identity:GatewayDispatcherLockIdentityV1,entered_at_utc}` while holding that exact logical lock slot; its inner service binding must byte-equal the universal wrapper. The dispatcher, dispatcher-lock, recovery-query, and process-termination IDs, service roles, implementation identities, schemas, and test vectors must be the four exact contracts in the plan row's resolved support-registry row. The gateway deterministically derives `dispatcher_lock_identity` from that row plus the enclosing `scope_id` and `effect_id`, recomputes `lock_key_digest`, and rejects any ambient, process-local, caller-selected, or generation-substituted lock. `GatewayProcessTerminationObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,service_registration_receipt_sha256,observation_generation,observation_contract_id,observation_result:"terminal",kernel_or_xpc_evidence_digest,observed_at_utc}` is the only terminal proof; PID/path absence is invalid. `GatewayDispatchAbsenceRangeV1 = {scope_id,prepared_scope_sequence,expected_marker_scope_sequence,intervening_rebind_scope_sequences,service_generation_chain_prefix_root,range_end_scope_sequence,range_end_scope_root,expected_recovery_scope_sequence,expected_child_kind:"effectDispatchEntered"}` is acyclic: range end is recovery sequence minus one and root equals the recovery envelope's outer previous-scope root.

`GatewayEffectRecoveryObservationBodyV1 = sameGenerationQueried({schema_version:1,original_prepared_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,registration_receipt_sha256,process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256,dispatcher_terminal_or_cancellation_observation_receipt_sha256,query_contract_id,authenticated_query_result,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,observed_at_utc}) | neverBegan({schema_version:1,original_prepared_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,service_generation_chain_prefix_root,old_process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,old_process_termination_observation_receipt_sha256,dispatcher_contract_id,dispatcher_lock_identity:GatewayDispatcherLockIdentityV1,dispatch_absence_range:GatewayDispatchAbsenceRangeV1,pre_effect_identity,observed_at_utc}) | terminatedAndQueried({schema_version:1,original_prepared_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,service_generation_chain_prefix_root,old_process_instance_identity:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,old_process_termination_observation_receipt_sha256,dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256,query_contract_id,authenticated_query_result,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,observed_at_utc})`. The `neverBegan` lock identity byte-equals the original marker slot that would have been used and is recomputed from the resolved support row, scope, and effect; a substituted namespace/storage/key cannot prove absence. Same-generation query requires authenticated cancellation/dispatcher terminality preventing a late result; cross-generation variants bind the complete intervening rebind-chain prefix and final live tip. Marker absence plus contiguous journal/persistent-lock state proves never-began; marker presence always queries. Root/self-reference, late-reply, omitted rebind, or bare PID/path proof fails.

`GatewayEffectCrossGenerationProofV1 = sameGenerationDirect({dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256}) | sameGenerationRecovered({dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256,recovery_observation_receipt_sha256}) | provedNotStarted({service_generation_chain_prefix_root,recovery_observation_receipt_sha256,reauthorization_receipt_sha256,new_dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256}) | mayHaveCrossed({service_generation_chain_prefix_root,recovery_observation_receipt_sha256,dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256})`. Marker absence permits one marker plus first call; direct proof has no future digest. Marker presence never permits a second call; same-generation loss uses `sameGenerationQueried`, while restart uses the chain-bound query. For proved-not-started, `GatewayEffectReauthorizationV1 = {schema_version:1,original_prepared_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,exact_intent_digest,new_service_generation_binding,new_fence_or_NA,recovery_observation_receipt_sha256,reauthorized_at_utc}` and its new dispatch marker are appended/fsynced as one dispatcher-lock atomic batch under the final live tip; recovery observes both or neither. Marker-present paths are reconcile-only. Support closure/Completed/K4 insertions bind every edge. Goldens cover multiple restarts before recovery, restart after recovery, lost reply, and every marker/call/Adoption cut.

For exact wire decoding the universal Adoption schema is `GatewayEffectAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,generation_transition:GatewayEffectCrossGenerationProofV1,effect_reauthorization_receipt_sha256_or_NA,result_kind,result_payload,post_effect_identity,adopted_at_utc,disposition:"executedAndAdopted"|"reconciledUniquePoststate"}`. Direct/proved-not-started require executed disposition; recovered/may-have-crossed require reconciled; reauthorization is present only for proved-not-started. Every identifier derives from and byte-equals Prepared.

Every post-admission, live-lease K4 build/device broker child produces authenticated `GatewayToolExecutionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,operation_id,child_id,request_registry_root,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_prefix_sha256,lease_fencing_token,executable_identity,argv_digest,authorized_at_utc,stdout_length,stdout_sha256,stderr_length,stderr_sha256,raw_result_length,raw_result_sha256,exit_status,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,disposition}`; K4 evidence must byte-equal the gateway-derived projection of these bodies. After the terminal lease event, attestation tools instead use `K4AttestationToolExecutionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,attestation_operation_id,child_id,request_registry_root,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,terminal_device_lease_chain_sha256,attester_policy_identity_digest,executable_identity,argv_digest,authorized_at_utc,stdout_length,stdout_sha256,stderr_length,stderr_sha256,raw_result_length,raw_result_sha256,exit_status,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,disposition}` and cannot carry or revive a lease token. Every request/result is sequence-numbered, length-framed, journaled, and bound by its wrapper to the parent invocation/run/authorization and either the current live fence or the final terminal lease chain; unknown/replayed/reordered request, stale/released-fence confusion, executable/argv drift, or effect outside its policy interval fails before execution. No candidate-supplied output can become evidence merely because its hashes are self-consistent.

Every gateway result uses one common authenticated envelope `GatewayReceiptV1 = {schema_version:1,receipt_kind,receipt_sequence,invocation_nonce,body,body_digest,previous_journal_root,journal_root,scope_id,scope_sequence,previous_scope_root,scope_root,authentication}`. Global `receipt_sequence` is contiguous uint64 within one installed gateway epoch; `scope_sequence` is independently contiguous within one signed execution scope; and `invocation_nonce` is 256-bit lowercase hex. `body_digest = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-RECEIPT-BODY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalBodyBytes)) || canonicalBodyBytes)`. Global sequence zero has `previous_journal_root = 64*"0"`; every later record repeats the immediately preceding root. `journal_root = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-JOURNAL-RECORD-V1\0" || previousJournalRootRaw32 || uint64be(receipt_sequence) || invocationNonceRaw32 || bodyDigestRaw32 || uint64be(len(receiptKindUTF8)) || receiptKindUTF8)`. Scope zero has `previous_scope_root = 64*"0"`; `scope_root = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-SCOPE-RECORD-V1\0" || scopeIDRaw32 || previousScopeRootRaw32 || uint64be(scope_sequence) || journalRootRaw32 || bodyDigestRaw32)`. `authentication = {key_id,epoch,signature_algorithm:"ecdsa-p256-sha256-low-s-v1",signature}` signs exactly `"QINAO-GATEWAY-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEnvelopeWithoutAuthenticationBytes)) || canonicalEnvelopeWithoutAuthenticationBytes`. The gateway fsyncs the same complete canonical envelope into its root-owned global journal plus per-scope accumulator before returning it; lookup by global or scope tuple must reproduce byte-identical bytes.

Every non-K4 call has a deterministic scope. `selection_binding_digest` hashes the complete tagged preselection/source-selection binding; `scope_id = SHA256("QINAO-GATEWAY-INVOCATION-SCOPE-V1\0" || selectionBindingDigestRaw32 || invocationNonceRaw32 || repositoryIdentityRaw32 || uint64be(len(subcommandUTF8)) || subcommandUTF8)`. Sequence zero is `GatewayInvocationScopeGenesisBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,scope_id,authority_key,operation_key,authority_key_preimage_digest,operation_key_preimage_digest,service_registration_receipt_sha256,selection_binding,repository_identity_sha256,subcommand,invocation_nonce,created_at_utc}`. Genesis, unique-authority index/reservation, and initial Accepted are the indivisible transaction defined above; children are fixed and Completed is terminal. Snapshot uses preselection; bootstrap and later use signed selection. Composite calls reference terminal pairs without merging scopes. Wrong binding/genesis/index, nonce reuse, cross-scope child, or gap fails.

For K4, `scope_id = SHA256("QINAO-K4-EXECUTION-SCOPE-V1\0" || producerAuthorizationSHA256Raw32 || uint64be(len(objectFormatUTF8)) || objectFormatUTF8 || uint64be(len(sourceTreeOIDBytes)) || sourceTreeOIDBytes)`, where the OID byte length is derived from the admitted repository object format rather than fixed to SHA-1. Scope sequence zero is `GatewayScopeGenesisBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,scope_id,service_registration_receipt_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,object_format,source_commit,source_tree,created_at_utc}`. Its immediate successor is the prepare-run `GatewayInvocationAccepted` envelope; policy admission is that accepted invocation's first child, and the matching `GatewayInvocationCompleted` follows all of its children. `gateway-prepublication-receipts.json` contains the complete contiguous K4 scope subsequence from genesis through exact `attesterInvocationCompleted`, including the `K4AttestedSealBodyV1` child before that completion, even though unrelated global receipts may interleave. Thus the public checker proves per-run completeness/signatures/root formulas, while authorized full verification additionally proves every envelope's global-journal inclusion. Specialized receipt schemas below are payloads under the universal child/effect wrappers; they do not invent a second unauthenticated wrapper. Caller-writable mode-`0600` copies are caches only. Forgery, deletion, scope/global sequence gap, cross-run/policy replay, stale epoch/key, bad signature, journal-fork/root mismatch, or a valid signature absent from the required accumulator/journal fails.

K4 has one verb-derived, non-drifting reservation rule. On first `prepare-run` entry **and every retry**, its authority preimage uses `run_state:notCreatedYet`; the daemon derives and queries `{producer_authorization_sha256,verb:"prepare-run",notCreatedYet}` before opening or consulting any run-state/cache leaf. If the row exists, the recorded Accepted and its child prefix determine recovery even when `run-state.json` is now present; a retry never upgrades the key to `present(...)`. Every later run-bound verb uses `present(run_state_sha256)`, but obtains that digest only by reopening the unique indexed successful prepare-run terminal-result pair and its run-state child under the same producer authorization/scope—not from caller input, a current cache leaf, filename discovery, or a second state table. A missing/failed/ambiguous prepare completion blocks the later verb. Tests crash after run-state journal publication, after cache publication, and after cache loss; every prepare retry reopens one Accepted and one child schedule, every later verb recovers the same digest from the completed journal chain, and a second key/nonce/Accepted, post-publication prepare `present` substitution, or caller/cache-derived later digest fails before append.

`GatewayToolExecutionBodyV1` is specifically the K4 post-admission/live-lease body, and `K4AttestationToolExecutionBodyV1` is specifically post-lease attestation. Bootstrap, candidate materialization/review, checker, Git, worktree, publication, and certification subcommands use their own closed body variants rather than fake K4 fields or defaults. Every subcommand receipt binds the complete invocation receipt SHA, exact argv/intent, credential commitments, status/effects, trusted effect time, and pre/post repository/filesystem identity. Inline `-c`, stdin code, shell/eval, unpinned modules, optimization, assertion-dependent security predicates, alternate roots, generic mutating porcelain, and candidate-side authority claims are rejected. Absence or mismatch of any fixed launcher/trust-anchor leaf leaves Task 0 BLOCKED.

`reviewer_trust_root` is the closed object `{schema_version:1,keys:[...]}`. `keys[]` is sorted by unsigned UTF-8 `(key_id,principal_id,role)` then numeric epoch, is unique by both `(key_id,epoch)` and `(principal_id,role,epoch)`, and each entry contains exactly `{public_key_encoding,public_key_der_spki_base64,public_key_sha256,key_id,principal_id,role,epoch,not_before_utc,not_after_utc,revocation_state}`. `public_key_encoding` is exactly `der-spki-base64`; key bytes are canonical padded DER-SPKI P-256 and raw lowercase SHA-256. `role` is one of `source-baseline-reviewer | w0-implementer | oracle-reviewer | platform-policy-reviewer | k4-capture-producer | k4-evidence-attester | k4-crypto-erasure-operator | ablation-policy-reviewer | deployment-policy-reviewer`. Revocation is exact `{"payload":[],"tag":"active"} | {"payload":[revoked_at_utc,reason_code],"tag":"revoked"}`. Root uses `QINAO-REVIEWER-TRUST-ROOT-V1\0`. Within an epoch, key/principal identities are unique; implementer, producer, attester, erasure operator, and platform reviewer are pairwise disjoint by key/principal. The source-selection signer resolves to source reviewer; later-role public keys grant no runtime authority absent their signed policy/operation. Mutations cover role, epoch, validity, revocation, collisions, wrapper/domain, and raw-vs-domain root substitution.

The source-selection signature covers exactly `"QINAO-SOURCE-BASELINE-SELECTION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(unsignedSelectionBytes)) || unsignedSelectionBytes`, where `unsignedSelectionBytes` is compact sorted-key canonical UTF-8 for that complete closed object with only `signature` removed and no trailing LF. `sourceBaselineSelectionSHA256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete signed canonical bytes. Thus the input bytes, all three source-state commitments, exact import units, Python/OpenSSL substrate, trust root, reviewer identity/role/epoch, time window, nonce, and algorithm are all signed; a partial projection or signature over a digest-only surrogate is invalid. Times and nonce use the execution-intent grammar below.

Every detached canonical-JSON signature rooted in the Task-0 allowlist—source-baseline selection, execution intent, CandidateUnit, worktree-provision spec, certification spec, Oracle selection, **W0 open-set capture authorization**, container-semantics probe authorization, **K4 platform policy authorization, K4 producer authorization, K4CryptoErasureReceiptV1 operator record**, ablation policy, and deployment policy—uses exactly `signature_algorithm = ecdsa-p256-sha256-low-s-v1`. This rule does **not** apply to Apple product/provisioning code signatures or the K4 artifact encryption/evidence-attestation CMS protocols, whose separate platform-native algorithms and verification rules remain authoritative. The detached signature field is standard padded Base64 of one minimal ASN.1 DER `SEQUENCE(INTEGER r, INTEGER s)`; `1 <= r,s < n`, both INTEGERs use minimal positive encoding, and `s <= n/2` for the fixed NIST P-256 order. Public-key DER must decode to exactly namedCurve P-256 and an uncompressed valid curve point; alternate PEM text, compressed/raw points, another curve/hash, BER/non-minimal DER, high-S malleability, trailing bytes, or Base64 aliases fail. Source-baseline selection is verified first by the trust-anchor-pinned native verifier; only after that success may the gateway reconstruct the pinned differential OpenSSL identity and require the same result. Every later detached record is likewise accepted only when native and differential verification agree. OpenSSL uses the fixed argv `dgst -sha256 -verify <reconstructed-pinned-SPKI-PEM> -signature <canonical-DER-signature> <exact-domain-preimage>` inside a private directory; no shell, config/provider override, caller path, algorithm negotiation, or network trust is permitted. The verifier strips OpenSSL/config injection environment variables, requires exit zero plus exact success grammar, and deletes temporary public-only/preimage files. A native/OpenSSL disagreement is always BLOCKED. Task-2 goldens include at least one valid vector for every listed schema and mutations for algorithm type/key type/curve/hash/encoding, r/s range, high-S, non-minimal DER, trailing data, executable drift, environment injection, verifier disagreement, and signature copied across domains.

The human review admits those exact public trust and verification-primitive bytes out of band; a key or root first declared by an unreviewed later proposal is never trusted. Task 2 embeds and test-pins the exact complete signed source-selection bytes/raw SHA-256, exact public trust bytes/digest, and exact verification executable identity directly from this already admitted selection, so it cannot invent or silently refresh either record. The review must explicitly resolve every index/worktree mismatch. Missing, ambiguous, wildcard, directory-recursive, unsigned, self-rooted, role/principal-colliding, algorithm-confused, or trust-root-drifting selection leaves Task 0 **BLOCKED**. The plan does not guess that the newest-looking bytes are approved.

During execution do not use `git commit --only` in the dirty source worktree. The preselection snapshot publishes three external mode-`0600` canonical records with common `{schema_version:1,source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,kind,entries,stream_sha256}`. Cached-index and tracked-worktree entries use only the tagged `{path,status,old,new}` schema declared above; each present side is `{mode,git_blob,content_length,raw_sha256}` computed from the stable-open bytes, never the raw diff's worktree zero OID. Fixed-config raw evidence uses `diff --no-renames --raw -z --full-index` plus binary patch streams for cached and tracked views and hashes the two byte strings under their distinct length-framed domains; these streams corroborate status/path/old identity but do not supply a worktree postimage OID. Unreviewed typechange/conflict is rejected. Untracked/ignored entries are `{path,classification,mode,content_length,content_sha256}`; raw content is transient, and their sorted array hashes under `QINAO-SOURCE-STATE-UNTRACKED-IGNORED-STREAM-V1\0`. Each `record_sha256` is raw SHA of the complete canonical record, not its stream root. The three no-replace output leaves and raw record SHAs are named in the snapshot result/output identities. All records reject defaults, private bytes/patch persistence, symlink/hardlink/path collision, or mutation during double hashing.

The importer's sole payload command is `plan-and-validate`; only the fixed gateway may launch it, and it receives read-only source/selection FDs. It is a powerless planner/validator: it snapshots the three records twice, reconstructs the signed unit requests, and returns canonical `SourceImportPlanV1 = {schema_version:1,source_baseline_selection_sha256,source_record_sha256s,ordered_commit_units,ordered_commit_unit_root}`. It cannot create a worktree, write a candidate/index/object/ref, or commit. `ordered_commit_unit_root = SHA256("QINAO-SOURCE-IMPORT-ORDERED-UNITS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalUnitArrayBytes)) || canonicalUnitArrayBytes)`. The native gateway first validates itself/trust anchor, selection/root/signature, importer, isolated Python/stdlib, and native/OpenSSL verifier identities, then requires exact fieldwise equality between the plan and the selection projection `{raw SHA-256s of cached_index/tracked_worktree/untracked_ignored,ordered_commit_units}` plus equality of the independently recomputed unit root. It never compares the unlike whole `SourceImportPlanV1` and source-selection objects.

Before any Git/ref/worktree/index/candidate write, first execution observes UTC once, enforces selection/key interval, then journals `GatewaySourceAdmissionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,status:"pass",admitted_at_utc,source_baseline_selection_sha256,source_state_snapshot:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,plan_publication_receipt_sha256,source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,cached_index_record_sha256,tracked_worktree_record_sha256,untracked_ignored_record_sha256,trusted_execution_gateway_sha256,trust_anchor_manifest_sha256,source_import_tool_sha256,admission_python_sha256,admission_python_stdlib_closure_root,signature_verifier_sha256,baseline_ref,baseline_worktree_slot,repository_worktree_root_identity,common_git_dir_identity_sha256}`. It then journals one immutable genesis `GatewaySourceImportRunStateBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_admission_receipt_sha256,phase:"admittedGenesis",admitted_at_utc,source_baseline_selection_sha256,plan_publication_receipt_sha256,source_head,source_head_tree,source_index_tree,source_record_sha256s,baseline_ref,baseline_worktree_slot,repository_identity,repository_worktree_root_identity,expected_initial_ref_state,ordered_commit_unit_root,commit_unit_count,git_service_identity_commitment,bootstrap_git_time_seconds,bootstrap_git_timezone:"+0000",state_root,recorded_at_utc}`; `bootstrap_git_time_seconds` is the run's already observed Accepted UTC second and is reused as both author/committer time for every unit. `state_root` hashes the object without itself under `QINAO-SOURCE-IMPORT-RUN-STATE-V1\0`. Progress exists only in later ordered child/effect receipts, never a rewritten run-state or newly sampled commit time. Both cache leaves copy complete envelopes; downstream uses `run_state_receipt_sha256` only.

Recovery is journal-first: neither cache leaf present reopens journal and copies admission then run-state; admission cache only revalidates its journal envelope and produces/copies the missing run-state child; run-state cache without matching journaled admission is BLOCKED; both caches are rehashed against journal. Cache loss is harmless; expiry resume proves original admission time. Tests cut before/after each journal append/cache copy and prove zero Git effect before the complete pair.

Only the native gateway invokes provision/materialize/validate/commit primitives. The immutable genesis run-state names the schedule; its append-only child/effect receipts are the sole progress log, and `run-state.json` remains a byte-identical cache of genesis. Any external cache/directory/ref/worktree/index mutation uses universal Prepared→Adoption.

Before baseline-ref CAS or any bootstrap filesystem/Git effect, gateway journals `GatewayBootstrapProvisionPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context:GatewayMutationContextV1,run_state_receipt_sha256,source_admission_receipt_sha256,source_selection_sha256,baseline_ref,baseline_worktree_slot,source_commit,source_tree,source_index_tree,repository_worktree_root_identity,common_git_directory_identity,provision_mode_rows,provision_mode_root,provision_directory_rows,provision_directory_root,provision_path_rows,provision_path_root,mutation_rows,mutation_plan_root}`. Mode rows use the exact ref/registration/HEAD/index/checkout/genesis tags above; directory rows are `{slot_ordinal,depth,relative_slot,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}` parent-before-child and path rows `{slot_ordinal,relative_path,source_image,pre_projection,expected_post_projection}`. Complete arrays length-frame under `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-PROVISION-MODE-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-PROVISION-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, and `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-PROVISION-PATH-PLAN-V1\0`; their disjoint indexed projections cover mutation rows. Mutation rows order baseline ref, registration, directories, HEAD, leaves, index, checkout seal, genesis, and `mutation_plan_root = SHA256("QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-PROVISION-MUTATION-PLAN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPlanWithoutRoot)) || canonicalPlanWithoutRoot)`.

`GatewayBootstrapEffectRowV1 = {effect_plan_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root,effect_ordinal,effect_plan_row_digest,effect_id,effect_kind,prepared_effect_receipt_sha256,effect_adoption_receipt_sha256,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity}`; kind is `baselineRefCAS | worktreeRegistration | perWorktreeGitDirectoryInstall | headInstall | worktreeDirectoryAdoption | worktreePathAdoption | indexInstall | checkoutInstall | gitObjectFanoutDirectoryAdoption | gitObjectAdoption | trustedTreeAdvance | unitRefCAS`. The four universal plan locators byte-equal the governing `effectPlan` child and tagged row. Effect identity is the matching closed union `ref | registration | filesystemObject | index | checkout | gitObjectDirectory | gitObject | trustedTree`; mismatch or variant leakage fails. Chain root is the canonical contiguous array under `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-EFFECT-CHAIN-V1\0`, ordered by frozen effect ordinal. `GatewayBootstrapTrustedTreeGenesisV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_receipt_sha256,source_admission_receipt_sha256,source_selection_sha256,baseline_ref,commit,tree,index_tree,worktree_identity,genesis_scope_sequence}` contains no bootstrap-final/result/Completed reference. `GatewayBootstrapProvisionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_receipt_sha256,bootstrap_provision_plan_receipt_sha256,baseline_ref,baseline_worktree_slot,ref_state_before,ref_state_after,repository_worktree_root_identity,common_git_directory_identity,per_worktree_git_directory_identity,registered_slot_digest,head_binding,index_tree,worktree_tree,mutation_outcome_rows,mutation_outcome_root,effect_rows,effect_chain_root,bootstrap_trusted_tree_genesis:GatewayBootstrapTrustedTreeGenesisV1,provisioned_at_utc}` embeds the exact plan/outcome join. Every plan row has exactly one outcome; existingVerified has an authenticated stable observation and no fabricated Prepared/Adoption, while each mutation has exactly one support chain. Recovery accepts only its frozen prefix `P(k)`.

`GatewayBootstrapChangeRowV1 = {status,path,old,new}` where image is `absent({reason:"add"|"delete"}) | present({git_mode,git_blob,content_length,raw_sha256,source})`; source is `parentTree({parent_commit,parent_tree,path}) | selectedInput({source_record_sha256,source_entry_key,source_path})`. A/M/D shapes are exact; new present always resolves one selected input and old present one parent-tree entry. Rows sort/unique by path and `bootstrap_change_root` uses `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-CHANGE-ROWS-V1\0`, never Candidate root.

Before any isolated-unit write, gateway journals `GatewayBootstrapIsolatedMaterializationPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_receipt_sha256,unit_ordinal,parent_tree,result_tree,change_rows,change_root,directory_create_rows,directory_create_root,path_rows,path_root,index_row,directory_seal_rows,directory_seal_root,mutation_rows,mutation_plan_root}` under `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-ISOLATED-MATERIALIZATION-PLAN-V1\0`; it uses fixed ordinals and the same closed state vocabulary. Directory-create rows run parent-before-child at `0700`, leaves install at final `0400`, index follows, and explicit directory-seal rows run child-before-parent `0700→0500`; each array root uses its complete named length-framed domain and joins the mutation rows bidirectionally. Its terminal `GatewayBootstrapIsolatedMaterializationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,plan_receipt_sha256,run_state_receipt_sha256,unit_ordinal,result_tree,result_index_tree,mutation_outcome_rows,mutation_outcome_root,review_directory_identity,read_only_tree_seal_observation_receipt_sha256,materialized_at_utc}` plus successful Completed forms `isolated_materialization_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1`. The final seal is two stable-FD no-effect walks, never recursive chmod.

Before **any canonical unit effect**, gateway then journals `GatewayBootstrapUnitPlanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,context:GatewayMutationContextV1,run_state_receipt_sha256,ordinal,parent_commit,parent_tree,result_tree,bootstrap_change_rows,bootstrap_change_root,result_commit_object,isolated_materialization_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,required_object_rows,required_object_root,object_fanout_directory_rows,object_fanout_directory_root,worktree_directory_rows,worktree_directory_root,worktree_path_rows,result_index_row,unit_ref_cas_row,trusted_tree_advance_row,mutation_rows,mutation_plan_root}`. Required-object row is `{slot_ordinal,object_type,git_object_id,raw_object_length,raw_object_sha256,source_image}` and covers every blob, child-before-parent tree, and commit independent of current ODB presence. Directory/path rows use explicit slot/depth/path/pre/post projections. Distinct roots use `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-REQUIRED-OBJECT-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-OBJECT-FANOUT-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-WORKTREE-DIRECTORY-PLAN-V1\0`, and `QINAO-BOOTSTRAP-UNIT-MUTATION-PLAN-V1\0`; the last hashes the complete plan without itself. The mutation profile restores each of the five production Swift old blobs, duplicate async helper, absent approved-proof attribute, JournalCLI locator, either seeded checker transport, Rust `18.0` target/comment, each floor-override hole, invalid backward-fixture entry, direct public test-executor call, and freeze assertion independently; every mutation hits its registry-named failure while a control passes. Lane mutations add `--disable-sandbox`, all-tests, forbidden process crossover, product/path/runpath/container/load/dispatch drift, omitted CoreML output, discovery/receipt-root mismatch, hidden skip/error, wrong RED outcome, or duplicate package execution and must fail while the Xcode-27 two-package golden passes. The two freeze suites prove synthetic sovereign receipts are `.skipped` with `actuator-evidence-unavailable`, public Qinao effect execution fails closed with `effectBrokerUnavailable`, and only the internal test seam exercises legacy gate behavior. Exact inventories/surfaces, terminal outcome sets, root/envelope/product identities, skip set, and every old/new blob are frozen in the Task-0 registry. All 47 safety postimages plus the final seeded checker pair enter `bootstrap_frozen_rows` and may not change in Tasks 1–6; a missing suite, comment-only “fix,” source-tree leak, or unlisted dependency blocks bootstrap.

- [ ] **Step 0E: Migrate only the reviewed privacy-clean fixture and summary tuple**

Then an audited one-shot K4 migration reads only the exact source-index blobs and explicitly admitted source-worktree fixture inputs, writes the sanitized tuple into that same clean worktree, and emits `source-manifest-v1.json` as an immutable import-provenance map from original source blob to initial sanitized output blob. It may not copy a directory recursively. That manifest is not a claim that later reviewed hardening bytes equal their initial import: the checker reconstructs its exact first-seal commit when validating the map, while separately privacy/inventory-checking the current stage-0 tuple. Task 3 may modify only its three explicitly listed fixture Swift paths; the producer binds the final live input tree independently through `inputProbeDigest`, so neither Task 3 nor the manifest rewrites history.

- [ ] **Step 0F: Validate, commit, and prove source-state preservation**

The next signed bootstrap unit contains exactly 25 additions: `scripts/check_qinao_preliminary_k4_source.py`, `scripts/test_check_qinao_preliminary_k4_source.py`, the exact 21 migrated fixture paths in the File Responsibility Map, and the exact two retained summary paths. Every expected status is `A`; there is no deletion because the old preliminary subtree is absent from the committed base and is never imported. The native bootstrap engine compares that exact sorted tuple, proves the old `spike/` prefix absent from the parent tree, runs the registry-pinned checker against its isolated result index with exact non-zero test IDs, and adopts the unit with message `evidence: seal privacy-clean K4 probe baseline`. Its receipt proves result-tree/commit equality and the unchanged source-state record digests. The summary contains only source-to-sanitized provenance/digests; it never embeds its own blob/commit identity. The checker source pins the reviewed summary blob unidirectionally, while the first-seal commit/tree is reconstructed from the authenticated Git edge and archived externally. Only the fixture/tooling subtree remains mutable through Task 3.

The bootstrap Completed envelope—not a branch-name lookup—binds the ordered import units and final commit/tree. Verify it with one closed native reader. That operation checks the approved tuple `f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b / e544727c57ffd340fde274b8caeded6bd39bde09 / 15e82ec63b9e9bf1ebf17575799b9474061a292e / a5599c402b110951aee108d18625153d3d90ddef9c93933c580970c0be0b71dd`, exactly one plan-publication edge whose only parent is the approved commit, zero later touches to plan/spec, the full required-path/read/import/suite closure, and exact four-unit order: 32-path W0 closure seed, three Artifact Mesh receipts, 47-path fail-closed safety patch, then 25-path sanitized K4 baseline. It reopens the authenticated `GatewayBootstrapFinalStateBodyV1`, all 24 modify-base rows and their exact 9/3/12 provenance partition, all 95 frozen rows and their exact 23/3/47/22 partition, absence of every final-`A` and future sequence-1 evidence path plus the preliminary `spike/` prefix, every baseline/safety checker PASS, source-state equality, and complete global/scope journal inclusion through the bootstrap successful Completed. Git traversal errors, empty ancestry output, missing paths, a status/path mismatch, a result without matching successful Completed, or a dirty bootstrap worktree are typed failures; no process substitution or command-substitution emptiness test participates.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"

"$GATE" git-read \

  --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/bootstrap-verify" \

  --bootstrap-completion-receipt "$QINAO_BOOTSTRAP_COMPLETION_RECEIPT" \

```

Existing foreign, attached, dirty, moved, advanced, duplicate-registered, or unjournaled partial state is BLOCKED; the gateway never deletes, switches, resets, or reuses it implicitly.

The native `w0.capture-open-set-successors.v1` tool-broker contract reopens the execution-provision terminal pair and bootstrap final state, requires worktree/index/ref clean at that commit/tree, proves each sequence-0 predecessor uniquely latest and stale for its recorded anchor, and runs registry-fixed read-only commands once in the powerless sandbox. Admission enforces `key.not_before_utc <= authorization.issued_at_utc <= Accepted.accepted_at_utc <= min(authorization.expires_at_utc,key.not_after_utc)`. Before sandbox launch, the daemon consumes the private-output env, stable-opens its approved parent, derives the domain-separated final path commitment, and matches authorization. It journals `GatewayPrivateBundlePublicationIntentV1 = {schema_version:1,scope_id,invocation_nonce,accepted_receipt_sha256,capture_authorization_sha256,parent_directory_device_inode_digest,volume_identity_sha256,final_path_commitment,staging_leaf_name_commitment,expected_staging_pre_state:"absent",expected_final_pre_state:"absent",expected_logical_destination_count:13,prepared_at_utc}` before creating staging or writing payload. Universal Prepared creates mode-`0700` staging no-replace, fsyncs parent, and adopts `GatewayPrivateBundleStagingAdoptedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,publication_intent_sha256,staging_leaf_name_commitment,parent_directory_device_inode_digest,staging_directory_device_inode_digest,volume_identity_sha256,mode:"0700",initial_directory_link_count_observation,directory_alias_observation_sha256,creation_observation_sha256,parent_fsync_observation_sha256,adopted_at_utc}`. Directory link count is stable-open observed and platform-policy-checked `>=2`, never hard-coded like a regular file; alias/hardlink absence is separately proved. Only after Adoption does retained staging dirfd enter the sandbox; final path never does.

Before each command launch, a universal Prepared binds command/argv, an absent private raw-capture leaf, and the exact broker custody identity. The capture leaf bytes are exactly `"QINAO-W0-RAW-CAPTURE-V1\0" || uint64be(stdout_length) || stdout || uint64be(stderr_length) || stderr || int32be(exit_status)`; the body never relies on two ambient files or newline parsing. The broker streams framed output, appends exit status only after durable wait, fsyncs/reopens it, and the daemon adopts `GatewayOpenSetRawCommandCaptureBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,series_id,command_ordinal,command_id,logical_argv,raw_capture_container_length,raw_capture_container_sha256,stdout_length,stdout_sha256,stdout_base64,stderr_length,stderr_sha256,stderr_base64,exit_status,capture_leaf_identity,broker_process_observation_sha256,captured_at_utc}`. Base64 is canonical padded encoding of exact bytes; decoded lengths/hashes and the independently replayed container must match. The broker does not acknowledge before Adoption; complete exact capture reconciles after crash, while partial/no-exit blocks rather than rerunning. Every downstream result/install references this Adoption.

`private_bundle_directory_identity` is exactly `{parent_directory_device_inode_digest,directory_device_inode_digest,path_commitment,volume_identity_sha256,mode:"0700",final_directory_link_count_observation,directory_alias_observation_sha256,initial_pre_state:"absent",publication_intent_sha256,publication_adoption_sha256,post_tree_root}`; final count is stable-open observed, `>=2`, and byte-equals seal/reopen for the same inode. `predecessor_rows[]` is authorization's exact pair extended only with `{admitted_receipt_body_digest,stale_anchor_verification_digest}` and roots under `QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-PREDECESSOR-RESULTS-V1\0`. `OpenSetCommandResultV1 = {ordinal,command_id,logical_argv,tool_execution_receipt_sha256,raw_capture_adoption_receipt_sha256,exit_status,stdout_length,stdout_sha256,stderr_length,stderr_sha256,transcript_role,destination_path,destination_content_length,destination_raw_sha256}` byte-equals contract/capture. Transcript is exact stdout, stderr, or `"QINAO-W0-PROCESS-TRANSCRIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(stdout_length) || stdout || uint64be(stderr_length) || stderr || int32be(exit_status)`; series receipts are compact canonical JSON without LF. `series_result_root` uses `QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-SERIES-RESULTS-V1\0`. `exact_destination_rows[]` is the path-sorted exact 13 `{path,source,target_git_mode,content_length,raw_sha256}` and `exact_destination_root = SHA256("QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-EXACT-DESTINATIONS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalDestinationRows)) || canonicalDestinationRows)`. `physical_blob_rows[]` is sorted/unique `{bundle_leaf:"blobs/<raw_sha256>",bundle_filesystem_mode:"0400",content_length,raw_sha256}` and `physical_blob_root = SHA256("QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-PHYSICAL-BLOBS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPhysicalRows)) || canonicalPhysicalRows)`. Many-to-one is legal only for identical bytes/length; manifest entries byte-equal physical rows and every logical row joins one. `evidence_root` intentionally rehashes the same destination rows under its distinct evidence domain, preventing domain substitution. `post_tree_root` uses `QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-PRIVATE-BUNDLE-TREE-V1\0` over manifest, explicit `blobs/` directory, and unique leaves.

Before any blob leaf, universal Prepared/dispatch/Adoption creates the `blobs/` subdirectory and emits `GatewayPrivateBundleSubdirectoryAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,publication_intent_sha256,staging_directory_adoption_receipt_sha256,relative_path:"blobs",parent_identity_before,directory_identity_after,parent_identity_after,mode:"0700",creation_observation_sha256,parent_fsync_observation_sha256,adopted_at_utc}`. Every blob-leaf Prepared names this receipt/dirfd; manifest remains at staging root. No sandbox or implicit mkdir can create it.

Every staging leaf uses a nested atomic publication; Prepared never authorizes writing its final name in place. `GatewayPrivateBundleLeafAdoptionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,publication_intent_sha256,staging_directory_adoption_receipt_sha256,leaf_ordinal,attempt_ordinal,temp_leaf_name_commitment,final_relative_leaf,expected_mode,expected_content_length,expected_raw_sha256,final_leaf_identity,rename_no_replace_observation_sha256,staging_fsync_observation_sha256,adopted_at_utc}`. For each manifest/blob leaf in final path order, the daemon journals Prepared with absent unpredictable sibling temp and absent final identities, creates the temp `O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW`, writes/fsyncs/reopens/verifies it, atomically `RENAME_EXCL`s temp to final, fsyncs the staging directory, then journals Adoption. If recovery finds a complete exact temp it performs only rename/adoption. A short or digest-different temp is never truncated, deleted, or accepted: after proving no live writer, a separate universal Prepared→Adoption quarantines it by no-replace rename into the invocation's immutable private quarantine and permits exactly attempt 1 with a fresh temp; a second partial blocks. An adopted final leaf is immutable and never regenerated.

After all leaf adoptions, `GatewayOpenSetPrivacyScanBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,privacy_contract_id,exact_destination_root,scan_rows,scan_root,scanned_at_utc,disposition:"privacyClean"}` stable-opens the 13 exact destination bytes and applies the Task-0-pinned forbidden grammar for user-home/workspace/account/device/profile/certificate/raw selector/secret material. Rows `{path,content_sha256,scanner_contract_id,match_count:0}` sort by path and root under `QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-PRIVACY-SCAN-V1\0`; any hit blocks without rewriting raw transcripts. Commands run against committed virtual/relative paths so correct bytes are naturally privacy-clean.

The daemon then reconstructs the tree twice. `PrivateBundleTreeWalkObservationV1 = {schema_version:1,walk_ordinal:1|2,leaf_rows,tree_projection_root,observed_at_utc}` uses `tree_projection_root = SHA256("QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-PRIVATE-BUNDLE-TREE-WALK-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalLeafRows)) || canonicalLeafRows)` over the projection excluding ordinal/time; observations are distinct envelopes while roots/rows byte-equal. It publishes no-effect `GatewayPrivateBundleTreeSealedBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,publication_intent_sha256,staging_directory_adoption_receipt_sha256,blobs_subdirectory_adoption_receipt_sha256,leaf_adoption_rows,leaf_adoption_root,exact_destination_root,physical_blob_root,privacy_scan_receipt_sha256,bundle_manifest_sha256,bundle_root,first_walk_observation_sha256,first_walk_post_tree_root,second_walk_observation_sha256,second_walk_post_tree_root,post_tree_root,staging_directory_device_inode_digest,final_directory_link_count_observation,directory_alias_observation_sha256,sealed_at_utc,disposition:"completeTreeSealed"}`. Walk rows equal final-path adoption rows; both roots equal post-tree root under their declared projection. Publication then uses Prepared/dispatch, same-parent atomic `RENAME_EXCL`, parent fsync, final stable reopen, and Adoption `GatewayPrivateBundlePublicationAdoptionV1 = {schema_version:1,publication_intent_sha256,staging_directory_adoption_receipt_sha256,tree_seal_receipt_sha256,publication_prepared_receipt_sha256,leaf_adoption_root,staging_tree_root,final_tree_root,final_directory_device_inode_digest,final_directory_link_count_observation,directory_alias_observation_sha256,final_path_commitment,rename_no_replace_observation_sha256,parent_fsync_observation_sha256,adopted_at_utc}`. Recovery admits only the exact intent/staging/subdirectory/leaf-prefix/privacy/sealed/renamed/adopted/result/Completed prefix; foreign/partial/different state blocks.

The daemon publishes `GatewayW0OpenSetSuccessorBundleBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,capture_authorization_sha256,execution_provision:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,bootstrap_final_commit,bootstrap_final_tree,publication_intent_sha256,staging_directory_adoption_receipt_sha256,tree_seal_receipt_sha256,publication_prepared_receipt_sha256,publication_adoption:GatewayPrivateBundlePublicationAdoptionV1,private_bundle_destination_commitment,private_bundle_directory_identity,predecessor_rows,predecessor_result_root,series_result_rows,series_result_root,exact_destination_rows,physical_blob_rows,evidence_root,bundle_manifest_sha256,bundle_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,captured_at_utc,status:"successorsCaptured"}` as the result child, then a successful Completed. `private_bundle_destination_commitment` equals both the authorization's committed digest and `private_bundle_directory_identity.path_commitment`—never the whole identity object—and `publication_adoption.final_tree_root == private_bundle_directory_identity.post_tree_root`. The result reopens every staging/leaf/seal/publication receipt and carries no body-only substitute. `evidence_root = SHA256("QINAO-W0-OPEN-SET-SUCCESSORS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalDestinationRowsBytes)) || canonicalDestinationRowsBytes)` over the exact 13 logical rows; `bundle_root` is independently recomputed from the unique manifest entries under `QINAO-CANDIDATE-BUNDLE-V1\0`. Repository identities byte-equal; the generator has no repository/index/ref write capability. Accepted time is no later than intent, staging adoption, every leaf adoption, tree seal, publication adoption, and `captured_at_utc`, in that order; capture is no later than `Completed.completed_at_utc`. The successor CandidateUnit satisfies `issued_at_utc >= Completed.completed_at_utc`, and candidate-materialize Accepted lies within both implementer-key and unit intervals. Backdated/pre-capture unit, expired/revoked authorization, result-before-Accepted, Completed-before-result, mismatched pair, unjournaled recovery, and re-signed time drift all fail.

Do **not** execute any Python successor emitter against the clean worktree; the final seeded checker rejects `--emit-successor-from` with `nativeGeneratorRequired` and remains verification-only. Native generation and Python validation are intentionally independent implementations. This step has an enforced two-phase review stop: phase A reopens the already successful execution-provision terminal pair **inside its bound execution worktree**, commitment-binds the authorized final private destination and its crash-safe publication intent, and performs capture only. No successor CandidateUnit, bundle contents/manifest assertion, materialization, review, or Git transaction is required or admitted before capture reaches successful Completed. A distinct `w0-implementer` then inspects the authenticated capture terminal pair, exact 13 logical destination rows, unique physical blob rows, manifest, publication adoption, bundle locator identity, and bundle root and signs one no-RED `adoptableCandidate` whose prerequisites are exactly `executionProvision` and `w0OpenSetCapture` terminal-result references, whose `bundle_provenance` is the matching `gatewayGenerated` pair/root, whose changes are exactly the 13 concrete `A` rows using `gatewayGeneratedBundle` materialization sources, and whose message is `evidence: refresh W0 open-set anchors`. Phase B reopens both terminal pairs and the same private directory identity, requires the unit/provenance/bundle to byte-equal the capture result, and only then materializes/reviews/adopts. The registry-marked `qinao-w0-open-set-successor-adoption-v1` profile independently runs the pinned Python checker against the isolated candidate result tree through its authenticated transports; requires both series to be contiguous `0,1`, predecessor-linked, current-anchor-valid, non-self-referential, and exact-open-set-equal; requires exact non-zero discovery/execution and log hashes; and mutation-tests every receipt/logical-row/physical-blob/publication-intent/adoption/path/root/predecessor/anchor/open-set/locator field plus every recovery cut point. Then `git-transaction` adopts that unit as the first row of `GatewayGitTransactionChainV1` and must reach successful Completed before Task 1.

Phase A—reopen the provisioned execution worktree, then capture and stop:

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"
: "${QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR:?stable approved encrypted external run directory}"
: "${QINAO_W0_OPEN_SET_CAPTURE_AUTHORIZATION:?signed capture authorization}"
: "${QINAO_W0_SUCCESSOR_BUNDLE:?authorized final private bundle destination}"
: "${QINAO_W0_EXECUTION_WORKTREE:?absolute provisioned execution worktree path}"
: "${QINAO_EXECUTION_PROVISION_REFERENCE:?external canonical execution-provision terminal-result pair record}"
GATE=/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate
CAPTURE_RESULT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/w0-open-set-successor-capture.json"
cd "$QINAO_W0_EXECUTION_WORKTREE"
"$GATE" git-read \
  --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION \
  --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/w0-open-set-provision-reopen-before-capture" \
  --operation verify-terminal-result-reference-v1 -- \
  --terminal-result-reference "$QINAO_EXECUTION_PROVISION_REFERENCE" \
  --expected-result-kind executionSymbolicWorktreeProvision \
  --require-clean-symbolic
```

**Files:**
