No lifecycle-tag/transaction-tag hash equality is asserted. The preimages are universal tagged-union values whose tag equals `boundary_kind` and whose sole payload is the following exact record:

- `query`: input `{mapping_digest,physical_source_id,schema_digest,record_path,query_key_base64,query_key_sha256}`; output `{observation,observed_fields}`. Both output fields are byte-identical to the enclosing snapshot record, including the complete sorted mapped-field projection.
- `denial`: input `{case_id,case_binding_digest,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,before_snapshot_state_root,decision_inputs_base64,decision_inputs_sha256}`; output `{denial_receipt_base64,denial_receipt_sha256,after_snapshot_state_root}`. The decoded decision inputs contain the complete case-fact schedule projection and must independently recompute the enclosing signed `case_binding_digest`.
- `transactionAttempt`: input `{case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,transaction_request_base64,transaction_request_sha256,attempted_physical_keys,before_snapshot_state_root}`; output `{attempt_receipt_base64,attempt_receipt_sha256,terminal_status,after_snapshot_state_root}`. `attempted_physical_keys[]` uses the same sorted/unique closed key record as committed touched keys and is independently derived from the decoded transaction request; it is participation evidence, not a claim of mutation. `terminal_status` is byte-identical to the enclosing invocation `status`.
- `committedChange`: input `{case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,transaction_request_base64,transaction_request_sha256,touched_physical_keys,before_snapshot_state_root}`; output `{change_receipt_base64,change_receipt_sha256,touched_physical_keys,after_snapshot_state_root}`.
- `serializer`: input `{case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,before_snapshot_root,after_snapshot_root,before_snapshot_state_root,after_snapshot_state_root,source_state_bindings,logical_row_preimage}`; output `{logical_row_preimage,logical_row_root}`. `source_state_bindings[]` is sorted/unique by `physical_source_id` and contains exactly `{physical_source_id,before_source_state_root,after_source_state_root}` for the snapshots' exact source set.
- `teardown`: input `{case_id,process_identity_digest,cache_identity_digest}`; output `{process_absence_receipt_base64,process_absence_receipt_sha256,cache_empty_receipt_base64,cache_empty_receipt_sha256}`.
- `coldOpen`: input `{case_id,prior_mapping_digest,legacy_fixture_bytes_root,legacy_source_state_bindings}`; output `{cold_open_receipt_base64,cold_open_receipt_sha256,prior_snapshots}`. `prior_snapshots[]` is sorted/unique by `(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)` and each member is exactly `{row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,snapshot}` with a complete `PhysicalSnapshotV1`.
- `migration`: input `{case_id,migration_contract_digest,prior_mapping_digest,current_mapping_digest,before_source_state_bindings}`; output `{migration_receipt_base64,migration_receipt_sha256,after_source_state_bindings}`.
- `migrationTransaction`: input `{case_id,migration_contract_digest,transaction_request_base64,transaction_request_sha256,before_source_state_bindings}`; output `{transaction_receipt_base64,transaction_receipt_sha256,after_source_state_bindings}`.

Every production `*_receipt_base64` field decodes under one closed `ProductionBoundaryReceiptV1` tagged union; it is never arbitrary “production-emitted bytes.” Its variants are:

- `denial(schema_version,authority_owner_id,storage_owner_id,case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,decision_inputs_sha256,before_snapshot_state_root,after_snapshot_state_root,transaction_boundary:notApplicable(decisionDeniedNoTransaction),status:denied,reason_code,receipt_nonce)`;
- `transactionAttempt(schema_version,storage_owner_id,case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,transaction_request_sha256,attempted_physical_keys,before_snapshot_state_root,after_snapshot_state_root,production_boundary_identity,native_transaction_receipt_base64,native_transaction_receipt_sha256,status:rolledBack|casLost,reason_code,receipt_nonce)`;
- `committedChange(schema_version,storage_owner_id,case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_digest,transaction_request_sha256,touched_physical_keys,before_snapshot_state_root,after_snapshot_state_root,production_boundary_identity,native_transaction_receipt_base64,native_transaction_receipt_sha256,status:succeeded,reason_code:absent,receipt_nonce)`;
- `processAbsence(schema_version,lifecycle_owner_id,case_id,process_identity_digest,absence_observation_base64,absence_observation_sha256,transaction_boundary:notApplicable(lifecycleTeardownNoTransaction),status:succeeded,receipt_nonce)` and `cacheEmpty` with the analogous cache identity/observation fields;
- `coldOpen(schema_version,lifecycle_owner_id,case_id,prior_mapping_digest,legacy_fixture_bytes_root,prior_snapshot_set_root,transaction_boundary:notApplicable(lifecycleColdOpenNoTransaction),status:succeeded,receipt_nonce)`;
- `migration(schema_version,lifecycle_owner_id,case_id,migration_contract_digest,prior_mapping_digest,current_mapping_digest,before_source_state_binding_root,after_source_state_binding_root,production_boundary_identity,native_lifecycle_receipt_base64,native_lifecycle_receipt_sha256,status:succeeded,receipt_nonce)`;
- `migrationTransaction(schema_version,lifecycle_owner_id,case_id,migration_contract_digest,transaction_request_sha256,before_source_state_binding_root,after_source_state_binding_root,production_boundary_identity,native_transaction_receipt_base64,native_transaction_receipt_sha256,status:succeeded,receipt_nonce)`.

The appropriate variant is fixed by the enclosing output field: denial→denial, attempt→transactionAttempt, change→committedChange, the two teardown receipts→processAbsence/cacheEmpty, cold-open→coldOpen, outer migration→migration, and migration-transaction→migrationTransaction. `prior_snapshot_set_root` and both source-binding roots use separate length-framed `QINAO-K3-PRODUCTION-BOUNDARY-*-SET-V1\0` domains over the complete arrays already carried by the invocation. `native_lifecycle_receipt_base64` decodes the exact production migration coordinator record `{schema_version,owner_id,lifecycle_nonce,migration_contract_digest,prior_mapping_digest,current_mapping_digest,before_source_state_binding_root,after_source_state_binding_root,result}` with `result=succeeded`; its raw hash cross-equals the field and the `migration` identity. Every owner/case/mapping/request/state/key/reason/status/identity/native receipt/nonce field byte-equals the invocation, selected mapping, and decoded production boundary identity. Canonical wrapper bytes have no extra/default field; their adjacent SHA-256 is the raw wrapper hash. Mutation tests replace a valid wrapper by another variant, replace only native bytes/owner/nonce, or rehash the complete envelope and still fail.

Every `*_base64` is canonical padded Base64 of its exact closed bytes and its adjacent SHA-256 must match. Every source-state-binding array uses the row-scoped type/order above and cannot collapse two rows or two equal-content sources; touched-key arrays use the exact closed record/order below. The cold-open binding set, outer migration input, and migration-transaction **before** bindings all equal the sorted prior-row snapshot projection. The outer migration output and migration-transaction **after** bindings both equal the sorted current-row read-back projection; the outer and transactional prior→current transition is byte-identical, and a changed migration cannot be represented as a no-change transaction. `logical_row_preimage` is exactly `{row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,logical_key,logical_state}` under the active row descriptor, where `logical_state = absent(caseReasonCode) | present(completeLogicalState)`. It contains no case-level outcome, expected value, or catalog identity. `logical_row_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-LOGICAL-ROW-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalLogicalRowPreimage)) || canonicalLogicalRowPreimage)`. That exact single-NUL domain literal is golden-pinned. The fixed domain-token map is `query→QUERY`, `denial→DENIAL`, `transactionAttempt→TRANSACTION-ATTEMPT`, `committedChange→COMMITTED-CHANGE`, `serializer→SERIALIZER`, `teardown→TEARDOWN`, `coldOpen→COLD-OPEN`, `migration→MIGRATION`, and `migrationTransaction→MIGRATION-TRANSACTION`. For each kind, `input_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-BOUNDARY-<TOKEN>-INPUT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalInputPreimage)) || canonicalInputPreimage)` and the analogous literal `...-OUTPUT-V1\0` formula computes `output_root`; `<TOKEN>` is substitution notation only and the nine expanded literal domains are golden-pinned. `receipt_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-BOUNDARY-INVOCATION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(bytesWithoutReceiptDigest)) || bytesWithoutReceiptDigest)`, removing only `receipt_digest`. Status is forced by kind: query/committedChange/serializer/teardown/coldOpen/migration/migrationTransaction are `succeeded`, denial is `denied`, and transactionAttempt is `rolledBack | casLost`. Query input/output are byte-identical to their enclosing snapshot record's key/observation; serializer roots/preimages equal the enclosing snapshots and emitted row preimage; writer and backward-pair invocations likewise cross-equal their enclosing case, mapping, source, fixture, migration, and touched-key fields. No opaque root without its complete sibling preimage is accepted.

A `PhysicalSnapshotV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,observation_boundary_id,observation_boundary,snapshot_symbol,mapping_digest,sources,snapshot_state_root,snapshot_root}`. `observation_boundary` is the closed union `singleSource(storage_owner_id,physical_source_id,transaction_boundary_id,snapshot_receipt_base64,snapshot_receipt_sha256) | fencedComposite(storage_owner_id,transaction_domain_id,engine_identity_digest,consistency_rule,source_boundaries,fence_receipt_base64,fence_receipt_sha256)`. `consistency_rule` has the sole v1 value `singleEngineStableReadFence`; cross-engine and wall-clock rules are unrepresentable. Each composite source boundary is exactly `{physical_source_id,transaction_boundary_id,native_snapshot_receipt_base64,native_snapshot_receipt_sha256,read_hwm_domain_digest,read_hwm,source_nonce,snapshot_receipt_base64,snapshot_receipt_sha256}` sorted/unique by source ID. All sources must be members of one selected `TransactionDomainV1`, and outer storage/domain/engine values equal that domain.

Decoded `NativeSnapshotReceiptV1` bytes are the closed union `sqliteRead(engine_identity_digest,transaction_domain_id,physical_source_id,sqlite_snapshot_number,schema_cookie,data_version,read_hwm_domain_digest,read_hwm,source_nonce,record_set_sha256) | swiftActorRead(engine_identity_digest,transaction_domain_id,physical_source_id,actor_isolation_epoch,actor_revision,read_hwm_domain_digest,read_hwm,source_nonce,record_set_sha256)`. Numeric fields are UInt64 and the engine-kind tag must match the selected domain. `ReadHWMDomainV1` is exactly `sqlite(engine_identity_digest,transaction_domain_id,boot_session_digest,sqlite_snapshot_number,schema_cookie,counter_contract_digest) | swiftActor(engine_identity_digest,transaction_domain_id,boot_session_digest,actor_isolation_epoch,counter_contract_digest)`; its digest is `SHA256("QINAO-K3-READ-HWM-DOMAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReadHWMDomainBytes)) || canonicalReadHWMDomainBytes)`, and every component is independently derived from the selected engine/runtime receipt and stage-0 counter contract. Canonical native bytes come from `native_snapshot_receipt_base64` and hash to `native_snapshot_receipt_sha256`; every repeated identity/HWM/nonce cross-equals the outer source boundary. `record_set_sha256` is exactly the enclosing source's `source_state_root` (the query-invocation-free record/field state projection), never `source_root`, so no evidence-hash cycle exists. Decoded `ProductionSnapshotReceiptV1` bytes are exactly `{schema_version:1,storage_owner_id,transaction_domain_id,engine_identity_digest,physical_source_id,native_snapshot_receipt_base64,native_snapshot_receipt_sha256,read_hwm_domain_digest,read_hwm,source_nonce,schema_digest,record_set_sha256}` and their full raw SHA-256 equals the adjacent `snapshot_receipt_sha256`; their identity fields cross-equal the source boundary, selected domain/source, and decoded `ProductionBoundaryIdentityV1.observation`.

Decoded `ProductionReadFenceReceiptV1` bytes are exactly `{schema_version:1,storage_owner_id,transaction_domain_id,engine_identity_digest,consistency_rule,read_hwm_domain_digest,begin_read_hwm,end_read_hwm,fence_nonce,native_fence_evidence,source_observations}`. `native_fence_evidence` is exactly `sqliteStableRead(sqlite_snapshot_number,schema_cookie,begin_data_version,end_data_version,begin_commit_sequence,end_commit_sequence) | swiftActorIsolation(actor_isolation_epoch,begin_revision,end_revision)` and its tag matches the domain engine. `source_observations[]` is the sorted/unique exact projection `{physical_source_id,transaction_boundary_id,native_snapshot_receipt_sha256,read_hwm,source_nonce,snapshot_receipt_sha256}` of `source_boundaries`. Under `singleEngineStableReadFence`, every source has the same HWM domain and `begin_read_hwm = each source read_hwm = end_read_hwm`; SQLite additionally requires equal begin/end data version and commit sequence plus one snapshot number/schema cookie across sources, while Swift actor requires one isolation epoch and equal begin/end revision. Fence raw bytes hash to `fence_receipt_sha256`. A single-source boundary decodes the same native/production snapshot receipts but needs no fence. An opaque consistency label/evidence, different engine/domain, unequal HWM/version/revision, missing source, or digest-only receipt fails.

`observation_boundary_id = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-OBSERVATION-BOUNDARY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObservationBoundaryBytes)) || canonicalObservationBoundaryBytes)`. Every contained query invocation has `present(transaction_boundary_id)` equal to its selected single/composite source boundary and the decoded query receipt cross-binds that identity. Within one trace/run, one observation-boundary ID maps to exactly one complete snapshot root (byte-identical dedup only), so independent before/after reads use distinct IDs even when state is equal. The snapshot's `selected_mapping` is uniquely resolved from its `mapping_digest`: ordinary/replay endpoints use the highest active current mapping; a backward prior snapshot uses the immediately preceding admitted mapping named by that projection; a backward current snapshot uses that projection's current mapping. The digest, source set, storage/authority owners, schema descriptors, and snapshot symbol must all equal that selected mapping. No verifier substitutes the active current mapping for a backward-prior snapshot. Mutation tests alter the rule, HWM domain/value, fence/source nonce, domain/engine/owner, source membership, native receipt, or no-intervening-commit proof independently and rehash all envelopes; every mutation fails.

`sources[]` is sorted/unique by `physical_source_id`; each source is exactly `{physical_source_id,schema_digest,source_state_root,source_root,records}`. `records[]` is sorted/unique by `(record_path,query_key_sha256)` and each record is exactly `{record_path,query_key_base64,query_key_sha256,observation,observed_fields,query_invocation}`. `observed_fields[]` is sorted/unique by `field_path` and each member is exactly `{field_path,physical_type,nullability,field_observation}`, where `field_observation = absent(caseReasonCode) | present(canonical_value_base64,value_sha256)`. Base64 is standard padded canonical Base64 of the complete kind-specific canonical physical key/record/value bytes. Record `observation` is the universal tagged-union wire `absent(caseReasonCode) | present(record_bytes_base64,record_sha256)`; `query_invocation` is the complete boundary record above and must be `boundary_kind=query`.

For state equality, `sourceStateRecords[]` is the exact `{record_path,query_key_base64,query_key_sha256,observation,observed_fields}` projection with `query_invocation` removed, and `source_state_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-SOURCE-STATE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSourceStateRecords)) || canonicalSourceStateRecords)`. `snapshotStateSources[]` is sorted/unique by `physical_source_id` and contains exactly `{physical_source_id,schema_digest,source_state_root}`; `snapshot_state_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-SNAPSHOT-STATE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSnapshotStatePreimage)) || canonicalSnapshotStatePreimage)`, where the preimage is exactly `{mapping_digest,sources:snapshotStateSources}`. Separately, `source_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-SOURCE-SNAPSHOT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalFullRecords)) || canonicalFullRecords)` and `snapshot_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-SNAPSHOT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSnapshotWithoutSnapshotRoot)) || canonicalSnapshotWithoutSnapshotRoot)` bind the complete observation evidence, including unique boundaries and query receipts. No-write/change semantics compare `snapshot_state_root`; serializer/trace/evidence integrity bind full `snapshot_root` as well.

For each snapshot, derive `requiredPhysicalInputs` as the exact union of every **selected mapping** key/state field's `physicalInputs` after applying the logical key; group by `(physical_source_id,record_path,query_key)` and retain every mapped `field_path/physical_type/nullability`. Ordinary receipt endpoints therefore use the active current mapping, while the two sides of a backward projection are checked independently against its prior and current mappings. Each snapshot must contain exactly those source groups, records, and observed fields—no missing, extra, duplicate, or unrelated source/input—and every schema/type/nullability value must byte-equal the selected mapping's `physicalSources` and field mappings. The query input preimage repeats the same selected source/schema/path/key; its output observation repeats the record and field observations. Independently of the producer, the parent decodes each present `record_bytes_base64` with the selected admitted source's exact `storageKind + schemaCanonicalizationID + schemaDigest`, applies the mapped physical types and NULL rules, and requires byte equality with every `observed_fields` value; an absent record requires the exact all-absent field projection and cannot carry a present field. Decode failure, trailing/unconsumed bytes, raw-record/field disagreement, nullable-to-required synthesis, or a field projection that is valid alone but not derived from that raw record fails before serialization. This makes an absent row a positive independent query receipt without making two state-identical observations unequal merely because their evidence ordinals differ. Mutation tests remove/add a source, record, or field; duplicate an equal-content source under another ID; swap field paths/types/nullability; substitute a sibling/prior/current mapping; and independently alter raw record bytes or one decoded field while recomputing every digest.

A `PhysicalObservationReceiptV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,case_id,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,mapping_revision,mapping_digest,authority_owner_id,storage_owner_id,extension_id,writer_symbol,physical_to_logical_serializer_symbol,mapping_physical_sources,before_snapshot,writer_observation,after_readback_snapshot,serializer_invocation,observation_nonce,writer_invocation_count,before_snapshot_invocation_count,after_readback_invocation_count,serializer_invocation_count,production_trace_root,production_event_ordinals,receipt_digest}`. Its schema/authority owner equals the registry entry; mapping digest/revision, authority/storage owners, extension ID, writer and serializer symbols equal the one highest active mapping/owning ExtensionGate member byte-for-byte. Every snapshot observation-boundary storage owner, source schema digest, decoded production identity owner, and query/writer event owner then equals that mapping storage owner; the serializer event owner equals its authority owner. No outer receipt owner/extension alias is caller-selected. `mapping_physical_sources[]` is the byte-identical `(physical_source_id,storage_path,storage_kind,storage_version,schema_canonicalization_id,schema_digest,transaction_domain_id)` projection from the active mapping, sorted by source ID; both snapshots are complete records above; `serializer_invocation` is a `boundary_kind=serializer` record whose preimages cross-equal both full snapshot roots, both state roots, and the complete emitted logical row preimage only. The parent later assembles the case outcome from the full row set; the physical serializer cannot read or emit it. `production_event_ordinals` is non-empty ascending/unique. `writer_observation` is exactly `notReached(caseReasonCode,completeDenialBoundary,touchedPhysicalKeys,beforeSnapshotStateRoot,afterSnapshotStateRoot) | reachedNoCommit(caseReasonCode,completeAttemptBoundaries,touchedPhysicalKeys,beforeSnapshotStateRoot,afterSnapshotStateRoot) | committed(completeWriterBoundaries,touchedPhysicalKeys,beforeSnapshotStateRoot,afterSnapshotStateRoot)` using the universal tagged-union wire; both roots equal the complete aggregate `PhysicalSnapshotV1.snapshot_state_root` values, so multi-source mappings have one unambiguous state commitment. Both complete-boundary arrays are non-empty, sorted/unique by `invocation_ordinal`, and contain full invocation preimages, never digest references. `touchedPhysicalKeys[]` is sorted/unique by `(physical_source_id,record_path,query_key_sha256)` and each member contains exactly those three fields. Mutations change each registry→mapping→outer receipt→snapshot boundary→decoded identity→event owner/extension link separately and rehash all envelopes; every mutation fails.

`notReached` requires writer count zero, one `boundary_kind=denial/status=denied` boundary, the exact pre-writer branch, equal before/after snapshot-state roots (full evidence roots remain independently distinct), an explicitly empty touched-key array, and no transaction/change boundary. `reachedNoCommit` requires the signed positive writer/attempt count to equal the exact attempt-array length; every member is `boundary_kind=transactionAttempt/status=rolledBack|casLost`, its input/output state roots form one contiguous no-change chain, the aggregate before/after state roots are equal, and touched keys are explicitly empty. `committed` requires the signed positive writer count to equal the complete writer-array length. That non-empty array may interleave zero or more failed `transactionAttempt` members with one or more `committedChange/status=succeeded` members in the exact signed phase order; every adjacent output/input state root is equal, each failed attempt preserves state, every committed change has distinct input/output state roots and advances a transition admitted by the row invariant, no later member may restore any earlier state root, the aggregate first/last state roots differ, and the sorted union of every committed member's non-empty touched-key set equals the aggregate touched-key array. The signed schedule fixes variant, reason, total count, exact successful-change count and phase identity—so A2 claim then handoff/spend, or another governed multi-phase transition, remains representable without merging two commits. Every embedded writer/denial boundary is byte-identical to its unique trace invocation and no trace writer boundary for the row lies outside the variant. `receipt_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-OBSERVATION-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)) || canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)`, again removing only `receipt_digest`. For one case, the complete receipt array is sorted/unique by unsigned `(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)` and `physical_observation_receipt_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PHYSICAL-OBSERVATION-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptArray)) || canonicalReceiptArray)`. Every receipt's `observation_nonce` must equal both its case's `production_trace.observation_nonce` and the wrapper-registered nonce; no independently chosen but well-formed nonce is legal. The set must equal the case's due-row set; both independent snapshot/read counts are exact, and every due mapper/serializer count is one even when the writer was correctly not reached. Mutation tests alter every key/type/tag/reason/count/preimage/domain, drop or reorder an intermediate attempt/change, merge two scheduled changes, substitute a no-change success, restore an earlier root, break the state-root chain, inject self-digest recursion, reorder receipts, reuse a receipt across cases/mappings/nonces, and recompute outer roots; independent semantic validation must still fail.

`production_trace` is the case-wide cross-row ordering witness. It contains exactly `trace_version:U = 1`, `turn_operation_digest:D`, `boot_session_digest:D`, `observation_nonce:S[k3-observation-nonce-v1,64]`, declaration-ordered non-empty `events`, sorted `boundary_invocations`, sorted `snapshot_evidence`, and `production_trace_root:D`. Every event is the exact record `{event_ordinal:U, monotonic_high_water_mark:U, event_kind:S[k3-production-event-kind-v1,128], owner_id:S[k3-owner-id-v1,128], symbol:S[k3-symbol-id-v1,256], evidence_owner:physicalRow(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)|mappingProjection(projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest,mapping_side)|decisionOnly(amendment_id), transaction_boundary_id:notApplicable(RC)|present(D), row_scope_schema_id:notApplicable(RC)|present(S[k3-row-scope-schema-id-v1,256]), key_digest:notApplicable(RC)|present(D), a2_timing_binding:notApplicable(RC)|present(R[(action_kind,E[cancel,terminalQuery]),(ordinal,U),(grant_request_digest,D),(grant_digest,D),(phase,E[claim,handoff,terminalObservation])]), time_observation:notApplicable(RC)|present(D,U), causal_parent_ordinals:A<U;ascendingUInt64;unique>, before_snapshot_root:notApplicable(RC)|present(D), after_snapshot_root:notApplicable(RC)|present(D), boundary_kind:notApplicable(RC)|present(E[query,denial,transactionAttempt,committedChange,serializer,teardown,coldOpen,migration,migrationTransaction]), boundary_receipt_digest:O<D>}`. `mapping_side` is the closed enum `replayCurrent | backwardPrior | backwardCurrent | backwardLifecycle`; it is present only in the mapping-projection variant. The first three select current/prior/current mapping respectively. `backwardLifecycle` selects **no one mapping**: teardown/cold-open bind the prior side; migration input/prior source descriptors bind the immediate predecessor; migration output/current descriptors and the migration transaction domain bind the current side; owner/symbol comes only from the signed static migration contract. Ordinals are exact contiguous UInt64 from zero; every causal parent is smaller than its child; `monotonic_high_water_mark` is strictly increasing in the exact `boot_session_digest` domain and cannot cross boot. Event transaction-boundary applicability and fixed reason are byte-identical to the matched invocation; decision-only events use `notApplicable(decisionOnly)`, and no raw digest can substitute for either tagged variant.

`boundary_invocations[]` is sorted/unique by `invocation_ordinal`, contains complete `PhysicalBoundaryInvocationV1` values, and is in exact bijection with events whose `boundary_kind` is present: `event_ordinal == invocation_ordinal`, and kind, symbol, transaction boundary, receipt digest, row/key scope derived from the invocation, and event contract all match byte-for-byte. A decision-only event has no invocation. Conversely, it is the exact byte-deduplicated union of every complete boundary embedded anywhere in the child's physical receipts or mapping projection observations; in particular, every row-scoped denial/transactionAttempt/committedChange is embedded exactly once in that row's complete writer variant. No central-only writer exception, digest-only, duplicate, or orphan boundary is legal.

`snapshot_evidence[]` is sorted/unique by `snapshot_root` and contains complete `PhysicalSnapshotV1` values. It equals exactly the deduplicated union of every complete snapshot in the child's physical receipts and mapping observations plus every snapshot selected by a present event root. A present event root resolves to one byte-identical member; a syntactically valid unbacked root fails. Every row-scoped member—including intermediate multi-commit and backward-prior snapshots—must independently satisfy its uniquely selected mapping's `requiredPhysicalInputs`, exact source/record/field set, raw-record decode/NULL rules, schema/type/nullability, snapshot-symbol, owner, and observation-boundary/query-invocation closure. Mutation tests drop/add/substitute one intermediate source/record/field, splice queries across observation boundaries, or use the current mapping for a backward-prior member while recomputing all roots and require failure.

Snapshot-root applicability is exact: query events present the same containing snapshot root in both fields; denial/transaction-attempt/committed-change/serializer events present their complete before and after snapshot roots; decision-only, teardown, cold-open, migration, and migration-transaction events use `notApplicable(noPhysicalSnapshotAtBoundary)` in both fields. For denial/transaction-attempt/committed-change/serializer, the resolved before/after snapshots' `snapshot_state_root` values must equal the invocation input/output before/after state roots respectively; query resolution cross-equals its complete record observation, and serializer additionally cross-equals both full roots. No mixed pair, unrelated well-formed snapshot, or rehashed state/full-root substitution is legal. Every physical-boundary event requires `boundary_kind=present(exactKind)` and its exact invocation; event kind/symbol and boundary kind agree with the closed per-amendment map. Only a genuinely logical decision event that invokes no physical boundary uses `boundary_kind=notApplicable(decisionOnly)` and an absent digest. No physical query is classified as decision-only.

Each event is emitted at the admitted production boundary under the wrapper nonce; no test-authored timestamp or log line is admitted. Only `a2.recoveryOrderDecision` may carry `present(a2_timing_binding)` and `present(time_observation)`; both are present together, the row-scope/key fields are mandatory and resolve the exact K4 provider-action-grant row, and every other event uses `notApplicable(notA2GrantObservation)` for the binding. The timing phase has exact GT-selected cardinality: `success` has one `claim` and one `handoff`; `expiredBeforeClaim`/`revokedBeforeClaim`/`terminalDenied` have one `terminalObservation`; and `expiredAfterClaim`/`revokedAfterClaim` have one `claim` and one `terminalObservation`. Each record repeats that action's exact kind, ordinal, request/grant identities and signed `(grantTimeDomainDigest,tick)`, and is causally paired with the unique physical lifecycle change event for the same grant/key and transition. Reservation/deadline has no invented observation event: its signed deadline remains in GT/grant state, and the reservation's physical event uses N/A time. Cancel and query lineages, equal-valued ticks, duplicate phases, an unpaired decision, or a time on any generic physical event fail.

`production_trace_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PRODUCTION-TRACE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(traceBytesWithoutRoot)) || traceBytesWithoutRoot)`. `evidence_owner` creates an exact disjoint ownership partition: current-row writer/read-back/serializer events belong to that physical row; backward/legacy lifecycle and projection-only events belong to the one mapping projection; pure control events belong to the amendment decision. Every physical receipt and mapping observation still lists every ordinal it references. If one current query/read event is reused by a mapping projection, it remains owned by the physical row and the mapping list is an explicit borrowed reference to the same complete invocation/snapshot; no duplicate event is emitted. The disjoint owned physical-row sets, owned mapping-projection sets, and decision-only set have a combined union equal to all trace ordinals, while every reference resolves to its unique owner. Missing/orphan/double-owned event or a projection lifecycle event forced into a row receipt fails.

Relation predicates consume exact trace subsequences in addition to final rows. A1 requires predecessor-head read/CAS winner before successor/grant activation and budget-use publication; A2 requires the signed cancel sequence before terminal-query sequence whenever both apply and orders marker/start/terminal observation exactly; A3 binds owner-loss detection before containment terminalization; A4 requires definitely-false PCC marker read before any respond-entry/mechanism-start event; A5 orders reservation before observation/terminal accounting; A6 orders lexeme decode/validation before budget observation commit. Exact N/A/denial cases have the corresponding denial event and no forbidden writer event. Swapping two individually legal events, changing a causal parent/high-water mark, removing a boundary, or recomputing every outer digest after reversing an A1/A2/A4 order must fail solely under the trace relation law.

The observation sink is registered by the wrapper before the test with an unguessable per-run nonce and accepts events only from the admitted production decision boundary, writer/transaction boundary, snapshot adapter, and exact mapping serializer symbols. Controlled-test source cannot import or call the sink's event constructors. Static Swift call-graph/SIL tests and runtime nonce/count tests require `public operation -> admitted decision boundary`, then the schedule-selected branch: `notReached` proves the exact denial edge bypasses the writer; `reachedNoCommit` proves writer/transaction attempt plus rollback/CAS-loss; `committed` proves writer/transaction/change receipt. All branches then require independent read-back and the exact `physicalToLogicalSerializerSymbol`. They reject a fixture builder, case-fact-to-outcome path, test-owned fake receipt, alternate mapper, mapper that reads admission facts/expected data, serializer invocation without actual source/read-back preimages, a forged committed receipt, zero writes relabeled one, one write relabeled zero, or a no-write case lacking the independent absence query. Schema-fixture mutations bypass each production node, substitute same-shaped fake receipts, or synthesize the logical answer directly and must fail before expected comparison.

After the child exits, the **parent wave runner**, which already loaded and independently validated the stage-0 controlled catalog, reopens the child receipt and suite admission map, validates every physical observation receipt against the exact active mapping chain, validates the complete production trace/receipt subsequence partition and cross-row ordering, validates registry/key/state/aggregate/relation laws, requires the observed state-fixture array to be the minimal transitive closure reachable from the observed outcome, and computes `observed_outcome_digest`. Only the parent then selects `expected_oracle_id/expected_outcome_digest`, requires full canonical outcome equality—not digest equality alone—and creates the sanitized aggregate observation containing all child preimages plus those parent-injected expected fields and computed digest. A child can pass its execution/shape contract yet the aggregate run still fails semantic/physical/trace/expected validation; no expected channel enters the child.

`realization_extension_id` must be the unique primary member carrying the fixture ID, and `required_edges` must equal the complete outer-edge projection. The wave runner revalidates the entire present object before accepting it into the aggregate receipt. A wrong-suite emitter, fake XCTest frame, log-only fixture, default `.case.01`, missing parameter variant, unrelated extension, synthetic physical receipt, direct case-input-to-outcome builder, `matched_count != 1`, or test that merely echoes a catalog digest cannot pass. Add tests for all prior receipt failures plus an expected-digest echo without an observed preimage, changed observed bytes under a copied digest, missing/extra/orphan state fixture, unresolved nested state reference, non-minimal closure, validly rehashed but relation-invalid outcome, wrong case-binding digest, missing/extra/swapped mapping receipt, source/change/read-back root mismatch, writer/mapper count drift, expected-catalog file access, and Python/Swift expected/observed-builder sharing.

During one cumulative invocation the wave runner keeps a unique in-memory map `(wave, suiteID) -> canonical sanitized child receipt`; a suite may map to exactly one receipt and every earlier active wave is rerun against the same admitted candidate snapshot. There is no hidden historical child-receipt input and no claim that an earlier temporary receipt is immutable. Before the temporary directory is destroyed, the aggregate receipt embeds each complete sanitized child object, its SHA-256, and `fixture_observation_root = SHA256("QINAO-FIXTURE-OBSERVATIONS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObservationArray)) || canonicalObservationArray)`. `canonicalObservationArray` is strict compact sorted-key UTF-8 JSON, sorted by `(suite_id,test_identifier,fixture_id,case_id)`, with no trailing LF and includes the complete observed outcome/state, physical receipt, production-trace boundary/snapshot, and mapping-projection observation preimages plus only the parent-injected expected identity/digest. Verification re-canonicalizes every embedded child, recomputes each physical/source/change/read-back/mapping/state/key/aggregate/outcome digest plus both child/observation roots, reopens the expected catalog and case bindings from candidate Git blobs, reconstructs the expected `(wave,suiteID)` map, and requires full typed observed-versus-expected equality. It rejects a digest-only observation, copied expected digest, replacement, split/merged suite receipts, path leakage, orphan state fixture, physical-bypass receipt, or any object/hash/root/count mismatch.

In the wave runner, replace the integer-only subprocess helper with an observation helper that tees stdout/stderr, records exact argv/exit code, and exposes allowlisted parsed counters. Publish the aggregate receipt exactly once through `publish_receipt_no_replace`: create canonical bytes in the admitted target directory through its pinned dirfd, fsync the temporary inode, link it to the absent destination with same-filesystem `os.link`, fsync the directory, and unlink the temporary name. `EEXIST`, symlink/substitution, or cross-filesystem drift fails without overwrite; `os.replace`/rename-over-target is forbidden. Publication happens after validation succeeds or fails. Its top-level schema is:

Before adding receipt serialization, close the runner's implementation call graph. These names and ownership boundaries are normative and `test_committed_runner_exposes_complete_mode_graph` asserts all of them and their single call sites:

```text
parse_cli(argv) -> (mode, namespace)
  mode = run | verify-receipt | verify-w0-scope

run_mode(namespace)
  -> admit_receipt_path
  -> admit_exact_git_root
  -> admit certification K4 verification FD before any observation [certification-only]
  -> candidate_index_snapshot
  -> load_canonical_gate_manifest
  -> load_and_validate_current_wave_manifests
  -> load_and_validate_controlled_oracle_catalog [deterministic/all only]
  -> observe_toolchain
  -> execute deterministic/platform phases
  -> candidate postflight after every attempted gate and once finally
  -> publish_receipt_no_replace

verify_receipt_mode(namespace)
  -> read canonical external receipt
  -> reopen candidate/manifest/spec/K4 predecessor from Git
  -> reopen controlled Oracle catalog from Git [deterministic/all only]
  -> derive all expected inventories and compare every receipt field
  -> no subprocess gate, repository write, or receipt write

verify_w0_scope_mode(namespace)
  -> load exact source/final commits
  -> walk the single-parent commit path and exact per-task tuples
  -> compare the exact final static tuple plus checker-emitted proof tuple
  -> no subprocess gate, repository write, or receipt write
```

The runner CLI shapes are closed. `run` is selected only when no verification selector is present and requires exactly `--root --manifest --through --receipt`, with optional `--lane` (default `all`) and `--candidate-tree`. The sole additional option is `--certification-k4-verification-receipt-fd 8`, accepted only for gateway-launched local-broker `--through W0 --lane all`; it is forbidden in ordinary CI/run shapes, must be admitted before repository observation or any gate, and cannot be supplied as a path or another FD. `run` rejects every `--expected-*`, `--source-commit`, and `--final-commit` argument. `verify-receipt` is selected by `--verify-receipt` and requires exactly the expected commit/tree/manifest/review-count/through/lane arguments listed below; only the K4 predecessor archive and external K4 verification receipt are optional, and it rejects run/scope arguments. `verify-w0-scope` is selected by `--verify-w0-scope` and requires exactly `--root --source-commit --final-commit`; it rejects manifest/lane/candidate/receipt/expected arguments. Parser failure happens before repository observation. Runner unit tests invoke ordinary run, certification run, and both verification modes plus every missing, mixed, duplicated, wrong-FD, empty, or extra combination; no `argparse` default may make an argument appear in another mode. Oracle admission arguments do not exist in this parser and are rejected as unknown.

Task 2 also creates and completes the separate **external Oracle admission verifier before it commits**. `scripts/check_qinao_external_oracle_admission.py` and its test are complete, self-contained Task-2 additions; Tasks 3–6 are forbidden from modifying, staging, importing semantic helpers from, or parent-edge-touching either path. The verifier exposes one public seam:

```text
verify_external_oracle_admission(
  root,
  source_baseline_selection_path,
  execution_intent_path,
  oracle_selection_path,
  oracle_catalog_path,
  receipt_path
)
  -> admit five external paths through pinned no-follow directory FDs
  -> before parsing any proposal semantics, admit_current_python_substrate()
     compares sys.executable realpath/regular-file bytes, implementation/version,
     imported non-built-in module closure, and closure root byte-for-byte with
     the Task-0-signed admission_python record
  -> byte-compare the human-admitted commit-safe Task-0 selection/trust-root bundle with
     TASK0_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1 and
     TASK0_REVIEWER_TRUST_ROOT_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1
  -> byte-compare and fully rederive the importer-produced
     TASK0_SOURCE_IMPORT_ADMISSION_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1, including its original
     in-window admitted_at_utc and source/import/substrate commitments
  -> reconstruct that selection's exact ordered import-unit commits/trees and
     every parent-edge touched tuple from Git
  -> verify the signed Task-4 execution intent, exact base/tree/write set,
     implementer principal/key/role/epoch, validity window, and nonce
  -> reconstruct the unique Task-2 admission-verifier commit/tree/blob/test pins
  -> verify the Oracle selection signature, nested intent byte identity, and
     reviewer/implementer key plus principal separation
  -> derive the exact 165 case IDs, 86 fixture IDs, six case-input schemas,
     case-fact table, required logical keys, nine row predicates, and six
     amendment relation predicates from code-owned reviewed constants
  -> validate/re-canonicalize the external registry, bindings, state fixtures,
     outcomes, every semantic predicate, and every nested/root digest
  -> publish one canonical success receipt with no replace
```

The module has no directly admitted host-shell entry point. Its only valid sandbox argv tail is the fixed FD-only shape `--verify --repository-root-fd 3 --execution-intent-fd 4 --oracle-selection-fd 5 --oracle-catalog-fd 6 --broker-fd 7`, launched exclusively by the Task-4 `python-script` gateway call below under `read-only-checker`; repository root, private input leaves, output/cache location, interpreter, and receipt publication remain native-owned. The parser rejects path-bearing legacy forms (`--root`, `--source-baseline-selection`, `--execution-intent`, `--oracle-selection`, `--oracle-catalog`, `--receipt`), `--ledger`, `--through`, candidate manifests, and every runner/owner-checker option. Conversely, all three runner modes reject every admission-only argument as unknown. The four input leaves are owner-held mode-`0600`, link-count-one regular files beneath already mode-`0700` real private directories, opened by the gateway with `O_NOFOLLOW` and stable pre/post `(device,inode,length,digest)` observations. `admit_current_python_substrate()` is the first sandbox operation after fixed FD decoding and before JSON parsing, Git, signature verification, or result emission. It requires the executing absolute `sys.executable` and resolved path to recompute the signed path commitments, then requires its stable ID, full regular-file raw SHA-256, implementation, exact version output, every loaded/importable non-built-in standard-library module path commitment/mode/digest, and closure root to equal the signed `admission_python` object embedded in `TASK0_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1`. Raw local paths remain process-private and never enter a committed constant, receipt, catalog, Ledger, or diagnostic. The module AST admits only its closed standard-library import set; `-I -S` must report isolated/safe-path/no-site state. No repository/script directory, current directory, user site, `PYTHONPATH`, `PYTHONHOME`, `.pth`, `sitecustomize`, `usercustomize`, dynamic loader injection, or third-party module path is legal. A replacement interpreter cannot choose a new expected identity because the expected commitments/bytes are frozen in the Task-2 source and independently signed in Task 0. The sandbox returns a non-authoritative structured result over the broker; only the gateway constructs and no-replace publishes the authenticated admission envelope.

First admission requires HEAD/tree at the signed base, an absent receipt leaf, and zero index/worktree changes on every signed Task-4 path. The verifier observes canonical UTC seconds once, requires that `admitted_at_utc` lie at or after both intent and selection issue times and no later than intent expiry, and publishes it inside the success receipt through a pinned dirfd, mode-`0600`, file/dir `fsync`, and no-replace link protocol. Resume is a distinct read-only state: an existing owner-held mode-`0600`, link-count-one canonical receipt supplies the immutable `admitted_at_utc`; the verifier rederives every other byte, proves that recorded time was inside the original validity window, requires unchanged four input bytes, base HEAD/tree, trust epoch/revocation facts, and frozen verifier identity, and permits current index/worktree changes only as an exact subset of the intent's signed twelve-path set. Resume may occur after `expires_at_utc`; expiry forbids a new admission, not continuation of the already admitted identical execution. It never rewrites or replaces the incumbent.

If a resumed input/base/path-set/frozen-verifier check fails, execution stops. A fresh intent/selection/receipt is legal only after the operator restores a clean signed base with no Task-4 changes through the separately reviewed preservation/rollback procedure; it cannot bless an in-flight modified candidate. A parse, trust, signature, canonicalization, registry, binding, fixture, outcome, semantic-law, or publication failure exits non-zero and never leaves a newly published file that can decode as a passing receipt.

`TASK0_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1`, `TASK0_REVIEWER_TRUST_ROOT_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1`, and `TASK0_SOURCE_IMPORT_ADMISSION_CANONICAL_BYTES_V1` are respectively the exact signed selection, public-only trust-root/role/principal/epoch allowlist, and importer-produced passing source-admission receipt from Task 0, embedded as reviewed canonical bytes plus separate domain-separated/raw digests in the frozen Task-2 verifier. They are validation policy/evidence, not a runtime authority or secret. The supplied source-baseline selection must be byte-identical—not merely validly signed under the same root—and its exact ordered import-unit parent/commit/tree/touched-path tuples, first-seal history, original in-window admission time, source records, and final Task-2 ancestry must reconstruct from Git and embedded receipt before the root is used for the Oracle domain. A second selection signed by the same key, a different import history with the same final bytes, a key/root supplied only by the Oracle proposal, a byte-equivalent key under another role/principal/epoch, an expired first admission, self-review, or a re-signed catalog under a test/unknown key fails.

Before Oracle review begins, the human who will implement Task 4 must publish one canonical external `qinao-w0-execution-intent-v1` record. It contains exactly `schema_version = 1`, the complete approved-spec tuple, `source_baseline_selection_sha256`, `reviewer_trust_root_sha256`, `execution_base_commit`, `execution_base_tree`, sorted `task4_controlled_paths`, `task4_controlled_path_set_root`, `implementer_key_id`, `implementer_principal_id`, `implementer_role = w0-implementer`, `implementer_epoch`, `issued_at_utc`, `expires_at_utc`, `nonce`, `signature_algorithm`, and `signature`. The exact path array is the same twelve-path tuple in Task 4 Step 9; `task4_controlled_path_set_root = SHA256("QINAO-TASK4-CONTROLLED-PATH-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPathArrayBytes)) || canonicalPathArrayBytes)`. The base commit/tree are the unique reopened Task-3 proof predecessor at which Task 4 will start. Times are canonical RFC 3339 UTC seconds ending in `Z`, expiry is strictly after issue and admission occurs within the closed interval, and `nonce` is exactly 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters representing 128 random bits.

The intent key must resolve in the Task-0 allowlist to that exact principal, role, and current epoch. Its signature covers `"QINAO-W0-EXECUTION-INTENT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(unsignedIntentBytes)) || unsignedIntentBytes`, where unsigned bytes are the same closed canonical object with only `signature` removed. `executionIntentSHA256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete signed canonical bytes. The Oracle selection contains both the complete nested `execution_intent` object and `execution_intent_sha256`, plus the repeated implementer key/principal/role/epoch; the verifier requires the external intent bytes, nested object bytes, digest, and repeats to agree exactly. It also requires current HEAD/tree to equal the intent base and later requires the staged Task-4 tuple to equal the signed path set before commit. This signed intent—not a guessed Git author string—is the machine-checkable Task-4 implementer identity and self-review boundary.

The trust-root schema makes `w0-implementer` and `oracle-reviewer` disjoint roles: no key ID or principal ID may occupy both roles in the admitted epoch. The Oracle reviewer must resolve under `oracle-reviewer`, and **both** reviewer key ID and reviewer principal ID must differ from the intent's implementer values. A shared principal with two keys, one key under two roles, expired/revoked epoch, changed base/path set, selection that embeds a different intent, or intent authored after its reviewer selection fails.

The Oracle selection pins the unique Task-2 commit that first added the standalone verifier, that commit's tree, the **complete** `scripts/check_qinao_external_oracle_admission.py` path/blob/raw SHA-256, and the complete `scripts/test_check_qinao_external_oracle_admission.py` path/blob/raw SHA-256. Admission proves those blobs are still the executing HEAD blobs and that neither path was touched on any parent edge between the verifier commit and the Task-4 predecessor, even if bytes were later restored. Tasks 3–6 may neither edit nor stage either path; there is no region/sentinel slice, transitive-helper exception, or reinterpretation of the receipt. The separately mutable runner/test are deliberately not verifier pins. The success receipt is the following closed object; the displayed hashes are types, and canonical bytes are compact sorted-key UTF-8 with no trailing LF:

The verifier never reads the pre-Task-4 K3 addendum or candidate Owner Ledger to decide case ownership, fields, keys, facts, expected states, or laws. Those inputs intentionally do not exist yet. Its Task-2 test module carries independent golden bytes and predicate vectors—not calls into the catalog author—and mutation-tests every trust/signature field, input file/path race, case/fact/key, tagged union, row invariant, cross-row law, minimal state closure, root, source/test pin, and no-replace race. Substrate mutations cover fake leading `PATH`, alternate `QINAO_ADMISSION_PYTHON`, interpreter bytes/version/implementation/realpath drift, `PYTHONPATH`/`PYTHONHOME`, `sitecustomize`/`.pth`, script-directory/current-directory stdlib shadow, dynamic-loader variables, extra import, stdlib byte/path/mode drift, and closure-root drift. Resume mutations retain an old canonical PASS receipt, then alter each input and the executing HEAD/source/substrate identity separately; the mode must return non-zero without changing the receipt, and the complete Step-0 shell must remain non-zero rather than reading the stale status. It must also reject a catalog whose author recomputes all hashes and signs with the trusted test key after making any semantic mutation. This passing receipt is therefore the sole pre-edit bootstrap fact; the post-embed loader below remains a distinct second implementation and later byte-compares the admitted inputs and receipt commitment.

`load_and_validate_current_wave_manifests(root, snapshot, through)` reads `docs/superpowers/specs/qinao-owner-ledger-v1.json` from the stage-0 blob recorded in `snapshot`, never from an unbound path. It duplicate-key-rejects and canonically validates exactly three records per active wave in wave/class order; checks the complete approved-spec tuple; validates closed M/CreateGate, E/A/ExtensionGate, and schema-fixture entry variants against the snapshot's exact `(mode,blob,path)` map; derives count/disposition/reason and the domain-separated digest; and rejects inactive/foreign/missing/extra records. W0 before Task 4 therefore fails deterministic/all with a typed missing-manifest error; platform does not call this function and emits `current_wave_candidates: null`. After the owner checker runs, `parse_owner_checker_transcript` requires its exact anchored per-wave lines and cross-compares every count/disposition/digest with these independently derived records before accepting its final historical/cardinality line. Tests mutate each side independently so neither the ledger nor checker stdout can self-attest.

`load_and_validate_controlled_oracle_catalog(root, snapshot)` is likewise deterministic/all-only and reads both the K3 addendum and Owner Ledger from their exact stage-0 blobs. It extracts the unique bounded controlled-catalog heading, duplicate-key-rejects the two canonical objects, validates the signed selection against the Task-0 trust binding and frozen Task-2 verifier identity, reconstructs the nine-entry logical row-scope registry, nine invariant tables, six relation laws, and all 165 binding/state/outcome relations, recomputes `oracleCellSchemaRoot` plus every nested/root digest, reconstructs the canonical pre-admission success receipt commitment, and cross-compares the ledger's singular `controlled_oracle_admission` plus six amendment records. This runner-side implementation is committed in Task 2 and shares no registry/case/key/predicate builder or semantic validation call with `verify_external_oracle_admission` or Task 4's owner helper; an AST/call-graph test permits only low-level SHA-256, strict JSON, the immutable public trust-root bytes, and fixed signature primitive reuse. It returns the immutable schema, binding, and catalog roots only after all shape and semantic checks pass. Platform skips the helper and records all three Oracle roots as null, allowing the pre-Task-4 K4 predecessor run; deterministic/all before Task 4 fails rather than fabricating roots. Unit tests replace either blob after snapshot, swap any root, remove a schema/case/law, add an orphan fixture, inject a future physical claim, or make the two sources self-consistent and validly re-signed but semantically wrong and require failure before any Swift subprocess.

All normal-mode exits after receipt admission go through `finish_run(state, failure_stage)`: it performs the final candidate postflight, converts unobserved fields to JSON `null`, validates the closed receipt object, and invokes `publish_receipt_no_replace` exactly once. Expected validation and subprocess failures do not escape this funnel. The only no-receipt cases are CLI parse failure, receipt-path admission failure, termination before Python control, and the documented no-replace publication race. An AST/call-count test rejects a second publisher or a direct success return from `run_mode`.
