`repository_observation` is the closed union `ephemeralCIReadOnlyGit({canonical_snapshot_body_sha256,session_digest,git_identity_sha256,fixed_environment_digest}) | localGatewayBroker({repository_snapshot_receipt_sha256,session_completed_receipt_sha256,session_digest})`. Both variants describe the same closed `GatewayRepositorySnapshotBodyV1`; the CI digest is raw SHA-256 of its canonical body bytes, while the local snapshot value is raw SHA-256 of the complete authenticated envelope carrying those body bytes. The local completed value is raw SHA-256 of the complete authenticated envelope whose payload is `GatewayRepositoryObservationSessionCompletedBodyV1`. The runner/gateway recompute the exact request, response, ordered request-response, and four table roots from the foundational schemas and literal domains above; this receipt projection does not introduce a second grammar or wildcard domain. In local mode `executables.git` is exactly `{"tag":"localGatewayBroker","payload":[]}` and no path/version is serialized; in CI it has the displayed tagged payload. Backend/tag drift, a missing terminal envelope, table-domain substitution, bare-body/envelope substitution, or invented local Git identity fails.

`k4_verification_input` is JSON `null` for every ordinary CI/development run. For the certification-only shape it is the exact non-null object `{receipt_sha256,head_commit,head_tree,transaction_chain_root,run_id,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,platform_policy_authorization_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,authorized_artifact_locator_commitment,projection_root,attestation_sha256,attested_receipt_sha256,candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256,proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256,k4_finalized_receipt_sha256,host_cdhash,extension_cdhash,verifier_key_id,verifier_principal_id,verifier_epoch,retention_policy_digest,retention_expires_at_utc,global_journal_inclusion_root}` projected from the complete authenticated FD-8 receipt after signature/scope/global-journal validation. It is admitted before `candidate_index_snapshot`, included in the canonical inner receipt preimage on pass or any later failure, and byte-equals the detached candidate, certification spec, policy authorization, policy, artifact locator, and final K4 envelopes. `verify-receipt --external-k4-verification-receipt` requires this exact non-null projection and equality of the complete external envelope raw SHA; without that argument it requires null. Missing, post-hoc addition, wrong FD, tampered/cross-run receipt, field omission, locator drift, or a final-all inner receipt created before K4 admission fails. The gateway's `GatewayFinalAllBodyV1` embeds and revalidates these same inner bytes.

`python_unit_contract_root` and `python_units` are likewise lane- and observation-closed. In `deterministic`/`all`, the root is recomputed from the exact compiled `PYTHON_UNIT_TEST_CONTRACTS_V1` records and `python_units` contains exactly 16 registry-ordered closed result rows. For each row, `module`, `source_paths`, `discovered_test_ids`, and `test_surface_root` byte-equal the registry record; `discovered_test_ids` is sorted/unique/non-empty; `executed_test_ids` equals it exactly; all four exceptional-ID arrays are sorted/unique and empty; `executed_test_count` is the checked positive array length; and `exit_code` is zero. `totals.python_unit_module_count` and `totals.python_unit_test_count` are checked sums and the latter is positive. In `platform`, both top-level fields and both totals are JSON `null`; on a deterministic/all failure before Python registry admission/execution, they are null, while a later failure retains the complete already observed rows. Partial module arrays never authorize PASS. The verifier reloads every module from its candidate-index blob closure, rediscovers IDs, reruns it, and recomputes all fields rather than trusting counters or stdout. Mutations omit/duplicate/reorder/rename a module or test, return zero discovery, add a skip/error/failure/unexpected success, change a source blob/root, or forge equal counters with unequal ID arrays and must fail.

The three mapping fields are lane- and observation-closed. In `deterministic`/`all`, every successfully reached wave carries the checkpoint independently reconstructed from its E/A records (the canonical empty checkpoint for W0/W1), a sorted `mapping_projection_receipts` array by `(projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest)`, and `mapping_projection_receipt_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PROJECTION-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptArrayBytes)) || canonicalReceiptArrayBytes)`. W0/W1 use the exact empty array and its non-null root; W2+ is non-empty exactly when a replay/backward proof is due. `totals.mapping_projection_receipt_count` is the checked sum. In `platform`, all three per-wave fields and the total are JSON `null`; on a deterministic/all failure before that wave's mapping admission they are likewise null, never empty/fabricated. Verification reopens mappings, child receipts, physical observation roots, and Git checkpoints and recomputes every field. Mutations change nullability, omit/add/reorder a proof receipt, copy a sibling root, alter a checkpoint, or keep the root while changing a receipt and must fail.

Each `swift[]` row corresponds one-to-one with a manifest Swift spec in manifest order. `manifest_spec_id = SHA256("QINAO-SWIFT-SPEC-ID-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSpecBytes)) || canonicalSpecBytes)`, where `canonicalSpecBytes` is that exact duplicate-key-rejected compact sorted-key manifest object with no trailing LF; the runner recomputes it and rejects collisions. `children[]` contains exactly one child per required suite, sorted by `suite_id`, and no suite may appear under two specs or children. Row listed/executed counts are the checked sums of its children; wave/totals are checked sums of rows. Every embedded child object is the exact sanitized canonical object produced before temporary cleanup. The verifier duplicate-key-rejects and re-canonicalizes it, recomputes `child_receipt_sha256` and the domain-separated `fixture_observation_root`, re-derives the suite's expected case/test/edge/oracle set from the candidate, and requires exact equality. A digest without its child preimage, a raw/unredacted child, one child covering two suites, or any hash/root/count unequal to its own recomputed value fails; equal empty roots/counts across ordinary-suite siblings are expected and legal.

`oracle_cell_schema_root`, `oracle_case_binding_root`, and `oracle_catalog_root` are deterministic candidate observations, not caller metadata. In a successful `deterministic` or `all` run, they are the exact 64-hex `oracleCellSchemaRoot`, `caseBindingRoot`, and `oracleCatalogRoot` independently recomputed from the stage-0 controlled Oracle Catalog and cross-checked against the stage-0 Owner Ledger; a missing, transposed, or different value fails before Swift execution. All three are JSON `null` in the disjoint `platform` lane and on a failure that occurs before the catalog is admitted. `verify-receipt` reopens both candidate blobs from Git, re-extracts and canonicalizes the catalog, recomputes all three roots, and compares all receipt fields; it never trusts a root printed by a checker or child test.

The numeric values above show types, not fixed W0 counts; the unit test compares the real canonical manifest with an exact expected inventory and computes the exact receipt counts from it. For candidate classes, W0 is fixed at `0/0/0`: all three records are `notApplicable`, use the three exact class-specific reason codes above, and prove that the candidate contains no production Create, E/A implementation, or new schema fixture. A `present` record in a later wave requires `count > 0` and `reason_code: null`; a `notApplicable` record requires `count == 0`, its class-specific reviewed reason, and no entries. Every record binds wave, the complete approved-spec commit/tree/path/blob/SHA-256 tuple, and canonical entries or reason; its domain-separated digest is emitted outside the record to avoid self-reference. The schema is closed: missing/extra fields, duplicate JSON keys, non-canonical serialization, malformed executable digests, and illegal lane-specific nulls fail. In `platform`, the receipt retains the same object keys but every non-executed deterministic observation—including Python-unit registry/results, per-wave required-path/scan/Swift/current-wave-candidate fields, and totals for review, historical Create receipts, owner/cardinality, Python modules/tests, globs, listed tests, and executed tests—is JSON `null`; it is never copied from the manifest or represented as zero. `--expected-review-path-count 54` in verification independently derives the static candidate inventory and compares the caller expectation to that derivation; it does **not** compare `54` to the platform receipt's null `totals.review_path_count`. In `deterministic`/`all`, those fields are exact integers/objects. Lane-shape mutation tests flip every null/non-null field. A central failure funnel owns the receipt state. On success, `failure_stage` and no observation are null. On failure, `failure_stage` is one closed enum (`git-root`, `candidate-index`, `manifest-path`, `manifest-bytes`, `manifest-schema`, `input-admission`, `toolchain`, `w6-preflight`, `python-unit-discovery`, `python-unit-run`, `negative-scan`, `checker`, `swift-list`, `swift-run`, `platform`, `candidate-postflight`, or `w6-postflight`), already observed values remain exact, and not-yet-observed values are JSON `null`; failure receipts never fabricate hashes, counts, device data, or toolchain output. The observation funnel always runs candidate postflight after an attempted Python-unit/negative-scan/checker/Swift/platform command, even if that command failed, before choosing the final failure stage. Only CLI parse failure, invalid/unwritable receipt admission, process termination before Python control can begin, or the no-replace publication race described below may lack this invocation's own receipt. Tests force every early and late failure stage.

`candidate_tree` is always the authenticated no-write candidate-index tree from `GatewayRepositoryObservationSessionV1.snapshot.index_tree`; `head_tree` is the independently observed `HEAD^{tree}`. Before the manifest or any other repository input is read, all lanes reject conflicts, tracked unstaged bytes, special index flags, and untracked paths—including ignored paths—so every subsequently observed worktree byte belongs to that candidate index. Every lane repeats the full snapshot after each attempted gate and at final postflight. W0–W5 may validate a fully staged candidate whose index tree differs from HEAD in ordinary development runs. W6 `all`/`deterministic`, and independently the certification-only `--through W0 --lane all` shape, require clean detached HEAD and exact equality among `--candidate-tree`, authenticated index tree, and HEAD tree throughout. No runner command writes a tree object. `device` is `null` for deterministic-only runs. Platform/all reads the already validated K4 `status.json` and records only platform, OS version/build, hashed UDID, install/launch, and phase monitor counts.

For every wave through the requested bound, the owner checker first emits exactly one line in canonical wave order:

```text
owner-ledger-wave: PASS wave=W0 m_creates=0 m_disposition=notApplicable m_manifest=c53feabe1a15a916489c08277605ac8005062b657abec117263d02d65ac84701 ea_extensions=0 ea_disposition=notApplicable ea_manifest=fffaa1fd71956874fff20ee6f3f249da3988febc571b4e14b46d005382408a3a schema_fixtures=0 schema_disposition=notApplicable schema_manifest=e320238db58ab4b7e21a6642d92eb8b17b2c1a7aff0ebff32635793eaac3a4d4
```

The three canonical W0 record byte lengths/digests are exactly `m_creates=511/c53feabe1a15a916489c08277605ac8005062b657abec117263d02d65ac84701`, `ea_extensions=518/fffaa1fd71956874fff20ee6f3f249da3988febc571b4e14b46d005382408a3a`, and `schema_fixtures=485/e320238db58ab4b7e21a6642d92eb8b17b2c1a7aff0ebff32635793eaac3a4d4`; tests recompute, rather than trust, these constants. Later-wave values vary but obey the same grammar and present/notApplicable invariants. For this W0 candidate the final line is exactly `owner-ledger: PASS historical_candidates=3 historical_receipts=3 owners=29 m_allowlist=14 create_permissions=14 controlled_documents=7`; a later wave derives its historical values from its exact reviewed receipts. The review checker emits exactly `qinao-review-candidate: PASS paths=<n>`. The runner accepts no other success grammar, requires exactly one wave line for every active wave and none for inactive waves, and records historical and current-wave values in separate receipt fields. In `deterministic` and `all`, historical/review/cardinality values and every active current-wave object are mandatory; each current category follows its typed disposition. In the disjoint `platform` receipt those non-executed fields are JSON `null`, never fabricated zeroes. Conversely, the deterministic receipt has `device: null`. Receipt validation tests assert these lane-specific shapes and the `all` receipt's exact union.

Implement that owner success grammar and `--through W0...W6` validation in this Task 2 commit, before the K4 predecessor can call the deterministic lane. Extend `scripts/test_check_qinao_owner_ledger.py` with exact stdout tests and mutations for a missing category/wave, duplicate category/wave, historical-only false pass, zero `present`, non-zero `notApplicable`, empty/unknown reason, stale spec pin, entry-count mismatch, changed manifest digest, inactive-wave output, zero/missing/non-decimal/duplicate fields, wrong cardinalities, and both legacy grammars. Later controlled-document edits may change validated digests and terms but must retain this machine interface unchanged.

For every command, compute `argv_sha256` over one exact byte grammar: unsigned 64-bit big-endian argument count, followed for each argument by unsigned 64-bit big-endian UTF-8 byte length and then those exact bytes. Then persist `argv_redacted`: paths inside the Git root become `$ROOT/<normalized-relative>`, private scratch/receipt paths become `$TMP/<typed-slot>`, the selected Xcode becomes `$XCODE/...`, and values after team/device/profile environment selectors, tokens, and future sensitive options become typed SHA-256 markers. Raw values live only long enough to hash in memory. Golden-vector tests cover zero arguments, an empty argument, `NUL` rejection, non-ASCII UTF-8, workspace paths, and temp paths. Stdout/stderr uses the same structured redaction before persistence; a whole-receipt byte scan rejects `/Users/`, runner account/home/workspace paths, raw env values, UUIDs, and UDIDs.

Toolchain observation is a shared preflight, not manifest-provided checker inventory. Every lane runs the selected `$DEVELOPER_DIR/usr/bin/xcodebuild -version`, fixed `/usr/bin/xcrun swift --version`, and `/usr/bin/xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-version` exactly once, records the outputs, and binds the xcrun-resolved Swift executable path/digest. Before approved evidence exists, a deterministic-only development receipt records `k4_attestation_match: null`; after it exists, every lane records `true` only after all observations match K4 `toolchain-attestation.json`; `false` is never a passing value. Thus the two independent CI shards cannot both pass on a toolchain that would fail `all`. Workflow/runner tests prove the shared-preflight ordering and checker-lane partition.

`k4_predecessor` is non-null for `platform`/`all` success and for deterministic success after approved evidence exists. `proof_commit` is the unique latest commit whose diff touches the exact approved-evidence subtree; that commit must be proof-only, its `proof_tree` is bound, and no later commit may touch evidence or platform-policy paths—even if final bytes are restored. `policy_commit` is the unique reviewed policy-only ancestor and its exact blob digest is bound separately. These values are reconstructed from Git, never trusted from status text or `/tmp`; pre-proof deterministic receipts use `null`. Task 6 requires the final `all` object to match Git exactly and mutation-tests touch-then-revert history.

The aggregate `--receipt` contract is enforced before repository validation: absolute normalized path, absent non-symlink leaf, real parent, outside the Git root, confined under `/tmp` or `RUNNER_TEMP`, and no symlink below that trusted root. The writer opens the parent with `O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW`, pins its device/inode by `fstat`, creates a mode-`0600` sibling through that directory FD, writes canonical bytes, flushes/file-`fsync`s, then uses dirfd-relative no-replace link publication, directory-`fsync`s the same pinned FD, and unlinks the sibling. `EEXIST` is a hard failure: incumbent bytes remain untouched and this invocation cannot publish a competing fail receipt, which is the sole admitted-output exception. Race tests replace the parent or create the final leaf between admission/publication and prove escape/overwrite is impossible.

Add mutation tests that corrupt each receipt counter/field, omit/add schema fields, suppress child receipt creation, reorder a platform checker ahead of deterministic/Swift work in `all`, replace/delete one manifest gate, supply a relative/canonical-symlink/`..` manifest alias, target an existing/repository/symlink receipt, introduce tracked-unstaged/untracked/special-flag input, mutate any W0–W6 lane during each gate kind, and force every subprocess failure. Each admitted-output case returns non-zero and writes `status=fail` with the failing exact command/exit except the proven no-replace race; every mutation is caught by postflight even when the mutating gate itself fails.

Add a mutually exclusive, read-only verification mode to the **same** runner so no unreviewed verifier file expands the surface:

```text
run_qinao_wave_gate.py
  --root <clean-candidate-root>
  --verify-receipt <external-receipt>
  --expected-head-commit <object-id>
  --expected-head-tree <object-id>
  --expected-manifest-sha256 <64-hex>
  --expected-review-path-count <integer>
  --expected-through <W0...W6>
  --expected-lane <all|deterministic|platform>
  [--expected-k4-predecessor-archive <canonical-archive>]
  [--external-k4-verification-receipt <fresh-attester-receipt>]

run_qinao_wave_gate.py
  --root <clean-candidate-root>
  --verify-w0-scope
  --source-commit <sealed-baseline-commit>
  --final-commit <candidate-commit>
```

`--verify-receipt` performs no gate and writes nothing. It rejects duplicate keys, non-canonical bytes, extra/missing fields, wrong lane nullability, and any expected-value mismatch; reopens the canonical manifest and derives the exact active waves/checkers/scans/Swift specs/suites/totals plus the frozen Python-unit registry rather than trusting receipt counters; reruns candidate/Git predecessor reconstruction read-only; and validates executable/toolchain/device shapes. It imports only the pure `parent_edge_path_touches` algorithm and injects the runner's sole `run_git_bytes` callback; it never calls the checker's Git wrapper or a second subprocess seam, and no receipt path may substitute `git log -- <path>`. It requires `receipt.approved_spec` to equal the complete compiled-in commit/tree/path/blob/SHA-256 tuple, reopens all five observations from Git/candidate bytes, and requires zero post-approval parent-edge touches, including side-branch touch/revert merged into an identical final tree; a receipt cannot self-declare this field. For final W0 it requires exact derived constants `required_paths=32`, `checkers=8` (`7 deterministic + 1 platform`), `negative_scans=1`, `swift_specs=2`, `required_suites=4`, `python_unit_modules=16`, exact positive registry-derived Python test count/root, `review_paths=54`, and owner shapes `29/14/14/7`. When a predecessor envelope is supplied, it verifies that authenticated closed schema/journal and requires exact equality with `receipt.k4_predecessor`; when the external K4 verification receipt is supplied, it cross-binds that authenticated envelope's digest, artifact digest, run/policy/projection identities, CDHashes, and verifier epoch. Add same-file unit mutations for every field, count, approved-spec pin/history (linear and merge-side touch/revert), canonicalization rule, Git predecessor/envelope, and external-receipt linkage. An AST/call-graph test proves the runner has no direct or indirect second Git executor.

The K4 predecessor is not a caller-authored archive. Its exact authenticated child payload is `K4PredecessorBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,proof_candidate_unit_sha256,proof_review_receipt_sha256,proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256,k4_finalized_receipt_sha256,candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256,evidence_root_sha256,platform_policy_blob_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,policy_commit,proof_commit,proof_tree,transaction_chain_root,parent_edge_verification_root,verified_at_utc,status:"pass"}`. `parent_edge_verification_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-PREDECESSOR-PARENT-EDGES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalCheckedEdgeArray)) || canonicalCheckedEdgeArray)` over every checked `{parent,commit,path,status}` edge for policy/evidence/frozen verifier paths. The external mode-`0600` cache contains the complete `GatewayReceiptV1<GatewayChildBodyV1<K4PredecessorBodyV1>>`; the native gateway is its sole writer and root-journal source. Existing target, symlink/hardlink, wrong owner/mode, invalid proof/review/Git/finalized chain, field mismatch, noncanonical envelope, missing Accepted/Completed, and publication race fail without overwrite. Unit tests mutate every payload/envelope/canonicalization/edge field.

`--verify-w0-scope` is also read-only. The same-file test pins three disjoint surfaces relative to the authenticated bootstrap-final tree: the exact 34 static `(status,path)` changes enumerated in Task 6; the checker-emitted exact K4 approved-evidence file tuple; and the exact 13-path W0 open-set successor tuple/root from Task 0 Step 3. It rejects merges, renames/copies, missing/extra/net-wrong changes, and any post-bootstrap production Swift/Rust/C/C++/Metal/SQL path outside the three isolated fixture files. It first reopens the bootstrap final/frozen roots, so the exact Task-0 safety postimages are allowed only as already-authenticated frozen baseline and any later touch fails. It walks every commit on the single-parent post-bootstrap ancestry path and checks each parent→commit touched tuple against that task's exact commit boundary, so an add/modify followed by restore/delete cannot disappear from the net diff. Mutations cover an extra production path, extra/missing successor evidence, wrong successor root/transaction Completed, rename, delete, case/Unicode alias, merge, and touch-then-revert history. It separately requires both frozen external-admission paths to be first-added by Task 2 and untouched on every later parent edge, and all 13 successor rows to be first-added by the initial normal transaction and untouched thereafter.

The module exposes no certification run-state writer/loader. Task 6 authority and recovery belong solely to signed `QinaoWorktreeProvisionSpecV1`, signed `QinaoCertificationSpecV1`, authenticated gateway receipts, and the root journal. The runner may emit ephemeral CI receipts under `RUNNER_TEMP`, but no caller-authored `{source_commit,final_commit,final_tree}` file can select a certification target or W1 base. Mutation tests prove a plausible local run-state file is ignored and cannot substitute for provision, transaction-chain, K4, certification, or handoff receipts.

- [ ] **Step 6: Obtain exact RED, GREEN, and the nine-path tooling transaction**

The RED review-only unit freezes all mutation/syntax/module/test IDs plus a passing harness and the exact missing-K4 predecessor denial contract. That denial is a positive meta-test requiring `stage = k4Predecessor`, `reason_code = approvedProofAbsent`, exact discovered/executed count one, and zero import/syntax/argument/parser/fixture errors—not “any non-zero”. The GREEN adoptable unit references the RED receipt, contains exactly the nine `A/M` paths listed below, and fixes message `test: add cumulative Qinao wave gate`.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"
: "${QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR:?stable approved encrypted external run directory}"
: "${QINAO_VALIDATION_CONTRACT_REGISTRY:?Task-0-pinned registry path}"
: "${QINAO_TASK2_RED_UNIT:?signed Task-2 review-only unit}"
: "${QINAO_TASK2_RED_BUNDLE:?Task-2 RED bundle}"
: "${QINAO_TASK2_GREEN_UNIT:?signed Task-2 adoptable unit}"
: "${QINAO_TASK2_GREEN_BUNDLE:?Task-2 GREEN bundle}"
GATE=/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate
T2_RED_MAT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task2-red-materialized.json"
T2_RED_REVIEW="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task2-red-review.json"
T2_GREEN_MAT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task2-green-materialized.json"
T2_GREEN_REVIEW="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task2-green-review.json"
T2_GIT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task2-git.json"
"$GATE" candidate-materialize --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/task2-red-materialize" --candidate-unit "$QINAO_TASK2_RED_UNIT" \
```

### Task 3: Satisfy the Real K4 Hard Predecessor

**Files and exact implementation steps:** Appendix A, “Normative implementation steps for Task 3,” below.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the privacy-clean probe-source baseline, Task 2 candidate-bound runner, an independently reviewed release Xcode/iOS build policy, explicit manual-signing profile selectors, and one physical target device.
- Produces: the hardening commit, a prior policy-only reviewed commit, then a proof-only commit containing one independently validated approved K4 evidence bundle.
- Ordering invariant: execute every Appendix A step now, through its proof-only commit, before entering Task 4. This is a hard W0 predecessor from approved addendum §10.4, not a later CI convenience. A beta/prerelease/unapproved toolchain or OS build, unavailable Enhanced Security entitlement, profile mismatch, install/launch/runtime failure, or missing physical device returns the architecture to review and leaves Task 4 forbidden.

- [ ] **Step 1: Execute Appendix A Steps 1–10 and record the three exact commits**

Expected commit order is: `(1) producer/checker/probe-source hardening`, `(2) reviewed release-policy only`, `(3) immutable approved evidence only`. Verify that the third commit is a descendant of the first two, that the platform receipt binds its index tree, and that no seven-document controlled merge exists in the range before the proof commit.

- [ ] **Step 2: Refuse the merge unless the predecessor is machine-green**

From the clean proof transaction, reopen the proof CandidateUnit, GREEN review, Git transaction, candidate-bundle publication, and finalized seal. The native reader independently reconstructs the unique proof/policy history, every digest, and the exact unchanged parent-edge sets, then emits the one authenticated predecessor cache:

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"
: "${QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR:?stable approved encrypted external run directory}"
: "${QINAO_K4_PROOF_CANDIDATE_UNIT:?signed proof unit}"

QINAO_K4_PROOF_GIT_RECEIPT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/k4-proof-git.json"
QINAO_K4_FINALIZED_RECEIPT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/k4-finalized-envelope.json"
K4_PREDECESSOR_RECEIPT="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/k4-predecessor.json"
```

Expected: PASS. The child payload is `K4PredecessorBodyV1`; its complete signed envelope is the only Task-4 predecessor authority. It reconstructs `proof_commit`, `proof_tree`, evidence root, `policy_commit`, policy blob, publication/finalized/Git/review roots, and proves the parent-edge touched-path set after evidence/policy publication is empty—not merely net-clean. Task 4 begins only from that exact commit. Never copy or relabel preliminary evidence to satisfy this step.

Task 2's complete `scripts/check_qinao_external_oracle_admission.py` and `scripts/test_check_qinao_external_oracle_admission.py` blobs are immutable predecessors, not part of this edit: Task 4 calls the former but may not modify, stage, or parent-edge-touch either path. Reconstruct the unique latest proof-only `proof_commit`/`proof_tree`, policy-only `policy_commit`/blob, and committed evidence/policy digests directly from Git, then optionally compare the archived predecessor record if present. Require current HEAD to equal `proof_commit` at Task 4 entry, recompute all digests, and rerun the K4 checker. If work resumed from a descendant, require `proof_commit` to be an ancestor and prove that neither the evidence/policy paths, either complete frozen Task-2 external-admission verifier/test path, nor any of the exact seven controlled documents changed in the intervening range.

Before authoring any Task-4 postimage bundle, run the already committed Task-2 verifier only as a powerless `read-only-checker` child. The gateway opens the signed/private inputs, passes fixed FDs, independently validates all identity/time/signature/Git facts, and publishes the authenticated admission envelope. Private input/cache paths survive interruption; rerun is legal only when the root journal and all bytes rederive identically.

```bash

: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?exact external Task-0 selection}"
```

The verifier independently checks reviewer separation, approved-spec pin, exact case-ID/fixture sets, all six input/fact schemas, the complete logical row-scope registry and nine predicate tables, all six cross-row laws, every case binding/key, the minimal state closure, every per-case state/outcome, catalog SHA/root, and canonical bytes without consulting the absent candidate ledger. The gateway independently constructs child payload `GatewayOracleAdmissionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,status:"pass",source_baseline_selection_sha256,source_import_admission_sha256,execution_intent_sha256,selection_sha256,catalog_sha256,case_id_set_root,oracle_cell_schema_root,case_binding_root,state_fixture_catalog_root,oracle_catalog_root,admission_verifier_version,admission_verifier_commit,admission_verifier_tree,admission_verifier_source_blob,admission_verifier_source_sha256,admission_verifier_test_blob,admission_verifier_test_sha256,implementer_key_id,implementer_principal_id,implementer_epoch,reviewer_key_id,reviewer_principal_id,reviewer_epoch,admission_python_id,admission_python_path_commitment,admission_python_real_path_commitment,admission_python_sha256,admission_python_version,admission_python_implementation,admission_python_stdlib_closure_root,execution_commit,execution_tree,task4_controlled_path_set_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,admitted_at_utc}`. The cache contains the complete authenticated envelope, not candidate JSON. A stale PASS cannot mask failed current revalidation because the gateway reopens Accepted/child/Completed plus journal and reexecutes the exact checker surface before byte comparison. Only after this passes may an external Task-4 bundle copy the exact selection/catalog bytes without parse-and-reserialize. Candidate review independently parses them and compares every root plus the admission envelope raw SHA with the singular ledger record. Missing/non-unique/drifting K4 or Oracle authority returns `BLOCKED_ORACLE_ROW_SCOPE_UNAVAILABLE` and blocks materialization/adoption.

The table is a W0 decision inventory, not an ExtensionGate receipt. `First manifest wave` is the first wave allowed to carry a non-empty implementation slice, not permission to realize a later physical slice early. When a decision spans owners, each owner/wave triple becomes one `ExtensionSlice` envelope with its own `sliceID` and singular `existingOwnerID`; the nested exact-symbol members own their `extensionID` values. Envelopes may share a `decisionID`, but no envelope may aggregate owners or move a writer. Each implementation wave converts only its admitted slices into non-empty manifests:

| Wave | Planned E/A decision slices that must become current-wave manifests |
|---|---|
| W1 | value-only portions of `model-material-manifest` and `plan-provider-selection`; `agent-value-contracts`, `delegation-slot-topology`, value-only portions of `context-continuity`, `information-sufficiency`, `continuation-policy-recovery`, `human-fit`, `provider-execution-vector`, `progressive-disclosure`, `source-delivery-gap`, `omega-evolution`, `source-mechanism-ablation` contract/test vocabulary, `certified-context-geometry`, `certified-operating-envelope`, `context-accounting`, `attempt-rebase`, `attempt-rebase-grant`, `provider-recovery-action`, `continuation-checkpoint`, `provider-profile-variants`, `structured-reasoning-rsi`, and `next-question-projection` |
| W2 | K3/storage portions of `k3-revocation-publication`, `delegation-slot-topology`, `steer-input-event`, `context-continuity`, `attempt-rebase`, `provider-recovery-action`, `omega-evolution`, and the profile/lease token-validator slice of `certified-context-geometry`, plus `encrypted-content-memory` |
| W3 | behavior/index/compiler portions of `historical-episode-retrieval`, `progressive-disclosure`, `context-accounting`, `certified-context-geometry`, `human-fit`, and `provider-execution-vector` |
| W4 | `model-material-manifest`, `plan-provider-selection`, `provider-private-realization`, `specialization-cache`, `continuation-checkpoint`, and local/system slices of `provider-profile-variants`, `certified-context-geometry`, and `certified-operating-envelope`; only single-step pure prepare/currentness behavior is admitted |
| W5 | `k4-material-authorization`, `publication-journal`, `zone-c-effect`, physical `attempt-rebase-grant`, remote `provider-recovery-action`, `remote-materialization-defer`, and PCC/custom-remote slices of `provider-profile-variants` |
| W6 | behavior/integration portions of `source-delivery-gap`, `structured-reasoning-rsi`, `next-question-projection`, non-runtime adoption/certification portion of `omega-evolution`, `mechanical-semantic-executor`, `source-mechanism-ablation`, `certification-e4-e5`, and `production-cutover` |

`CANONICAL_DECISION_PHASES_V1` is the sole reviewed machine source of truth. It contains exactly one record for each of the 36 decision IDs and, for every decision, a non-empty sorted tuple of `{wave, ownerIDs[]}` phases. Every owner is one of the exact seventeen existing Owner Ledger IDs. The checker derives `EXPECTED_SLICES[wave]` as the Cartesian expansion of decision, matching phase, and owner. `introductionWave` is the minimum phase wave. Both the 36-row human inventory and the per-wave text block below are generated golden output from that constant (including their owner-display text), and tests require byte-for-byte equality; neither table is parsed as an independent authority. Each expanded triple produces exactly one `ExtensionSlice` envelope. `sliceID` is deterministically `qinao.<lowercase-wave>.<decisionID>.<existingOwnerID-with-dots-replaced-by-hyphens>.v1`. An envelope contains a non-empty, globally sorted, unique `extensions[]`; each extension has its own derived `extensionID` and singular symbol/path/fixtures. Thus one governed owner slice can honestly extend multiple symbols without duplicating the triple or collapsing them into prose. No omitted, extra, duplicate, foreign-owner, earlier/later-wave, or differently named slice is legal. W0 is the empty map and is represented only by the three typed `notApplicable` records.
