```text
W4 continuation-checkpoint: cache.scope, execution.plan-provider-router, provider.package-boundary
W4 provider-profile-variants: execution.plan-provider-router, model.manifest-invocation, provider.package-boundary
W4 certified-context-geometry: execution.plan-provider-router, provider.package-boundary, runtime.certification
W4 certified-operating-envelope: execution.plan-provider-router, runtime.certification
W5 k4-material-authorization: sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle
W5 publication-journal: release.spool-publication
W5 zone-c-effect: effect.zone-c-saga
W5 attempt-rebase-grant: sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle
W5 provider-recovery-action: provider.package-boundary, semantics.layercell, sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle, state.k3-control-nucleus
W5 remote-materialization-defer: effect.zone-c-saga, provider.package-boundary, semantics.layercell, sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle, state.k3-control-nucleus
W5 provider-profile-variants: execution.plan-provider-router, model.manifest-invocation, provider.package-boundary, sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle, state.k3-control-nucleus
W6 source-delivery-gap: runtime.turn-operation
W6 structured-reasoning-rsi: runtime.semantic-dag, semantics.layercell, state.k3-control-nucleus
W6 next-question-projection: runtime.turn-operation, semantics.layercell
W6 omega-evolution: production.cutover, runtime.certification
W6 mechanical-semantic-executor: runtime.semantic-executor
W6 source-mechanism-ablation: production.cutover, runtime.certification
W6 certification-e4-e5: runtime.certification
W6 production-cutover: production.cutover
```

The unit test asserts exactly `101 triples / 36 decisions / 17 owners`, exact per-wave `triple/decision` counts `W1 36/23`, `W2 11/9`, `W3 7/6`, `W4 16/8`, `W5 18/7`, and `W6 13/8`, every phase owner is a member of the ledger's exact seventeen-ID set, and exact generated equality for both Markdown tables. For a `present` E/A record, the set of envelope triples must equal the current wave's derived set; every envelope's extension array must be non-empty and every derived `extensionID` unique across the candidate. `schema_fixtures` must then equal the exact fixture union of every nested extension. A future wave cannot satisfy anti-vacuity with one arbitrary valid extension, one empty envelope, or all suites but an incomplete decision set.

Every `ExtensionSlice` envelope has this closed outer schema:

```text
sliceID
decisionID
introductionWave
wave
approvedSpecCommit + approvedSpecTree + approvedSpecPath + approvedSpecBlob + approvedSpecSHA256
existingOwnerID
extensions[]
oracleMappingChainCheckpoint =
  {throughWave, predecessorMappingChainRoot, currentMappingChainRoot,
   appendedMappings[] = {rowScopeSchemaID, mappingRevision, mappingDigest}}
transactionDomains[] =
  {transactionDomainID, domainRevision, activationWave, predecessorDomain,
   storageOwnerID, engineKind, engineIdentityPreimage,
   engineIdentityDigest, coordinatorSymbol, atomicPhysicalSourceIDs[],
   atomicSourceDescriptors[], nativeReceiptSchemaID,
   nativeReceiptSchemaDigest}
realizedPlannedFixtureEdges[] =
  {fixtureID, requiredSliceID, realizationWave,
   requiredExtensionIDs[], suiteID}
noOwnerMoveProof(ownerBefore, ownerAfter, proofDigest)
```

Every member of `extensions[]` has this closed ExtensionGate schema; no field is optional and no prose-only row can satisfy it:

```text
extensionID
existingRepositoryPath
exactSymbol
wireOrSchemaVersion
canonicalizationID + canonicalizationDigest
requiredSuiteIDs[]
realizedPlannedFixtureIDs[]
currentFixturePaths[]
backwardFixture = present(paths[]) | notApplicable(reasonCode, proofDigest)
futureFixturePaths[]
storageProof = present(paths[], proofDigest) | notApplicable(reasonCode, proofDigest)
replayProof = present(paths[], proofDigest) | notApplicable(reasonCode, proofDigest)
recoveryProof = present(paths[], proofDigest) | notApplicable(reasonCode, proofDigest)
oracleRowRealizations[] =
  {oracleCellSchemaRoot, rowScopeSchemaID, authorityOwnerID,
   storageOwnerID, activationWave, mappingRevision, revisionKind,
   predecessorMapping = absent(firstRealization) | present(mappingDigest),
   physicalSources[] =
     {physicalSourceID, storagePath, storageKind, storageVersion,
      schemaCanonicalizationID, schemaDigest, transactionDomainID},
   keyFieldMappings[] / stateFieldMappings[] =
     {logicalFieldName,
      physicalInputs[] =
        {physicalSourceID, recordPath, fieldPath, physicalType, nullability},
      conversionID, conversionContractDigest},
   writerSymbol, transactionSnapshotBoundary,
   physicalToLogicalSerializerSymbol,
   mappingCoreDigest,
   replayProjectionProof = present(projectionProof),
   backwardProjectionProof =
     notApplicable(firstRealization|coverageOnlyPhysicalIdentity, outerBackwardProofDigest) |
     present(projectionProof)}
noWriterMoveProof(writerBefore, writerAfter, proofDigest)
```

The envelope's five approved-spec fields must exactly equal the enclosing candidate record's complete pin; aliases or partial pins fail. `extensionID` is the domain-separated SHA-256 of the four UTF-8 fields `(sliceID,existingRepositoryPath,exactSymbol,wireOrSchemaVersion)`, encoded as `"QINAO-EXTENSION-ID-V1\0"` followed by a `uint64be` length and exact bytes for each field. It is recomputed, never caller-named. `requiredSuiteIDs` is non-empty/sorted/unique. For W1–W6, the union across every nested member must equal the wave manifest's exact required-suite set; every ID must appear in the future-suite ownership table and its child receipt must prove listed/executed counts greater than zero.

`realizedPlannedFixtureIDs` is always present on every nested member, sorted, and unique. It is empty unless that exact member is the concrete primary realization member for a controlled K3 fixture in the current wave. The ledger deliberately does **not** carry a caller-written `realization_symbol`: placement of the fixture ID on one actual member binds realization to that member's derived `extensionID`, exact path, symbol, version, canonicalization, owner, and primary `sliceID`. For every fixture whose `realization_wave` is current, its ID must occur on exactly one nested member of its exact `realization_slice_id`; no ID may occur early, late, or on a different slice/member.

`oracleRowRealizations` is always present and is a static **availability map**, never a per-case exact-set assertion. A record may occur only on a nested member whose record `authorityOwnerID`, enclosing `existingOwnerID`, `storageOwnerID`, and registry `authority_owner_id` are all byte-identical, and whose slice is in the required-edge closure of at least one current fixture using that schema; this is authority placement, not necessarily the fixture's primary member. It is empty on every member that is not such an authority-owning dependency. The cumulative candidate collapses byte-identical duplicates into a global available mapping chain. Each case then selects only the exact schema/key subset independently derived from its signed case binding; mappings needed by a sibling A2/A3 fixture may coexist on their shared dependency and are neither extra case rows nor permission to emit them.

Every `transactionDomains` and `physicalSources` entry is closed. The envelope-level `transactionDomains[]` is sorted/unique by `transactionDomainID` and is an append-only cumulative owner/coordinator catalog, not a row-mapping-local list. Byte-identical domain records may be repeated by multiple slices and collapse globally; a same-ID/different-byte duplicate fails. Domain revisions are positive contiguous UInt64 per `(storageOwnerID,coordinatorSymbol)`: revision 1 has `predecessorDomain=absent(firstDomain)`; each later revision immutably names the immediate predecessor and has a strictly later `activationWave`. No predecessor is backpatched with retirement state. At evaluated wave W, the active revision is the unique highest revision whose activation is at or before W; existence of its successor derives predecessor retirement at that successor's activation. `atomicPhysicalSourceIDs` is sorted/unique/non-empty and `atomicSourceDescriptors[]` is its exact sorted/unique one-to-one `{physical_source_id,storage_path,storage_kind,storage_version,schema_canonicalization_id,schema_digest}` projection across mapping revisions active at **that domain revision's activation wave**, including sources used by sibling row mappings. A mapping references the domain revision active at its own activation, its local descriptor byte-equals the catalog member, and one mapping may reference only a subset of that domain universe. Historical domains resolve against historical mappings and remain valid after derived retirement; only the current leaf is checked against latest active mappings. Dangling-at-its-own-wave, forked/missing successor, or cross-owner membership fails.

`engineKind` is exactly `sqliteConnection | swiftActor`. `engineIdentityPreimage` is the closed union `sqliteConnection(storage_owner_id,coordinator_symbol,database_identity_contract_symbol,database_identity_contract_digest,atomic_source_descriptors) | swiftActor(storage_owner_id,coordinator_symbol,actor_type_symbol,actor_instance_identity_contract_digest,atomic_source_descriptors)`. Its `atomic_source_descriptors` is the complete catalog array for that domain revision; every symbol/contract digest resolves to an exact stage-0 production declaration and complete canonical descriptor. `engineIdentityDigest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-PRODUCTION-ENGINE-IDENTITY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEngineIdentityPreimage)) || canonicalEngineIdentityPreimage)`, and the union tag must equal `engineKind`. `coordinatorSymbol` repeats the preimage symbol byte-for-byte. `nativeReceiptSchemaID` is exactly `qinao-k3-sqlite-native-transaction-receipt-v1 | qinao-k3-swift-actor-native-transaction-receipt-v1`; and `nativeReceiptSchemaDigest` hashes its complete descriptor. The domain preimage is exactly `{domain_revision,activation_wave,predecessor_domain,storage_owner_id,engine_kind,engine_identity_digest,coordinator_symbol,atomic_physical_source_ids,atomic_source_descriptors,native_receipt_schema_id,native_receipt_schema_digest}` and `transactionDomainID = SHA256("QINAO-K3-TRANSACTION-DOMAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalDomainPreimage)) || canonicalDomainPreimage)`. A physical source/schema/engine membership change appends and activates one successor domain in the same wave (thereby deriving predecessor retirement) and forces `physicalChange` mapping revisions for every still-active affected row mapping; it never rewrites history. Thus equal source labels, locally truncated membership, silent historical reinterpretation, or an opaque engine digest cannot manufacture equal domains.

The decoded `NativeTransactionReceiptV1` is the closed union `sqliteCommit(storage_owner_id,transaction_domain_id,engine_identity_digest,native_transaction_nonce,begin_sequence,commit_sequence,covered_physical_source_ids,affected_table_ids,sqlite_total_changes_before,sqlite_total_changes_after,commit_status) | swiftActorCommit(storage_owner_id,transaction_domain_id,engine_identity_digest,native_transaction_nonce,begin_sequence,commit_sequence,covered_physical_source_ids,actor_isolation_epoch,prior_revision,committed_revision,commit_status)`. `commit_status` is exactly `committed | rolledBack | casLost`; sequences/revisions are UInt64; arrays are sorted/unique and non-empty. Canonical decoded bytes use the domain-selected schema and their raw SHA-256 equals `native_transaction_receipt_sha256` in `ProductionBoundaryIdentityV1`; storage/domain/engine, nonce/sequences, status, and covered sources cross-equal that identity and enclosing boundary. The complete raw native receipt is embedded in the corresponding production `*_receipt_base64`, so the runner decodes it rather than trusting duplicated JSON fields. A committed SQLite receipt additionally has `sqlite_total_changes_after > sqlite_total_changes_before` and its affected table IDs resolve exactly to the covered mapping sources; a committed actor receipt has `committed_revision = prior_revision + 1`. A no-change/failed receipt cannot back `committedChange`.

Every `physicalSources` entry has `storageKind` exactly `sqliteTable | swiftRecord | actorSnapshot`; `storagePath` is an exact stage-0 path, `storageVersion` is its non-empty wire/DDL version, `schemaCanonicalizationID` is the kind-specific checker constant, and `schemaDigest` is the domain-separated digest of the complete canonical DDL or record/snapshot field descriptor. Every key/state field has exactly one same-name mapping in registry declaration order and no extra mapping. A field mapping has a non-empty ordered `physicalInputs` array; each input resolves one admitted source, exact table/record path, exact column/property path, closed kind-specific physical type, and `nonNull | nullable`. `conversionID` comes from a code-owned closed allowlist and `conversionContractDigest` binds its exact input types, null/error rules, output descriptor, byte/endian/text normalization, and implementation symbol.

For v1, every one transaction-partition group that mutates state must touch only sources with one byte-identical `transactionDomainID`. The shared decoded production identity/native receipt's `covered_physical_source_ids` equals the sorted non-empty **group-wide union** of the source IDs in every member invocation's row-local `touched_physical_keys`; every member's local set is a subset and the union has no unrepresented source. The domain's complete `atomicPhysicalSourceIDs` is the independently mapped capability superset and must contain that group union. Multiple SQLite tables are legal only when one decoded native SQLite commit receipt covers the group union; arbitrary SQLite/actor/multi-engine atomicity is rejected. Migration follows the same rule. A future distributed/coordinator proof needs a new identity variant and reviewed schema; v1 never infers atomicity from equal caller labels. An unknown/unmapped logical field, unconsumed physical source field that can affect the projection, free-form expression, ambiguous many-to-one conversion, nullable-to-required default, schema digest/version/domain drift, cross-domain touched key, source omitted from the domain/group union/native receipt, opaque engine identity, or native-receipt schema/status/sequence mismatch fails before a fixture runs. Schema-fixture mutations enumerate these arrays directly and change every source, engine/domain preimage/ID, coordinator/receipt schema, group/local/covered-source set, field path, type, nullability, conversion, and digest one at a time.

`mappingRevision` is a positive UInt64 per `rowScopeSchemaID`, and `revisionKind` is exactly `firstRealization | physicalChange | coverageOnly`. Revision 1 has `revisionKind=firstRealization`, `activationWave == registry.realization_wave`, `predecessorMapping = absent(firstRealization)`, and backward N/A. Its `firstRealizationPreimage` is exactly `{approved_spec,oracle_cell_schema_root,row_scope_schema_id,registry_realization_wave,mapping_revision:1,current_mapping_core_digest,predecessor_disposition:"absent(firstRealization)",legacy_fixture_paths:[],migration_contract:"notApplicable(firstRealization)"}` and `outerBackwardProofDigest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-FIRST-REALIZATION-NO-BACKWARD-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalFirstRealizationPreimage)) || canonicalFirstRealizationPreimage)`. Every later revision increments by exactly one, activates in the current wave, and references the immediately previous domain-separated `mappingDigest`. `physicalChange` must change at least one physical source/field/conversion/version/symbol contract and carries non-empty backward plus replay projection proofs. `coverageOnly` is legal only when a newly realized case first uses an already active schema while every physical/source/field/conversion/symbol byte remains identical to the predecessor; it carries a non-empty replay proof over the enlarged exact case set and `backwardProjectionProof=notApplicable(coverageOnlyPhysicalIdentity, outerBackwardProofDigest)`. For that variant, `physicalIdentityProjection` is exactly `{authorityOwnerID,storageOwnerID,physicalSources,keyFieldMappings,stateFieldMappings,writerSymbol,transactionSnapshotBoundary,physicalToLogicalSerializerSymbol}` copied from one mapping core. `coverageIdentityPreimage` is exactly `{row_scope_schema_id,predecessor_mapping_digest,predecessor_physical_identity_projection,current_mapping_core_digest,current_physical_identity_projection}`; the two projections must be byte-identical, and `outerBackwardProofDigest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-COVERAGE-ONLY-IDENTITY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalCoverageIdentityPreimage)) || canonicalCoverageIdentityPreimage)`. No other field removal list or digest domain is accepted, so coverage-only cannot mask a physical change. `mappingCoreDigest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-ORACLE-PHYSICAL-MAPPING-CORE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalMappingCoreBytes)) || canonicalMappingCoreBytes)`, where core bytes are the complete mapping record with only `mappingCoreDigest`, `replayProjectionProof`, and `backwardProjectionProof` removed. `mappingDigest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-ORACLE-PHYSICAL-MAPPING-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalMappingBytes)) || canonicalMappingBytes)`, where the full record has no self `mappingDigest` field but does contain the verified core digest and both proof variants. This removes proof/mapping hash recursion while binding every proof into the final mapping identity. Mutation tests alter every first-realization/no-change proof field or domain independently and recompute outer hashes; both implementations must reject.

A closed `projectionProof` contains exactly `schema_version = 1`, `projection_kind = replay|backward`, `row_scope_schema_id`, `prior_mapping_digest = absent(firstRealization)|present(D)`, `current_mapping_core_digest`, the exact outer `replayProof` or `backwardFixture` path array and proof digest, sorted non-empty `covered_case_ids`, `covered_case_set_root`, sorted non-empty `schema_fixture_paths`, `legacy_projection`, `expected_logical_projection_root`, and `proof_digest`. `legacy_projection = notApplicable(firstRealizationOrReplayOnly) | present(priorMappingDigest,priorPhysicalToLogicalSerializerSymbol,legacyPhysicalFixturePaths,legacyPhysicalFixtureBytesRoot,migrationContractDigest)`. A present legacy path array is non-empty, stage-0 exact, and its root is domain-separated over every path/mode/blob plus complete old-format fixture bytes; the migration contract binds exact prior/current physical schema digests, cold-open entry point, production migration symbol, transaction boundary, failure/rollback rules, and current serializer symbol.

Covered cases equal **all** cases for that schema realized at or before `activationWave`; backward excludes cases first introduced in that same wave and compares prior-versus-current mapping, while replay includes every earlier and current case. Therefore the first wave that adds any case to an already active schema must append exactly one next mapping revision in that wave: `coverageOnly` when the physical mapping is unchanged, otherwise `physicalChange`. Each revision's replay receipt is produced exactly once for its activation-wave proof and remains immutable historical evidence in cumulative receipts. Separately, current sufficiency requires every active `(case_id,row_scope_schema_id)` pair to occur in the highest active revision's replay receipt; older-revision receipts may contain earlier pairs but are historical-only and cannot satisfy or conflict with current coverage. `covered_case_set_root`, legacy-fixture root, logical-projection-set root, and `proof_digest` use separate length-framed domains `QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PROJECTION-CASE-SET-V1\0`, `QINAO-K3-MAPPING-LEGACY-FIXTURE-SET-V1\0`, `QINAO-K3-MAPPING-LOGICAL-PROJECTION-SET-V1\0`, and `QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PROJECTION-PROOF-V1\0`; the last hashes the complete proof object with only `proof_digest` removed. The outer paths/digest must equal the enclosing proof preimage byte-for-byte, every current/legacy fixture path is an exact stage-0 schema fixture, and `expected_logical_projection_root` is independently derived under that shared neutral set domain from the exact target-schema logical rows projected from the signed Oracle state fixtures/outcomes for those cases. `notApplicable(firstRealization, outerBackwardProofDigest)` is legal only on revision 1; `notApplicable(coverageOnlyPhysicalIdentity, outerBackwardProofDigest)` is legal only on a proven coverage-only revision. Either digest must equal its complete enclosing historical/no-change proof preimage; an arbitrary 64-hex value, digest without preimage, partial case set, current-format bytes mislabeled legacy, wrong outer proof, or proof copied across mapping revisions fails.

The child-owned `mapping_projection_observations[]` closes the runtime dataflow without exposing expected data. It is sorted/unique by `(projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest)` and has exactly one record for every static proof due for that case. Each record is exactly `{schema_version:1,case_id,projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest,observation,production_event_ordinals}`. `production_event_ordinals` is ascending/unique and selects every boundary in the payload. A `MappingRowObservationV1` is exactly `{row_scope_schema_id,key_digest,snapshot,serializer_invocation,logical_row_preimage,logical_row_root}`; snapshot and serializer are complete physical records above, the serializer output repeats the exact row preimage/root, and arrays are sorted/unique by `(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)`. This serializer is a read-only projection: both its before/after full roots equal this one `snapshot.snapshot_root`, both state roots equal `snapshot.snapshot_state_root`, and every before/after source-state binding equals the same snapshot source set byte-for-byte; any claimed mutation while serializing fails.

`observation` is exactly `replay(current_row_observations) | backward(legacy_fixtures,teardown_invocation,cold_open_invocation,prior_mapping_digest,prior_serializer_symbol,prior_row_observations,migration_contract_digest,migration_invocation,migration_transaction_invocation,current_mapping_digest,current_serializer_symbol,current_row_observations)`. Replay carries no legacy/lifecycle field. Backward carries complete legacy fixture bytes and complete boundary preimages but no expected Oracle ID/digest/root. For either variant, row arrays equal the exact due key set for the target schema in that case, including multiple keys of the same schema; every row has one independent snapshot and exactly one serializer. The cold-open invocation's row-keyed `prior_snapshots[]` equals `prior_row_observations[].snapshot` exactly; its source bindings and both migration binding sides equal the corresponding prior/current row snapshot projections. Every complete boundary and snapshot in this observation is byte-identical to the unique member in the same child trace's `boundary_invocations[]` or `snapshot_evidence[]`, and `production_event_ordinals` selects exactly those invocations. Missing/extra row, singular-row collapse, expected-data field, digest-only lifecycle evidence, or observation not selected by the static proof fails before parent comparison.

At runtime the parent runner constructs one `MappingProjectionReceiptV1` per static replay/backward proof due through the selected wave. The record is exactly `{schema_version:1,projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,mapping_revision,current_mapping_core_digest,current_mapping_digest,projection_proof,covered_case_ids,covered_child_receipts,covered_physical_observations,actual_logical_projection_root,proof_observation,receipt_digest}`. `projection_kind`, mapping IDs/digests, proof, and sorted `covered_case_ids` are byte-identical to the static mapping proof. `covered_child_receipts[]` is sorted/unique by `case_id` and each entry is exactly `{case_id,suite_id,test_identifier,child_receipt_sha256}`. `covered_physical_observations[]` is sorted/unique by `case_id` and each entry is exactly `{case_id,physical_observation_receipt_root}` using the complete per-case set-root formula above. Both arrays have exactly the covered case set.

For each covered case, `logical_projection_preimage` is exactly `{case_id,case_binding_digest,row_scope_schema_id,logical_rows}`. `logical_rows[]` is the complete observed logical row array sorted by `(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)` with every key/state field under the frozen descriptor; mapping identity is deliberately excluded so prior/current revisions can prove semantic equality and remains bound by the outer receipt. It contains no whole-case outcome; the independent Oracle aggregate/relation-law path validates that separately. `logical_projection_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-CASE-LOGICAL-PROJECTION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalLogicalProjectionPreimage)) || canonicalLogicalProjectionPreimage)`. The `case_projection_roots[]` array below contains these exact independently recomputed roots. `actual_logical_projection_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-LOGICAL-PROJECTION-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalCaseProjectionRootArray)) || canonicalCaseProjectionRootArray)` and must equal `projection_proof.expected_logical_projection_root`. Each case preimage must also equal its complete observed child projection and the signed expected target-schema row projection; equality of only roots is insufficient.

`proof_observation` uses the universal tagged-union wire and is exactly `replay(case_projection_roots) | backward(case_projection_roots,paired_backward_observations)`. `case_projection_roots[]` is sorted/unique by `case_id`, has exactly `{case_id,logical_projection_root}`, and equals the covered case set. Replay is legal only when `projection_kind=replay`. Backward is legal only when `projection_kind=backward` and revision is greater than one; its pair array is sorted/unique by `case_id` and also equals the covered case set. There is no empty or generic observation variant.

Each parent-built `PairedBackwardObservationV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,case_id,case_binding_digest,expected_oracle_id,expected_outcome_digest,legacy_fixtures,legacy_fixture_bytes_root,teardown_invocation,cold_open_invocation,prior_mapping_digest,prior_serializer_symbol,prior_row_observations,prior_logical_projection_preimage,prior_logical_root,migration_contract_digest,migration_invocation,migration_transaction_invocation,current_mapping_digest,current_serializer_symbol,current_row_observations,current_logical_projection_preimage,current_logical_root,pair_digest}`. The parent copies every runtime field byte-for-byte from the child's validated backward observation, then injects only the expected identity/digest. `legacy_fixtures[]` is sorted/unique by path and every entry is exactly `{path,mode,blob,raw_bytes_base64,raw_sha256}`; the standard padded Base64 decodes to the exact stage-0 blob bytes and `legacy_fixture_bytes_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-LEGACY-FIXTURE-BYTES-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalLegacyFixtureArray)) || canonicalLegacyFixtureArray)`. Teardown, cold-open, migration, and migration-transaction are complete boundary records. Both row arrays contain complete `MappingRowObservationV1` values sorted/unique by `(row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)` and equal the exact target-schema due-key set. `prior_logical_projection_preimage` and `current_logical_projection_preimage` are complete `{case_id,case_binding_digest,row_scope_schema_id,logical_rows}` values reconstructed from their respective row arrays; their roots use the per-case formula above. `pair_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PAIRED-BACKWARD-OBSERVATION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPairWithoutDigest)) || canonicalPairWithoutDigest)`, removing only `pair_digest`.

Across all pairs in one backward receipt, the unsigned-path-sorted union of `legacy_fixtures` must equal `projection_proof.legacy_projection.legacyPhysicalFixturePaths`; recomputing the exact static `QINAO-K3-MAPPING-LEGACY-FIXTURE-SET-V1\0` preimage over those path/mode/blob/full decoded bytes must equal its `legacyPhysicalFixtureBytesRoot`. The per-pair `legacy_fixture_bytes_root` is an additional scoped commitment, never a substitute. Every pair's `prior_mapping_digest`, prior serializer, and migration contract equal the static legacy projection and immediate predecessor mapping; its `current_mapping_digest`/serializer equal the outer active mapping. Every teardown, cold-open, prior-serializer, migration, migration-transaction, read-back-query, and current-serializer boundary digest occurs exactly once in that case's covered child `production_trace`, in that causal order, under the same wrapper nonce.

The runner requires exactly one prior and one current serializer for **each due target-schema row**, real teardown then cold-open then migration then independent current read-back reachability, byte-identical complete prior/current row preimages, and equality with the signed expected row projection. It rejects cache-warm substitution, current bytes used on the legacy side, missing legacy bytes, singular-row collapse, synthetic migration/transaction receipts, old/new mapper bypass, source-ID/root aliasing, or a digest without its complete preimage. For the outer record, `receipt_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PROJECTION-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)) || canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)`, removing only `receipt_digest`. The complete `mapping_projection_receipts[]` is sorted/unique by `(projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest)` and `mapping_projection_receipt_root = SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-PROJECTION-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptArray)) || canonicalReceiptArray)`. Both the complete array and set root are embedded in the wave receipt and independently reconstructed by `--verify-receipt`; no caller-supplied digest can replace a preimage. Mutation tests alter every common/variant/pair field, swap tags or arrays, introduce a self-hash, alias source roots, omit one case/row/preimage, break static-legacy or trace membership, and recompute every outer digest; semantic verification must still fail.

Duplicates of one `(rowScopeSchemaID,mappingRevision)` must be byte-identical. The highest complete chain revision is the sole active mapping. If a later wave changes a shared storage version/schema digest or any physical field/conversion, that wave must publish a `physicalChange` next revision for **every active logical schema that references the changed source**, rerun all earlier/current fixtures through the new mappings, and prove backward/replay equality; it may not require byte-identical W2 physical bytes forever or silently reinterpret an old mapping. If a later wave merely adds a case to an existing schema, it must still publish the exact `coverageOnly` revision and replay proof above. A missing predecessor, missing coverage revision, forked revision, changed bytes under coverage-only, unchanged bytes mislabeled physical-change, partial shared-schema migration, second active mapper/writer, or future schema before its realization wave fails.

`oracleMappingChainCheckpoint` makes that chain append-only across accepted waves. All E/A envelopes in one wave carry the same checkpoint. `predecessorMappingChainRoot` is the immediately preceding active wave's `currentMappingChainRoot` (or `SHA256("QINAO-K3-MAPPING-CHAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(2) || "[]")` for the first E/A wave); `appendedMappings` is the exact sorted set first activated in this wave and may be empty; `currentMappingChainRoot` hashes the complete globally sorted cumulative `(rowScopeSchemaID,mappingRevision,mappingDigest)` array with the same domain. For every earlier wave, the runner reconstructs the unique single-parent commit that first introduced that exact E/A manifest record, extracts its checkpoint, and requires that record and every then-existing mapping revision to remain byte-identical in every descendant candidate; the staged current wave may only append the declared next revisions. Aggregate receipts bind every per-wave predecessor/current root. Removing a prior revision, rewriting an earlier checkpoint, restoring revision 1 after revision 2, touch-then-revert, or presenting a valid current chain that is not an append-only extension of Git-reconstructed history fails.

`realizedPlannedFixtureEdges` is an outer-envelope field, never a nested-member field. It is sorted/unique by `(fixtureID, requiredSliceID)` and is non-empty only on the fixture's exact primary `realization_slice_id` envelope. That primary envelope contains exactly one edge for every Cartesian pair `(fixture_id, required_slice_id)` planned for fixtures realized in the current wave. Each closed edge repeats the planned `realizationWave` and `suiteID`; its sorted `requiredExtensionIDs` must equal the **complete** nested `extensions[].extensionID` set of the named current-wave ExtensionSlice or earlier-wave ExtensionSlice record in the same cumulative candidate snapshot—never a caller-selected subset. The manifest intentionally carries no future child-receipt digest: static current/earlier-wave records are validated before Swift execution. As the runner reruns each active wave, it cross-joins the edge's exact suite/fixture/member expectations with the current invocation's unique child receipt, retains that sanitized receipt by `(wave,suiteID)`, and embeds it in the aggregate receipt. The Owner Ledger checker validates only the static edge/slice/member closure; it never claims access to temporary child receipts. The runner requires global exact equality with the ledger-planned edge set, validates every referenced extension against the named singular-owner envelope, and rejects an empty, subset, extra, duplicate, foreign, wrong-wave, wrong-owner, wrong-member, wrong-suite, or receipt-drift edge. Unrelated and non-primary envelopes carry an empty edge array. W0 is the explicit exception: it has no E/A slice, realizes zero amendment fixtures/edges, and its four freeze suites are bound directly by the W0 manifest.

Each storage/replay/recovery proof is a closed tagged union. `present` requires a non-empty sorted exact stage-0 path set and domain-separated proof digest; `notApplicable` requires an empty path set, a decision/symbol-specific reason from a checker constant, and a digest over the scoped owning task plus repository history. Generic `N/A`, an unknown reason, an empty present proof, or a transient value claiming storage fails. Arrays are canonically sorted, paths are unique mode-`100644` stage-0 candidate blobs, `introductionWave` equals the decision inventory and cannot move later/earlier, and `ownerBefore == ownerAfter == envelope.existingOwnerID`. Every nested member separately proves `writerBefore == writerAfter`; only the same controlled merge may explicitly retire that member's old writer and prove one successor under the unchanged owner. A missing/empty envelope, path/symbol/version/canonicalization/suite/fixture disposition/proof fails. `currentFixturePaths` contains at least one canonical current vector. `backwardFixture.notApplicable` is legal only when no historical wire version exists and its proof binds repository history; it cannot create a synthetic backward fixture. `futureFixturePaths` contains at least one unknown-version/unknown-tag rejection vector. Each active wave's `schema_fixtures` count is the union of all current/backward-present/future fixture paths referenced by every nested extension and therefore cannot be zero when `ea_extensions` is present. Every current realized fixture/case must map to at least one concrete schema-fixture path and one non-zero child-test execution. `candidateTree` is deliberately not serialized inside an envelope, extension, or ledger record: the owner checker computes the enclosing stage-0 index tree after reading all entry blobs and emits it beside the domain-separated record digests, eliminating a self-referential tree hash.

Canonical array order is closed rather than implied by “sorted.” `wave_candidate_manifests` uses `(waveOrderIndex,candidateClassOrderIndex)`; M `entries` use candidate path, E/A `entries` use `sliceID`, and schema-fixture `entries` use `(extensionID,path,fixtureRole)`. `extensions` uses `extensionID`; `realizedPlannedFixtureIDs`, suite IDs, path arrays (including legacy/current mapping fixture paths), allowed amendment IDs, allowed reason codes, and physical source IDs use unsigned UTF-8 bytes; `realizedPlannedFixtureEdges` uses `(fixtureID,requiredSliceID)`; `oracleRowRealizations` uses `(rowScopeSchemaID,mappingRevision)`; `oracleMappingChainCheckpoint.appendedMappings` uses `(rowScopeSchemaID,mappingRevision,mappingDigest)`; `physicalSources` uses `physicalSourceID`; each mapping's key/state fields preserve the registry's declared field order; `physicalInputs` uses `(physicalSourceID,recordPath,fieldPath)`; production-trace causal-parent ordinals and each receipt's event subsequence are ascending unique UInt64; `mapping_projection_receipts` uses `(projection_kind,row_scope_schema_id,current_mapping_core_digest)` and nested paired backward observations use `case_id`; child `listed_identifiers` and `runs` use `test_identifier`; aggregate children use `suite_id`; schema fixtures use `state_fixture_id`; outcomes/case bindings use `case_id`; nested `row_key_bindings` use `(row_role,row_scope_schema_id,key_digest)`; nested `caseFacts` use `(fact_id,canonicalFactValueBytes)` with unique `fact_id`; row invariants use `invariant_id`; and Oracle aggregate rows/deltas use `(rowScopeSchemaID,keyDigest)`. Declaration-order arrays—wave order, descriptor fields, enum/tag variants, production-trace events, failure-stage precedence, command phases, the six case-input schemas, six relation laws, and six controlled amendments—retain their explicitly listed order and must not be lexically resorted. Mutation tests transpose every adjacent pair in each array class while leaving object-key canonicalization intact and require rejection.

#### Canonical cross-plan value flow

```text
ModelMaterialManifestArtifactID
  -> exact selected variant digest
  -> BASSpecializationKey(material + variant + immutable URL + device/OS/runtime + options + namespace)
  -> BASCacheHandleReceipt(post-specialization policy/bookmark/state; bookmark is not identity)
  -> CertifiedBackendProfile(exact SKU/OS/path/options/evidence/verdict)
  -> BASExecutionPlan(exact independently certified arm)
  -> K1 reservation/HeavyPhase
  -> K2 immutable pin
  -> K3 allocation/claim/use lineage
```

```text
BASContextCapsule.attemptFrame
  = root + snapshot + generation vector + task + budget lease ref + grant ref

BASContextCapsule.providerStep
  = reopened K3 allocation receipt
  + one canonical BASProviderExecutionRef
  + exact role-specific R5 reservoir/grounding request OR R6 descriptor
  + exact BASExecutionPlan/profile/policy/receipt references
```

No optional all-fields capsule, predicted future ID, copied remaining budget, raw CoT, ambient tool/database handle, or cross-window mutable prompt is permitted.

```text
BASDelegationProposal(proposalID + digest)
  -> complete matching-unoccupied slot set from one exact K3 allocation head
  -> first slot by canonical (slotOrdinal, slotID)
  -> one allocation-head CAS binds proposalID/digest -> slotID
  -> identical replay reopens; changed bytes are corruption; CAS loser reopens/recomputes
```

```text
ConversationWorkspace (stable visible identity)
  -> Mission / Objective / WorkUnit
  -> exactly one active bounded Attempt
  -> ContextContinuityManifest + exact owner rows/receipts
  -> strict-quiescent K3 rebase_pending
  -> K4 reserved-inactive successor grant
  -> one K3 active-head CAS + BASAttemptRebaseReceipt
  -> K4 activation or terminal denial / activationPending
```

Process/session loss never serializes or resurrects Swift tasks, stacks, mailboxes, credentials, Provider sessions, KV/SSM, or unresolved call permits. Same-boot volatile proof may narrow continuation only for the same live owner/boundary; cross-boot recovery reopens durable owner truth and otherwise starts a separately admitted WorkUnit after every predecessor boundary is terminal.

```text
L8 hard-eligible capability metadata
  -> L7 selects exact owner/schema/purpose
  -> L3 compiles compact projection
  -> optional full-schema request ends the current internal step
  -> value-only proposal fills a predeclared slot
  -> fresh L7/L11/L14 decision -> fresh L3 compile -> L2 plan -> K1 -> K3 allocation
```

There is no in-place context expansion, ToolCatalog, SkillRegistry, or disclosure manager. Provider accounting is two-stage: L3 emits one pure canonical `BASContextAccountingRequest`; the exact Provider package returns an immutable receipt; L3 reopens it before final packing. A missing or mismatched receipt makes that profile ineligible rather than falling back to a global token cap.

```text
W6 phase 1: pure prepare every frozen member
W6 phase 2: materialize/reopen every invocation + all admission/budget/conflict receipts
W6 phase 3: per-member fresh currentness recheck + exact local claim OR remote K3/K4/K3 handoff
```

Any phase-1/2 mismatch starts zero mechanisms. Completion-order events are progress only; required results join in frozen source order. Effect/publication siblings never enter the Provider task group. An indeterminate member remains nonterminal and blocks its required join.

```text
InputEvent(eventID + exact Workspace/WorkUnit/Attempt/generation/head + digest)
  -> semantic/admission/cancellation/rebase disposition candidate
  -> runtime.turn-operation binds exact receipts
  -> K3 expected-head/eventID/digest CAS
  -> exactly one acceptedCurrent | staleRejected | relocalizedAccepted |
     cancelRequested | durablyDeferred | strictRebaseRequested |
     independentWorkUnitAccepted | denied fact
```

```text
ΩE entryKey = canonical(sourceOwnerID, sourceProtocolVersion, logicalSlotID)
  -> strictly increasing, globally unique entries
  -> exactly one exhaustive present | absent sourceState per key
  -> present = expected head + source-content Artifact + snapshot/HWM + complete generation-vector
     + policy/deletion/revocation/key epochs + authority/visibility/erasure scopes
  -> absent = sealed owner/protocol absence proof + snapshot/HWM + complete generation-vector
     + the same epochs/scopes + typed .notPresent head/content tags
  -> target = exact owner/protocol/slot + expected-head-or-absent tagged variant
     + candidate Artifact/digest + purpose/operation + readSetRoot
  -> L10 -> L11 -> L13 -> K3 stage -> L14 -> K4 seal -> K3 activation/append
```

The ΩE wire fixes tagged field order. Omission, `nil`, empty bytes, zero digests, defaults, path-only evidence, caller-selected subsets, two variants for one key, a present entry carrying an absence proof, or an absent entry without both typed `.notPresent` tags is invalid before root calculation. Tests mutate every present/absent field, target expected-head-or-absent tag, tuple order, generation/epoch/scope, and mapped owner; each mutation fails before K3 staging. Non-runtime code/weight/schema/prompt/skill/policy candidates stop at `runtime.certification` and `production.cutover`; they cannot use runtime-state adoption.

For live output, each source identity is the tagged value `{sourceOwnerID, sourceStreamID, scope, cursorProtocolVersion}`; Attempt scope exhaustively binds Workspace/WorkUnit/Attempt/generation, while process/device/Provider/effect/publication scopes use their own closed tagged payload. A delivery-gap projection binds that identity, last acknowledged cursor, frozen replay HWM, exact source head/root/range at synthesis, and skipped extent as `known(count,range)|unavailable(reasonCode,sourceOwnerProofDigest)`. The unavailable variant is legal only when the exact source owner proves that count/range cannot be known; an observer cannot downgrade a knowable extent. `runtime.turn-operation` derives `expectedSourceManifest` from the frozen DAG and exact K3 branch/boundary, Provider containment, response/visibility, effect, and publication topology. Every expected source is exactly one of `present(cursor,HWM)`, `provedAbsent(exact source-owner proof)`, owner-contract `notApplicable`, or `unavailable/quarantined(reason,lastProvableCursor)`. There is no synthetic global cursor, recoverable global head, or cross-domain total order.

The closed subscriber state machine is exactly `live(identity,subscriberEpoch,lastDeliveredCursor,lastAcknowledgedCursor,stateRevision) | detached(identity,subscriberEpoch,gap,signal: queuedControl|outOfBand,stateRevision) | replaying(identity,subscriberEpoch,gap,replayCursor,lastAcknowledgedCursor,stateRevision)`. From `live`, a replay-required event that cannot enter the bounded data queue is never overwritten: reserved control capacity yields `detached(...queuedControl)`; if control capacity is also full, `runtime.turn-operation` atomically records `detached(...outOfBand)`. Either detached state rejects every later live event. Poll/resubscribe may only reopen or synthesize the same source-bound gap and enter `replaying`. Reattachment to `live` is legal iff replay is acknowledged through the frozen per-source HWM and the next live cursor is its exact successor. Identity/scope/protocol/head-range drift during replay creates a new explicit gap and remains incomplete. Coalescing may replace telemetry samples only when it preserves the exact gap/count/range marker. A missing source, forged/omitted gap, detach/reattach discontinuity, unbounded queue, or cross-scope cursor/HWM substitution marks the projection incomplete: authorized partial/gap UI may render, but command, effect, publication, and response completion remain unclaimed. Adapter/IO cannot choose the expected set.

Acknowledgement is one exact source-state CAS, not a caller-written cursor. The request binds the complete source identity, current `subscriberEpoch`, expected `stateRevision`, and the exact next cursor in the delivered-but-unacknowledged contiguous prefix; the owner recomputes that cursor from its delivery row and requires it to be `<= lastDeliveredCursor`. A winning CAS advances `lastAcknowledgedCursor` by exactly one and increments the revision. A byte-identical replay of that winning request is an idempotent no-op returning the same receipt. Stale/duplicate-with-different-bytes, skipped/future/never-delivered, cross-source/scope/protocol, wrong-epoch, or wrong-revision acknowledgements are rejected without cursor/state/completion change. Acknowledgement and detach linearize on the same source-state CAS: if detach wins, a late acknowledgement cannot erase or narrow the gap; if acknowledgement wins, a later proved overflow detaches from the advanced acknowledged cursor and cannot be suppressed. No acknowledgement may advance command/effect/publication/response completion unless the entire expected-source manifest is independently complete.

`BASSourceDeliveryContractTests` and `BASLiveDeliveryManifestTests` have distinct typed fixtures and non-zero matched counts for normal in-order enqueue plus acknowledgement remaining `live` and advancing exactly one source-local `lastAcknowledgedCursor`; byte-identical acknowledgement replay; stale/different-byte duplicate, skipped, future, never-delivered, cross-source/scope/protocol, wrong-epoch, and wrong-revision acknowledgements; both deterministic ack-vs-detach schedules with exact final cursor/gap/revision and zero false-completion oracles; queued-control detach; out-of-band detach; an overflow/late-event race in which the out-of-band detach linearizes first and the late event's mechanism-start count is exactly zero; late-live rejection; same-gap resubscribe; replay-through-HWM plus successor reattach; early/skip reattach rejection; replay drift creating a second gap; marker-preserving telemetry coalescing; missing expected source; omitted/forged skipped extent; knowable extent downgraded to unavailable; source-A cursor/HWM/head under source B; duplicate source; forbidden global cursor; authorized partial rendering; and proof that every incomplete case blocks command/effect/publication/response completion. The normal-live, acknowledgement, and race fixtures assert exact state/cursor/gap/revision/start-count oracles, so a degenerate implementation that always detaches, trusts an arbitrary future acknowledgement, clears a winning gap, or merely drops the late event cannot pass.

```python
def test_existing_owners_cover_material_revoke_publication_and_cutover(self) -> None:
    ledger = json.loads(
        (ROOT / "docs/superpowers/specs/qinao-owner-ledger-v1.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    owners = {item["owner_id"]: item for item in ledger["owners"]}
    required = {
        "model.manifest-invocation": "ModelMaterialManifestArtifactID",
        "execution.plan-provider-router": "sourceSpecialization|AOT",
        "provider.package-boundary": "CacheHandleReceipt",
        "state.k3-control-nucleus": "RevocationFenceReceipt",
        "sovereign.k4-durable-lifecycle": "minimum material authorization epoch",
        "release.spool-publication": "journal finalize",
        "effect.zone-c-saga": "one boundary anchor",
        "runtime.certification": "immutable E5",
        "production.cutover": "Join 2",
        "runtime.turn-operation": "BASDelegationProposal",
        "semantics.layercell": "HumanFitProjection",
        "runtime.semantic-dag": "stable slotID",
        "state.snapshot-contracts": "ContextContinuityManifest",
        "state.context-compiler": "BASContextAccountingRequest",
        "cache.scope": "BASSpecializationKey",
        "state.memory-content-erasure": "encrypted content-addressed artifact",
        "runtime.semantic-executor": "already authorized frozen DAG",
    }
    positive_owner_fields = (
        "domain",
        "authority_owner",
        "mutable_state_owner",
        "storage_owner",
        "recovery_owner",
        "retirement_gate",
    )
    for owner_id, term in required.items():
        card = {field: owners[owner_id][field] for field in positive_owner_fields}
        self.assertIn(term, json.dumps(card, ensure_ascii=False))
        self.assertNotIn(
            term,
            json.dumps(owners[owner_id]["forbidden"], ensure_ascii=False),
            f"{owner_id} launders positive ownership through a prohibition",
        )

def test_k3_companion_contains_all_six_approved_amendments(self) -> None:
    expected_ids = (
        "qinao.k3.strict-quiescent-inherited-budget-rebase.v1",
        "qinao.k3.provider-recovery-finite-ordinals.v1",
        "qinao.k3.inprocess-owner-loss-same-boot.v1",
        "qinao.k3.pcc-pre-entry-marker.v1",
        "qinao.k3.max-cost-no-downward-credit.v1",
        "qinao.k3.profile-lease-token-bounds.v1",
    )
    observed = load_and_validate_k3_amendment_manifest(ROOT)
    self.assertEqual(tuple(item.amendment_id for item in observed), expected_ids)
    self.assertTrue(all(item.slice_ids for item in observed))
    self.assertTrue(all(item.planned_fixtures for item in observed))
    self.assertEqual(sum(len(item.planned_fixtures) for item in observed), 86)
    self.assertEqual(
        sum(len(fixture.cases) for item in observed for fixture in item.planned_fixtures),
        165,
    )
    for item in observed:
        fixtures = item.planned_fixtures
        self.assertEqual(item.oracle_canonicalization_id, "qinao-k3-oracle-outcome-json-v1")
        self.assertTrue(item.state_fixture_entries_closed)
        self.assertTrue(item.state_fixture_digests_match)
        self.assertTrue(item.outcome_state_references_match)
        self.assertTrue(item.oracle_row_scope_registry_matches)
        self.assertTrue(item.oracle_cell_schema_root_matches)
        self.assertTrue(item.oracle_case_binding_root_matches)
        self.assertTrue(item.state_fixture_catalog_root_matches)
        self.assertTrue(item.oracle_catalog_root_matches)
        self.assertEqual(
            {fixture.fixture_class for fixture in fixtures},
            {"acceptingExpected", "rejecting"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            tuple(fixture.fixture_id for fixture in fixtures),
            tuple(sorted(fixture.fixture_id for fixture in fixtures)),
        )
        for fixture in fixtures:
            self.assertIn(fixture.realization_slice_id, fixture.required_slice_ids)
            self.assertTrue(fixture.suite_id)
            self.assertTrue(fixture.cases)
            for case in fixture.cases:
                self.assertEqual(case.expected_oracle_id, case.case_id + ".oracle.v1")
                self.assertEqual(len(case.expected_outcome_digest), 64)
                self.assertTrue(case.expected_oracle_matches)
    self.assertTrue(all(item.section_digest_matches for item in observed))

def test_external_agent_sources_are_exact_design_evidence_not_runtime_dependencies(self) -> None:
    exact_pins = (
        "OpenAI Codex 0fb559f0f6e231a88ac02ea002d3ecd248e2b515",
        "Pi 8dc78834cde4e329284cf505f9e3f99763df5529",
        "Hermes Agent 36f2a966c7f9f69987494b867c3dcf96b69a5766",
        "Claude Agent SDK Python 2d4ef9466427970a6db5e567253bae2b2825010a",
    )
    for path in (ARCHITECTURE, MASTER, RUNTIME):
        contents = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for exact_pin in exact_pins:
            self.assertIn(exact_pin, contents, str(path))
        self.assertIn("design evidence only", contents, str(path))
    controlled = "\n".join(
        path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for path in (ARCHITECTURE, MASTER, CONTRACTS, SEMANTIC, SILICON, SOVEREIGN, RUNTIME)
    )
    for forbidden in (
        "import pi-agent",
        "import hermes-agent",
        "import claude-agent-sdk",
        "node_modules/@openai/codex",
    ):
        self.assertNotIn(forbidden, controlled)

def test_w4_w6_executor_delivery_and_retirement_boundaries_are_exact(self) -> None:
    silicon = SILICON.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    runtime = RUNTIME.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for term in (
        "single-Provider-step pure prepare/currentness-recheck hooks",
        "no all-member batch implementation in W4",
        "incumbent high-level Agent",
    ):
        self.assertIn(term, silicon)
    for term in (
        "two all-member barriers",
        "per-member start gate",
        "completion-order events are non-authoritative",
        "frozen source order",
        "expectedSourceManifest",
        "unavailable/quarantined",
        "canonical incumbent-retirement manifest",
    ):
        self.assertIn(term, runtime)
    self.assertLess(
        runtime.index("canonical incumbent-retirement manifest"),
        runtime.index("mechanical semantic-DAG executor activates"),
    )

def test_omega_read_set_and_ablation_are_canonical_and_non_authoritative(self) -> None:
    semantic = SEMANTIC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    runtime = RUNTIME.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    controlled = semantic + runtime
    for term in (
        "(sourceOwnerID, sourceProtocolVersion, logicalSlotID)",
        "strictly increasing bytewise order",
        "sourceState",
        ".notPresent",
        "readSetRoot",
        "mechanism-alone",
        "pre-registered pairwise/high-risk interactions",
    ):
        self.assertIn(term, controlled)
    self.assertIn("runtime.certification", controlled)
    self.assertIn("production.cutover", controlled)
    self.assertIn("executor cannot select proposal adoption", runtime)

def test_named_40_30_tier_is_conditional_not_architecture_completion(self) -> None:
    for contents in (
        ARCHITECTURE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
        MASTER.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
        RUNTIME.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
    ):
        self.assertIn("cold40", contents)
        self.assertIn("sustained30", contents)
        self.assertIn("product or marketing surface explicitly claims or selects", contents)
        self.assertIn("ordinary selection", contents)
        self.assertIn("base production release can be complete", contents)

def test_k4_proof_has_an_honest_producer_and_semantic_entitlement_comparison(self) -> None:
    sovereign = SOVEREIGN.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for term in (
        "approved release-build allowlist",
        "proof-production runbook",
        "exact seven-key Enhanced Security projection",
        "signed team and application identifiers are public binary identity",
        "raw device UDID never enters repository evidence",
    ):
        self.assertIn(term, sovereign)
    self.assertNotIn(
        'cmp "$EVIDENCE/host-signed-entitlements.plist"',
        sovereign,
    )
    self.assertNotIn(
        'cmp "$EVIDENCE/extension-signed-entitlements.plist"',
        sovereign,
    )
```

Each selected heading must resolve exactly once **inside its source document**, and the scoped body ends at the next heading of equal or shallower depth. Scope IDs are globally unique and selectors are unique within one record; intentional cross-record selector reuse is legal and required where orthogonal decisions share an owning task. For every witness attached to a scope, each of that decision's terms must occur inside at least one selector of that same scope; a decision cannot obtain coverage from unrelated scope-level terms or another scope. The selector/decision, scope-term, and decision-witness tables are generated golden projections from the same constants and tests require byte-for-byte equality. The test stores these strings directly rather than scraping this plan at runtime. Its fail-closed Markdown section scanner admits visible normative heading, paragraph, list-item, and table-cell text plus their inline-code children. Required scope/witness terms found only inside an HTML comment, fenced/indented code block, example-only blockquote, link destination, or hidden metadata do not count; forbidden terms are scanned over the complete raw bounded section so hiding a prohibition cannot make it disappear. Mutations move each witness to a comment, code fence, or example block; attach one decision to the wrong scope; replace its witness with another decision's term; duplicate/omit a decision; and must fail. The 36 decision IDs occur exactly once across the sixteen non-attribution trace records; cross-wave/owner phases remain exhaustively governed by `CANONICAL_DECISION_PHASES_V1`, not by duplicating a weak trace label.

The test also asserts every suite in `scripts/qinao_wave_gates_v1.json` is either already named in its owning 2026-07-15 domain task or appears in the exact future-suite table added by Step 5. This prevents the manifest from inventing a suite that no implementation step creates.
