E4 reopens its content-derived exact ID/bytes/digest. No new owner, writer, generic policy store/registry, or bare-Codable put is legal. Code contains no default policy, threshold, sample count, or metric fallback. Missing/self-reviewed/expired policy, weak/zero threshold, missing metric/guardrail, unknown cohort/profile, post-signature change, or a policy chosen after observations blocks E4. `BASDeploymentJoinTests` mutate every numeric/identity field and require a new independently signed policy rather than reinterpretation.

Immutable E5 is a closed `E5IdentityV1` record, not a free-form report. It contains exactly `sourceCandidateTree`, `releasePayloadDigest`, `runtimeImageDigest`, `buildAttestationDigest`, `canaryReleaseTree`, `cohortDigest`, `authorityMode`, `evaluatedExecutorID`, `authoritativeExecutorID`, `executorTopologyDigest`, `executorABIDigest`, `executorMaterialDigest`, `executorProfileDigest`, `incumbentRetirement = present(canaryManifestDigest) | provedAbsent(proofDigest)`, `E4ID`, `sourceMechanismAblationSeal = {artifactID,digest,verdict}`, `observationPolicyArtifactID`, `observationPolicyDigest`, `observationWindow`, `observationExpiresAt`, `certificationEpoch`, `policyEpoch`, `securityEpoch`, `revocationEpoch`, `knownIssueSetDigest`, `incidentSetDigest`, `evidenceRoot`, and `expansionVerdict = eligible | ineligible(reasonDigest) | indeterminate(reasonDigest)`. `releasePayloadDigest` covers every executable, linked image, resource, entitlement, runtime configuration, and model/material reference while excluding only the separately sealed cohort/deployment/retirement manifests. `authorityMode` is exactly `incumbentAuthoritative_newExecutorShadow | namedExecutorAuthoritative`. The first variant requires the incumbent as `authoritativeExecutorID`, the distinct tested new executor as `evaluatedExecutorID`, and a proved-absent retirement manifest. The second requires `evaluatedExecutorID == authoritativeExecutorID` and binds the canary tree's cohort-scoped incumbent-retirement manifest digest.

E4 reopens the same evaluated executor ID, the exact signed expansion-policy Artifact, and an already `eligible` current `SourceMechanismAblationSealV1`; it freezes both ID/digest pairs before deployment. The sealed canary tree and Join 1 receipt carry them byte-identically. Runtime Task 7 unit 4 may execute the expansion policy but cannot choose, relax, or replace it after seeing observations. E4, canary tree, Join 1, build attestation, runtime image, observations, and E5 must project the same source/payload/topology/ABI/material/profile/policy/ablation identities byte-for-byte. `runtime.certification` alone computes `expansionVerdict` under the separately preregistered expansion policy: only complete, current, statistically sufficient, non-regressing evidence with every quality/security/privacy/recovery/thermal guardrail passing and no open incident is `eligible`; missing, stale, underpowered, conflicting, epoch-drifting, or open-incident evidence is `indeterminate`, while a proved regression/guardrail failure is `ineligible`. This verdict remains distinct from the ablation verdict, but E5 is invalid unless the embedded ablation seal independently remains `eligible`; a post-observation policy/seal change invalidates rather than reinterprets E5.

Join 2 accepts only an E5 whose `expansionVerdict` **and** reopened `sourceMechanismAblationSeal.verdict` are both exactly `eligible`. It then adds `fullReleaseTree`, a strict-superset `fullCohortDigest`, the cohort-expansion proof, and `fullRetirement = present(fullManifestDigest, retirementExpansionProof) | provedAbsent(proofDigest)`. Before joining, `production.cutover` atomically reopens the expansion-policy Artifact, ablation-seal Artifact, current time, seal/policy observation expiry, baseline/case-set/workload/cohort/profile identities, certification/policy/security/revocation epochs, and known-issue/incident digests. Expiry, ID/digest mismatch, or any identity/epoch drift converts eligibility to indeterminate and requires a new ablation seal plus canary/E5 rather than replaying the old evidence. `ReleaseTreeDeltaV1` proves that canary→full changes only the exact cohort/deployment manifest and, for an already authoritative new executor, the full incumbent-retirement manifest; every executable/source/resource/entitlement/runtime-configuration/model payload remains bound to the byte-identical `sourceCandidateTree`, `releasePayloadDigest`, `runtimeImageDigest`, and `buildAttestationDigest`. The full tree's sealed projection of `authorityMode`, `evaluatedExecutorID`, `authoritativeExecutorID`, executor topology/ABI/material/profile digests, observation-policy Artifact/digest, ablation-seal Artifact/digest/verdict, epochs/known-issue/incident identities, and E4/E5 evidence lineage is byte-identical to E5; both observation windows and evidence roots remain immutable. `cohortExpansionProof` enumerates every newly added member and proves the same sealed ProductType/SKU/SoC/memory/OS/runtime/region/policy/profile eligibility used by E5; a member outside that closed partition requires a distinct E4/canary/E5 chain and cannot be hidden inside a set-theoretic superset. If E5 retirement is `provedAbsent`, full retirement must be the same proved-absent value. If E5 retirement is present, the full digest is intentionally distinct and `retirementExpansionProof` must prove a strict canary-cohort→full-cohort expansion under the same incumbent set, successor executor, policy/root, and source/payload identity. A shadow/incumbent-authoritative E5 may expand only that same incumbent-authoritative topology. Full production authority for the new executor is legal only when that exact evaluated executor was already authoritative in the canary E5 after the canonical cohort retirement. `BASDeploymentJoinTests` positively cover both incumbent-authoritative shadow expansion and same-executor authoritative retirement expansion, and reject expansion or ablation ineligible/indeterminate/missing verdicts; missing or ID-equal/digest-different or digest-equal/ID-different policy/seal; stale seal/policy evidence; baseline/case-set/workload/cohort/profile drift; expired E5; post-E5 revocation, policy/security/certification epoch drift, known-issue change, or opened incident; shadow-to-authoritative promotion; evaluated/authoritative executor substitution; topology/ABI/material/profile drift; missing/non-strict/wrong-root retirement expansion; absent-to-present retirement substitution; non-superset or incomparable cohort; a strict superset containing one ineligible or wrong-partition member; reused one-tree joins; E4/E5 evidence-lineage drift; and any non-allowlisted source, executable, resource, entitlement, or runtime-configuration delta.

Each unit has its own RED/GREEN evidence and commit. A Release tree can contain at most one semantic/adoption scheduler: it either keeps the incumbent authoritative with the new executor shadowed, or carries the exact retirement manifest that makes the new executor the sole authority for the named cohort. Neither join may share a commit with the other. Rollback names and deploys another independently certified sealed one-authority tree. The status-document commit remains after the cutover and cannot mutate the certified production tree.

```bash
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"

: "${QINAO_TASK4_GREEN_BUNDLE:?Task-4 GREEN bundle}"

T4_RED_REVIEW="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task4-red-review.json"

T4_GREEN_REVIEW="$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/results/task4-green-review.json"

  --bundle-directory "$QINAO_TASK4_RED_BUNDLE" --bundle-manifest "$QINAO_TASK4_RED_BUNDLE/candidate-bundle-manifest-v1.json" \
  --review-directory "$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/review/task4-red" --result-receipt "$T4_RED_MAT"
"$GATE" candidate-review --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/task4-red-review" --candidate-unit "$QINAO_TASK4_RED_UNIT" \
  --materialization-receipt "$T4_RED_MAT" --validation-contract-registry "$QINAO_VALIDATION_CONTRACT_REGISTRY" \
  --expected-outcome exact-red --result-receipt "$T4_RED_REVIEW"
"$GATE" candidate-materialize --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/task4-green-materialize" --candidate-unit "$QINAO_TASK4_GREEN_UNIT" \
  --bundle-directory "$QINAO_TASK4_GREEN_BUNDLE" --bundle-manifest "$QINAO_TASK4_GREEN_BUNDLE/candidate-bundle-manifest-v1.json" \
  --review-directory "$QINAO_W0_RUN_DIR/review/task4-green" --result-receipt "$T4_GREEN_MAT"
"$GATE" candidate-review --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/task4-green-review" --candidate-unit "$QINAO_TASK4_GREEN_UNIT" \
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add regression tests for every demonstrated checker bypass**

In `scripts/test_check_k4_platform_proof.py`, first extend `make_proof` to emit the new bound artifacts/status fields listed in Step 5, using only deterministic synthetic fixture values, then add these tests before changing the checker:

```python
def setUp(self) -> None:
    self.temporary_directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
    self.addCleanup(self.temporary_directory.cleanup)
    self.root = Path(self.temporary_directory.name)

def load_json(self, path: Path) -> dict:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

def load_status(self, evidence: Path) -> dict:
    return self.load_json(evidence / "status.json")

def write_status(self, evidence: Path, value: dict) -> None:
    (evidence / "status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

def rewrite_json_artifact(self, evidence: Path, name: str, value: dict) -> None:
    self.rewrite_artifact(
        evidence,
        name,
        (json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode("utf-8"),
    )

def test_real_beta_xcode_cannot_self_label_as_release(self) -> None:
    evidence, team_id = self.make_proof(self.root)
    self.rewrite_artifact(
        evidence,
        "xcode-version.txt",
        b"Xcode 27.0\nBuild version 27A5194q\n",
    )
    status = self.load_status(evidence)
    status["toolchain"].update({
        "xcode_version": "27.0",
        "xcode_build": "27A5194q",
        "release_channel": "release",
    })
    self.write_status(evidence, status)
    self.assertIn("approved release-build allowlist", "\n".join(
        checker.validate_platform_proof(self.root, evidence, expected_team_id=team_id)
    ))

def test_zero_or_single_profile_commitment_cannot_pass(self) -> None:
    evidence, team_id = self.make_proof(self.root)
    status = self.load_status(evidence)
    status["signing"]["host_profile_uuid_sha256"] = "0" * 64
    status["signing"].pop("extension_profile_uuid_sha256")
    self.write_status(evidence, status)
    errors = checker.validate_platform_proof(self.root, evidence, expected_team_id=team_id)
    self.assertTrue(any("host and extension profile" in item for item in errors), errors)

def test_fail_and_pass_or_duplicate_runtime_events_are_rejected(self) -> None:
    evidence, team_id = self.make_proof(self.root)
    self.rewrite_artifact(
        evidence,
        "runtime-seed-events.jsonl",
        b'{"event":"fail","error":"send_failed"}\n{"event":"pass"}\n',
    )
    errors = checker.validate_platform_proof(self.root, evidence, expected_team_id=team_id)
    self.assertTrue(any("contradictory or duplicate runtime event" in item for item in errors), errors)

def test_runtime_trace_must_be_probe_written_and_device_copied(self) -> None:
    evidence, team_id = self.make_proof(self.root)
    attestation = self.load_json(evidence / "runtime-trace-attestation.json")
    attestation["source"] = "host_postprocessed_log"
    self.rewrite_json_artifact(evidence, "runtime-trace-attestation.json", attestation)
    errors = checker.validate_platform_proof(self.root, evidence, expected_team_id=team_id)
    self.assertTrue(any("device app container" in item for item in errors), errors)
```

Also add mutation tests for: presence of `BetaVersion.plist`; Xcode or device-OS build absent from policy; Xcode/Swift/SDK/signature/version-plist mismatch; fake PATH tool; identical host/extension profile commitment; signer leaf absent from its profile; wrong development class/team/App ID; omitted or decoder-defaulted trusted-clock keys; a one-null/one-non-null trusted-clock pair; a trusted-clock object or raw SHA that differs from the Task-0 anchor; raw UDID/workspace leakage; simulator/device-family spoof; install JSON failure; wrong/missing launch persistent identifier; stale run/source/build identity; launch/terminate/relaunch/copy failure; either phase monitor count other than one; unchanged helper PID; out-of-order/missing/repeated seed/reopen events; non-WAL/non-FULL pragma; missing commit/reopen/wrong HWM; host-access success; retained DB/WAL/SHM; non-zero pending/unexpected count; and a signed extension whose seven-key capability projection differs from the release template.

Create `docs/superpowers/specs/qinao-apple27-release-platform-policy-v1.json` with exact initial bytes:

```json

  ],

  "operation_contract_registry_root": null,
  "policy_authorization": {"payload": [], "tag": "awaitingExternalSignature"},
  "w0_capture_producers": [],
```

Empty allowlists, `expected_team_id: null`, `k4_trusted_clock_policy: null`, `k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256: null`, `awaitingExternalReview`, and `awaitingExternalSignature` are intentional fail-closed state, not missing values; this initial policy cannot authorize capture. The top-level key set is exactly the one shown above in both awaiting and final states: both trusted-clock keys are mandatory, `null` is legal only while awaiting review, and no decoder may omit, inject, or default either key. The final policy is byte-closed by these exact member schemas, with no additional/defaulted keys:

- `approved_platform_pairs` contains exactly one `K4ApprovedPlatformPairV1 = {xcode_version,xcode_build,xcode_official_apple_url,xcode_source_sha256,ios_version,ios_build,ios_official_apple_url,ios_source_sha256,effective_from_utc,effective_until_utc,reviewed_at_utc}`. URLs are immutable HTTPS Apple origins; both digests are 64 lowercase hex; builds are public releases and not in `denied_prerelease_builds`; `effective_from_utc <= reviewed_at_utc < effective_until_utc`.
- `w0_capture_producers` contains exactly one `K4CaptureProducerPolicyV1 = {producer_key_id,producer_public_key_sha256,producer_principal_id,producer_role:"k4-capture-producer",producer_epoch,custody_principal_id,authorization_usage:"k4-w0-capture-authorization-only",operation_contract_registry_root,not_before_utc,not_after_utc,revocation_state}`. Its key/principal/epoch and public-key digest exactly match one active Task-0 trust-root entry; its registry root equals the top-level policy field; policy does not introduce a new key.
- `w0_evidence_attesters` contains exactly one `K4EvidenceAttesterPolicyV1 = {attester_key_id,attester_principal_id,attester_role:"k4-evidence-attester",attester_epoch,attestation_signing_identity,artifact_envelope_identity}`. `attestation_signing_identity` is exactly `{certificate_der_sha256,public_key_algorithm:"p256",public_key_sha256,leaf_fingerprint_sha256,chain_fingerprints_sha256,algorithm:"cms-signeddata-sha256-object-signer-v1",digest_algorithm_oid:"2.16.840.1.101.3.4.2.1",signature_algorithm_oid:"1.2.840.10045.4.3.2",certificate_usage:6,key_usage:["digitalSignature"],extended_key_usage_oids,not_before_utc,not_after_utc,custody_principal_id,keychain_acl_requirement_sha256,revocation_state}`. `artifact_envelope_identity` is exactly `{recipient_key_id,certificate_der_sha256,public_key_sha256,leaf_fingerprint_sha256,chain_fingerprints_sha256,algorithm:"cms-envelopeddata-recipient-v1",certificate_usage:5,key_usage:["keyEncipherment"],extended_key_usage_oids,not_before_utc,not_after_utc,encryption_custody_principal_id,decryption_custody_principal_id,keychain_acl_requirement_sha256,revocation_state}`. Chain arrays are non-empty, leaf-to-anchor ordered, and unique. EKU arrays are unsigned-UTF-8 sorted/unique dotted OIDs. Signing and envelope public keys, certificate fingerprints, key IDs, and custody principals are pairwise distinct from each other, the producer, platform reviewer, and product-signing leaves.
- `artifact_retention_policy` becomes exactly `{"tag":"approved","payload":[K4ArtifactRetentionPolicyV1]}` where the one payload object is `{retention_policy_id,effective_from_utc,effective_until_utc,retention_starts_at:"ciphertextPublicationTime",maximum_retention_seconds,approved_encrypted_volume_identity_sha256,locator_commitment_domain:"QINAO-K4-RETENTION-LOCATOR-V1",cloud_sync:"forbidden",backup:"forbidden",crypto_erasure_receipt_schema:"qinao-k4-crypto-erasure-receipt-v1"}`. `maximum_retention_seconds` is a reviewed positive uint64 and the interval is non-empty. `retention_policy_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-RETENTION-POLICY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalK4ArtifactRetentionPolicyV1Bytes)) || canonicalK4ArtifactRetentionPolicyV1Bytes)`. The immutable retention start is the gateway-observed `published_at_utc` in the complete authenticated `GatewayReceiptV1<GatewayChildBodyV1<K4CiphertextPublicationBodyV1>>`, repeated in captured phase, public projection, attestation, approved status, and verification receipt; filesystem times are never evidence. One private normalized locator on the approved encrypted volume yields `authorized_artifact_locator_commitment = retention_locator_commitment = SHA256("QINAO-K4-RETENTION-LOCATOR-V1\0" || approvedEncryptedVolumeIdentityRaw32 || uint64be(len(normalizedLocatorUTF8)) || normalizedLocatorUTF8)`. The producer's `external_artifact_path_commitment`, run state, ciphertext publication, status, attestation, verify-existing, and certification's `k4_private_artifact_path_commitment` must byte-equal that one value; raw locator text never leaves native custody.

Crypto erasure is monotonic authority, never file absence. `K4CryptoErasureKeyV1 = {retention_policy_digest,run_id,ciphertext_sha256,recipient_key_id,authorized_artifact_locator_commitment}` and its digest uses `QINAO-K4-CRYPTO-ERASURE-KEY-V1\0`. Operator record is exact `K4CryptoErasureReceiptV1 = {schema_version:1,erasure_key:K4CryptoErasureKeyV1,erasure_method,key_epoch_before,key_epoch_after,artifact_disposition,erased_at_utc,operator_key_id,operator_principal_id,operator_role:"k4-crypto-erasure-operator",operator_epoch,nonce,signature_algorithm,signature}`. `unsignedErasureReceiptBytes` is compact sorted-key duplicate-key-rejected canonical UTF-8 for that complete closed record with only the top-level `signature` removed and no trailing LF; the operator signs exactly `"QINAO-K4-CRYPTO-ERASURE-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(unsignedErasureReceiptBytes)) || unsignedErasureReceiptBytes`. `signature_algorithm` is the common Task-0 low-S P-256 algorithm and native plus differential verification must agree. Everywhere, `operator_receipt_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete signed canonical record. `erasure_method = destroyPerRunKey({credential_identity_digest,destruction_contract_id}) | revokeRecipientEpoch({credential_identity_digest,revocation_entry_receipt_sha256})`; disposition is `ciphertextRetainedUnusable({file_identity}) | ciphertextUnlinked({pre_identity,parent_fsync_observation_sha256})`. Key epoch must advance; `erased_at_utc` lies inside one still-active, unrevoked key interval whose key/principal/role/epoch byte-equal both the Task-0 trust root and `k4_crypto_erasure_operator_policy` allowlist.

`crypto-erasure-register` has its own semantic authority keyed by `K4CryptoErasureKeyV1`. Before deriving that authority, reserving it, or appending Accepted, the gateway stable-opens the operator record, rejects duplicate/unknown/noncanonical keys, recomputes its signed-record SHA and erasure-key digest, and verifies the detached signature, role, epoch, interval, and revocation state above. It then runs Accepted → universal effect plan/Prepared/dispatch → `K4ErasureResultObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,erasure_key,method,key_identity_before,key_identity_after,credential_process_identity,artifact_identity_before,artifact_identity_after,platform_result_digest,observed_at_utc,disposition:"erasureProved"}` → registry CAS/Adoption → successful Completed. Registry entry is `K4CryptoErasureRegistryEntryBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,registry_ordinal,previous_registry_root,erasure_key,erasure_key_digest,operator_receipt_sha256,operator_receipt,erasure_result_observation_receipt_sha256,erasure_result_observation,recorded_at_utc,registry_root}` and `registry_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-CRYPTO-ERASURE-REGISTRY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEntryWithoutRegistryRoot)) || canonicalEntryWithoutRegistryRoot)`. Embedded `operator_receipt` byte-equals the stable-open complete signed canonical bytes and its raw SHA equals `operator_receipt_sha256`. Unique-key CAS accepts only one byte-identical entry. The operator record is not a `GatewayReceiptV1`: its detached signature authorizes erasure, while the independent gateway-signed journal/Adoption/Completed chain proves observation and registry commitment; neither layer can replace the other. Goldens reject a wrong/missing uint64 length frame or domain, signature copied across domains, signature included in its own preimage, reordered/noncanonical/trailing-LF/duplicate/unknown-key input, high-S or algorithm/key/principal/role/epoch/window/revocation drift, any signed-field mutation, SHA/embedded-byte mismatch, a gateway-valid envelope around an invalid operator signature, a valid operator record without one journal-included successful terminal pair, and either key class signing the other layer. `K4CryptoErasureRegistrySnapshotV1 = {queried_erasure_key:K4CryptoErasureKeyV1,queried_erasure_key_digest,registry_hwm,registry_root,key_index_root,lookup}` uses `absentAtHWM({matching_entry_receipt_sha256s:[]}) | present({entry_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1})`; absence is recomputed by authenticated key index/replay through HWM. Path/cache absence is powerless.

Trusted time is pinned. The two mandatory top-level policy members make one closed transition: `k4_trusted_clock_policy` changes from explicit `null` to the exact `K4TrustedClockPolicyV1 = {clock_policy_id,implementation_sha256,boot_clock_source,utc_source,platform_time_attestation_source_ids,maximum_reanchor_uncertainty_ms,maximum_forward_skew_seconds,rollback_disposition:"block",schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}` object already admitted in the Task-0 anchor, and `k4_trusted_clock_policy_sha256` changes from explicit `null` to `SHA256(canonicalK4TrustedClockPolicyV1Bytes)`, where those bytes are compact sorted-key canonical UTF-8 with no trailing LF. The gateway independently recomputes that raw SHA and requires the final policy object/SHA to byte-equal the Task-0 anchor object/SHA, including schema digest and test-vector digest; W5 cannot select or revise the clock policy. The policy-authorization preimage includes both final clock members, while `platform_policy_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical policy only after its nested authorization signature is inserted. The clock SHA hashes only the clock object and the policy has no `platform_policy_sha256` member, so the order is acyclic. `K4ClockPredecessorV1 = genesis({installation_authorization_sha256}) | sameBoot({previous_clock_observation_receipt_sha256}) | reanchored({previous_boot_session_commitment,platform_time_attestation_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,source_id,attested_utc,uncertainty_ms})`. `K4TrustedClockObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,clock_policy_id,boot_session_commitment,monotonic_tick_ns,observed_utc,predecessor:K4ClockPredecessorV1,journal_utc_floor,global_journal_hwm,observed_at_utc}` requires monotonic tick/UTC floor nondecrease; reanchor source resolves policy and uncertainty stays bounded. Otherwise cross-boot/rollback fails closed.
- `operation_contract_registry` becomes the complete canonical `K4OperationContractRegistryV1` object defined below, not a digest-only placeholder. `operation_contract_registry_root` becomes the exact recomputed non-zero 64-lowercase-hex `K4_OPERATION_CONTRACT_REGISTRY_ROOT`; both are independently reviewed and signed in the same policy. Producer, attester, checker, and gateway all parse the same candidate-index policy bytes, recompute the object/root, and never carry copied registry constants as authority. Producer authorization, policy admission, run state, device lease, every operation intent, capture receipt, projection, attestation, status, and final verification receipt repeat the root byte-identically.
- `container_absence_semantics` becomes exactly `{"tag":"approved","payload":[K4ContainerAbsenceSemanticsV1]}`. Its one object is `{schema_version:1,xcode_build,devicectl_raw_sha256,help_grammar_sha256,probe_receipt_sha256,host_bundle_id:"com.qinao.K4SpikeHost",domain_type:"appDataContainer",subdirectory:"Library/Application Support",accepted_result_schema,accepted_result_schema_digest,predicate_id,predicate_golden_table_digest,reviewed_at_utc}`. It is independently reviewed from a signed release-Xcode/physical-iOS-27 read-only spike before final policy authorization. The accepted result is the actual frozen typed JSON/error shape, not an invented disposition string; unknown build/tool/result, permission failure indistinguishable from absence, or inability to establish a unique negative predicate leaves K4 BLOCKED.
- `gateway_receipt_verification_key` becomes exact `GatewayReceiptVerificationKeyV1 = {key_id,epoch,public_key_encoding:"der-spki-base64",public_key_der_spki_base64,public_key_sha256,signature_algorithm:"ecdsa-p256-sha256-low-s-v1",not_before_utc,not_after_utc,revocation_state,task0_trust_anchor_manifest_sha256}`. Its exact shared-field projection `{key_id,epoch,public_key_encoding,public_key_der_spki_base64,public_key_sha256,not_before_utc,not_after_utc,revocation_state}` byte-equals the Task-0 source-selection-pinned trust-anchor entry; its fixed algorithm and separately bound manifest digest are additional policy fields, so whole-object equality is neither claimed nor required. It is covered by the independent platform-policy reviewer signature and cannot introduce or rotate a key. Public/off-host checker reads this policy object from the candidate index, pins key/epoch/interval/revocation and manifest digest, then verifies every gateway envelope without default Keychain or caller trust. Missing policy bytes, self-declared key in evidence, or external-manifest mismatch fails.

The policy checker duplicate-key-rejects and canonicalizes all objects. It requires exactly the fenced top-level key set in both states: `status = awaiting-reviewed-platform-pair` requires both trusted-clock members to be explicit `null`, while `status = reviewed-release-platform-pair` requires both non-null, recomputes the clock-object raw SHA, and requires object/SHA byte-equality with the Task-0 anchor. Omission, parser-supplied defaults, a half-transition, or clock-policy drift fails. It enforces exactly one member in each final list; validates every time, key usage, chain, custody separation, retention bound, URL/build/source digest, and trust-root cross-reference; and rejects unknown keys, empty final arrays, placeholders, duplicate records, unreviewed roots, and a policy whose status is final while any tagged field remains awaiting.

The container predicate has a non-circular bootstrap. Before final policy signature, a Task-0-trusted platform reviewer signs external mode-`0600` `K4ContainerSemanticsProbeAuthorizationV1 = {schema_version:1,source_commit,source_tree,xcode_version,xcode_build,xcode_bundle_identity_sha256,devicectl_path_commitment,devicectl_raw_sha256,help_grammar_sha256,device_selector_commitment,host_bundle_id:"com.qinao.K4SpikeHost",domain_type:"appDataContainer",subdirectory:"Library/Application Support",operation_contract_id:"k4.read-only.container-semantics-probe.v1",expected_effect_class:"readOnly",issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,reviewer_key_id,reviewer_principal_id,reviewer_role:"platform-policy-reviewer",reviewer_epoch,signature_algorithm,signature}`. Its signature domain is `QINAO-K4-CONTAINER-SEMANTICS-PROBE-AUTHORIZATION-V1\0` over length-framed canonical bytes without signature. Through native `tool-broker`, the gateway admits that exact release-Xcode/device commitment, performs only the frozen read-only query, and emits child payload `GatewayK4ContainerSemanticsProbeBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,probe_authorization_sha256,source_commit,source_tree,xcode_build,devicectl_raw_sha256,help_grammar_sha256,device_selector_commitment,argv_digest,exit_status,raw_result_length,raw_result_sha256,accepted_result_schema,accepted_result_schema_digest,predicate_id,predicate_golden_table_digest,pre_effect_identity,post_effect_identity,probed_at_utc,disposition:"readOnlySemanticsObserved"}`. Pre/post device identities must be equal and the invocation's effect projection must be empty. `probe_receipt_sha256` in final policy is raw SHA-256 of that complete authenticated child envelope, whose Accepted/Completed envelopes and global-journal inclusion are also reopened during policy admission. The authorization contains no final-policy digest, so the final policy may safely bind the observation without a hash cycle. Unknown/ambiguous result, mutation, missing journal edge, or expired/revoked authorization leaves K4 BLOCKED.

The final `policy_authorization` is the closed tagged value `{"tag":"approved","payload":[{"reviewer_key_id":S,"reviewer_principal_id":S,"reviewer_role":"platform-policy-reviewer","reviewer_epoch":U,"issued_at_utc":S,"expires_at_utc":S,"nonce":S,"signature_algorithm":"ecdsa-p256-sha256-low-s-v1","signature":S}]}`; the awaiting value is exactly the empty-payload object shown above. The key/principal/epoch must resolve an active `platform-policy-reviewer` entry in the independently gateway-pinned Task-0 trust root and be distinct from every producer/attester/envelope custody principal. Its detached signature covers `"QINAO-K4-PLATFORM-POLICY-AUTHORIZATION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPolicyWithoutSignatureBytes)) || canonicalPolicyWithoutSignatureBytes`, where the complete byte-closed policy above retains the approved authorization object and removes only its nested `signature`; verification requires the Task-0 native low-S verifier first and the pinned differential OpenSSL verifier to agree, **not CMS**. The checker reconstructs the Task-0 trust root from the signed source selection and gateway-pinned digest; a key first introduced by this policy is never a trust root. The authorization issuance and expiry lie within the reviewer key, platform pair, producer, attester-signing, envelope-recipient, retention-policy, and gateway-receipt-verification-key intervals, and every referenced revocation state is active at issuance. This is an exact certification allowlist under the existing K4/runtime-certification evidence boundary, not a production trust registry: it can verify only this W0 evidence domain and cannot authorize runtime state, publication, or effects. `/usr/bin/security cms` is used only for the separately pinned artifact-encryption and evidence-attestation protocols; neither depends on W5 production trust. Both builds must be public releases. The capture/checker never browse or infer release status at validation time.

- [ ] **Step 3: Make the device write the canonical runtime trace**

In the isolated fixture, add value-only `SpikeRunIdentity`/`SpikeRuntimeTrace` schema 1 values. The producer supplies a cryptographically random non-secret `runID`. Both source identities use the exact unsigned-UTF-8 sorted/unique tree row `{path,mode,content_length,content_sha256}`. `input_probe_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-INPUT-PROBE-TREE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalInputTreeRows)) || canonicalInputTreeRows)` over the exact stage-0 committed fixture tree, excluding exactly one absent/generated filename, `Shared/GeneratedRunIdentity.swift`, and nothing else. The private build copy adds that file with `input_probe_digest`, `runID`, and build nonce, then computes `built_probe_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-BUILT-PROBE-TREE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalBuiltTreeRows)) || canonicalBuiltTreeRows)` over the complete post-injection tree. Run state, stable identifiers, device messages, captured evidence, and attestation byte-equal these two names; `source_probe_tree_digest`, camel-case wire fields, or alternate tree grammars are prohibited. Device messages/files bind only `input_probe_digest + runID + buildNonce`; they never embed `built_probe_digest`. Producer/attester evidence binds `built_probe_digest -> input/run/nonce -> product CDHashes`. Mutation tests add/rename/exclude a file, change mode/length/domain, and prove there is no fixed-point/self-reference. Files are run-specific (`runtime-<runID>-seed.json`, `runtime-<runID>-reopen.json`, and `events-<runID>-<phase>.jsonl`), so a stale fixed-path success can never satisfy a new run.

The extension—not the host—opens a run-scoped database in its private Application Support container, reads back `journal_mode=WAL` and `synchronous=FULL`, commits a nonce/HWM marker in one transaction, closes, and reports structured values/path hash/helper-PID hash. The raw path exists only transiently in same-run XPC memory so the host can attempt one exact open and record denial; it is forbidden from every trace/log/receipt/evidence byte. Phase `seed` validates one Monitor plus direct/async reply, SQLite pragmas/commit, zero pending/unexpected, cleanup, and terminal pass, then atomically publishes its two files. The host cancels XPC, calls `AppExtensionProcess.invalidate()`, releases every strong connection/process reference, and proves old-helper PID absence with a bounded independent device-process observation; `onInterruption` is optional corroboration, not required for expected invalidation. Only then may the producer terminate the launched host, prove termination, relaunch the exact installed binary, and require different host/helper process identities. Phase `reopen` opens the existing extension-private database, reads the exact marker/HWM, proves the host denial, emits one phase Monitor, cleanup, and terminal pass, then atomically publishes. Reuse of the old helper PID blocks proof. No database/WAL/SHM bytes or private path enter evidence.

Add compile/source tests in `scripts/test_capture_k4_platform_proof.py` for run-specific paths, embedded source/build identity, same-directory temporary write plus rename, exact phase/event order, WAL/FULL read-back, close/reopen/HWM, host-access denial, and prohibition on deriving either trace/event log from console text. Detached launch stdout is never an evidence source. W0 uses one mandatory route, never runtime fallback: the extension relays only the same-run trace—not DB/path bytes—through the authenticated nonce/correlation-bound XPC channel into the Host container; capture copies from that Host container with the exact device/source/destination/result tuple below. The run state and producer authorization bind `runtime_evidence_route = authenticatedHostRelayV1`; direct extension-container copy is unrepresentable. Capture independently binds extension emission, XPC receipt, Host write, and copied bytes. Do not claim a separate runtime signature unless a future reviewed key lifecycle/schema is added.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement one audited proof producer**

Create `scripts/capture_k4_platform_proof.py` using Python standard library only. Its exact CLI is:

```text
capture_k4_platform_proof.py
  prepare-run
  --repository-root-fd 3
  --private-run-directory-fd 4
  --producer-authorization-fd 5
  --broker-fd 6
  --source-probe scripts/fixtures/qinao-k4-platform-probe
  --projection-name projection
  --external-attestation-name external-attestation.cms
  --candidate-bundle-name approved-candidate-bundle

capture_k4_platform_proof.py
  capture-or-verify
  --repository-root-fd 3
  --private-run-directory-fd 4
  --producer-authorization-fd 5
  --broker-fd 6
  --source-probe scripts/fixtures/qinao-k4-platform-probe
  --projection-name projection

capture_k4_platform_proof.py
  finalize-bundle-or-verify
  --repository-root-fd 3
  --private-run-directory-fd 4
  --producer-authorization-fd 5
  --broker-fd 6
  --projection-name projection
  --external-attestation-name external-attestation.cms
  --candidate-bundle-name approved-candidate-bundle

capture_k4_platform_proof.py
  seal-finalized-or-verify
  --repository-root-fd 3
  --private-run-directory-fd 4
  --producer-authorization-fd 5
  --broker-fd 6
  --proof-git-transaction-receipt-fd 7
  --candidate-bundle-name approved-candidate-bundle
```

FD numbers and logical names above are literal and complete. The gateway opens/validates the FDs, clears every raw Xcode/device/team/profile/recipient/keychain/identity environment variable, and resolves those values only inside daemon↔worker messages from the signed authorization commitments. Candidate Python receives no absolute private path, selector, key nickname, identity, or credential. `attest_k4_external_artifact.py` uses the same repository/run/broker FD pattern; `attest-or-verify` is granted only the separately custody-bound decrypt/sign broker contracts, while `verify-existing` is granted decrypt/read-only verification and no sign contract. Negative ACL tests prove producer cannot enumerate/use decryption or signing keys and attester cannot perform device/build mutations.

Before `prepare-run`, the producer supplies one external mode-`0600` canonical `K4ProducerAuthorizationV1`. Its closed object is `{schema_version:1,platform_policy_sha256,source_commit,source_tree,operation_contract_registry_root,run_directory_path_commitment,external_artifact_path_commitment,projection_path_commitment,attestation_path_commitment,candidate_bundle_path_commitment,final_evidence_path:"docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-proof-approved",device_selector_commitment,host_profile_selector_commitment,extension_profile_selector_commitment,host_bundle_id:"com.qinao.K4SpikeHost",extension_bundle_id:"com.qinao.K4SpikeHost.EnhancedSecurity",runtime_evidence_route:"authenticatedHostRelayV1",producer_key_id,producer_principal_id,producer_role:"k4-capture-producer",producer_epoch,issued_at_utc,expires_at_utc,nonce,signature_algorithm,signature}`. `platform_policy_sha256` is the raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical stage-0 policy bytes. The candidate-bundle commitment authorizes only the external sealed bundle; the repository-relative final path is consumed only by the later proof CandidateUnit/Git transaction. The key/principal/epoch must occur in both the gateway-pinned Task-0 trust root and final reviewer-signed policy allowlist, be active/in-window, and be distinct from policy reviewer, attester, envelope, and product-signing principals. Signature preimage is `"QINAO-K4-PRODUCER-AUTHORIZATION-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalAuthorizationWithoutSignature)) || canonicalAuthorizationWithoutSignature` under the Task-0 low-S P-256 protocol. Every selector/path field other than the fixed repository-relative literal is a commitment, never raw private text. The gateway validates this record before launching the producer; producer and attester independently revalidate it and bind its raw SHA-256. Mutations change either bundle ID, route, registry root, or destination commitment while re-signing every outer object and must fail.

Before the first `prepare-run`, the gateway observes trusted UTC time and no-replace publishes `control/policy-admission.json` as a cache of one complete common `GatewayReceiptV1`. Its child payload is exactly `K4PolicyAdmissionBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,source_commit,source_tree,operation_contract_registry_root,platform_policy_sha256,platform_policy_authorization_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,producer_key_id,producer_principal_id,producer_epoch,platform_pair_digest,attester_policy_identity_digest,retention_policy_digest,admitted_at_utc,admission_disposition:"admittedForNewCapture"}`. `platform_policy_authorization_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the exact canonical approved `policy_authorization` tagged object; `platform_pair_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-PLATFORM-PAIR-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalSelectedK4ApprovedPlatformPairV1Bytes)) || canonicalSelectedK4ApprovedPlatformPairV1Bytes)`. The retention digest uses the formula above, and every repeated field byte-equals its independently recomputed value. Parent binding exists only in `GatewayChildBodyV1`, with no nested authentication or specialized receipt digest. `policy_admission_receipt_sha256` always means raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical authenticated gateway envelope; its common `body_digest` is never substituted for it. Admission requires that time within policy authorization, platform pair, producer, signing, envelope, gateway-receipt key, and retention-policy effective intervals and every revocation state active. A new device/build/encryption/signing effect after any relevant expiry or revocation is forbidden. Crash resume before expiry reopens the exact receipt and authenticates it against the root-owned append-only gateway journal; after expiry it may perform only read-only reconciliation/verification and safe exact-owned cleanup—never a new launch, build, encrypt, or sign. Historical `verify-existing` remains legal only when admission, ciphertext publication, and CMS signing times were each in-window and current policy has not explicitly revoked acceptance; it cannot turn an expired authorization into a fresh effect. The complete receipt is included in the private container and its raw SHA-256 is repeated by run state, captured phase, projection, attestation, status, and final verification receipt. Forged, replayed, cross-run, cross-policy, wrong-key/epoch, missing-journal, or self-consistent caller-written receipts fail.

`prepare-run` receives every deterministic value through the exact CLI above. On first absent state only it obtains `run_id` as 128 CSPRNG bits and `build_nonce` as 256 CSPRNG bits, lowercase hex, and publishes them once; resume adopts them only after all deterministic fields rederive byte-identically. `K4RunStateV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,source_commit,source_tree,operation_contract_registry_root,platform_policy_path,platform_policy_blob,platform_policy_sha256,platform_policy_authorization_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,policy_admitted_at_utc,input_probe_digest,run_directory_path_commitment,external_artifact_path_commitment,projection_path_commitment,attestation_path_commitment,candidate_bundle_path_commitment,final_evidence_path,device_selector_commitment,host_profile_selector_commitment,extension_profile_selector_commitment,host_bundle_id,extension_bundle_id,runtime_evidence_route,producer_authorization_sha256,producer_key_id,producer_principal_id,producer_epoch,envelope_recipient_key_id,envelope_recipient_fingerprint,run_id,build_nonce,run_state_digest}`. The two bundle IDs, route, operation registry root, and all five path commitments equal the signed authorization literals; `external_artifact_path_commitment` also equals the unique authorized artifact-locator commitment. `run_state_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-RUN-STATE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRunStateWithoutDigest)) || canonicalRunStateWithoutDigest)`. It has no raw path, UDID, selector, account, decryption identity, attestation identity, or key material.

`run_state_sha256` everywhere else means raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical `control/run-state.json` bytes including `run_state_digest`; it is never the semantic `run_state_digest`. Likewise `platform_policy_sha256` always means raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical candidate-index policy bytes, never an inner authorization digest.

`K4StableIdentifiersV1` is the closed progressive record `{run_id,build_nonce,input_probe_digest,built_probe_digest,host_bundle_id,extension_bundle_id,host_cdhash,extension_cdhash,install_identity,seed_host_process_identity,seed_extension_process_identity,reopen_host_process_identity,reopen_extension_process_identity,seed_output_identity,reopen_output_identity}`. Every observation-dependent field uses exactly `notObservedYet(priorOrdinal) | absent(provedByObservationDigest) | present(valueDigest,observationDigest)`; fixed fields are never tagged. An ordinal may advance only the fields its operation contract owns, never regress or change a present value, and every result carries the full resulting record.

All four private phase files use one exact non-authoritative state wire `K4PhaseReceiptV1 = {schema_version:1,phase,run_state_sha256,predecessor_phase,phase_payload,receipt_digest}`. `predecessor_phase` is `notApplicable(firstPhase) | present(phase,receipt_digest)` and is first-phase N/A only for prepared. `phase_payload` is the closed union `prepared(prepared_inputs_root) | captured(policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_sha256,operation_journal_root,capture_input_receipt_sha256,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,ciphertext_published_at_utc,retention_policy_digest,retention_expires_at_utc,projection_root) | attested(captured_receipt_sha256,attestation_sha256,attester_policy_identity_digest,attestation_signing_time_utc) | finalized(attested_receipt_sha256,approved_evidence_root,status_sha256,publication_manifest_sha256,candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256,proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256,proof_commit,proof_tree)`. `ciphertext_published_at_utc` equals the authenticated gateway receipt and `retention_expires_at_utc` is its checked UTC-seconds addition by the policy maximum, capped strictly before every relevant identity/policy expiry. The external candidate bundle is published first, a signed proof CandidateUnit is reviewed and adopted by Git transaction second, and the private `finalized.json` phase file is published third binding both authenticated receipts plus exact proof commit/tree. Neither public leaf set contains its own publication/Git/finalized identity, so no digest cycle is introduced. Tag equals `phase`; predecessor is exactly the preceding phase file; and `receipt_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-PHASE-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPhaseReceiptWithoutDigest)) || canonicalPhaseReceiptWithoutDigest)`. `prepared_inputs_root` hashes the exact `{run_state_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,input_probe_digest,tool_identity_root}` under `QINAO-K4-PREPARED-INPUTS-V1\0`. A phase file never claims gateway authentication by itself.

After native revalidation and no-replace publication/adoption of exact `attested.json`, `attest-or-verify` publishes one no-effect universal child envelope whose closed payload is `K4AttestedSealBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,attested_phase_receipt_sha256,attested_phase_receipt_digest,captured_receipt_sha256,pre_attestation_verification_receipt_sha256,cms_signing_receipt_sha256,attestation_sha256,attester_policy_identity_digest,attestation_signing_time_utc,sealed_at_utc,disposition:"attestedAfterIndependentVerification"}`. At seal publication it reopens its Accepted envelope, every already published pre-attestation/CMS/effect-adoption support edge, and their global-journal entries, then byte-rederives the phase file; it cannot require its own future Completed. The attester publishes successful Completed only after the seal child. Every later consumer—including prepublication construction—must reopen that matching terminal Completed and require the seal in its ordered children. The external cache `results/k4-attested-envelope.json` contains the complete `GatewayReceiptV1<GatewayChildBodyV1<K4AttestedSealBodyV1>>`; only its raw SHA-256 is `attested_receipt_sha256` or prerequisite kind `k4Attested`. A bare `attested.json` digest cannot satisfy either name.

After native revalidation and no-replace publication/adoption of that exact `finalized.json`, `seal-finalized-or-verify` publishes one universal child envelope whose closed payload is `K4FinalizedSealBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,attested_receipt_sha256,finalized_phase_receipt_sha256,finalized_phase_receipt_digest,candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256,proof_candidate_unit_sha256,proof_review_receipt_sha256,proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256,proof_commit,proof_tree,approved_evidence_root,publication_manifest_sha256,sealed_at_utc,disposition:"finalizedAfterGit"}`. It reopens the CandidateUnit, GREEN review, Git transaction, bundle publication, authenticated attested seal, phase predecessor, commit/tree, and global/root-scope journal edges. The external cache `results/k4-finalized-envelope.json` contains the complete `GatewayReceiptV1<GatewayChildBodyV1<K4FinalizedSealBodyV1>>`; only raw SHA-256 of that complete envelope is `k4_finalized_receipt_sha256`. Missing/mismatched phase, bare phase substitution, receipt-without-journal, or envelope-before-Git is BLOCKED. This authenticated envelope—not `finalized.json` or its `receipt_digest`—is consumed by Task 3, certification, and W1 handoff.

K4 naming is uniform: every field ending `_receipt_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of the **complete canonical receipt file or authenticated gateway envelope**; an internal field named `receipt_digest` or common gateway `body_digest` is its domain-separated semantic digest and is never substituted. The checker reconstructs prepared/captured/attested/finalized phase bytes from their closed inputs. Exact captured bytes, CMS bytes, policy-derived attester identity digest, and CMS-extracted signing time uniquely reconstruct bare `attested.json`, so only `attested_phase_receipt_sha256` is independently recomputable from those phase inputs. `K4ApprovedStatusV1.attested_receipt_sha256` instead means the authenticated seal-envelope raw SHA and must be reopened from the signed scope/global journal and matched to the reconstructed phase SHA/digest; it cannot be derived from bare phase bytes. Mutation tests swap phase SHA, seal-envelope SHA, semantic digest, or reconstructed bytes and require failure.

Every named K4 root is byte-closed. Unless a more specific object follows, a leaf-set root uses canonical compact JSON of the globally unsigned-UTF-8 sorted/unique array `{path,mode,content_sha256,content_length}` and `SHA256(<literal-domain> || uint64be(len(bytes)) || bytes)`. `tool_identity_root` uses domain `QINAO-K4-TOOL-IDENTITY-SET-V1\0` over the sorted/unique complete executable-identity objects for the gateway CLI, daemon, worker, sandbox runner, strict CMS decoder, xcrun, devicectl, xcodebuild, codesign, security, and plutil. The five gateway-side objects byte-match the Task-0 trust-anchor identities/projections and none may be collapsed into a vague `gateway` alias. `product_root` uses `QINAO-K4-PRODUCT-ROOT-V1\0` over only `raw/products/host-app.qbundle`. `profile_projection_root` uses `QINAO-K4-PROFILE-PROJECTION-ROOT-V1\0` over the closed object `{host_profile_uuid_sha256,extension_profile_uuid_sha256,host_profile_content_sha256,extension_profile_content_sha256,host_signer_leaf_sha256,extension_signer_leaf_sha256,host_entitlements_sha256,extension_entitlements_sha256}`; raw profiles/certificates remain only nested in the encrypted product. `runtime_root` uses `QINAO-K4-RUNTIME-ROOT-V1\0` over the exact eight `raw/runtime/` entries in the private-manifest table. `source_root` uses `QINAO-K4-SOURCE-ROOT-V1\0` over only `raw/source/source-probe.qbundle`. `build_root` uses `QINAO-K4-BUILD-ROOT-V1\0` over the exact four entries `raw/build/private-overlay.qbundle`, `raw/build/archive-result.json`, `raw/build/host-build-settings.json`, and `raw/build/extension-build-settings.json`. `private_container_root` uses `QINAO-K4-PRIVATE-CONTAINER-ROOT-V1\0` over the exact 66-entry plaintext container bytes.

The public roots are deliberately acyclic. `projection_root` uses `QINAO-K4-PRE-ATTESTATION-PROJECTION-ROOT-V1\0` over exactly the 20 Step-5 files other than `status.json`, `signed-products-external-attestation.cms`, and `gateway-prepublication-receipts.json`; captured binds only this P0 root. Attestation signs P0/captured and is therefore not a member of its own input. After CMS creation, the gateway-receipt projection below is fixed; finalization creates it plus `status.json` and the CMS, then computes `approved_evidence_root` with `QINAO-K4-APPROVED-EVIDENCE-ROOT-V1\0` over the exact 23-file Step-5 inventory. `K4ApprovedStatusV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,status:"approved",run_id,source_commit,source_tree,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_sha256,gateway_operation_receipts_root,operation_journal_root,capture_input_receipt_sha256,captured_receipt_sha256,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,ciphertext_published_at_utc,retention_policy_digest,retention_locator_commitment,retention_expires_at_utc,projection_root,attestation_sha256,attested_receipt_sha256,gateway_prepublication_receipts_sha256,gateway_receipt_projection_root,host_cdhash,extension_cdhash,app_absence_result_digest,container_absence_result_digest}`. It contains neither its own digest nor `approved_evidence_root`/publication-manifest/finalized-receipt identity. `status_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of those canonical bytes. `publication_manifest_sha256` is raw SHA-256 of canonical external `K4PublicationManifestV1 = {schema_version:1,entries:[the exact 23 path/mode/content_sha256/content_length records],approved_evidence_root}`; that manifest is not a repository-evidence member. The finalized receipt can therefore bind all three without a cycle. `private_capture_manifest_sha256` is forbidden terminology: the capture receipt carries the manifest's defined `manifest_digest`, not a second raw hash. Golden tests mutate membership, category, domain, length framing, path order, mode, one content byte, and every attempted self-reference.

`prepare-run` owns the four-leaf prefix transaction `policy-admission.json → producer-authorization.json → run-state.json → prepared.json`. The first leaf is the complete authenticated gateway receipt copied from its root-owned journal, not caller JSON. With all absent the gateway admits policy, the producer validates/copies the external authorization, then publishes run-state and prepared in order, each no-replace/fsynced. Any proper prefix revalidates every incumbent, rederives all deterministic fields, adopts validated randoms only from run state, and publishes only its suffix; all present are reopened read-only and byte-compared. Any non-prefix state, run-state without either authority predecessor, prepared without all three predecessors, non-byte-identical external authorization, or gateway-journal mismatch is BLOCKED. These five prefixes are the only legal members of the 16-state matrix. Tests terminate before/after all four publications. No command infers phase from a filename.

Every device-facing call is governed by a closed journal. `K4OperationIntentV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,ordinal,operation_id,operation_class,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_prefix_sha256,lease_fencing_token,previous_result:notApplicable(firstOperation)|present(D),stable_identifiers_before,children,precondition_contract_id,precondition_observation,postcondition_contract_id,reconciliation_contract_id,failure_route_id,intent_digest}`. `precondition_observation` is the closed union `notApplicable(priorTerminalFailure) | present(observation,tool_execution_receipt_sha256s)`; the present array is non-empty, ordered by the contract's child/query IDs, resolves into the gateway capture aggregate, and binds the current lease-prefix SHA/fence/time. Candidate-authored or unreceipted observations are unrepresentable. `children[]` contains one entry for every operation except 04, which has exactly two declaration-ordered entries (`a`,`b`). Each `K4OperationChildIntentV1` is `{child_id,executable_identity,argv_contract_id,argv_preimage,argv_digest}`; `executable_identity` is `{path_commitment,real_path_commitment,raw_sha256,code_identity:notApplicable(unsignedSystemTool)|present(cdhash,designated_requirement),version_digest}`. `argv_preimage[]` is an ordered array of `literal(utf8) | committedSecret(name,sha256) | derived(name,valueDigest)`; it exposes no raw secret and independently reconstructs the actual private argv. `argv_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-ARGV-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalArgvPreimage)) || canonicalArgvPreimage)`, and `intent_digest` uses the analogous complete-object-without-digest domain `QINAO-K4-OPERATION-INTENT-V1\0`.

Decrypted `K4OperationEvidenceV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,ordinal,operation_id,run_state_sha256,intent_digest,previous_result_digest:notApplicable(firstOperation)|present(D),stable_identifiers_before,evidence_variant,plaintext_digest}`. The variant is `direct(child_results,postcondition_observation) | reconciled(child_reconciliation_queries,postcondition_observation,original_result:unavailable(crashAfterEffectBeforeEvidenceSeal)) | indeterminate(child_reconciliation_queries,observation_or_reason,known_identifier_commitments,original_result:unavailable(crashAfterEffectBeforeEvidenceSeal)) | skipped(reason)`. `child_results[]` has the same ordered child IDs/count as the intent and each member is `{child_id,gateway_tool_receipt_sha256,argv_digest,exit_status,raw_result_base64,raw_result_sha256}`. `child_reconciliation_queries[]` has that same shape/order and each member is `{child_id,gateway_tool_receipt_sha256,argv_digest,exit_status,reconciliation_query_base64,reconciliation_query_sha256}`. `exit_status` is the same closed tagged value from the authenticated tool payload: `exited(code:int32) | signaled(signal:uint8,core_dumped:bool) | timedOut(contract_id) | spawnFailed(reason_code)`; no lossy `exit_code` projection exists. The gateway tool receipt, not candidate bytes, is the source of every argv/result/status/time/fence/pre/post identity. `reason` is exactly `preconditionNotReached | priorTerminalFailure | unsafeForeignOrAmbiguousIdentity`. `plaintext_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-EVIDENCE-PLAINTEXT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEvidenceWithoutDigest)) || canonicalEvidenceWithoutDigest)`. The no-replace CMS EnvelopedData file's raw hash is `evidence_sha256`; plaintext close+unlink follows sealing and cryptographic erasure is handled separately.

`K4OperationResultV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,ordinal,operation_id,run_state_sha256,intent_digest,evidence_kind,evidence_sha256,evidence_plaintext_digest,tool_execution_receipt_digests,stable_identifiers_after,postcondition_contract_id,postcondition_observation,completion,result_digest}`. `tool_execution_receipt_digests[]` is the same ordered `{child_id,gateway_tool_receipt_sha256,argv_digest,exit_status}` projection as evidence. `completion` is `succeededDirect(childStatuses) | succeededReconciled() | failedDirect(childStatuses,failure_code) | indeterminate(reason_code) | skipped(reason)`, with `childStatuses[]` the exact ordered `{child_id,exit_status}` array, consistent with evidence and the compound predicate. `result_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-RESULT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalResultWithoutDigest)) || canonicalResultWithoutDigest)`. Every operation contract pins complete canonical schemas/digests for its pre/post observations and reconciliation result; a base64 value is decoded and structurally/predicate validated, never treated as opaque success.

Failure before or outside one ordinal is also terminal and authenticated. The fixed capture/build schedule has exactly 13 registry-ordered slots, and its stage order is `prepare → leaseAcquire → build → deviceOperation → captureSeal → encryption → projection → attestation → candidateBundlePublication → proofAdoption → finalizedSeal`. `K4CaptureTerminalFailureV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,run_state,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,failure_stage,reason_code,capture_build_slots,completed_operation_result_sha256s,failing_operation_result,skipped_operation_ordinals,terminal_device_lease,cleanup_receipt_sha256s,partial_output_disposition,failed_at_utc,failure_digest}`. `run_state` is `notObservedYet(beforeRunStatePublication) | present(run_state_sha256)`; the first variant is legal only for `failure_stage = prepare`, while every later stage requires present. `failure_stage` is one member of that ordered set; each build slot is exactly `completed({tool_receipt_sha256}) | failed({tool_receipt_sha256,reason_code}) | skipped({prior_failure_stage})`, and the array always contains all 13 ordinals. `terminal_device_lease` is `notAcquired(prepare|leaseAcquire) | present(terminal_device_lease_chain_sha256)`; because acquisition precedes the first build child, `present` is mandatory for **every** build or later failure and the final chain includes the safe release/expiry edge, while pre-acquisition failures cannot fabricate a chain. `failing_operation_result` is `notApplicable(beforeDeviceOperations) | present(sha256) | notApplicable(noDeviceOperationFailedAfterCompletedSchedule)`. The first variant is exact for prepare/leaseAcquire/build before device operations; `present` is exact only for `deviceOperation` and identifies the sole failed/indeterminate ordinal around which completed/skipped arrays partition 00–11; the final variant is exact for captureSeal and every later stage and requires all twelve operation-result SHAs completed successfully with no skipped ordinal. `partial_output_disposition[]` is the complete sorted `{path_commitment,state}` array with state `retainedSealedPrivate | exclusivelyDeletedUnpublished | unresolvedBlocked`; unresolved output can never be approved. `failure_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-CAPTURE-TERMINAL-FAILURE-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalWithoutDigest)) || canonicalWithoutDigest)`. The daemon terminates/releases any acquired lease and performs only exact-owned cleanup before publishing this payload and failed Completed envelope; capture/build failure therefore cannot be disguised as a malformed 12-operation success record. Mutations force each build-slot failure to carry the authenticated acquire→release/expire closure and reject `notAcquired(build)`. Encryption, projection, attestation, external publication, proof-adoption, and final-seal failures use the later-stage partition. If the existing journal/lease/output prefix is corrupt or cannot prove one unique terminal disposition, W0 remains **BLOCKED**: recovery requires a separately designed administrator protocol outside this plan. An incident note or crypto-erasure record may document custody but cannot authorize restart, overwrite, or a new effect here.

`K4CaptureTerminalFailureV1` begins only after the successful `K4PolicyAdmissionBodyV1` leaf exists and its mandatory `policy_admission_receipt_sha256` has been authenticated. A policy denial, malformed authorization, unavailable trust anchor, or other failure before that first leaf terminates the enclosing invocation through the universal failed `GatewayInvocationCompletedBodyV1`, with no K4 capture-terminal payload, run state, device lease, or capture/build schedule. The `notObservedYet(beforeRunStatePublication)` variant therefore means “after admission but before run-state publication”; it never fabricates an admitted policy receipt. Mutation tests force both sides of this boundary.

The exact operation registry is:

| ordinal / ID | class | argv contract | precondition → required postcondition | reconciliation contract | failure route |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `00 reconcileInstalledProbe` | read-only | `devicectl.device.info+installedApps.v1` | device/policy match → exact-owned installed identity or proved absence | repeat same read-only inventory | `R00` |
| `01 installProbe` | mutation | `devicectl.device.install.app.v1` | proved absence → installed exact run/build/CDHash plus persistent ID | inventory exact run/build, never reinstall if ambiguous | `R01` |
| `02 launchSeed` | mutation | `devicectl.process.launch.seed.v1` | exact install + immediate host/helper absence under device lease → seed host/helper process identities and one armed host state machine that will publish seed evidence then call `AppExtensionProcess.invalidate()` exactly once | process+run/phase/invalidation-ack query | `R02` |
| `03 copySeedEvidence` | copy | `devicectl.device.copy.seed.v1` | exact seed pass identity → exact absent private destination becomes matching trace/events | inspect destination and device source identity; never recopy if ambiguous | `R03` |
| `04 verifyExtensionInvalidated` | observation-copy | `devicectl.device.info.seed-invalidation.v1` | op-02-authorized seed state machine and copied seed pass → device-authored invalidate ack, old helper absent, connection invalidated | repeat process/XPC/ack query; no device mutation; local destination is no-replace | `R04` |
| `05 terminateSeedHost` | cleanup mutation | `devicectl.process.terminate.seed.v1` | exact seed host → seed host absent | process absence query | `R05` |
| `06 launchReopen` | mutation | `devicectl.process.launch.reopen.v1` | old processes absent/exact install + immediate host/helper absence under device lease → distinct reopen host/helper | process+run handshake query | `R06` |
| `07 copyReopenEvidence` | copy | `devicectl.device.copy.reopen.v1` | exact reopen pass identity → exact absent private destination becomes matching trace/events | inspect destination and device source identity; never recopy if ambiguous | `R07` |
| `08 terminateReopenHost` | cleanup mutation | `devicectl.process.terminate.reopen.v1` | exact reopen host or proved never-started → reopen host absent | process absence query | `R08` |
| `09 uninstallProbe` | cleanup mutation | `devicectl.device.uninstall.v1` | exact-owned install or proved absent → app absent | installed-app inventory; never uninstall foreign/ambiguous | `R09` |
| `10 verifyAppAbsence` | read-only | `devicectl.device.info.app-absence.v1` | cleanup chain complete → app absent | repeat exact query | `R10` |
| `11 verifyOwnedDataClosure` | read-only | `devicectl.device.info.owned-data-closure.v1` | exact extension-private cleanup ack + app absent → policy-selected host-container negative predicate | repeat exact host query under the reviewed semantics selection | `R11` |

The policy-pinned `K4OperationContractRegistryV1` exact top level is `{schema_version:1,device_operation_contracts,device_operation_contracts_root,capture_build_contracts,capture_build_contracts_root,attestation_tool_contracts,attestation_tool_contracts_root,output_budget_contracts,output_budget_contracts_root,invocation_schedule_contract,invocation_schedule_contract_root}`. `K4DeviceOperationContractRowV1 = {ordinal,operation_id,operation_class,child_contract_ids,argv_contract_ids,precondition_contract_id,postcondition_contract_id,reconciliation_contract_id,failure_route_contract_id,output_budget_class_id,slot_dispositions}`; class is `readOnly | mutation | copy | observationCopy | cleanupMutation`, child/argv arrays are declaration-order unique/nonempty, slot dispositions are declaration-order closed `direct | reconciled | indeterminate | skipped(reason_codes)`, and rows are exact ordinals 0...11 unique by operation ID. Every table column and child expansion below byte-equals one row, all referenced predicate/argv/reconcile/failure/budget IDs resolve once, and no orphan exists. Device root length-frames rows under `QINAO-K4-DEVICE-OPERATION-CONTRACTS-V1\0`; capture-build and attestation tuples use their named roots.

`K4InvocationScheduleContractV1 = {schema_version:1,slots}` uses declaration-ordered `{ordinal,invocation_kind,receipt_role,receipt_kind,presence}` with role `genesis | accepted | noEffectChild | effectPrepared | effectDispatchEntered | effectAdoption(result_kind) | completed` and presence `required | taggedOptional(allowed_reason_codes)`. It contains fixed base slots from all registries; recovery insertion is separately indexed. Schedule/instance roots use their named domains. Direct/reconciled/indeterminate vary only via permitted dispositions. `operation_contract_registry_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRegistryBytes)) || canonicalRegistryBytes)`; every request root equals it. Policy signs complete object/root/golden bytes.

`K4OutputBudgetContractRowV1 = {budget_class_id,max_stdout_bytes,max_stderr_bytes,max_raw_result_bytes,max_decoded_observation_bytes,max_evidence_plaintext_bytes}` sorts/uniques by ID and has exactly `query-v1` values `1048576/262144/1048576/1048576/2097152` and `bounded-effect-v1` values `262144/262144/262144/262144/1048576`; root length-frames these two rows under `QINAO-K4-OUTPUT-BUDGET-CONTRACTS-V1\0`. Query applies to 00, 04-a, 10, 11; bounded-effect to all other children including 04-b. Device rows/child expansions join a class bidirectionally. Worker streams into fixed-cap FDs; overflow/truncation fails. Aggregate decoded+Base64/text cap remains `268435456` within container cap and worst-case schedule is proved before capture.

For copy/observation-copy operations the actual argv is exactly `xcrun devicectl device copy from --device <committed-UDID> --domain-type appDataContainer --domain-identifier <bundle-id> --source <run-relative-path> --destination <broker-private-staging-path> --json-output <distinct-broker-private-staging-json>`; JSON output is command metadata, never the payload. Because `devicectl` accepts paths rather than caller FDs, the gateway creates an unpredictable owner-only mode-`0700` child beneath the approved encrypted run volume, verifies it by dirfd immediately before spawn, and passes only paths inside that subtree. After exit it reopens/validates exact bytes, fsyncs, then publishes payload and result separately to their authorization-bound absent final names with `renameatx_np(RENAME_EXCL)` and records both edges in its journal. A staging/final destination-only/result-only crash is adopted only through the authenticated tool receipt and is never blindly recopied. The same staging-then-exclusive-rename rule applies to every `--json-output`, `security cms -o`, xcode archive, and other path-only tool output. Op 02's launch arguments contain the run/build nonce and exact state-machine mode; authenticated app/XPC code—not the host-side producer—owns the later one-shot invalidation. The op-02 intent therefore precedes and authorizes that effect, while op 04 performs no device mutation: its second child only copies the already-authored ack into the private run directory through that adoption-safe broker contract. The registry's canonical bytes and `K4_OPERATION_CONTRACT_REGISTRY_ROOT = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(bytes)) || bytes)` are independently policy-pinned and golden-tested in producer, attester, and checker.

For avoidance of a prose-only registry, every `argv_contract_id` expands to one exact ordered token template below. `S(name)` is the private raw value whose SHA-256 equals the intent's same-name `committedSecret`; `D(name)` is a path/value independently derived from run state or the preceding result and represented by the same-name `derived` atom. `O(NN)` expands in place to the two option pairs `--timeout 120 --json-output D(opNN-result-json)` (compound children use `NN-a`/`NN-b`). Its displayed position is normative: it precedes install/launch positional arguments so it can never become an app argument. Literal tokens are shown verbatim. `--console`, provisioning-update flags, and extra environment arguments are forbidden; parser tests invoke each template's pinned Xcode-27 help grammar and reject moving `O` across a positional boundary.

| ordinal | Exact ordered argv template before common suffix |
|---|---|
| 00 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device info apps --device S(deviceUDID) --bundle-id com.qinao.K4SpikeHost O(00)` |
| 01 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device install app --device S(deviceUDID) O(01) D(signedHostAppPath)` |
| 02 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device process launch --device S(deviceUDID) --launch-persistent-identifier D(installPersistentIdentifier) O(02) com.qinao.K4SpikeHost --qinao-run-id D(runID) --qinao-build-nonce D(buildNonce) --qinao-mode seed-and-invalidate` |
| 03 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device copy from --device S(deviceUDID) --domain-type appDataContainer --domain-identifier com.qinao.K4SpikeHost --source D(seedEvidenceRelativePath) --destination D(absentSeedPayloadPath) O(03)` |
| 04 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device info processes --device S(deviceUDID) O(04-a)` followed by the exact read-only host-container ack copy template from 03 with `D(invalidationAckRelativePath)`/`D(absentInvalidationAckPath)`/`O(04-b)` |
| 05 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device process terminate --device S(deviceUDID) --pid D(seedHostPID) O(05)` |
| 06 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device process launch --device S(deviceUDID) --launch-persistent-identifier D(installPersistentIdentifier) O(06) com.qinao.K4SpikeHost --qinao-run-id D(runID) --qinao-build-nonce D(buildNonce) --qinao-mode reopen` |
| 07 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device copy from --device S(deviceUDID) --domain-type appDataContainer --domain-identifier com.qinao.K4SpikeHost --source D(reopenEvidenceRelativePath) --destination D(absentReopenPayloadPath) O(07)` |
| 08 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device process terminate --device S(deviceUDID) --pid D(reopenHostPID) O(08)` |
| 09 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device uninstall app --device S(deviceUDID) O(09) com.qinao.K4SpikeHost` |
| 10 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device info apps --device S(deviceUDID) --bundle-id com.qinao.K4SpikeHost O(10)` |
| 11 | `/usr/bin/xcrun devicectl device info files --device S(deviceUDID) --domain-type appDataContainer --domain-identifier com.qinao.K4SpikeHost --subdirectory Library/Application\ Support O(11)` |

Ordinal 04 is the only compound contract: one read-only query plus one adoption-safe local observation copy, with two declaration-ordered child intents/evidence/result projections and separate output paths/digests. Every other row, including 11, has one child. Op 11 never treats the extension bundle ID as a documented `appDataContainer` domain and never hardcodes a guessed `domainNotInstalledOrAbsent` token. It requires the complete prior device-authored extension-private store-destruction ack, op-10 app absence, and the exact host-container negative result/predicate frozen by the signed `K4ContainerAbsenceSemanticsV1`. A generic tool/permission/transport failure cannot prove absence. Each precondition/postcondition/reconciliation ID expands to `{schema_version:1,contract_id,observation_schema,observation_schema_digest,predicate_id,predicate_golden_table_digest}`. The observation schema is the exact relevant devicectl JSON result descriptor plus the run/build/process/source fields named in the table; the predicate golden table contains success, typed command failure, wrong device/bundle/run/build/PID/path, missing/duplicate JSON key, and ambiguous/foreign identity. Those complete constant objects—not this typography—are policy-pinned and frozen in both implementations/tests before the registry root is computed.

Before the first of the 13 capture/build registry children—including source snapshot/injection—the gateway acquires a persistent exclusive lease in its root-owned registry. Let `L(x) = uint64be(len(xUTF8)) || xUTF8`; `registry_key_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-DEVICE-LEASE-KEY-V1\0" || deviceSelectorCommitmentRaw32 || L(hostBundleID) || L(extensionBundleID))`, so bundle boundaries are unambiguous. `lease_id` is exactly 128 CSPRNG bits as lowercase hex, generated only for the initial acquire. Every event is an authenticated `GatewayReceiptV1` whose `GatewayChildBodyV1.payload` is exact `K4DeviceLeaseEventV1 = {schema_version:1,event,lease_id,registry_key_digest,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,previous_fencing_epoch:notApplicable(firstAcquire)|present(U),fencing_epoch,fencing_token,effective_at_utc,expires_at_utc,reason_code}`. `event` is `acquire | renew | recover | release | expire`; `reason_code` is the matching closed member `initialCapture | preExpiryRenewal | daemonRecovery | normalCompletion | incidentCleanup | deadlineElapsed`; invalid event/reason pairs fail. `fencing_epoch` is monotonic uint64; `fencing_token = SHA256("QINAO-K4-DEVICE-LEASE-FENCE-V1\0" || registryKeyDigestRaw32 || uint64be(epoch) || gatewayInvocationNonceRaw32)`. Acquire requires no live incumbent; renewal retains the same epoch only before expiry; gateway-process recovery always advances the epoch after proving no competing live holder; release/expire terminalize that epoch. A crashed process never “reacquires byte-identically.”

The root registry and append-only receipt journal are the lease authority. Immediately before **every K4 build/device child, including read-only precondition/reconciliation queries**, the native worker/daemon reopens current registry state, policy/admission intervals, worker session, and exact intent token; stale/expired/released fence or cross-run observation fails before execution. For any non-empty event prefix, `device_lease_chain_prefix_sha256 = SHA256("QINAO-K4-DEVICE-LEASE-PREFIX-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalEventEnvelopeArray)) || canonicalEventEnvelopeArray)`; that exact raw SHA and current token enter each build request and operation intent. `control/device-run-lease-chain.json` is canonical `{schema_version:1,events:[complete ordered GatewayReceiptV1 envelopes],final_disposition,chain_sha256}` with two to 64 events (at minimum one acquire plus one release/expiry), contiguous fencing transitions, `final_disposition = releasedNormally | releasedAfterIncident | expiredAfterCleanup`, and `chain_sha256 = SHA256("QINAO-K4-DEVICE-LEASE-CHAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalWithoutDigest)) || canonicalWithoutDigest)`. Final disposition must match the terminal event/reason. The final chain is a private-container control leaf and its full authenticated envelopes enter the final gateway-receipt projection. The gateway releases only after op 11 or an independently signed incident/cleanup decision.

`device_lease_chain_sha256` means raw SHA-256 of the complete canonical `control/device-run-lease-chain.json` bytes including `chain_sha256`; it is distinct from both semantic `chain_sha256` and a live prefix SHA.

The lease prevents two admitted Qinao captures from sharing the device/bundle tuple but is not treated as proof against foreign software. Therefore immediately before op 02 and op 06, inside the same broker request and after all earlier predicates, the gateway repeats the pinned process query and requires exact host/helper absence. The launch argv never contains `--terminate-existing`: if any process appears after the query, launch fails safely and enters the fixed route rather than terminate or take over it. Tests cover concurrent acquisition, expiry, renewal, crash/recovery fencing advance, stale-token effect, forged registry copy, journal fork, and a foreign same-bundle process raced between plan precondition and effect boundary; all require zero unauthorized termination/effect.

Success advances to the next ordinal.
