On any direct failure, indeterminate result, or unsafe identity, failure routes are exact: `R00` skips 01–08, permits 09 only for an exact-owned incumbent, then requires 10–11; `R01` skips 02–08 except any already-reached exact-owned cleanup precondition, then requires 09–11; `R02` skips 03,06,07 and evaluates 04,05,08,09,10,11 in order; `R03` evaluates 04,05, skips 06,07, then evaluates 08–11; `R04` evaluates 05, skips 06,07, then 08–11; `R05` skips 06,07 then evaluates 08–11; `R06` skips 07 then evaluates 08–11; `R07` evaluates 08–11; `R08` evaluates 09–11; `R09` evaluates 10–11; `R10` evaluates 11; `R11` terminates. “Evaluates” means execute/reconcile only if its exact-owned precondition is proved, otherwise publish `skipped(preconditionNotReached|unsafeForeignOrAmbiguousIdentity)`. Every other forward ordinal receives `skipped(priorTerminalFailure)`. No undefined “cleanup suffix” exists.

Each ordinal has one exact three-leaf state machine. Evidence/result without intent is BLOCKED. Intent asks the native gateway to run the declared reconciliation/effect: proved-crossed yields reconciled evidence/result; proved-not-crossed executes the effect once and yields direct evidence/result; ambiguous yields indeterminate evidence/result and enters its fixed failure route. The gateway constructs the canonical evidence/result itself from its authenticated tool-execution bodies, then its `seal-operation-evidence-result-v1` broker contract CMS-encrypts evidence and owns the crash-safe two-leaf publication `op-NN-evidence.cms → op-NN-result.json`. The corresponding child payload is `K4EvidenceSealBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_prefix_sha256,lease_fencing_token,ordinal,intent_digest,tool_execution_receipt_sha256s,evidence_plaintext_digest,evidence_sha256,result_digest,authorized_at_utc}`; the complete envelope's raw hash is `evidence_seal_receipt_sha256`, and its common `body_digest` is not a second receipt digest. Parent binding exists only in `GatewayChildBodyV1`. Intent+evidence with no result is adoptable only through the root journal's byte-identical envelope; without it, it is BLOCKED—producer never decrypts or re-packs. Result without exact evidence, two evidence leaves, wrong chain/digest, or extra leaf is BLOCKED. All three present are checked against the seal receipt; the attester later decrypts and fully validates plaintext/tool-receipt equality. It never retries an uncertain effect and never regenerates/overwrites nondeterministic CMS. Cleanup mutates only exact-owned identities. Any indeterminate, failed, or unsafe-skipped result makes approval impossible, but the complete routed journal and private failure disposition are still sealed after safe cleanup.

After all 12 results exist, capture has two mutually exclusive ordered recovery products. On full success, `capture-input-receipt.json` is exactly `{schema_version:1,producer_authorization_sha256,run_state_sha256,prepared_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,operation_journal_root,private_capture_manifest_digest,product_root,profile_projection_root,runtime_root,source_root,build_root,app_absence_result_digest,container_absence_result_digest,disposition:"success",receipt_digest}` with `private_capture_manifest_digest` equal to the sole defined `control/private-capture-manifest-v1.json.manifest_digest` and `receipt_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-CAPTURE-INPUT-RECEIPT-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)) || canonicalReceiptWithoutDigest)`. Journal-incomplete/receipt-absent resumes the next ordinal; journal-incomplete/receipt-present is BLOCKED; journal-complete/receipt-absent rederives and no-replace publishes it; both complete rederive and byte-compare it.

If any routed result is failed/indeterminate/unsafe-skipped, capture-input and ciphertext/projection remain absent and the producer instead no-replace publishes mode-`0600` `failure-receipt.json = {schema_version:1,producer_authorization_sha256,run_state_sha256,prepared_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,operation_journal_root,first_terminal_ordinal,first_terminal_result_digest,routed_result_digests,app_absence_result,container_absence_result,cleanup_disposition,incident_code,receipt_digest}`. `routed_result_digests[]` has exactly twelve `{ordinal,result_digest}` entries; absence results are `proved(D) | unavailable(reasonCode)`; cleanup is `complete | incomplete | unsafeAmbiguity`; and digest domain is `QINAO-K4-FAILURE-RECEIPT-V1\0` over the object without `receipt_digest`. Journal incomplete/failure present, failure+capture-input, or any mismatched incumbent is BLOCKED; complete journal/failure absent derives it once; complete journal/failure present is read-only reverified. It is local incident evidence, never an approval input.

Only then is the canonical container built and encrypted. Producer sends the container FD plus recipient **commitment** to the gateway's `cms-encrypt-v1` broker; the native gateway alone resolves the raw recipient/keychain through its root-owned input channel. The gateway first journals the exact universal Prepared effect, then encrypts and exclusively publishes ciphertext, then journals an Adoption whose result payload is `K4CiphertextPublicationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,device_lease_chain_sha256,capture_input_receipt_sha256,private_container_root,recipient_key_id,recipient_fingerprint,authorized_artifact_locator_commitment,ciphertext_sha256,authorized_at_utc,published_at_utc,retention_policy_digest,retention_expires_at_utc}`. The locator byte-equals producer authorization/run state/retention/certification. The complete authenticated Adoption envelope's raw hash is `ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256`; its common `body_digest` is not renamed `receipt_digest`, and its external cache is replayable from the journal. Prepared plus absent poststate performs the effect once; Prepared plus the unique exact ciphertext poststate publishes only Adoption; ciphertext without Prepared, a mismatched incumbent, or an ambiguous poststate is BLOCKED. Both journal edges plus ciphertext reverify recipient, locator, time/fence/policy, and ciphertext digest without decryption. Producer never receives a decryption identity/keychain and never performs ciphertext-only recovery by decrypting.

After the exact ciphertext pair, producer derives the public projection solely from already sealed roots and the ciphertext receipt. It builds an absent private sibling directory containing exactly the 20 P0 leaves, writes the canonical publication manifest beside rather than inside that sibling, and fsyncs the complete tree. Over the already accepted producer invocation's broker FD it sends the closed `publish-directory-v1` request with class `private-projection`, source/destination/manifest path commitments, expected root, and parent accepted-receipt SHA; the daemon/worker resolve private paths, exclusively publish, and return the authenticated child receipt. The sandbox never recursively invokes the CLI. Projection-without the exact ciphertext pair is BLOCKED; ciphertext-pair-only republishes only this uniquely derived directory; an incumbent projection is recursively byte-compared without opening a writer. Captured absent is then derived from those exact roots; an incumbent captured receipt is reverified byte-for-byte.

Attestation requires exact captured and runs under the distinct `k4-attester` gateway profile. It decrypts and validates privately, publishes/adopts only one no-replace CMS plus `attested.json`, and never invokes a device/build mutation. `finalize-bundle-or-verify` requires exact attested, builds the exact 23-leaf approved tree as a private sibling on the approved encrypted volume, and fsyncs every file/directory. Over its accepted broker channel it requests class `approved-candidate-bundle`; the gateway exclusively publishes the authorization-bound **external** immutable bundle and authenticated candidate-bundle publication receipt, never repository bytes. A signed proof CandidateUnit binds only attested receipt plus exact bundle manifest/root/publication receipt; isolated review validates all 23 files, and `git-transaction` alone adopts/commits them. Only after that transaction succeeds does the separate `seal-finalized-or-verify` invocation reopen the Git receipt/root journal and publish/adopt non-public `finalized.json` binding candidate-bundle receipt, proof Git transaction receipt, commit, and tree. Crash tests cover every leaf edge and every boundary between external publication, CandidateUnit review, Git adoption, and finalized seal. No resume branch replaces an incumbent or asks one invocation to span the external signature/review gap.

Both directory publications are external effects. Universal Prepared/dispatch precedes one `GatewayEffectAdoptionBodyV1` whose `result_payload` is exact `K4DirectoryPublicationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,destination_class,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,source_directory_path_commitment,destination_directory_path_commitment,manifest_sha256,expected_root,observed_source_root,observed_destination_root,published_at_utc,rename_result,adoption_disposition}`. For P0, class is `private-projection` and the complete **Adoption envelope** SHA is `projection_publication_receipt_sha256`; final evidence uses `approved-candidate-bundle` and its Adoption envelope is the candidate-bundle publication receipt. `renamedExclusive/newlyPublished` requires direct executed disposition; `destinationAlreadyByteIdentical/adoptedExactIncumbent` is legal only through authenticated query plus `reconciledUniquePoststate`. Cross-class/path/root reuse, repository destination, partial equality, body-only locator, or success without Prepared/dispatch/RENAME_EXCL-or-reconciliation fails.

Tests terminate before/after every intent, effect return, evidence, result, capture-input, ciphertext, projection, phase receipt, attestation, and directory publication. They exhaust all legal/illegal three-leaf and outer-product states, every `R00...R11` route, direct/reconciled/indeterminate/skipped retagging, wrong predecessor, duplicate effect, foreign identity, and mismatched but well-formed incumbent.

An inconsistent/partial incumbent that cannot be independently reconstructed is BLOCKED, not deleted. Restart requires a separately authenticated operator incident/crypto-erasure record, verified destruction of the old ciphertext key/leaf under retention policy, and a **new** run directory, run ID, artifact path, and signed operator authorization; no command in this plan performs that recovery automatically. The crash matrix above requires byte-identical successful resume whenever reconciliation is conclusive and deterministic blocking otherwise.

The producer must:

1. require exact Git root, normalized repository-relative source/output, no symlink component, and an absent output path on first creation; on resume, permit only the exact owner-held incumbent ciphertext/projection/attestation/output leaves admitted by the state table and open them read-only;
2. require `developer-dir` to resolve beneath one Xcode app, require bundle ID `com.apple.dt.Xcode`, verify the Apple anchor/designated code requirement, reject `Contents/Resources/BetaVersion.plist`, bind `Contents/version.plist`, require its `ProductBuildVersion` to match `xcodebuild -version`, capture exact Xcode/Swift/iPhoneOS SDK outputs, and require the Xcode/device-OS pair in the reviewed allowlist;
3. copy only source/project/fixture inputs into a private temporary probe, replace every beta template snapshot/generated metadata/log with fresh release-Xcode outputs, and reject any beta/preliminary log in the candidate probe;
4. unsigned-build/typecheck the exact public shapes, then generate a private per-target xcconfig/project overlay mapping `K4SpikeHost` to host bundle ID `com.qinao.K4SpikeHost` and its profile, and `K4SpikeExtension` to extension bundle ID `com.qinao.K4SpikeHost.EnhancedSecurity` and its separate profile, under `CODE_SIGN_STYLE=Manual`. These two exact IDs are repeated in policy, producer authorization, run state, StableIdentifiers, every operation contract, signed products, and cleanup queries. Parse `xcodebuild -showBuildSettings` separately for both targets and require distinct exact profile/team/bundle values before archive; repository placeholder IDs, swapped/collapsed IDs, or one global selector fails. The proof tool never uses `-allowProvisioningUpdates` and never mutates Developer Portal state; profile creation/refresh is a separate explicitly authorized operator preflight;
5. find the ExtensionKit product under `Host.app/Extensions/`, strictly verify both signatures, decode both embedded **development** profiles only in the private temporary directory, validate separate non-zero/distinct profile UUID commitments, team, bundle IDs, App IDs, validity interval, and the extension's exact seven Enhanced Security keys, and prove each signed binary's leaf certificate is present in the corresponding profile `DeveloperCertificates` set;
6. retain the exact signed Host product only inside the encrypted private artifact; that product necessarily contains its two embedded profiles and signing certificates. Never create a separately extracted profile/certificate/device-list leaf, and never place those raw bytes, raw UUID/UDID, or account paths in the public projection/repository. Retain only the two privacy-clean actual-entitlement plists as separately listed private/public projections;
7. use `devicectl --json-output` for detailed physical-device facts, install, detached launch, termination, relaunch, and app-container copy; reject simulator/platform drift and hash the raw UDID. Bind install `launchServicesIdentifier` into launch via `--launch-persistent-identifier`, require the lease-bound immediate process-absence check above, forbid `--terminate-existing`, bind launch PID/result, never use blocking `--console`, and retrieve only files whose run/source/build identities match this invocation;
8. retrieve both app-written phase traces/event logs. Require seed order `monitor -> direct -> sqlite_pragmas -> sqlite_commit -> async -> cleanup -> pass` and reopen order `monitor -> sqlite_reopen -> host_access_denied -> cleanup -> pass`; require one of each phase event, exact correlations/routes/responder/delivery, WAL/FULL, exact marker/HWM, changed process identities, zero pending/unexpected replies, and no FAIL event anywhere;
9. Before container construction, `capture-or-verify` publishes `control/gateway-operation-receipts.json = K4GatewayCaptureReceiptsV1`. Its thirteen capture/build IDs are exactly the `capture_build_contracts` array embedded in the policy's complete operation registry: `sourceTreeSnapshot, injectRunIdentity, unsignedShapeTypecheck, generatePrivateSigningOverlay, showHostBuildSettings, showExtensionBuildSettings, archiveSignedProducts, verifyHostSignature, verifyExtensionSignature, decodeHostProfile, decodeExtensionProfile, projectHostEntitlements, projectExtensionEntitlements`. One logical authenticated gateway envelope exists per ID; its payload may bind an internal bounded attempt/adoption journal but cannot add a second logical result. The closed aggregate object is `{schema_version:1,operation_contract_registry_root,capture_build_receipts,operation_tool_execution_receipts,evidence_seal_receipts,capture_build_root,operation_tool_execution_root,evidence_seal_root,aggregate_root}`. `capture_build_receipts[]` has exactly 13 registry-ordered complete envelopes.

Each device ordinal owns exactly eight registry slots: `precondition, primary-a, primary-b, postcondition, reconcile-a, reconcile-b, failure-route, adoption-check`; inapplicable slots are typed N/A and present slots contain exactly one complete envelope. Op 04 alone may use both primary slots. Op 02/op 06's immediate process-absence recheck occurs inside the same launch broker request and is authenticated as that receipt's `pre_effect_identity`, not a separate receipt. `operation_tool_execution_receipts[]` is every and only present slot referenced by intents/evidence/results, ordered by receipt sequence. A clean direct-success run is exactly 25 receipts (12 preconditions + 13 primary children); the proof-by-construction maximum is `12 ordinals × 8 slots = 96`. `evidence_seal_receipts[]` contains exactly twelve ordinal-ordered complete seal envelopes. Roots use distinct domains `QINAO-K4-GATEWAY-CAPTURE-BUILD-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0`, `QINAO-K4-GATEWAY-OPERATION-TOOL-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0`, and `QINAO-K4-GATEWAY-SEAL-RECEIPT-SET-V1\0`; `aggregate_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-GATEWAY-CAPTURE-RECEIPTS-V1\0" || captureBuildRootRaw32 || operationToolExecutionRootRaw32 || evidenceSealRootRaw32)`. `gateway_operation_receipts_root` everywhere else is exactly this semantic `aggregate_root`; it is not raw SHA-256 of the control file. Every build/slot/seal hash resolves once, and unused/duplicate/missing envelopes fail. It also publishes the final lease-chain control file; both files reopen every envelope against the per-scope accumulator and root journal.

   It computes `operation_journal_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-OPERATION-JOURNAL-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalJournalLeafArray)) || canonicalJournalLeafArray)` over the exact **42-leaf** path/mode/full-byte array: six control leaves `control/policy-admission.json`, `control/producer-authorization.json`, `control/run-state.json`, `control/prepared.json`, `control/device-run-lease-chain.json`, `control/gateway-operation-receipts.json`, and for each ordinal 00–11 exactly `journal/op-NN-intent.json`, `journal/op-NN-evidence.cms`, and `journal/op-NN-result.json`, globally unsigned-UTF-8 sorted. It then no-replace publishes `control/capture-input-receipt.json`; success container has those **43** exact control/journal leaves, one manifest leaf, and the 22 raw leaves below—**66 entries total**. It excludes failure receipt, ciphertext and its later gateway receipt, projection, `captured.json`, attestation, `attested.json`, approved output, and `finalized.json`, preventing self-hash cycles. The plaintext-container root, journal root, ciphertext digest, run-state digest, policy-admission/producer-authorization digests, manifest digest, capture-input-receipt digest, lease chain, and gateway operation receipt roots are repeated in projection, captured receipt, later attestation, and approved status.

   The complete container is CMS-encrypted to the policy-pinned encryption-only organizational recipient and published in the state-machine order above; capture deliberately does **not** create the canonical approved directory. A distinct `k4-attester` gateway invocation launches `attest_k4_external_artifact.py` only with repository/run/producer-authorization/broker FDs and committed logical leaf names. The native worker resolves the raw decryption keychain/private identity and attestation signing identity from the policy-bound credential set; none appears in shell, environment, Python argv, receipt, or repository. The attester is the only initial requester of the no-replace attestation transaction, decrypts privately through the broker, recomputes the exact journal leaf set/root and every three-leaf transition, redoes codesign/CMS/profile/leaf/source/build/runtime/cleanup checks, and requests CMS signing with the separate policy-pinned non-revoked organizational identity. Its existing-attestation branch is read-only and rederives exact bytes/state. Later `verify-existing` reconstructs the full journal from ciphertext without the original plaintext run directory. Encryption and signing certificates/keys/usages are distinct and neither may be a product-signing leaf. `check_k4_platform_proof.py` remains side-effect-free; its CMS/certificate checks are brokered by the native verifier and it receives only structured privacy-clean results. Mutations omit/swap/retag one intent/evidence/result, alter its chain edge, or preserve the capture-input digest while changing the journal and must fail;
10. `finalize-bundle-or-verify` verifies the attestation signature/epoch/freshness and exact run/artifact/projection/policy/phase digests, adds it to a private sibling projection, invokes the pure semantic `validate_platform_proof` seam against that external candidate bundle, scans every would-be public byte for forbidden raw profile/device/account/workspace values, and asks the gateway to publish or byte-adopt only the external `approved-candidate-bundle`. The signed proof CandidateUnit, isolated GREEN review, and `git-transaction` are the sole repository adoption path; `seal-finalized-or-verify` runs only afterward and binds the exact Git transaction receipt/commit/tree. Before initial capture, query/reconcile any installed probe and reject an unknown build/run. After success or failure, cancel/invalidate, terminate, uninstall the exact bundle, and verify app/container absence; cleanup is query/reconcile-only and never blindly repeats a possibly crossed operation. Cleanup failure is sealed in the private failure receipt and blocks approval. Preliminary evidence is untouched.

Subprocess execution uses verified absolute executables only: system `/usr/bin/{xcrun,codesign,security,plutil,git}` and `$DEVELOPER_DIR/usr/bin/xcodebuild` or another exact executable resolved inside the already verified Xcode bundle. Producer, attester, checker, and review-candidate entry points run only under the Task-0-signed `QINAO_ADMISSION_PYTHON` with `-I -B -S`; before any device/artifact effect each independently recomputes its interpreter/real-path commitments, bytes/version/implementation and full standard-library closure. Any sibling code is loaded only by a pinned exact Git blob/path through an explicit verified file loader, never through `sys.path`; the committed K4 test pins prove this works under isolated safe-path mode. The producer binds canonical tool commitments, digests, code-signature/version facts in the attestation, uses a minimal allowlisted environment with exact `DEVELOPER_DIR`, and clears toolchain/Python/dynamic-loader override variables. A fake leading `PATH`, alternate Python, `GIT_*`, `PYTHON*`, and script-directory-shadow test proves no surrogate runs. No shell inside the tools, manifest-provided command, `eval`, or user-provided executable is permitted.

The private path contract is executable and applies jointly to the capture root, run directory, broker journals, build/archive/product staging, projection sibling, final candidate-bundle sibling, process `HOME`/`TMPDIR`, plaintext-container FD backing, attester decrypt directory, and ciphertext parent—not only the final ciphertext. Every root is an absolute normalized path outside the repository, iCloud/Dropbox/CI-artifact roots, and backup-managed roots; has no symlink component; is caller-owned mode `0700`; and resolves to the same policy-pinned approved encrypted-local-volume identity. The gateway derives that identity from the opened volume/device facts and requires its digest to equal `approved_encrypted_volume_identity_sha256`; an environment string cannot self-assert encryption. Sensitive leaves are mode `0600`, link-count one, no-replace, fsynced, and excluded from backup/indexing. An absent ciphertext leaf is required on first creation, or the exact owner-held incumbent is admitted read-only on resume. A mismatch, unexpected incumbent, cross-volume temp path, unencrypted/default `/tmp`, or no-replace race fails rather than overwriting. Plaintext uses a per-run cryptographic protection domain on the approved encrypted APFS volume; close+unlink is cleanup, not a claim of physical byte wiping. Destruction semantics require authenticated per-run key destruction/crypto-erasure receipt. The attester has its own custody-owned subtree inaccessible to producer.

Plaintext container grammar is fixed ASCII magic `QINAO-K4-PRIVATE-CONTAINER-V1\0`, uint64-BE entry count `66`, then the globally sorted path/mode/content-length/content records; total plaintext is at most `2147483648` bytes. Only the CMS EnvelopedData ciphertext is retained. The attester decrypts to a private mode-`0600` FD in its separate per-run cryptographic domain on the same approved volume, validates/re-packs to byte identity, closes/unlinks it, and obtains key-destruction evidence before claiming cryptographic erasure. APFS copy-on-write means overwrite/unlink is never described as verified physical wiping. Tests cover exact 66-entry membership/count, truncated/extra/duplicate/path-alias/oversize containers, decrypt with wrong identity/keychain, signing key incorrectly reused for encryption, ciphertext tamper, nondeterministic repack, wrong owner/mode, existing/racing leaf, repository/cloud/backup/default-temp/cross-volume path, volume-identity drift, over-retention, and cleanup failure.

`control/private-capture-manifest-v1.json` is exactly `{schema_version:1,entries,raw_entry_count:22,raw_maximum_bytes:1587544064,manifest_digest}`. Each sorted/unique entry is `{path,mode:"0600",max_bytes,artifact_kind,source_contract_id,content_sha256}`; `manifest_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-PRIVATE-CAPTURE-MANIFEST-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalManifestWithoutDigest)) || canonicalManifestWithoutDigest)`. Its `entries` tuple is exactly:

| path | max bytes | artifact kind / source contract |
|---|---:|---|
| `raw/build/archive-result.json` | 16777216 | buildOutput / xcodeArchiveResultV1 |
| `raw/build/extension-build-settings.json` | 8388608 | buildOutput / extensionBuildSettingsV1 |
| `raw/build/host-build-settings.json` | 8388608 | buildOutput / hostBuildSettingsV1 |
| `raw/build/private-overlay.qbundle` | 33554432 | buildInput / generatedSigningOverlayV1 |
| `raw/device/device-info.json` | 16777216 | device / physicalDeviceFactsV1 |
| `raw/entitlements/extension.plist` | 1048576 | entitlement / actualExtensionEntitlementsV1 |
| `raw/entitlements/host.plist` | 1048576 | entitlement / actualHostEntitlementsV1 |
| `raw/products/host-app.qbundle` | 1073741824 | signedProduct / canonicalSignedHostProductV1 |
| `raw/runtime/invalidation-ack.json` | 4194304 | runtime / deviceInvalidationAckV1 |
| `raw/runtime/reopen-events.jsonl` | 67108864 | runtime / deviceReopenEventsV1 |
| `raw/runtime/reopen-trace.json` | 67108864 | runtime / deviceReopenTraceV1 |
| `raw/runtime/runtime-route-attestation.json` | 4194304 | runtime / authenticatedRouteAttestationV1 |
| `raw/runtime/seed-events.jsonl` | 67108864 | runtime / deviceSeedEventsV1 |
| `raw/runtime/seed-trace.json` | 67108864 | runtime / deviceSeedTraceV1 |
| `raw/runtime/source-build-identity.json` | 4194304 | runtime / sourceBuildIdentityV1 |
| `raw/runtime/sqlite-reopen-attestation.json` | 4194304 | runtime / sqliteReopenAttestationV1 |
| `raw/signatures/extension-details.log` | 16777216 | signature / codesignDetailsExtensionV1 |
| `raw/signatures/extension-verify.log` | 16777216 | signature / codesignStrictVerifyExtensionV1 |
| `raw/signatures/host-details.log` | 16777216 | signature / codesignDetailsHostV1 |
| `raw/signatures/host-verify.log` | 16777216 | signature / codesignStrictVerifyHostV1 |
| `raw/source/source-probe.qbundle` | 67108864 | source / exactStage0ProbeTreeV1 |
| `raw/toolchain/toolchain-facts.json` | 8388608 | toolchain / verifiedAppleToolchainFactsV1 |

`qbundle` uses the same fixed magic/count/sorted length-framed regular-file grammar recursively, allows product modes `100644|100755` and source/overlay mode `100644`, strips no bytes, and rejects symlink/hardlink/xattr/device/case/Unicode aliasing. `host-app.qbundle` alone contains the exact signed Host.app tree and therefore its nested extension, embedded profiles, and signature material; there is no separately extracted raw profile/certificate/device-list entry. The source qbundle is private build provenance only and is never copied into approved repository evidence. Any missing/extra manifest/control/raw entry, wrong count/mode/limit/source contract, or content digest mismatch fails before encryption.

Define both logical external-artifact and repository-evidence root digests with the same domain-separated grammar: UTF-8 tag, uint64-BE entry count, then globally sorted normalized relative path; for each entry, uint64-BE path length/path bytes, fixed mode/type, uint64-BE content length, and exact content bytes. Only regular files with approved modes are permitted; directories are implicit; symlinks, hardlinks, xattrs, devices, duplicate/case-colliding/Unicode-alias paths, and extras fail. Capture, attester, checker, runner, and W6 use golden vectors and independently recompute the digest.

The attester is organizational and independent: its leaf/key and custody principal differ from producer and both host/extension signing leaves. CMS validation builds a private temporary keychain containing only policy-pinned chain material, decodes SignedData, then runs the fixed `security verify-cert` path at CMS signing time and compares leaf/anchor fingerprints/EKU/usage; it never trusts an arbitrary local Keychain. Mutations cover same leaf/key/custody, locally trusted but unpinned signer, pinned signer absent from the default keychain, and incomplete/wrong chain.

Update the status schema and bound root artifacts:

This is the complete unsigned-UTF-8 sorted `K4_PUBLIC_EVIDENCE_PATHS_V1` inventory: exactly **23 regular files**—20 pre-attestation projections plus the gateway-receipt projection, CMS, and status. A prefix, glob, directory-recursive rule, source fixture, product, profile, certificate, DB, or extra file is forbidden. In particular `scripts/fixtures/qinao-k4-platform-probe/` remains a stage-0 tooling input and never appears below approved evidence.

Replace `profile_uuid_sha256` with `host_profile_uuid_sha256` and `extension_profile_uuid_sha256`. All identity commitments must be lowercase SHA-256, non-zero, and semantically matched to their redacted projections. `toolchain-attestation.json` binds the combined Apple-platform policy digest, official source records, verified absolute tool paths/digests/signatures, Xcode bundle signature facts, `version.plist` digest, absence of beta marker, and exact Xcode/Swift/SDK output. The checker receives the candidate root explicitly, materializes the policy bytes from the candidate index, and rejects worktree-only or self-declared release status.

The real checker has no caller-supplied Team ID: it reads the exact public `expected_team_id` from the candidate-index policy blob and compares both signed products and retained projections. `validate_platform_proof(root, evidence, expected_team_id=...)` remains the pure semantic seam used by synthetic tests/finalize, but production CLI rejects any Team-ID override. This prevents CI secrets or caller environment from redefining signing identity.

Parse runtime event JSON structurally; console strings are non-evidence. Reject every `FAIL`, duplicate, missing, reordered, stale-run, wrong-source/build, or contradictory event. Verify the trace attestation says `device_app_container`, binds exact install persistent identifier/launch PIDs/termination/copy results, and binds both phase files. Verify `sqlite-reopen-attestation.json` proves extension-private WAL/FULL, transactional marker/HWM, changed helper process, reopen equality, and host denial; reject DB/WAL/SHM persistence.

Signed Host/Extension products and raw profiles remain in one access-controlled immutable external artifact because Mach-O CMS/profile payloads may contain personal certificate/device identity. A second independent verifier reruns `codesign --verify --strict`, compares CodeDirectory/CDHash, Info.plist, entitlements, source/build identity, Apple-signed profile CMS, and leaf-certificate membership, then emits `signed-products-external-attestation.cms` containing only the signed privacy-clean payload. The repository checker validates CMS chain/signature and all retained public semantics; full revalidation requires authorized retrieval of the external artifact. It never claims a redacted projection or digest is itself a CMS/binary verification.

The three CMS protocols are disjoint and use these exact broker-owned argv templates. Inputs are stable FD-verified paths; every `-o` names an initially absent unpredictable broker-owned staging leaf and is exclusively renamed to the authorized final name only after validation/fsync. Encryption is `/usr/bin/security cms -E -u 5 -r S(encryptionRecipientNickname) -k D(encryptionCertificateOnlyKeychain) -i D(plaintextContainer) -o D(stagingCiphertext)`. The producer-visible keychain contains only the pinned public recipient/chain and no private key. Decryption is `/usr/bin/security cms -D -u 5 -k D(decryptionKeychain) -i D(ciphertext) -o D(stagingPlaintext)`, after the gateway proves that the custody-isolated keychain has exactly one usable private identity whose public key/fingerprint equals policy; the human-readable identity environment is a commitment cross-check and is not passed as an unsupported `security cms` selector. Signing is `/usr/bin/security cms -S -u 6 -H SHA256 -G -N S(attestationSigningIdentity) -k D(attestationSigningKeychain) -i D(wrapper) -o D(stagingAttestation)`. Decode/verify is `/usr/bin/security cms -D -u 6 -k D(policyChainOnlyVerificationKeychain) -i D(attestation) -o D(stagingRecoveredWrapper)`. Certificate-path verification then uses `/usr/bin/security verify-cert -N -L -p basic -d D(cmsSigningTimeGMT)`, followed by leaf-first repeated `-c D(certificateDER)` and exactly one `-r D(pinnedRootDER)`. CMS usage `6`, the policy-pinned object-signer EKU/key usage, recovered signer chain/fingerprints, and decoded signing-time attribute are all independently required. No default keychain/search list/network, SHA-1/default hash, password argv, stdin/stdout payload, ambiguous nickname, extra recipient/signer, alternate usage, or missing `-N/-k/-i/-o/-G` is accepted. Golden argv tests use the pinned macOS `security cms`/`verify-cert` grammar and reject flag reorder that changes parsing.

`security cms -D` proves/recovers content but is not treated as a signing-time extractor. The signed worker contains a strict, non-negotiating DER SignedData metadata decoder whose binary/code identity and test-vector-set digest are pinned in `worker_identity` and `tool_identity_root`. It accepts exactly one definite-length DER ContentInfo/SignedData, one signer, embedded content, SHA-256 digest, the policy-pinned signature algorithm, one canonical signingTime signed attribute, and no duplicate/unknown critical structure. It emits closed `K4CMSSignedDataMetadataV1 = {schema_version:1,content_type_oid,signed_data_version,digest_algorithm_oid,signature_algorithm_oid,signer_count,signer_identifier_digest,certificate_fingerprint_sha256s,embedded_content_sha256,detached_content:false,signing_time_utc,metadata_digest}`. `certificate_fingerprint_sha256s` is the non-empty, unique leaf-to-anchor ordered array of lowercase raw-DER SHA-256 values and byte-equals the policy-pinned attestation-signing chain, including its leaf exactly once. `metadata_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-CMS-SIGNEDDATA-METADATA-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalWithoutDigest)) || canonicalWithoutDigest)`. Recovered wrapper bytes, DER metadata, `security cms` verification, and `verify-cert` chain/time must agree. BER/indefinite length, duplicate attribute, fractional/non-UTC/noncanonical time, multiple signer, detached content, or parser/command disagreement fails.

The CMS-signing effect's Adoption result payload is exact `K4CMSSigningBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,pre_attestation_verification_receipt_sha256,wrapper_sha256,attestation_signing_identity_digest,attestation_signing_keychain_commitment,cms_argv_digest,attestation_sha256,cms_metadata,authorized_at_utc,published_at_utc}`. `attestation_signing_identity_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-ATTESTATION-SIGNING-IDENTITY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalPolicyAttestationSigningIdentityBytes)) || canonicalPolicyAttestationSigningIdentityBytes)` and must select the sole active attester policy identity. `attestation_signing_keychain_commitment = SHA256("QINAO-K4-ATTESTATION-SIGNING-KEYCHAIN-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalCredentialProjectionBytes)) || canonicalCredentialProjectionBytes)` over exact `{native_credential_set_id,keychain_store_identity_sha256,access_group_commitment,keychain_acl_requirement_sha256,certificate_der_sha256,public_key_sha256}` resolved by the native worker, never a raw keychain path. `cms_argv_digest` byte-equals the `argv_digest` in the authenticated CMS-sign tool result carried by this Adoption's predecessor chain; no second argv vocabulary exists. Publication occurs only after Prepared, successful signing, strict CMS revalidation, exclusive rename, and fsync; `cms_metadata.signing_time_utc` equals the attested-phase time and is within two trusted-clock seconds of `published_at_utc`. The complete Adoption envelope's raw SHA is `cms_signing_receipt_sha256`. Mutations swap policy identity, credential set, ACL, argv digest, or Adoption/Prepared edge and fail.

Before signing, the native worker/daemon complete all decrypt/container/product/profile/runtime/policy checks and publish an authenticated gateway envelope whose `GatewayChildBodyV1.payload` is `K4PreAttestationVerificationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,gateway_operation_receipts_root,device_lease_chain_sha256,capture_input_receipt_sha256,captured_receipt_sha256,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,projection_root,attester_policy_identity_digest,verified_at_utc,verification_disposition:"eligibleToSign"}`. Its full-envelope raw hash is `pre_attestation_verification_receipt_sha256`; signing is forbidden unless that exact journal entry is current and all intervals/fences remain valid.

Attestation body bytes are UTF-8 canonical JSON with sorted unique keys, compact separators, and no trailing LF. `K4ExternalAttestationBodyV1` is exactly `{schema_version:1,attestation_schema:"qinao-k4-external-attestation-v1",run_id,source_commit,source_tree,input_probe_digest,built_probe_digest,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,platform_policy_authorization_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,gateway_operation_receipts_root,device_lease_chain_sha256,operation_journal_root,capture_input_receipt_sha256,captured_receipt_sha256,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,ciphertext_published_at_utc,retention_policy_digest,retention_locator_commitment,retention_expires_at_utc,projection_root,host_bundle_id,extension_bundle_id,host_cdhash,extension_cdhash,host_profile_uuid_sha256,extension_profile_uuid_sha256,app_absence_result_digest,container_absence_result_digest,attester_key_id,attester_principal_id,attester_epoch,attester_policy_identity_digest,verification_completed_at_utc,pre_attestation_verification_receipt_sha256}`. `attester_policy_identity_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-ATTESTER-POLICY-IDENTITY-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalK4EvidenceAttesterPolicyV1Bytes)) || canonicalK4EvidenceAttesterPolicyV1Bytes)`. Compute `domain_sha256 = SHA256("QINAO-K4-EXTERNAL-ATTESTATION-V1\0" || uint64be(body-length) || body)`, then create the canonical wrapper `{"body":<body-object>,"domain_sha256":"..."}\n` and sign it with the exact argv above. The CMS signing-time attribute, rather than filesystem time, is copied into `attested.json`; it must be no earlier than `verification_completed_at_utc`, no more than two trusted-clock seconds later, and inside all policy/certificate/retention intervals. The checker decodes/verifies the dedicated non-personal organizational chain, pins policy leaf/anchor/public-key fingerprints and object-signer usage/epoch, recomputes the body commitment, and compares recovered wrapper bytes exactly without depending on local Keychain trust. Mutations cover every body field plus unknown/self-injected/revoked/stale keys, old epoch, SHA-1/default/wrong usage, duplicate verifier, replayed run, altered locator/artifact/policy/product digest, non-canonical bytes, signing-time skew, and expired retention.

After the attester invocation has completed and before status, finalizer creates `gateway-prepublication-receipts.json = K4GatewayPrepublicationReceiptsV1`. `K4_GATEWAY_ATTESTATION_TOOL_REGISTRY_V1` is the exact ordered ID tuple `decryptPrivateContainer, validatePrivateContainer, verifyHostCodesign, verifyExtensionCodesign, decodeHostProfile, decodeExtensionProfile, verifyProfileCertificateMembership`; it yields exactly seven logical post-lease tool Adoption envelopes before the separate CMS-sign Adoption envelope. The public object's exact schema is `{schema_version:1,service_registration_envelopes,invocation_service_generation_rows,invocation_service_generation_root,producer_authorization,scope_receipts,scope_cutoff_sequence,scope_cutoff_receipt_sha256,scope_genesis_receipt_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,invocation_schedule_contract_root,invocation_schedule_instance,invocation_schedule_instance_root,recovery_insertion_rows,recovery_insertion_root,invocation_accepted_receipt_sha256s,invocation_completed_receipt_sha256s,effect_prepared_receipt_sha256s,effect_dispatch_entered_receipt_sha256s,effect_recovery_observation_receipt_sha256s,effect_reauthorization_receipt_sha256s,process_termination_observation_receipt_sha256s,capture_build_receipt_sha256s,device_lease_event_receipt_sha256s,operation_tool_execution_receipt_sha256s,evidence_seal_receipt_sha256s,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,projection_publication_receipt_sha256,attestation_tool_receipt_sha256s,pre_attestation_verification_receipt_sha256,cms_signing_receipt_sha256,attested_seal_receipt_sha256,gateway_receipt_projection_root}`. `producer_authorization` is the complete signed canonical privacy-clean commitment-only object; its raw SHA equals every repeated `producer_authorization_sha256`, and the public checker verifies its Task-0/policy key, role, signature, interval, registry, and path/selector commitments without learning raw values.

`gateway_journal_epoch` is exactly the receipt authentication-key epoch, not an alias for service epoch. `service_registration_envelopes[]` contains every complete dedicated registration referenced by a K4 Accepted/rebind, sorted/unique by `(gateway_journal_epoch,receipt_sequence,raw_sha256)`; external-scope envelopes are not `scope_receipts`. `K4InvocationServiceGenerationRowV1 = {invocation_accepted_receipt_sha256,initial_registration_receipt_sha256,service_generation_chain_rows,service_generation_chain_root,invocation_completed_receipt_sha256,final_registration_receipt_sha256,final_observation_generation}` has one row per Accepted. Initial registration byte-equals `Accepted.body.service_registration_receipt_sha256`; rows/root/final pair byte-equal Completed's corresponding service-chain/final fields, and all registrations occur once externally. Rows root under `QINAO-K4-INVOCATION-SERVICE-GENERATIONS-V1\0`. `K4RecoveryInsertionRowV1 = {after_base_slot_ordinal,recovery_ordinal,kind,receipt_sha256,trigger_receipt_sha256,effect_plan_root_or_NA,effect_ordinal_or_NA,effect_id_or_NA,service_registration_tip_sha256}` uses kind `serviceRebind | effectDispatchEntered | effectRecoveryObservation | effectReauthorization | processTerminationObservation`. Normal first dispatch is a fixed base support slot; only a reauthorization marker is a dynamic insertion. Recovery rows are secondary indexes anchored between fixed business slots and root under `QINAO-K4-RECOVERY-INSERTIONS-V1\0`. `attested_seal_receipt_sha256` byte-equals the sole seal child/status field.

`scope_receipts[]` is the contiguous K4 execution-scope sequence through `attesterInvocationCompleted`. The fixed schedule plus **primary** category arrays/scalars (Prepared, first dispatch, recovery observation, reauthorization, process termination, rebind service-chain rows, Adoption business categories, policy/pre-attestation/seal, Accepted, Completed) partitions every post-genesis receipt exactly once after excluding the complete scope array itself and secondary indexes. Service-generation and recovery-insertion rows are secondary indexes: each indexed receipt must occur once in the primary set and every eligible primary receipt must occur once in its index; double indexing is not double partition membership. External dedicated registrations remain outside scope receipts. Missing support, Adoption without Prepared/dispatch, schedule/index/category disagreement, external registration inside scope, or any gap/extra fails.

`gateway_receipt_projection_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-GATEWAY-PREPUBLICATION-RECEIPTS-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalObjectWithoutRoot)) || canonicalObjectWithoutRoot)`. The projection exposes no raw selectors, raw argv, stdout/stderr, profile/certificate bodies, paths, numeric UID/session, or secrets—only privacy-clean canonical gateway bodies/commitments. Final publication's later receipt cannot be a member and remains externally journal-verifiable. Public checker verifies every gateway signature and complete per-scope chain and cross-binds ciphertext, lease, build/tool results, evidence, CMS, and status; full artifact verification additionally reopens the global root journal. Forged/reordered/omitted/extra/cross-run receipts fail.

The same attester implements distinct read-only/idempotent `verify-existing`; candidate CLI receives only repository/run/broker FDs plus committed logical names and cannot sign, rewrite, publish, or mutate. It revalidates container, products, profiles, source/build, CMS, policy, retention, repository, and typed global-journal closure, then publishes exact `K4VerifyExistingBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,status:"pass",gateway_journal_epoch,gateway_authentication_key_id,head_commit,head_tree,transaction_chain_root,run_id,run_state_sha256,policy_admission_receipt_sha256,producer_authorization_sha256,platform_policy_sha256,platform_policy_authorization_sha256,operation_contract_registry_root,private_container_root,ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256,ciphertext_sha256,authorized_artifact_locator_commitment,projection_root,attestation_sha256,attested_receipt_sha256,candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256,proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256,k4_finalized_receipt_sha256,host_cdhash,extension_cdhash,host_profile_uuid_sha256,extension_profile_uuid_sha256,verifier_key_id,verifier_principal_id,verifier_epoch,retention_policy_digest,retention_expires_at_utc,trusted_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,erasure_registry_snapshot:K4CryptoErasureRegistrySnapshotV1,policy_acceptance_snapshot:K4PolicyAcceptanceSnapshotV1,key_revocation_snapshot:K4KeyRevocationSnapshotV1,retention_state:"retainedActiveInWindow",global_journal_inclusion_proof:K4GlobalJournalInclusionRowV1[],global_journal_inclusion_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,verified_at_utc}`. `K4PolicyAcceptanceSnapshotV1` and `K4KeyRevocationSnapshotV1` are exact `{registry_hwm,registry_root,target_identity_digest,lookup:active({entry_receipt_sha256})}`; erasure snapshot carries the exact queried key/digest and `absentAtHWM({matching_entry_receipt_sha256s:[]})`. `verified_at_utc` byte-equals the authenticated clock observation, is no later than expiry, and all target states are active/absent.

Inclusion rows use only the epoch-aware type below and one authentication key; `global_journal_inclusion_root = SHA256("QINAO-K4-GLOBAL-JOURNAL-INCLUSION-PROOF-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalProofRows)) || canonicalProofRows)`. The verify child is followed, immediately before successful Completed, by a second chained trusted-clock observation; Completed embeds that receipt SHA and requires its `completed_at_utc <= retention_expires_at_utc`. An expired/erased/revoked/stale-clock proof cannot form a successful terminal pair. External `k4-verify-existing.json` is only a mode-`0600` cache of the complete child envelope; certification reopens child plus successful Completed. Proof-row, epoch/root, clock, registry, locator, identity, or run substitution fails.

When an owner-held mode-`0600`, link-count-one cache already exists, the same operation enters read-only resume: it duplicate-key-rejects and re-canonicalizes the incumbent, verifies its signature/parent/scope/global-journal inclusion, redoes every artifact/repository/identity/retention check, treats `verified_at_utc` as the immutable original observation time, rederives every other payload field, and requires byte identity without opening a writer or changing the file. An invalid incumbent or missing/mismatched Accepted/Completed envelope fails and is never replaced. Tests cover absent creation, exact resume, ciphertext/container/profile/product/policy/CDHash tamper, stale run/epoch/retention, wrong root/tree/identity/keychain, existing mismatched/racing output, repository/artifact mutation during verification, and any attempted signing or write outside first publication.

The cached verify result is historical evidence, never a fresh-time lease. After each new certify Accepted reopens the independent verify-existing terminal pair, it freezes a lease effect plan and acquires custody through universal Prepared/dispatch/Adoption. `K4CustodyStableFDRowV1 = {role:"ciphertext"|"bundleDirectory"|"bundleManifest",volume_identity,locator_commitment,device_inode_generation_commitment,kind,mode,owner_commitment,link_count,length,content_or_tree_root}` occurs in exact role order and `stable_fd_set_digest = SHA256("QINAO-K4-CUSTODY-STABLE-FD-SET-V1\0" || uint64be(len(canonicalRows)) || canonicalRows)`. Adoption result is `K4CertificationCustodyLeaseBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,verify_existing_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,lease_id,lease_holder_process_instance:GatewayProcessInstanceIdentityV1,service_generation_binding,erasure_key:K4CryptoErasureKeyV1,authorized_artifact_locator_commitment,approved_volume_identity,stable_fd_rows,stable_fd_set_digest,erasure_registry_snapshot:K4CryptoErasureRegistrySnapshotV1,policy_acceptance_snapshot:K4PolicyAcceptanceSnapshotV1,key_revocation_snapshot:K4KeyRevocationSnapshotV1,acquired_at_utc}`. Identity fields project exact rows; registry snapshots carry HWM/root/target active-or-absent lookup, not bare counters.

The gateway holds stable FDs and the key's shared erasure-registry lock until Completed; erasure/delete/replace needs the exclusive side. After service restart, a frozen lease-rebind effect uses Prepared/dispatch/Adoption and may reacquire the same `lease_id` only when all rows/snapshots are byte-identical and the service chain reaches the final live tip; otherwise block. The root journal atomically commits Completed's final checks plus lease-registry release transition while the OS lock is held, fsyncs, then unlocks. Acquisition/rebind/release crash cuts are golden-tested; an unjournaled OS lock is never authority.

Before `GatewayFinalAllBodyV1`, that Accepted appends exactly one fresh `K4RetentionWindowObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,verify_existing_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,custody_lease_adoption_receipt_sha256,retention_policy_digest,retention_expires_at_utc,trusted_clock_observation_receipt_sha256,accepted_at_utc,checked_at_utc,erasure_registry_snapshot:K4CryptoErasureRegistrySnapshotV1,policy_acceptance_snapshot:K4PolicyAcceptanceSnapshotV1,key_revocation_snapshot:K4KeyRevocationSnapshotV1,retention_disposition:"currentlyActive",repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after}`. Retry reopens it; a successful historical Completed is not appended. Spec carries only expected policy IDs/digests/volume/clock/erasure-registry/custody contracts, never future receipt. Lookup is exact `absentAtHWM({matching_entry_receipt_sha256s:[]})` and activity snapshots remain active. Immediately before final-all, certification, handoff, and Completed the daemon appends a chained fresh clock observation; each payload embeds its own observation SHA, and each step rechecks nondecreasing registry roots/HWMs, target absence/activity, stable FDs, and time ≤ expiry under the held lock. An old clock observation cannot span a long-running gate. Goldens race expiry/rollback/reboot/revocation/erase/unlink/replace at every suffix boundary.

`global_journal_inclusion_proof` uses exact typed closure `transitive_terminal_prerequisite_envelopes(body)`. Task 0 pins these complete `K4VerifyInclusionSeedRowV1 = {field_path,locator_kind}` rows: child envelopes at `/policy_admission_receipt_sha256`, `/ciphertext_publication_receipt_sha256`, `/attested_receipt_sha256`, `/candidate_bundle_publication_receipt_sha256`, `/proof_git_transaction_receipt_sha256`, and `/k4_finalized_receipt_sha256`; signed externals at `/producer_authorization_sha256` and `/platform_policy_authorization_sha256`; content-not-locators at `/transaction_chain_root`, `/private_container_root`, `/projection_root`, and `/attestation_sha256`. Rows sort/unique by field path and use only `childEnvelope | terminalResult | signedExternal | contentDigestNotLocator`.

`GatewayReceiptDependencyEdgeRowV1 = {source_payload_schema_digest,source_field_path,edge_kind,target_payload_schema_digest_or_NA,cardinality:"exactlyOne"|"zeroOrOne"|"orderedMany"}` sorts/uniques by `(source_payload_schema_digest,source_field_path,edge_kind)` inside pinned `GatewayReceiptDependencyEdgeRegistryV1 = {schema_version:1,rows,registry_root,schema_digest,test_vector_set_sha256}`. It includes universal Child→parent Accepted→Accepted service registration, result child→unique successful Completed, Completed→Accepted plus complete service-generation registration/rebind chain, Adoption→Prepared/dispatch/recovery/reauthorization, and publication/support edges. BFS follows only registry edges; unknown schema/field/kind or ambiguous cardinality blocks. It excludes only current future child/Completed, fixes one authentication-key `gateway_journal_epoch`, and sorts exact `K4GlobalJournalInclusionRowV1 = {gateway_journal_epoch,receipt_sequence,receipt_sha256,journal_root,scope_id,scope_sequence,scope_root}` by `(gateway_journal_epoch,receipt_sequence,receipt_sha256)`. Removing an implicit edge or relabeling content as receipt fails.

For entitlements, compare semantic dictionaries, not plist bytes:

- host contains no `com.apple.security.hardened-process*` key;
- extension contains the exact seven-key release-template projection;
- host/extension team and application identifiers match the intended team and probe metadata;
- production Task 6 later repeats the same semantic projection check while separately validating its different production bundle IDs. It never whole-file `cmp`s isolated and production plists.

Freeze the complete, non-empty discovery surfaces of `scripts.test_attest_k4_external_artifact`, `scripts.test_capture_k4_platform_proof`, and `scripts.test_check_k4_platform_proof` into the K4-hardening validation profile consumed by Step 7. The RED proposal runs all three only through the candidate-review sandbox against its isolated result tree, with optimization disabled by the gateway and the candidate-index blobs/stdlib closure pinned. It also performs a native read-only absence observation for `docs/superpowers/evidence/qinao-k4-platform-proof-approved`; candidate Python cannot use a path-existence assertion as authority.

The native gateway derives HEAD/ref/index/worktree/history and the full trusted transaction chain; a shell cannot nominate the final commit/tree. It runs the fixed `observe-w0-final-candidate-v1` read contract, requires clean symbolic execution worktree, the complete monotonic transaction chain, exact approved-spec ancestry/blob/no-later-touch, K4 finalized seal, no merge/rename/touch-revert, and the three disjoint scope surfaces below. Its authenticated child payload is exactly `GatewayFinalCandidateObservationBodyV1 = {schema_version:1,status:"pass",source_commit,source_tree,final_commit,final_tree,final_git_transaction:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,transaction_chain_root,execution_worktree_identity,head_binding,index_tree,worktree_tree,clean_status_digest,approved_spec,approved_spec_parent_edge_derivation_contract_id,approved_spec_parent_edge_rows,approved_spec_parent_edge_verification_root,static_scope_contract_id,static_scope_root,w0_open_set_successor_git:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,w0_open_set_successor_root,approved_evidence_root,k4_final_publication:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,k4_finalized:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,history_derivation_contract_id,history_verification_rows,history_verification_root,repository_identity_before,repository_identity_after,observed_at_utc}`.

`ApprovedSpecParentEdgeImageV1 = absent([]) | present({git_mode,git_blob})`; `ApprovedSpecParentEdgeRowV1 = {edge_ordinal,parent_ordinal,parent_commit,commit,path,status,old_image:ApprovedSpecParentEdgeImageV1,new_image:ApprovedSpecParentEdgeImageV1}` is contiguous in deterministic topological `(commit,parent_ordinal,path)` order and covers every parent edge from the admitted source-exclusive ancestry through final, including every side parent before the observer rejects merges. It proves exact approved-spec blob continuity, no rename, no later touch, and touch-revert cannot disappear. Its root uses `QINAO-APPROVED-SPEC-PARENT-EDGE-VERIFICATION-V1\0` over the complete embedded rows. `GitTransactionHistoryEdgeRowV1 = {ordinal,parent_commit,commit,tree,git_transaction_terminal:GatewayTerminalResultReferenceV1,change_root,trusted_tree_advance_adoption_receipt_sha256}` is contiguous from bootstrap/provision genesis through final; every terminal reopens a successful Completed, parent/tree/change/advance joins the preceding row, and the last row byte-equals final fields. Its distinct root uses `QINAO-GIT-TRANSACTION-HISTORY-VERIFICATION-V1\0`. The two derivation contract IDs uniquely resolve distinct Task-0 registry rows/implementations/vectors and cannot be swapped.

`static_scope_root` uses `QINAO-W0-STATIC-SCOPE-V1\0` over the exact sorted 34-row tuple below; `w0_open_set_successor_root` uses the Task-0 domain over exactly 13 rows; `approved_evidence_root` remains independent. The observer reopens every terminal pair, bootstrap frozen/modify-base root, and embedded edge and requires the three path sets disjoint. Mutations omit/reorder/rename/touch-revert/merge an edge, substitute either root/domain/derivation, or alter a terminal/advance and must fail. The external observation leaf is only a cache of the complete result envelope and is not consumable until successful Completed. The provision spec repeats that terminal-result pair plus all commit/tree/chain fields. A W0 implementer then signs a `certificationDetached` spec from that pair. No mutable `run-state.json`, branch name, or caller-computed object ID is authority.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"
: "${QINAO_W0_CERTIFICATION_DIR:?stable approved encrypted certification root}"
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
GATE=/Library/PrivilegedHelperTools/com.qinao.execution-gate
FINAL_OBSERVATION="$QINAO_W0_CERTIFICATION_DIR/final-candidate-observation.json"
"$GATE" git-read \
  --source-selection-env QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION \
  --repository-root "$PWD" \
  --invocation-receipt-directory "$QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT/w0-final-candidate-observation" \
  --operation observe-w0-final-candidate-v1 -- \
  --static-scope-contract qinao-w0-static-scope-34-v1 \
  --manifest-sha256 1805ba8d60446a5890bdcf15a08f12dadc91268258021f32d37b0fd0f76cd1a7 \
  --result-receipt "$FINAL_OBSERVATION"
```

The exact static scope contains these 34 `(status,path)` records, globally sorted by path—no glob, basename, directory placeholder, or immutable approved-spec path is part of the modification set. Status is computed against the authenticated bootstrap-final tree: all 24 `M` paths must appear in `GatewayBootstrapFinalStateBodyV1.bootstrap_modify_base_rows`, while all 10 `A` paths must be absent there and in that tree.

- [ ] **Step 2: Provision the exact detached certification worktree**

At the Step-1 review stop, the signed provision spec must bind the observation receipt's final commit/tree and transaction-chain root, fixed slot `.worktrees/qinao-coreai-w0-certification`, detached mode, clean expected state, and no ref transition. Execute only:

```bash
set -euo pipefail
: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"
: "${QINAO_GATEWAY_RECEIPT_ROOT:?stable gateway receipt root}"

Expected: absent state creates exactly one registered detached worktree; a complete incumbent is reopened read-only. Ref-only, admin-dir-only, checkout-only, or other proper prefixes advance only with matching root-journal edges. Attached, dirty, moved, duplicate, wrong-commit/tree/index, foreign, or unjournaled state is BLOCKED and never deleted/reset.

- [ ] **Step 3: Sign one complete certification spec and run the local full gate**

After reopening the provision receipt, the W0 implementer signs `QinaoCertificationSpecV1`. It binds the fixed final transaction chain; detached receipt; approved K4 policy/evidence/private-artifact commitments; manifest blob/SHA; validation-registry/profile; the stable certification root; and fixed receipt names. The profile enumerates every Python module separately with exact non-zero test IDs/counts and test-surface root, the four non-empty Swift suites, both CI lane contracts, structural/owner/history/privacy checks, and K4 full-artifact verification. `unittest` aggregate exit zero or `Ran 0 tests` can never satisfy a module slot.

: "${QINAO_SOURCE_BASELINE_SELECTION:?signed source selection path}"

  --handoff-receipt "$W1_HANDOFF_RECEIPT"
```

The gateway first publishes authenticated `K4VerifyExistingBodyV1`, then supplies that exact receipt as a mandatory **initial** `final-all` input; it never creates a final-all receipt first and patches the dependency later. It runs every registered check in the detached tree under read-only sandbox/FDS, independently checks repository identity before and after each child, computes the closed structural root, and finally publishes certification then handoff. Expected counts remain manifest `32/8/1/2/4`, owner shapes `29/14/14/7`, review paths `54`, W0 candidate classes `0/0/0` with three typed N/A digests, positive historical candidate/receipt totals, and exact four Swift suites. Any test import/syntax/argument/parser/fixture error, zero discovery, omitted module, failed K4 revalidation, or repository drift is a typed failure—not RED and not PASS.

Run the identical `certify` command again. It must perform read-only revalidation and return byte-identical cached child/certification/handoff envelopes; no new effect or timestamp is allowed. Then verify the handoff through the native closed reader:

Optional operator archival may copy the spec and envelopes after eligibility, but it is non-authoritative and outside this v1 state machine; none is committed into the tree it certifies. Only the composite eligibility proof's final commit/tree/transaction-chain tuple is the W1 base. Branch names, mutable run state, dirty source worktree, preliminary evidence, an uncompleted handoff cache, or earlier receipts are not implementation authority.

## Plan Self-Review and Completion Criteria

Run once in the separately reviewed **pre-execution plan-publication session** before handing this plan to an executor. This read-only lint is outside the gateway-governed W0 execution described above, reads only this plan, creates no candidate/authority/evidence, and is not an acceptance substitute for any Task-0+ gate:

```bash
set -euo pipefail
PLAN=docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-19-qinao-coreai-agent-controlled-document-convergence.md
test -s "$PLAN"
python3 -B - "$PLAN" <<'PY'
import hashlib, re, sys
from pathlib import Path
text = Path(sys.argv[1]).read_bytes().decode("utf-8", "strict")
def require(condition, code):
    if not condition:
        raise SystemExit(code)
require(len(re.findall(r"^### Task [0-6]:", text, re.MULTILINE)) == 7, "task-count")
fences = len(re.findall(r"^```", text, re.MULTILINE))
require(fences > 0 and fences % 2 == 0, "fence-balance")
bad = [
    "T" + "BD", "TO" + "DO", "FIX" + "ME", "implement la" + "ter",
    "fill in det" + "ails", "Similar t" + "o", "Add appropr" + "iate",
    "Write tests for the ab" + "ove",
]
require(not any(value in text for value in bad), "placeholder-token")
required = [
    "architecture design, convergence master, and five domain plans",
    "29 owners / 14 M allowlist entries / 14 create permissions / 7 controlled documents",
    "f5901898684229cb56a61afecb8ea52cc3d52b2b",
    "e544727c57ffd340fde274b8caeded6bd39bde09",
    "15e82ec63b9e9bf1ebf17575799b9474061a292e",
    "a5599c402b110951aee108d18625153d3d90ddef9c93933c580970c0be0b71dd",
    "0fb559f0f6e231a88ac02ea002d3ecd248e2b515",
    "8dc78834cde4e329284cf505f9e3f99763df5529",
    "36f2a966c7f9f69987494b867c3dcf96b69a5766",
    "2d4ef9466427970a6db5e567253bae2b2825010a",
    "stable slotID", "ContextContinuityManifest", "readSetRoot",
    "expectedSourceManifest", "pccRespondEntered",
    "two all-member barriers", "per-member start gate",
    "w0_normative_gate_and_isolated_probe_only_no_production_create",
    "w0_normative_gate_and_isolated_probe_only_no_production_extension",
    "w0_no_extension_schema_fixture",
    "production publication remains disabled through W5",
    "product or marketing surface explicitly claims or selects",
    "Join 1", "immutable E5", "Join 2",
]
require(all(value in text for value in required), "required-witness")
require("python3 -m py" + "_compile" not in text, "forbidden-pycompile")
require(re.search(r"owner-ledger: PASS candidates=\d+", text) is None, "legacy-owner-grammar")
anchor = "- [ ] **Step 3: Create the canonical manifest**"
manifest = text.split(anchor, 1)[1].split("```json\n", 1)[1].split("\n```", 1)[0]
require(len(manifest.encode()) == 14065, "manifest-byte-count")
require(hashlib.sha256(manifest.encode()).hexdigest() == "ca528691e684b0dc1b44551129a202899234372fdcca1ecf5858406d7ee08b39", "manifest-sha256")
require(all(line == line.rstrip(" \t") for line in text.splitlines()), "trailing-whitespace")
PY
git diff --check -- "$PLAN"
```

The implementation governed by this published plan is complete only when:

- every adopted addendum decision has an existing owner, exact controlled-document task, wave, test suite, and gate;
- the canonical manifest is byte-pinned, candidate-index-bound, root-confined, cumulative, mutation-tested, and emits a structured receipt;
- review candidate and both CI lanes are enabled and non-duplicative;
- all seven controlled documents and ledger are atomically consistent at 29/14/14/7;
- the K4 proof is produced by the audited tool under release Xcode/profile/device inputs, committed as a proof-only tree, and only then passes the independent platform lane;
- final `--lane all` passes on a clean detached exact tree;
- no W1 production implementation begins while any W0 condition is red.
